===== BEGIN SOURCE 62 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_book.py | sha256=4079306928342b9b43178f6ad6e1106c70dd8198f9e83b2d09e8cb3f8e8a270e =====
"""Bounded offline Exchange level2_batch replay; no socket or live imports."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

from instant_coverage import CoverageMonitor, _integer


VERSION = "instant-book-replay-v1-draft-amendment-1"
CONTRACT = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    / "docs/research/INSTANT_BOOK_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
)
MAX_LEVELS = 100000
MAX_QUOTE_AGE_MS = 10000


def _decimal(value, *, positive):
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError("boolean is not a book amount")
    try:
        result = Decimal(str(value))
        number = float(result)
    except (InvalidOperation, ValueError, OverflowError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("invalid book amount") from exc
    if (
        not result.is_finite()
        or not math.isfinite(number)
        or result < 0
        or (positive and (result <= 0 or number <= 0))
    ):
        raise ValueError("invalid book amount")
    if result > 0 and number <= 0:
        raise ValueError("book amount underflows reader representation")
    return result


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BookState:
    product_id: str
    receipt_ts_ms: int
    available_ts_ms: int
    receipt_seq: int
    connection_id: int | str
    reason: str | None
    observation_ts_ms: int | None
    book_change_ts_ms: int | None
    bbo_change_ts_ms: int | None
    best: tuple[str, str, str, str] | None
    exchange_time: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BookView:
    version: str
    product_id: str
    cutoff_ts_ms: int
    knowledge_asof_ts_ms: int
    book_state_available: bool
    reason: str | None
    bid: str | None
    ask: str | None
    bid_size: str | None
    ask_size: str | None
    mid: float | None
    sampled_pressure: float | None
    observation_ts_ms: int | None
    book_change_ts_ms: int | None
    bbo_change_ts_ms: int | None
    observation_age_ms: int | None
    book_change_age_ms: int | None
    bbo_change_age_ms: int | None
    available_ts_ms: int | None
    connection_id: int | str | None
    exchange_time: str | None
    full_feed_verified: bool
    target_sample_adapter_supported: bool
    contract_sha256: str
    implementation_sha256: str
    monitor_sha256: str


class BookReplay:
    """Last fully processed book state, with original receipt age and known-at time.

    Quote state is not complete feed certification. The existing receipt/ID
    monitor retains its own narrower scope. No state here enters Sample/V1/V2.
    An injectable processing clock is a trusted test input, not historical proof.
    """

    def __init__(
        self, products, *, clock_ms=None, packet_limit=None, extended_capacity=False
    ):
        raw = CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        cfg = json.loads(raw)
        if (cfg["version"], cfg["max_levels_per_product"], cfg["max_quote_age_ms"]) != (
            VERSION,
            MAX_LEVELS,
            MAX_QUOTE_AGE_MS,
        ):
            raise ValueError("book contract and implementation differ")
        self.monitor = CoverageMonitor(
            products,
            quote_channel="level2_batch",
            clock_ms=clock_ms,
            packet_limit=packet_limit,
            extended_capacity=extended_capacity,
        )
        self.contract_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        self.source_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        self.connection = None
        self.books = {}
        self._startup_books = None
        self.last_heartbeat = {}
        self.history = {p: [] for p in self.monitor.products}
        self.awaiting_ack = set(self.monitor.products)
        self.completed = {}  # receipt_seq -> this packet's book completion.
        self.failed = False

    def _invalidate(self, product, reason):
        self.books.pop(product, None)
        self.pending.append((product, reason))

    @staticmethod
    def _best(book):
        bid, ask = max(book["buy"]), min(book["sell"])
        if bid >= ask:
            raise ValueError("locked or crossed book")
        return tuple(map(str, (bid, ask, book["buy"][bid], book["sell"][ask])))

    def _snapshot(self, message):
        book = {"buy": {}, "sell": {}}
        for side, key in (("buy", "bids"), ("sell", "asks")):
            levels = message.get(key)
            if not isinstance(levels, list) or not levels or len(levels) > MAX_LEVELS:
                raise ValueError("bounded nonempty book side required")
            for row in levels:
                if not isinstance(row, list) or len(row) != 2:
                    raise ValueError("snapshot level must be [price,size]")
                price, size = (_decimal(v, positive=True) for v in row)
                if price in book[side]:
                    raise ValueError("duplicate snapshot price")
                book[side][price] = size
        return book

    def _update(self, previous, message):
        changes = message.get("changes")
        if not isinstance(changes, list) or not changes or len(changes) > MAX_LEVELS:
            raise ValueError("bounded nonempty change batch required")
        book = {side: previous[side].copy() for side in ("buy", "sell")}
        for row in changes:
            if not isinstance(row, list) or len(row) != 3 or row[0] not in book:
                raise ValueError("update must be [buy_or_sell,price,absolute_size]")
            side, price, size = (
                row[0],
                _decimal(row[1], positive=True),
                _decimal(row[2], positive=False),
            )
            if size == 0:
                book[side].pop(price, None)
            else:
                book[side][price] = size
        if not book["buy"] or not book["sell"]:
            raise ValueError("book side exhausted")
        return book

    def ingest(self, envelope):
        if self.failed:
            raise ValueError("book replay failed; no more input accepted")
        try:
            self.monitor.ingest(envelope)
            self.pending = []
            self._consume(envelope)
            finished = _integer(self.monitor.clock_ms())
            if finished < self.monitor.last[3]:
                raise ValueError("book processing clock regressed")
            self.monitor.last = (*self.monitor.last[:3], finished)
            receipt, seq = envelope["receipt_ts_ms"], envelope["receipt_seq"]
            # Monitor records from this packet are not book knowledge until the
            # book has processed the same packet (e.g. a heartbeat gap).
            self.completed[seq] = max(finished, receipt + 1)
            for product, reason in self.pending:
                book = self.books.get(product)
                self.history[product].append(
                    BookState(
                        product,
                        receipt,
                        max(finished, receipt + 1),
                        seq,
                        self.connection,
                        reason,
                        book["observation"] if book else None,
                        book["book_change"] if book else None,
                        book["bbo_change"] if book else None,
                        self._best(book) if book else None,
                        book["exchange_time"] if book else None,
                    )
                )
        except Exception:
            self.failed = True
            raise

    def _consume(self, env):
        if env.get("source", "ws") != "ws":
            return
        receipt = env["receipt_ts_ms"]
        message = env["message"]
        kind, product = message.get("type"), message.get("product_id")
        if self.connection != env["connection_id"]:
            self.connection = env["connection_id"]
            self._startup_books = {} if kind == "connection_opened" else None
            self.last_heartbeat.clear()
            self.awaiting_ack.update(self.history)
            for product in self.history:
                self._invalidate(product, "subscription_unconfirmed")
        if kind in ("connection_closed", "error", "queue_overflow") or (
            isinstance(kind, str) and kind.startswith("audit_")
        ):
            self._startup_books = None
            self.last_heartbeat.clear()
            self.awaiting_ack.update(self.history)
            for pid in self.history:
                self._invalidate(pid, kind)
            return
        if kind == "subscriptions":
            staged = self._startup_books or {}
            self._startup_books = None
            for pid in self.history:
                if pid not in self.monitor.active:
                    self.last_heartbeat.pop(pid, None)
                    self.awaiting_ack.add(pid)
                    self._invalidate(pid, "subscription_unconfirmed")
                else:
                    self.awaiting_ack.discard(pid)
                    if pid in staged:
                        # Stamp activation with this acknowledgment. The staged
                        # book keeps its original observation and change ages.
                        self.books[pid] = staged[pid]
                        self.pending.append((pid, None))
                    elif pid not in self.books:
                        self._invalidate(pid, "awaiting_snapshot")
            return
        if product not in self.history:
            return
        if kind == "heartbeat" and product in self.monitor.active:
            previous = self.last_heartbeat.get(product)
            if previous is not None and receipt - previous > 5000:
                self._invalidate(product, "heartbeat_gap_requires_snapshot")
            self.last_heartbeat[product] = receipt
        if kind not in ("snapshot", "l2update"):
            return
        unconfirmed = product not in self.monitor.active or product in self.awaiting_ack
        staging = unconfirmed and self._startup_books is not None
        if unconfirmed and not staging:
            self._invalidate(product, "subscription_unconfirmed")
            return
        books = self._startup_books if staging else self.books
        previous = books.get(product)
        if kind == "l2update" and previous is None:
            if not staging:
                self._invalidate(product, "awaiting_snapshot")
            return
        try:
            candidate = (
                self._snapshot(message)
                if kind == "snapshot"
                else self._update(previous, message)
            )
            if sum(len(candidate[s]) for s in ("buy", "sell")) > MAX_LEVELS:
                raise ValueError("total book level bound exceeded")
            best = self._best(
                candidate
            )  # Validate the final whole batch, never intermediate states.
        except (ValueError, TypeError, InvalidOperation):
            if staging:
                books.pop(product, None)
                return
            self._invalidate(
                product,
                (
                    "invalid_book_snapshot"
                    if kind == "snapshot"
                    else "invalid_book_update"
                ),
            )
            return
        changed = previous is None or any(
            candidate[s] != previous[s] for s in ("buy", "sell")
        )
        # Compare amounts, not spellings: "3" -> "3.00" is not a BBO change.
        bbo_changed = previous is None or tuple(map(Decimal, best)) != tuple(
            map(Decimal, self._best(previous))
        )
        candidate.update(
            observation=receipt,
            book_change=receipt if changed else previous["book_change"],
            bbo_change=receipt if bbo_changed else previous["bbo_change"],
            exchange_time=(
                message.get("time") if isinstance(message.get("time"), str) else None
            ),
        )
        books[product] = candidate
        if not staging:
            self.pending.append((product, None))

    def _validate_view(self, product, cutoff, asof):
        if self.failed:
            raise ValueError("failed book replay cannot be queried")
        _integer(cutoff)
        _integer(asof)
        if product not in self.history or cutoff % 5000 or asof < cutoff:
            raise ValueError("known product, grid cutoff and causal asof required")

    def _records_at(self, product, cutoff, asof):
        state = next(
            (
                r
                for r in reversed(self.history[product])
                if r.receipt_ts_ms < cutoff and r.available_ts_ms <= asof
            ),
            None,
        )
        latest = next(
            (
                r
                for r in reversed(self.monitor.transport)
                if r["product_id"] == product
                and r["receipt_ts_ms"] < cutoff
                and r["available_ts_ms"] <= asof
                and self.completed[r["receipt_seq"]] <= asof
            ),
            None,
        )
        return state, latest

    def view(self, product, cutoff, *, asof=None):
        asof = cutoff if asof is None else asof
        self._validate_view(product, cutoff, asof)
        return self._view_from_records(
            product, cutoff, asof, *self._records_at(product, cutoff, asof)
        )

    def _view_from_records(self, product, cutoff, asof, state, latest):
        reason = state.reason if state else "no_known_book"
        age = (
            cutoff - state.observation_ts_ms
            if state and state.observation_ts_ms is not None
            else None
        )
        if state and reason is None:
            if latest is None or latest["reason"] != "heartbeat_observed":
                reason = "heartbeat_unavailable"
            elif cutoff - latest["receipt_ts_ms"] > 5000:
                reason = "stale_heartbeat"
            elif age > MAX_QUOTE_AGE_MS:
                reason = "stale_book_observation"
        best = state.best if state and reason is None else (None,) * 4
        if best is None:
            best = (None,) * 4
        bid, ask, bid_size, ask_size = best
        return BookView(
            VERSION,
            product,
            cutoff,
            asof,
            reason is None,
            reason,
            bid,
            ask,
            bid_size,
            ask_size,
            float(bid) / 2 + float(ask) / 2 if bid is not None else None,
            (
                float(
                    (Decimal(bid_size) - Decimal(ask_size))
                    / (Decimal(bid_size) + Decimal(ask_size))
                )
                if bid_size is not None
                else None
            ),
            state.observation_ts_ms if state else None,
            state.book_change_ts_ms if state else None,
            state.bbo_change_ts_ms if state else None,
            age,
            (
                cutoff - state.book_change_ts_ms
                if state and state.book_change_ts_ms is not None
                else None
            ),
            (
                cutoff - state.bbo_change_ts_ms
                if state and state.bbo_change_ts_ms is not None
                else None
            ),
            state.available_ts_ms if state else None,
            state.connection_id if state else None,
            state.exchange_time if state else None,
            False,
            False,
            self.contract_sha256,
            self.source_sha256,
            self.monitor.source_sha256,
        )

"""Offline, evidence-timed trade coverage monitor. No sockets or live hooks.

Heartbeat ID reconciliation is a scoped receipt/ID check, not authentication
or proof of book continuity. Processing time is measured now on replay; it
must never be replaced by the historical receipt time.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from bisect import bisect_left
from collections import Counter
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time

VERSION = "instant-coverage-monitor-v1-draft-amendment-1"
CONTRACT = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    / "docs/research/INSTANT_COVERAGE_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
)
MAX_PACKETS = 100000
WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT = 250000
TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT = 500000
MAX_ID_SPAN = 100000
MAX_HEARTBEAT_GAP_MS = 5000
# Amendment 1: adjacent failed proofs may be re-checked as one span. One race
# spans two proofs, so four allows two consecutive races. Engineering limits,
# not fitted to outcomes; the span limit also bounds added proof latency.
MAX_MERGED_PROOFS = 4
MAX_MERGED_SPAN_MS = 10000
MERGEABLE_REASONS = ("missing_live_match_ids", "receipt_ids_outside_watermarks")
INTERVAL_SCOPE = "receipt_interval_ws_trade_ids_only"
MERGED_SCOPE = "merged_adjacent_receipt_intervals_ws_trade_ids_only"


def _integer(value):
    if type(value) is not int or value < 0:
        raise ValueError("nonnegative integer required")
    return value


def _hash(path):
    result = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            result.update(block)
    return result.hexdigest()


def _amount(value):
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError("invalid match amount")
    try:
        result = Decimal(str(value))
    except InvalidOperation as exc:
        raise ValueError("invalid match amount") from exc
    if not result.is_finite() or result <= 0:
        raise ValueError("invalid match amount")
    return result


class CoverageMonitor:
    """Retain bounded evidence and answer known-at queries without rewriting them.

    clock_ms is injectable for deterministic tests. It is a trusted processing
    clock, not a way to authenticate historical knowledge. CLI always uses now.
    Results deliberately cannot be passed to ReplayStore.add_health: this
    monitor verifies trades, not quotes, queue durability or exchange latency.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        products,
        *,
        quote_channel="ticker",
        clock_ms=None,
        packet_limit=None,
        extended_capacity=False,
    ):
        packet_ceiling = (
            TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT if extended_capacity else WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT
        )
        if (
            packet_limit is not None
            and not 1 <= _integer(packet_limit) <= packet_ceiling
        ):
            raise ValueError("coverage packet limit exceeds the supported range")
        self._packet_limit = packet_limit
        if (
            not isinstance(products, (list, tuple))
            or not products
            or len(products) > 32
            or any(not isinstance(p, str) or not p for p in products)
            or len(set(products)) != len(products)
            or quote_channel not in ("ticker", "level2_batch")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "explicit unique products and supported quote channel required"
            )
        raw = CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        cfg = json.loads(raw)
        merge = cfg.get("reconciliation") or {}
        if (
            cfg["version"],
            cfg["max_packets"],
            cfg["max_id_span"],
            cfg["max_heartbeat_gap_ms"],
            merge.get("max_merged_proofs"),
            merge.get("max_merged_span_ms"),
            tuple(merge.get("mergeable_reasons", ())),
        ) != (
            VERSION,
            MAX_PACKETS,
            MAX_ID_SPAN,
            MAX_HEARTBEAT_GAP_MS,
            MAX_MERGED_PROOFS,
            MAX_MERGED_SPAN_MS,
            MERGEABLE_REASONS,
        ):
            raise ValueError("coverage contract and implementation differ")
        self.contract_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        self.source_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        self.products = tuple(products)
        self.required_channels = {"matches", "heartbeat", quote_channel}
        self.clock_ms = clock_ms or (lambda: time.time_ns() // 1000000)
        self.last = None
        self.connection = None
        self.active = set()
        self.beats = {}
        # Continuity epoch per product, advanced at every lifecycle barrier in
        # receipt-sequence order: connection change, audit/transport/queue
        # failure, or lost/re-added required membership. Receipt milliseconds
        # and a reused connection ID cannot identify a barrier; this can.
        self.epochs = {p: 0 for p in products}
        self.seen = {}
        self.trades = {p: [] for p in products}
        self.times = {p: [] for p in products}
        self.proofs = []
        self.transport = []
        self.conflicts = []
        self.failed = False
        self.packet_count = 0

    def _state(self, product, receipt, known, seq, reason):
        self.transport.append(
            dict(
                product_id=product,
                receipt_ts_ms=receipt,
                available_ts_ms=known,
                receipt_seq=seq,
                connection_id=self.connection,
                reason=reason,
            )
        )

    def _barrier(self, products):
        for product in products:
            self.epochs[product] += 1
            self.beats.pop(product, None)

    def ingest(self, envelope):
        if self.failed:
            raise ValueError("failed monitor cannot accept more input")
        try:
            counts = tuple(
                len(rows) for rows in (self.proofs, self.transport, self.conflicts)
            )
            self._ingest(envelope)
            # The evidence is not known before its computation finishes. A
            # replay's processing clock is now, not the saved event receipt.
            finished = _integer(self.clock_ms())
            if finished < self.last[3]:
                raise ValueError("processing completion clock regressed")
            self.last = (*self.last[:3], finished)
            known = max(finished, self.last[1] + 1)
            for rows, count in zip(
                (self.proofs, self.transport, self.conflicts), counts
            ):
                for record in rows[count:]:
                    record["available_ts_ms"] = known
        except Exception:
            self.failed = True
            raise

    def _ingest(self, env):
        self.packet_count += 1
        limit = MAX_PACKETS if self._packet_limit is None else self._packet_limit
        if self.packet_count > limit:
            raise ValueError("packet bound exceeded; prefix is not a complete capture")
        seq, receipt, mono = map(
            _integer,
            (env["receipt_seq"], env["receipt_ts_ms"], env["receipt_monotonic_ns"]),
        )
        processed = _integer(self.clock_ms())
        if processed < receipt or (
            self.last
            and (
                seq != self.last[0] + 1
                or receipt < self.last[1]
                or mono < self.last[2]
                or processed < self.last[3]
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("clock, receipt order or local logger sequence failed")
        self.last = (seq, receipt, mono, processed)
        # A receipt at x is excluded from state observed strictly before x.
        known = max(processed, receipt + 1)
        source = env.get("source", "ws")
        if source not in ("ws", "rest", "rest_recovery"):
            raise ValueError("unknown source; never disguise recovery as WebSocket")
        if source != "ws":
            return
        connection = env["connection_id"]
        if (
            type(connection) not in (int, str)
            or connection == ""
            or (type(connection) is int and connection < 0)
        ):
            raise ValueError("connection identity required")
        if connection != self.connection:
            self.connection = connection
            self.active.clear()
            self._barrier(self.products)
            for product in self.products:
                self._state(product, receipt, known, seq, "subscription_unconfirmed")
        message = env["message"]
        kind, product = message.get("type"), message.get("product_id")
        # scripts/coinbase_feed_audit.py records its failures as audit_* markers.
        if kind in ("connection_closed", "error", "queue_overflow") or (
            isinstance(kind, str) and kind.startswith("audit_")
        ):
            self.active.clear()
            self._barrier(self.products)
            for pid in self.products:
                self._state(pid, receipt, known, seq, kind)
            return
        if kind == "subscriptions":
            subscriptions = {}
            for channel in message["channels"]:
                name, ids = channel["name"], channel["product_ids"]
                if (
                    not isinstance(name, str)
                    or not isinstance(ids, list)
                    or any(not isinstance(p, str) for p in ids)
                ):
                    raise ValueError("invalid subscription acknowledgement")
                # Coinbase can acknowledge level2_batch under its legacy name.
                channel = "level2_batch" if name == "level2_50" else name
                subscriptions.setdefault(channel, set()).update(ids)
            active = {
                p
                for p in self.products
                if all(p in subscriptions.get(c, ()) for c in self.required_channels)
            }
            for pid in self.products:
                # Lost or newly (re)gained membership ends continuity; a
                # healthy repeated acknowledgement does not.
                if pid not in active or pid not in self.active:
                    self._barrier((pid,))
                self._state(
                    pid,
                    receipt,
                    known,
                    seq,
                    (
                        "awaiting_heartbeat"
                        if pid in active
                        else "subscription_unconfirmed"
                    ),
                )
            self.active = active
            return
        if product not in self.trades:
            return
        if kind == "match":
            trade_id = _integer(message["trade_id"])
            price, size = _amount(message.get("price")), _amount(message.get("size"))
            side = message.get("side")
            if side not in ("buy", "sell"):
                side = None
            key, signature = (product, trade_id), (price, size, side)
            if key in self.seen:
                old, first_receipt = self.seen[key]
                if old != signature:
                    self.conflicts.append(
                        dict(
                            product_id=product,
                            start_ts_ms=first_receipt,
                            end_ts_ms=receipt + 1,
                            available_ts_ms=known,
                        )
                    )
                return
            self.seen[key] = (signature, receipt)
            self.times[product].append(receipt)
            # Attribution keeps the first receipt time and its receipt-order
            # epoch; a barrier at the same millisecond never moves it.
            self.trades[product].append(
                (receipt, trade_id, connection, self.epochs[product])
            )
        elif kind == "heartbeat" and product in self.active:
            watermark = _integer(message["last_trade_id"])
            previous = self.beats.get(product)
            epoch = self.epochs[product]
            if previous:
                start, low, prior_seq, prior_epoch = previous
                if prior_epoch != epoch:
                    raise ValueError("baseline survived a continuity barrier")
                if receipt > start:
                    left, right = (
                        bisect_left(self.times[product], t) for t in (start, receipt)
                    )
                    ids = {
                        tid
                        for _, tid, owner, era in self.trades[product][left:right]
                        if owner == connection and era == epoch
                    }
                    delta = watermark - low
                    reason = None
                    if receipt - start > MAX_HEARTBEAT_GAP_MS:
                        reason = "heartbeat_gap"
                    elif delta < 0:
                        reason = "watermark_regression"
                    elif delta > MAX_ID_SPAN:
                        reason = "id_span_bound"
                    elif any(not low < tid <= watermark for tid in ids):
                        reason = "receipt_ids_outside_watermarks"
                    elif len(ids) != delta:
                        reason = "missing_live_match_ids"
                    self.proofs.append(
                        dict(
                            product_id=product,
                            start_ts_ms=start,
                            end_ts_ms=receipt,
                            available_ts_ms=known,
                            lower_trade_id_exclusive=low,
                            upper_trade_id_inclusive=watermark,
                            observed_unique_trade_count=len(ids),
                            expected_trade_count=max(delta, 0),
                            verified=reason is None,
                            reason=reason,
                            connection_id=connection,
                            continuity_epoch=epoch,
                            start_heartbeat_seq=prior_seq,
                            end_heartbeat_seq=seq,
                            scope=INTERVAL_SCOPE,
                        )
                    )
                    if reason in MERGEABLE_REASONS:
                        self._reconcile(product, connection, epoch)
                # Same-millisecond heartbeat baselines are ambiguous: retain the
                # older watermark so a new one cannot erase boundary trades.
                else:
                    self._state(
                        product, receipt, known, seq, "heartbeat_time_collision"
                    )
                    return
            self.beats[product] = (receipt, watermark, seq, epoch)
            self._state(product, receipt, known, seq, "heartbeat_observed")

    def _reconcile(self, product, connection, epoch):
        """Re-check the trailing run of adjacent failed proofs as one span.

        Only a heartbeat/match ordering race is repairable: the merged span must
        satisfy the same exact rule (expected IDs == unique IDs received on this
        connection), so a truly lost ID still fails. Trade receipt times and
        bucket counts are untouched. ingest stamps the merged proof with the
        closing heartbeat's processing completion, so nothing is backdated.
        """
        run = []
        for proof in reversed(self.proofs):
            if proof["product_id"] != product:
                continue
            # Verified intervals and earlier merged proofs have no reason, so
            # they end the run: merges stay disjoint and a nested race fails closed.
            if (
                len(run) == MAX_MERGED_PROOFS
                or proof["reason"] not in MERGEABLE_REASONS
                or proof["connection_id"] != connection
                or proof["continuity_epoch"] != epoch
                or (
                    run
                    and (
                        proof["end_ts_ms"] != run[-1]["start_ts_ms"]
                        or proof["upper_trade_id_inclusive"]
                        != run[-1]["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
                    )
                )
            ):
                break
            run.append(proof)
        # Smallest closing run first, so two separate races stay separate proofs.
        for size in range(2, len(run) + 1):
            first, last = run[size - 1], run[0]
            start, end = first["start_ts_ms"], last["end_ts_ms"]
            low = first["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
            high = last["upper_trade_id_inclusive"]
            if end - start > MAX_MERGED_SPAN_MS or high - low > MAX_ID_SPAN:
                return
            left, right = (bisect_left(self.times[product], t) for t in (start, end))
            ids = {
                tid
                for _, tid, owner, era in self.trades[product][left:right]
                if owner == connection and era == epoch
            }
            if ids != set(range(low + 1, high + 1)):
                continue
            self.proofs.append(
                dict(
                    product_id=product,
                    start_ts_ms=start,
                    end_ts_ms=end,
                    available_ts_ms=None,
                    lower_trade_id_exclusive=low,
                    upper_trade_id_inclusive=high,
                    observed_unique_trade_count=len(ids),
                    expected_trade_count=high - low,
                    verified=True,
                    reason=None,
                    connection_id=connection,
                    continuity_epoch=epoch,
                    start_heartbeat_seq=first["start_heartbeat_seq"],
                    end_heartbeat_seq=last["end_heartbeat_seq"],
                    scope=MERGED_SCOPE,
                    merged_proof_count=size,
                    supersedes_end_heartbeat_seqs=sorted(
                        p["end_heartbeat_seq"] for p in run[:size]
                    ),
                )
            )
            return

    def _validate_assessment(self, product, start, end, asof):
        if self.failed:
            raise ValueError("failed monitor cannot produce assessments")
        for value in (start, end, asof):
            _integer(value)
        if product not in self.trades or start >= end or asof < end:
            raise ValueError(
                "known product, completed interval and asof >= end required"
            )

    def assess(self, product, start, end, *, asof):
        self._validate_assessment(product, start, end, asof)
        state = next(
            (
                s
                for s in reversed(self.transport)
                if s["product_id"] == product and s["available_ts_ms"] <= asof
            ),
            None,
        )
        spans = [
            p
            for p in self.proofs
            if p["product_id"] == product
            and p["start_ts_ms"] < end
            and p["end_ts_ms"] > start
            and p["available_ts_ms"] <= asof
        ]
        conflict = any(
            c["product_id"] == product
            and c["start_ts_ms"] < end
            and c["end_ts_ms"] > start
            and c["available_ts_ms"] <= asof
            for c in self.conflicts
        )
        return self._assess_filtered(product, start, end, asof, state, spans, conflict)

    def _assess_filtered(self, product, start, end, asof, state, spans, conflict):
        transport_reason = (
            state["reason"] if state else "no_known_subscription_or_heartbeat"
        )
        if state and state["reason"] == "heartbeat_observed":
            transport_reason = (
                None
                if asof - state["receipt_ts_ms"] <= MAX_HEARTBEAT_GAP_MS
                else "stale_heartbeat"
            )
        # A failed proof is superseded only once a merged proof covering it is
        # known. The failed record itself is never rewritten.
        superseded = {
            seq
            for p in spans
            if p["scope"] == MERGED_SCOPE
            for seq in p["supersedes_end_heartbeat_seqs"]
        }
        spans = [
            p
            for p in spans
            if p["scope"] == MERGED_SCOPE or p["end_heartbeat_seq"] not in superseded
        ]
        reason = (
            "conflicting_trade_id"
            if conflict
            else next((p["reason"] for p in spans if not p["verified"]), None)
        )
        cursor, available, prior, merged_used = start, end, None, 0
        if reason is None:
            for span in sorted(spans, key=lambda p: (p["start_ts_ms"], p["end_ts_ms"])):
                if span["end_ts_ms"] <= cursor:
                    continue
                if span["start_ts_ms"] > cursor:
                    break
                # Touching timestamps alone do not prove the ID sequence
                # continued, and nothing joins across a continuity barrier.
                if prior and (
                    span["connection_id"] != prior["connection_id"]
                    or span["continuity_epoch"] != prior["continuity_epoch"]
                    or span["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
                    != prior["upper_trade_id_inclusive"]
                ):
                    break
                prior = span
                merged_used += span["scope"] == MERGED_SCOPE
                cursor = span["end_ts_ms"]
                available = max(available, span["available_ts_ms"])
                if cursor >= end:
                    break
            if cursor < end:
                reason = "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"
        return dict(
            version=VERSION,
            product_id=product,
            start_ts_ms=start,
            end_ts_ms=end,
            knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
            receipt_id_check_passed=reason is None,
            receipt_id_check_reason=reason,
            receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms=available if reason is None else None,
            receipt_id_check_merged_proofs=merged_used if reason is None else 0,
            transport_observed_ok=transport_reason is None,
            transport_reason=transport_reason,
            full_feed_verified=False,
            quote_coverage_verified=False,
            quote_reason="order_book_not_verified_by_this_monitor",
            scope="offline_receipt_and_trade_id_evidence_not_live_eligibility",
            contract_sha256=self.contract_sha256,
            implementation_sha256=self.source_sha256,
        )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("packets", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--products", nargs="+", required=True)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--quote-channel", choices=("ticker", "level2_batch"), default="ticker"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out-dir",
        type=Path,
        required=True,
        help="new absent directory outside input directory",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    source, output = args.packets.resolve(), args.out_dir.resolve()
    if output == source.parent or source.parent in output.parents:
        raise ValueError("output must be outside the preserved input directory")
    before = _hash(source)
    monitor = CoverageMonitor(args.products, quote_channel=args.quote_channel)
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    try:
        opener = gzip.open if source.suffix == ".gz" else open
        with opener(source, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            for line in stream:
                monitor.ingest(json.loads(line))
        if not monitor.packet_count:
            raise ValueError("empty input is not a completed coverage check")
        if _hash(source) != before:
            raise ValueError("source changed while checking")
        intervals = [p for p in monitor.proofs if p["scope"] == INTERVAL_SCOPE]
        repaired = {
            (p["product_id"], seq)
            for p in monitor.proofs
            if p["scope"] == MERGED_SCOPE
            for seq in p["supersedes_end_heartbeat_seqs"]
        }
        proof_path = output / "trade_coverage_proofs.jsonl"
        with proof_path.open("x", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as stream:
            for proof in monitor.proofs:
                stream.write(json.dumps(proof, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
        report = dict(
            version=VERSION,
            status="complete_offline_check_only",
            input_sha256=before,
            implementation_sha256=monitor.source_sha256,
            contract_sha256=monitor.contract_sha256,
            packet_count=monitor.packet_count,
            unique_ws_matches=len(monitor.seen),
            proof_count=len(intervals),
            verified_proofs=sum(p["verified"] for p in intervals),
            proof_reasons=dict(Counter(p["reason"] or "verified" for p in intervals)),
            merged_proofs=len(monitor.proofs) - len(intervals),
            failed_proofs_reconciled=len(repaired),
            failed_proofs_unreconciled=sum(
                not p["verified"]
                and (p["product_id"], p["end_heartbeat_seq"]) not in repaired
                for p in intervals
            ),
            products=list(monitor.products),
            required_channels=sorted(monitor.required_channels),
            first_proof_available_ts_ms=min(
                (p["available_ts_ms"] for p in monitor.proofs), default=None
            ),
            source_last_receipt_ts_ms=monitor.last[1] if monitor.last else None,
            proof_file_sha256=_hash(proof_path),
            conflicts=len(monitor.conflicts),
            full_feed_verified=False,
            feed_gate_pass=False,
            quote_coverage_verified=False,
            live_integration=False,
            timing="actual_offline_processing_clock_not_historical_receipt_backdating",
        )
        (output / "REPORT.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(report, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
            newline="\n",
        )
        print(json.dumps(report, sort_keys=True))
    except Exception as exc:
        (output / "FAILURE.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(dict(status="failed", reason=str(exc))) + "\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
            newline="\n",
        )
        raise


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 64 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_delayed_decision.py | sha256=2312423761c7220fee5f9528183e641c01638b1c6ca0b352d15b73abc204a16d =====
"""Offline decision snapshots and later cutoff-anchored grading."""

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

from instant_coverage import _integer
from instant_input_evidence import BENCHMARK, GRID_MS, _json, _sha
from instant_publication import PublicationTiming, publication_timing
from instant_targets import V2_CONTRACT_PATH, load_contract
from instant_timed_baseline import PACE_MS
from instant_timed_features import build_timed_features


DECISION_VERSION = "instant-delayed-decision-v1-draft"
GRADE_VERSION = "instant-later-cutoff-grade-v1-draft"
SOURCE = Path(__file__)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DecisionEvidence:
    version: str
    product_id: str
    direction: str
    feature_cutoff_ts_ms: int
    knowledge_asof_ts_ms: int
    feature_computation_completed_ts_ms: int
    decision_completed_ts_ms: int
    feature_json: str
    anchor_quotes_json: str
    implementation_sha256: str

    @property
    def sha256(self):
        return _sha(_json(asdict(self)).encode("utf-8"))


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ObservedPublication:
    decision: DecisionEvidence
    timing: PublicationTiming
    observation_recorded_ts_ms: int
    durable_publication_verified: bool = False


def prepare_decision(recorder, product, cutoff, direction, *, asof):
    """Freeze a diagnostic input row before recording a publication."""
    row = build_timed_features(recorder, product, cutoff, direction, asof=asof)
    quotes = {
        pid: recorder._quote_at(pid, cutoff, asof)[0]
        for pid in dict.fromkeys((product, BENCHMARK))
    }
    features_json, quotes_json = _json(row), _json(quotes)
    implementation = _sha(SOURCE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8"))
    return DecisionEvidence(
        DECISION_VERSION,
        product,
        direction,
        cutoff,
        asof,
        row["computation_completed_ts_ms"],
        recorder._now(),
        features_json,
        quotes_json,
        implementation,
    )


def _check_prefix(recorder, decision, row):
    baseline = row["baseline"]
    if recorder.inventory_sha256 != baseline["inventory_sha256"] or (
        recorder.inventory_recorded_ts_ms != baseline["inventory_recorded_ts_ms"]
    ):
        raise ValueError("decision and recorder inventory differ")
    sources = (
        ("input", recorder),
        ("book", recorder.book),
        ("monitor", recorder.book.monitor),
    )
    if any(
        baseline[f"{name}_implementation_sha256"] != source.source_sha256
        or baseline[f"{name}_contract_sha256"] != source.contract_sha256
        for name, source in sources
    ):
        raise ValueError("decision and recorder source provenance differ")
    count, digest, _ = recorder._prefix_at(decision.knowledge_asof_ts_ms)
    if (count, digest) != (
        baseline["known_packet_count"],
        baseline["known_packet_prefix_sha256"],
    ):
        raise ValueError("decision and recorder evidence prefix differ")


def observe_publication(
    recorder, decision, *, publication_id, published, max_feature_age_ms=None
):
    """Bind an observed timestamp. Its source remains a caller assertion."""
    if recorder.failed:
        raise ValueError("failed evidence recorder")
    _integer(published)
    row = json.loads(decision.feature_json)
    _check_prefix(recorder, decision, row)
    timing = publication_timing(
        publication_id=publication_id,
        product_id=decision.product_id,
        feature_cutoff=decision.feature_cutoff_ts_ms,
        inputs_available=decision.feature_computation_completed_ts_ms,
        decision_completed=decision.decision_completed_ts_ms,
        published=published,
        max_feature_age_ms=max_feature_age_ms,
    )
    recorded = recorder._now()
    if published > recorded:
        raise ValueError("publication time has not been reached")
    return ObservedPublication(decision, timing, recorded)


def _input_trade_windows(row):
    baseline = row["baseline"]
    product, cutoff = row["product_id"], row["feature_cutoff_ts_ms"]
    for pid in dict.fromkeys((product, BENCHMARK)):
        yield pid, baseline["sigma_start_ts_ms"] - GRID_MS, baseline["sigma_end_ts_ms"]
    yield product, baseline["pace_start_ts_ms"], baseline["pace_end_ts_ms"]
    yield product, cutoff - PACE_MS, cutoff
    yield product, row["candle_start_ts_ms"], row["candle_end_ts_ms"]


def _mid(quote):
    bid, ask = float(quote["bid"]), float(quote["ask"])
    return bid + (ask - bid) / 2


def _forward_metrics(quotes, product, direction, volatility, trades, horizon, cfg):
    first_coin, first_btc = (_mid(quotes[0][pid]) for pid in (product, BENCHMARK))
    sign = 1 if direction == "up" else -1
    path = [
        sign
        * (
            (math.log(_mid(point[product])) - math.log(first_coin))
            - volatility["beta"]
            * (math.log(_mid(point[BENCHMARK])) - math.log(first_btc))
        )
        for point in quotes
    ]
    sigma = max(volatility["sigma_1m"], cfg["sigma_floor_1m"])
    rules = cfg["impulse"]
    entry = quotes[0][product]
    spread = (float(entry["ask"]) - float(entry["bid"])) / first_coin
    required = max(
        rules["terminal_sigma_multiple"] * sigma * math.sqrt(horizon / 60),
        rules["minimum_terminal_log_return"],
        rules["entry_spread_multiple"] * spread,
    )
    terminal, peak = path[-1], max(path)
    retention = terminal / peak if peak > 0 else None
    tolerance = cfg["comparison_tolerance"]
    verified = (
        terminal + tolerance >= required
        and retention is not None
        and retention + tolerance >= rules["minimum_peak_retention"]
        and trades >= rules["minimum_forward_matches"]
    )
    return dict(
        status="ready",
        reason=None,
        cutoff_verified_forward=verified,
        terminal_residual_log=terminal,
        required_terminal_log=required,
        peak_residual_log=peak,
        retention=retention,
        forward_match_count=trades,
    )


def _mature_grade(recorder, decision, row, end, asof, horizon, cfg):
    product, start = decision.product_id, decision.feature_cutoff_ts_ms
    missing = [
        name for name, result in row["features"].items() if result["status"] != "ready"
    ]
    if missing:
        return dict(
            status="unavailable",
            reason="decision_features_unavailable",
            missing_features=missing,
        )

    # Later conflicts may invalidate an input proof but cannot change the
    # frozen feature, baseline, or anchor price that the decision actually used.
    for pid, left, right in _input_trade_windows(row):
        _, _, reason, _ = recorder._trade_window(pid, left, right, asof)
        if reason:
            return dict(
                status="unavailable",
                reason=reason,
                evidence_scope="decision_trade_window",
                product_with_missing_evidence=pid,
            )

    trade_count = None
    for pid in dict.fromkeys((product, BENCHMARK)):
        _, trades, reason, _ = recorder._trade_window(pid, start - GRID_MS, end, asof)
        if reason:
            return dict(
                status="unavailable",
                reason=reason,
                evidence_scope="outcome_trade_window",
                product_with_missing_evidence=pid,
            )
        if pid == product:
            trade_count = sum(start < trade.receipt_ts_ms < end for trade in trades)

    anchor_quotes = json.loads(decision.anchor_quotes_json)
    quotes = []
    for cutoff in range(start, end + 1, GRID_MS):
        point = {}
        for pid in anchor_quotes:
            quote, _ = recorder._quote_at(pid, cutoff, asof)
            if not quote["available"]:
                return dict(
                    status="unavailable",
                    reason=quote["reason"],
                    evidence_scope="outcome_quote",
                    missing_quote_ts_ms=cutoff,
                    product_with_missing_evidence=pid,
                )
            if cutoff == start and quote != anchor_quotes[pid]:
                return dict(
                    status="unavailable",
                    reason="decision_anchor_quote_changed",
                    product_with_missing_evidence=pid,
                )
            point[pid] = quote
        quotes.append(anchor_quotes if cutoff == start else point)

    return _forward_metrics(
        quotes,
        product,
        decision.direction,
        row["baseline"]["volatility"],
        trade_count,
        horizon,
        cfg,
    )


def grade_publication(recorder, publication, horizon_seconds, *, asof):
    """Compute a new offline grade without modifying the decision snapshot."""
    if recorder.failed:
        raise ValueError("failed evidence recorder")
    cfg, impulse_sha = load_contract(V2_CONTRACT_PATH)
    if (
        type(horizon_seconds) is not int
        or horizon_seconds not in cfg["outcome_horizons_seconds"]
    ):
        raise ValueError("unsupported horizon")
    _integer(asof)
    if not publication.observation_recorded_ts_ms <= asof <= recorder._now():
        raise ValueError(
            "grading as-of must include recorded publication and have elapsed"
        )
    decision = publication.decision
    row = json.loads(decision.feature_json)
    _check_prefix(recorder, decision, row)
    end = decision.feature_cutoff_ts_ms + horizon_seconds * 1000
    count, prefix, pending_packet = recorder._prefix_at(asof)
    result = dict(
        version=GRADE_VERSION,
        grading_packet_count=count,
        grading_packet_prefix_sha256=prefix,
        decision_sha256=decision.sha256,
        publication_id=publication.timing.publication_id,
        published_ts_ms=publication.timing.published_ts_ms,
        product_id=decision.product_id,
        direction=decision.direction,
        feature_cutoff_ts_ms=decision.feature_cutoff_ts_ms,
        horizon_seconds=horizon_seconds,
        horizon_end_ts_ms=end,
        knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
        publication_before_horizon_end=publication.timing.published_ts_ms < end,
        impulse_rule_source_sha256=impulse_sha,
        scope="offline_cutoff_outcome_diagnostic",
        evaluation_only=True,
        fitting_allowed=False,
        status="pending",
        reason="horizon_not_mature",
        cutoff_verified_forward=None,
    )
    if asof >= end:
        # A lower-level monitor may have processed a packet while the final
        # payload is pending. Decline grading across that partial handoff.
        if (
            pending_packet
            and pending_packet.receipt_ts_ms < asof
            and pending_packet.processing_started_ts_ms <= asof
        ):
            result.update(
                status="unavailable", reason="grading_payload_processing_incomplete"
            )
        else:
            result.update(
                _mature_grade(recorder, decision, row, end, asof, horizon_seconds, cfg)
            )
    result["grade_computation_completed_ts_ms"] = recorder._now()
    return result

===== BEGIN SOURCE 65 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_delayed_eligibility.py | sha256=5ead9c0dd9a79cea3e0b836c7a2340888c7d7c7eb4b9bf5b619f79c0b5d3c2d3 =====
"""Research-only delayed eligibility for an explicitly planned cutoff cohort."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
import json

from instant_coverage import _integer
from instant_delayed_decision import _check_prefix
from instant_input_evidence import BENCHMARK, DIRECTIONS


VERSION = "instant-delayed-eligibility-v1-draft"
FEATURES = ("F1", "F2", "F3", "F4", "F5")
BASELINES = ("volatility", "pace")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LocalDelivery:
    decision_sha256: str
    commit_observed_ts_ms: int
    consumer_seen_ts_ms: int | None


def _component_reasons(components, names, asof, clock):
    reasons = {}
    for name in names:
        row = components[name]
        if row["status"] == "ready":
            if _integer(row[clock]) > asof:
                raise ValueError("component evidence is later than decision as-of")
        elif row["status"] == "unavailable":
            reasons[name] = row["reason"]
        else:
            raise ValueError("unsupported component status")
    return reasons


def _delivery(decision, observation, deadline_age_ms):
    if observation is None:
        return dict(status="not_measured", commit_age_ms=None, consumer_age_ms=None)
    if observation.decision_sha256 != decision.sha256:
        raise ValueError("local delivery does not match decision digest")
    commit = _integer(observation.commit_observed_ts_ms)
    seen = observation.consumer_seen_ts_ms
    if commit < decision.decision_completed_ts_ms or (
        seen is not None and _integer(seen) < commit
    ):
        raise ValueError("local delivery clock precedes its input")
    cutoff = decision.feature_cutoff_ts_ms
    age = None if seen is None else seen - cutoff
    if age is None:
        status = "consumer_unobserved"
    elif deadline_age_ms is None:
        status = "observed"
    else:
        status = "within_bound" if age <= deadline_age_ms else "after_bound"
    return dict(status=status, commit_age_ms=commit - cutoff, consumer_age_ms=age)


def assess_cohort(
    recorder, cutoff, *, asof, decisions, local_deliveries=None, deadline_age_ms=None
):
    """Account for every recorder-declared product and direction at one cutoff.

    The caller chooses a post-warmup research cutoff. These are scoped input
    and local delivery diagnostics; this adapter emits no study eligibility.
    """
    _integer(cutoff)
    _integer(asof)
    if (
        recorder.failed
        or cutoff not in recorder.cutoffs
        or not recorder.inventory_recorded_ts_ms <= cutoff <= asof <= recorder._now()
    ):
        raise ValueError("declared cutoff and elapsed evidence as-of required")
    if deadline_age_ms is not None and _integer(deadline_age_ms) < 0:
        raise ValueError("nonnegative diagnostic deadline required")

    planned = [
        (product, direction)
        for product in recorder.products
        for direction in DIRECTIONS
    ]
    by_slot = {}
    for decision in decisions:
        key = (decision.product_id, decision.direction)
        if key not in planned or key in by_slot:
            raise ValueError("decision is outside the cohort or duplicated")
        if (decision.feature_cutoff_ts_ms, decision.knowledge_asof_ts_ms) != (
            cutoff,
            asof,
        ) or not asof <= decision.feature_computation_completed_ts_ms <= decision.decision_completed_ts_ms:
            raise ValueError("decision clocks do not match the cohort")
        by_slot[key] = decision
    deliveries = {} if local_deliveries is None else local_deliveries
    if deliveries.keys() - by_slot.keys():
        raise ValueError("local delivery without a planned decision")

    rows = []
    for key in planned:
        product, direction = key
        decision = by_slot.get(key)
        row = dict(
            product_id=product,
            direction=direction,
            role="benchmark" if product == BENCHMARK else "non_benchmark",
            input_status="decision_missing",
            feature_reasons=None,
            baseline_reasons=None,
            anchor_reasons=None,
            complete_vector_candidate=False,
            delivery=_delivery(decision, deliveries.get(key), deadline_age_ms),
        )
        if decision is not None:
            features = json.loads(decision.feature_json)
            _check_prefix(recorder, decision, features)
            row["feature_reasons"] = _component_reasons(
                features["features"], FEATURES, asof, "evidence_available_ts_ms"
            )
            row["baseline_reasons"] = _component_reasons(
                features["baseline"], BASELINES, asof, "inputs_available_ts_ms"
            )
            anchors = json.loads(decision.anchor_quotes_json)
            row["anchor_reasons"] = {
                pid: anchors[pid]["reason"]
                for pid in dict.fromkeys((product, BENCHMARK))
                if anchors[pid]["available"] is not True
            }
            ready = not (
                row["feature_reasons"]
                or row["baseline_reasons"]
                or row["anchor_reasons"]
            )
            row["input_status"] = "ready" if ready else "input_unavailable"
            row["complete_vector_candidate"] = ready and product != BENCHMARK
        rows.append(row)
    return dict(
        version=VERSION,
        cutoff_ts_ms=cutoff,
        knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
        planned_rows=len(planned),
        deadline_age_ms=deadline_age_ms,
        inventory_sha256=recorder.inventory_sha256,
        study_eligible=False,
        rows=rows,
    )

===== BEGIN SOURCE 66 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_diagnostics.py | sha256=55944b5d489c7d824d6b2e8be7377a89d388d403212f6fe4cca91b77fe7378ef =====
"""Versioned offline reporting; does not alter target labels or live scores."""

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import instant_targets as target


CONTRACT_PATH = target.CONTRACT_PATH.with_name(
    "INSTANT_DIAGNOSTIC_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
)


def provenance():
    raw = CONTRACT_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
    cfg = json.loads(raw)
    return dict(
        diagnostic_version=cfg["version"],
        diagnostic_contract_sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
        diagnostic_implementation_sha256=hashlib.sha256(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
    )


def fraction_reason(label, peak):
    if label.get("pre_window_reference_status") != "ready":
        return (
            label.get("pre_window_reference_reason")
            or "pre_window_reference_unavailable"
        )
    if not target.finite(peak):
        return "invalid_pre_window_peak"
    if peak <= 0:
        return "nonpositive_pre_window_peak"
    return None


def alert_report(evaluator, label, publish_ts_ms, *, strict_trigger=False):
    """Keep the original result intact; append explicitly named diagnostics."""
    if not evaluator.is_v2:
        raise ValueError("diagnostics require the explicit v2 target draft")
    timing = (
        evaluator.alert_timing(label, publish_ts_ms)
        if strict_trigger
        else evaluator.forward_alert_timing(label, publish_ts_ms)
    )
    output = {**timing, **provenance()}
    output.setdefault("pre_window_consumed_fraction", None)
    output.update(
        target_contract_sha256=evaluator.contract_sha256,
        target_evaluator_sha256=evaluator.implementation_sha256,
        pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason=None,
        pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction=None,
        pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction_reason=None,
        pre_window_to_forward_peak_ts_ms=None,
        whole_window_peak_precedes_anchor=None,
        pre_window_reference_unavailable_reason=(
            label.get("pre_window_reference_reason")
            or "pre_window_reference_unavailable"
            if label.get("pre_window_reference_status") != "ready"
            else None
        ),
        publication_unavailable_reason=(
            timing.get("reason") or "publication_unavailable"
            if timing["timing"] in ("unavailable", "outside_event_window")
            else None
        ),
    )
    whole_reason = fraction_reason(label, label.get("pre_window_peak_residual_log"))
    if timing["timing"] in ("unavailable", "outside_event_window"):
        reason = whole_reason or timing.get("reason") or "publication_unavailable"
        output["pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason"] = reason
        output["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction_reason"] = reason
        return output
    output["pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason"] = whole_reason
    if label.get("pre_window_reference_status") != "ready":
        output["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction_reason"] = whole_reason
        return output
    reference = label["pre_window_reference_ts_ms"]
    sign = 1 if label["direction"] == "up" else -1
    peak_ts = label["peak_ts_ms"]
    forward_peak = evaluator._residual(reference, peak_ts, label["beta"], sign)
    alternative_reason = fraction_reason(label, forward_peak)
    output.update(
        pre_window_to_forward_peak_ts_ms=peak_ts,
        pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction_reason=alternative_reason,
        whole_window_peak_precedes_anchor=(
            label["pre_window_peak_ts_ms"] < label["anchor_ts_ms"]
        ),
    )
    if alternative_reason is None:
        output["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction"] = (
            evaluator._residual(
                reference, timing["price_snapshot_ts_ms"], label["beta"], sign
            )
            / forward_peak
        )
    return output


def forward_catalog_report(labels):
    """Annotate sampling-edge/gap censoring without changing catalog selection."""
    labels = list(labels)
    status = {}
    for row in labels:
        key = tuple(
            row[k]
            for k in ("product_id", "direction", "horizon_seconds", "anchor_ts_ms")
        )
        if key in status:
            raise ValueError("duplicate catalog anchor")
        status[key] = row.get("status")
    output = []
    for row in target.forward_event_catalog(labels):
        key = tuple(row[k] for k in ("product_id", "direction", "horizon_seconds"))
        previous = status.get((*key, row["anchor_ts_ms"] - 5000))
        output.append(
            {
                **row,
                **provenance(),
                "previous_anchor_status": previous,
                "left_censored": previous != "ready",
                "left_censor_reason": (
                    None
                    if previous == "ready"
                    else previous or "previous_anchor_missing"
                ),
            }
        )
    return output

===== BEGIN SOURCE 67 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_directional_exposure.py | sha256=638db051a2eb20c66431281606b384cec101f7cb2114b3dd72e045d89692f424 =====
"""Opt-in directional exposure draft from explicit caller eligibility assertions.

No feed-evidence producer, target change, fitting or live integration. The
existing association module's joint exposure report retains its semantics.
"""

from collections import Counter
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import instant_association as assoc


VERSION = "instant-directional-exposure-v1-draft"
CONTRACT = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    / "docs/research/INSTANT_DIRECTIONAL_EXPOSURE_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
)


def provenance():
    raw = CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
    cfg = json.loads(raw)
    if (
        cfg["version"] != VERSION
        or cfg["association_version"] != assoc.VERSION
        or cfg["grid_ms"] != assoc.GRID_MS
        or tuple(cfg["directions"]) != assoc.DIRECTIONS
        or tuple(cfg["horizons_seconds"]) != assoc.HORIZONS
        or cfg["fitting_allowed"] is not False
        or cfg["live_integration"] is not False
        or cfg["feed_gate_pass"] is not False
    ):
        raise ValueError("directional contract and implementation differ")
    return dict(
        version=VERSION,
        contract_sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
        implementation_sha256=hashlib.sha256(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
        association_dependency=assoc.provenance(),
        eligibility_source="caller_assertions_not_authenticated_feed_evidence",
        eligibility_producer_implemented=False,
        evaluation_only=True,
        physical_onset_defined=False,
        operational_missed_move_rates_enabled=False,
        feed_gate_pass=False,
        study_frozen=False,
        fitting_allowed=False,
        live_integration=False,
    )


def _key(product, direction, ts):
    if direction not in assoc.DIRECTIONS:
        raise ValueError("unsupported eligibility direction")
    return assoc._text(product), direction, assoc._grid(ts)


def _slots(records, asof):
    slots = {}
    for record in records:
        if (
            not isinstance(record, dict)
            or any(
                key not in record
                for key in ("product_id", "direction", "ts_ms", "available_ts_ms")
            )
            or any(
                type(record.get(flag)) is not bool for flag in assoc.ELIGIBILITY_FLAGS
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "identity, clocks and explicit boolean eligibility flags required"
            )
        key = _key(record["product_id"], record["direction"], record["ts_ms"])
        available = assoc._integer(record["available_ts_ms"])
        if available > key[2] or key[2] > asof:
            raise ValueError("eligibility must be known by its cutoff <= as-of")
        if key in slots:
            raise ValueError("duplicate directional eligibility slot")
        slots[key] = {flag: record[flag] for flag in assoc.ELIGIBILITY_FLAGS}
    # Both boards must explicitly account for every submitted product/cutoff.
    # A missing eligibility row is never silently treated as an excluded coin.
    for product, direction, ts in slots:
        other = "down" if direction == "up" else "up"
        counterpart = slots.get((product, other, ts))
        if counterpart is None:
            raise ValueError("both directional eligibility records required")
        if (
            slots[(product, direction, ts)]["universe_member"]
            != counterpart["universe_member"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("boards must share the declared decision universe")
    return slots


def exposure_report(labels, eligibility, *, asof_ts_ms, ranker_slots=None):
    """Per-board causal exposure and own-direction retrospective gradability.

    Eligibility needs (product_id, direction, ts_ms, available_ts_ms) and four
    explicit booleans. Ranker slots are (product_id, direction, ts_ms) triples.
    Both directions are recorded for each submitted product/cutoff. This does
    not prove that the caller supplied the complete intended market universe.
    """
    assoc._integer(asof_ts_ms)
    metadata = provenance()
    rows, contract, evaluator = assoc._labels(labels, asof_ts_ms)
    slots = _slots(eligibility, asof_ts_ms)
    rankers = {}
    for name, records in (ranker_slots or {}).items():
        parsed = [_key(*record) for record in records]
        scored = set(parsed)
        if len(parsed) != len(scored):
            raise ValueError("duplicate directional ranker slot")
        if not scored <= slots.keys():
            raise ValueError("ranker slots must be recorded eligibility slots")
        rankers[assoc._text(name)] = scored
    boards, all_eligible_times = {}, set()
    for direction in assoc.DIRECTIONS:
        board = {(p, ts): f for (p, d, ts), f in slots.items() if d == direction}
        eligible, exclusions = set(), Counter()
        for key, flags in board.items():
            failed = next((f for f in assoc.ELIGIBILITY_FLAGS if not flags[f]), None)
            if failed is None:
                eligible.add(key)
            else:
                exclusions[failed] += 1
        all_eligible_times.update(ts for _, ts in eligible)
        scored_by_ranker = {
            name: {(p, ts) for p, d, ts in scored if d == direction}
            for name, scored in rankers.items()
        }
        shared = (
            set.intersection(*scored_by_ranker.values())
            if scored_by_ranker
            else set(eligible)
        )
        horizons = {}
        for horizon in assoc.HORIZONS:
            gradable, reasons = set(), Counter()
            for product, ts in sorted(eligible):
                label = rows.get((product, direction, horizon, ts))
                if ts + horizon * 1000 > asof_ts_ms:
                    reason = "immature_trailing_horizon"
                else:
                    outcome, reason = assoc._primary(label)
                    if outcome != "ungradable":
                        gradable.add((product, ts))
                if reason:
                    reasons[reason] += 1
            horizons[str(horizon)] = dict(
                horizon_seconds=horizon,
                gradable_slot_count=len(gradable),
                gradable_product_hours=assoc._hours(len(gradable)),
                ungradable_eligible_slot_count=len(eligible) - len(gradable),
                ungradable_reasons=dict(sorted(reasons.items())),
                gradable_fraction_of_eligible=assoc._ratio(
                    len(gradable), len(eligible)
                ),
                comparison=dict(
                    identical_complete_rows=[
                        list(k) for k in sorted(shared & gradable)
                    ],
                    identical_complete_row_count=len(shared & gradable),
                    definition="eligible_own_direction_ready_and_scored_by_every_ranker",
                    natural_ranker_coverage={
                        name: dict(
                            eligible_scored_slot_count=len(scored & eligible),
                            gradable_scored_slot_count=len(scored & gradable),
                        )
                        for name, scored in sorted(scored_by_ranker.items())
                    },
                ),
            )
        boards[direction] = dict(
            recorded_slot_count=len(board),
            decision_eligible_slot_count=len(eligible),
            decision_eligible_product_hours=assoc._hours(len(eligible)),
            board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product=assoc._hours(
                len({ts for _, ts in eligible})
            ),
            exclusions_by_first_failed_flag={
                flag: exclusions[flag] for flag in assoc.ELIGIBILITY_FLAGS
            },
            horizons=horizons,
        )
    universe = Counter(
        ts
        for (_, direction, ts), flags in slots.items()
        if direction == "up" and flags["universe_member"]
    )
    return dict(
        **metadata,
        knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof_ts_ms,
        target_contract_sha256=contract,
        target_evaluator_sha256=evaluator,
        universe_size_by_cutoff=[
            [ts, universe[ts]] for ts in sorted({ts for _, _, ts in slots})
        ],
        wall_clock_hours_with_either_board_eligible=assoc._hours(
            len(all_eligible_times)
        ),
        decision_universe_independent_of_future_labels=True,
        opposite_direction_label_required=False,
        legacy_joint_report_changed=False,
        boards=boards,
    )
