===== BEGIN SOURCE 68 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_eligibility_replay.py | sha256=2b4f3aa2758bdaeec414017b7a291081097e6a7fbacc70ab2bc9558b2b2fee7f =====
"""Compare four evidence waits on a preserved, digest-pinned local journal."""

import argparse
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import asdict
import json
from pathlib import Path
from shutil import copyfile
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
import time

from instant_delayed_decision import DecisionEvidence, prepare_decision
from instant_delayed_eligibility import FEATURES, LocalDelivery, assess_cohort
from instant_input_evidence import DIRECTIONS, GRID_MS, InputEvidenceRecorder, _file_sha
from instant_stream_journal import iter_packets, read_local_publication, read_segment


OFFSETS_MS = (0, 1500, 2500, 5000)


class RecordedClock:
    """Rebuild fully processed packet prefixes using their recorded end times."""

    def __init__(self, recorded):
        self.recorded = recorded
        self.steps = iter(())
        self.replaying = False

    def __call__(self):
        if self.replaying:
            return next(self.steps)
        return max(self.recorded, time.time_ns() // 1_000_000)

    def feed(self, recorder, row):
        packet = row["processing"]
        start = packet["processing_started_ts_ms"]
        end = packet["processing_completed_ts_ms"]
        # The journal did not persist the intermediate monitor and book
        # completion clocks. Treat both as known at full payload completion.
        self.steps = iter((start, start, end, end, end))
        self.replaying = True
        prior = len(recorder.trades)
        recorder.ingest(row["envelope"])
        actual_trade = (
            asdict(recorder.trades[-1]) if len(recorder.trades) > prior else None
        )
        if (
            next(self.steps, None) is not None
            or asdict(recorder.packets[-1]) != packet
            or actual_trade != row["trade"]
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "recorded packet or first-receipt trade differs from replay"
            )


def _reconstruct(db, segment):
    manifest = segment["manifest"]
    plan = manifest["inventory"]
    clock = RecordedClock(manifest["inventory_recorded_ts_ms"])
    clock.steps = iter((clock.recorded,))
    clock.replaying = True
    recorder = InputEvidenceRecorder(
        plan["products"],
        plan["first_cutoff"],
        plan["stop_cutoff"],
        clock_ms=clock,
        packet_limit=manifest["max_packets"],
        extended_capacity=manifest.get("extended_capacity", False),
        indexed=True,
    )
    for prefix, provider in (
        ("input", recorder),
        ("book", recorder.book),
        ("monitor", recorder.book.monitor),
    ):
        if (
            manifest[f"{prefix}_implementation_sha256"] != provider.source_sha256
            or manifest[f"{prefix}_contract_sha256"] != provider.contract_sha256
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"recorded {prefix} provenance differs from replay")
    if (
        recorder.inventory_sha256 != manifest["inventory_sha256"]
        or recorder._index.source_sha256 != manifest["index_implementation_sha256"]
    ):
        raise ValueError("recorded inventory or index differs from replay")
    first_ack = None
    for row in iter_packets(db):
        clock.feed(recorder, row)
        envelope = row["envelope"]
        if (
            first_ack is None
            and envelope["message"]["type"] == "subscriptions"
            and recorder.book.monitor.active == set(recorder.products)
        ):
            first_ack = envelope["receipt_ts_ms"]
    clock.replaying = False
    if len(recorder.packets) != segment["packets"] or first_ack is None:
        raise ValueError("incomplete replay or no full subscription acknowledgment")
    return recorder, first_ack


def _feature_signature(decision):
    data = json.loads(decision.feature_json)
    return {
        name: (row["status"], row.get("reason"), row.get("value"))
        for name, row in data["features"].items()
    }


def _observed(db, cutoff, replay, observers):
    decisions, deliveries = [], {}
    for simulated in replay:
        slot = (simulated.product_id, simulated.direction)
        identity = f"diagnostic-{cutoff}-{slot[0]}-{slot[1]}"
        receipt, record = read_local_publication(db, identity)
        actual = DecisionEvidence(**record["decision"])
        if (actual.product_id, actual.direction) != slot or _feature_signature(
            actual
        ) != _feature_signature(simulated):
            raise ValueError("replayed +5s feature status, reason, or value differs")
        decisions.append(actual)
        observed = observers.pop(identity)
        if (
            observed["decision_sha256"] != receipt.decision_sha256
            or observed["commit_observed_ts_ms"] != receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms
        ):
            raise ValueError("local observer is not linked to the original outbox row")
        deliveries[slot] = LocalDelivery(
            receipt.decision_sha256,
            receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms,
            observed["consumer_seen_ts_ms"],
        )
    return decisions, deliveries


def _summary(cohorts):
    products = {}
    for cohort in cohorts:
        for row in cohort["rows"]:
            item = products.setdefault(
                row["product_id"],
                dict(
                    planned=0,
                    full_inputs_ready=0,
                    non_benchmark_candidates=0,
                    feature_ready=dict.fromkeys(FEATURES, 0),
                    feature_missing=Counter(),
                ),
            )
            item["planned"] += 1
            item["full_inputs_ready"] += row["input_status"] == "ready"
            item["non_benchmark_candidates"] += row["complete_vector_candidate"]
            for feature in FEATURES:
                reason = (
                    row["feature_reasons"][feature]
                    if feature in row["feature_reasons"]
                    else None
                )
                if reason is None:
                    item["feature_ready"][feature] += 1
                else:
                    item["feature_missing"][f"{feature}:{reason}"] += 1
    return products


def compare(db, *, expected_sha256, attempt, observations):
    # The journal readers also support crash recovery. Use a verified scratch
    # copy so SQLite cannot change the preserved source, even on open.
    with TemporaryDirectory(prefix="moonwalking-eligibility-") as temporary:
        scratch = Path(temporary) / "replay.sqlite"
        copyfile(db, scratch)
        if (
            _file_sha(scratch) != expected_sha256
            or attempt["journal_sha256"] != expected_sha256
        ):
            raise ValueError("preserved journal digest differs")
        segment = read_segment(scratch)
        if segment["status"] != "finished":
            raise ValueError("only a finished preserved journal can be compared")
        recorder, ack = _reconstruct(scratch, segment)
        if (
            ack != attempt["first_ack_ts_ms"]
            or set(recorder.products) != set(attempt["products"])
            or attempt["cohort_evidence_offset_ms"] != OFFSETS_MS[-1]
            or segment["publications"] != len(observations)
        ):
            raise ValueError("preserved run does not match its attempt report")
        observers = {row["publication_id"]: row for row in observations}
        first = ((ack + attempt["warmup_ms"] + GRID_MS - 1) // GRID_MS) * GRID_MS
        cutoffs = range(first, first + GRID_MS * attempt["cohort_count"], GRID_MS)
        by_offset = {}
        archived = []
        for offset in OFFSETS_MS:
            cohorts = []
            for cutoff in cutoffs:
                asof = cutoff + offset
                if asof > recorder.packets[-1].available_ts_ms:
                    raise ValueError("as-of exceeds the captured evidence prefix")
                count, digest, following = recorder._prefix_at(asof)
                decisions = [
                    prepare_decision(recorder, product, cutoff, direction, asof=asof)
                    for product in recorder.products
                    for direction in DIRECTIONS
                ]
                cohort = assess_cohort(recorder, cutoff, asof=asof, decisions=decisions)
                cohort.update(
                    known_packet_count=count,
                    known_packet_prefix_sha256=digest,
                    payload_in_progress=bool(
                        following
                        and following.processing_started_ts_ms
                        <= asof
                        < following.processing_completed_ts_ms
                    ),
                )
                cohorts.append(cohort)
                if offset == OFFSETS_MS[-1]:
                    original, delivered = _observed(
                        scratch, cutoff, decisions, observers
                    )
                    archived.append(
                        assess_cohort(
                            recorder,
                            cutoff,
                            asof=asof,
                            decisions=original,
                            local_deliveries=delivered,
                            deadline_age_ms=6000,
                        )
                    )
            by_offset[str(offset)] = dict(products=_summary(cohorts), cohorts=cohorts)
        if observers:
            raise ValueError("unaccounted original local observations")
        real_rows = [row for cohort in archived for row in cohort["rows"]]
        return dict(
            scope="preserved_journal_fully_processed_prefix_diagnostic",
            journal_sha256=expected_sha256,
            packets=segment["packets"],
            first_ack_ts_ms=ack,
            offsets_ms=list(OFFSETS_MS),
            replay_by_offset=by_offset,
            original_plus_5s_observed=dict(
                rows=len(real_rows),
                replay_feature_status_reason_value_parity=len(real_rows),
                max_commit_age_ms=max(
                    row["delivery"]["commit_age_ms"] for row in real_rows
                ),
                max_consumer_age_ms=max(
                    row["delivery"]["consumer_age_ms"] for row in real_rows
                ),
                consumer_within_6000ms=sum(
                    row["delivery"]["status"] == "within_bound" for row in real_rows
                ),
            ),
            feed_gate_pass=False,
            study_frozen=False,
            fitting_allowed=False,
        )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("journal", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("attempt", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("observations", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--sha256", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    attempt = json.loads(args.attempt.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    observations = [
        json.loads(line)
        for line in args.observations.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    ]
    report = compare(
        args.journal,
        expected_sha256=args.sha256,
        attempt=attempt,
        observations=observations,
    )
    with args.out.open("x", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as stream:
        stream.write(json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 69 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_evidence_index.py | sha256=4a3420f010e4736aba7bfbbce6e755212e8ef8a62bd957eb0aa4fcced8b65701 =====
"""Append-only indexes for the timed recorder's existing evidence rules."""

from bisect import bisect_left, bisect_right
from decimal import Decimal, getcontext
from functools import lru_cache
import hashlib
from operator import attrgetter, itemgetter
from pathlib import Path

from instant_input_evidence import _packet_line, _sha


CHECKPOINT_PACKETS = 128
receipt_ts = attrgetter("receipt_ts_ms")
available_ts = attrgetter("available_ts_ms")
receipt_key = itemgetter("receipt_ts_ms")
available_key = itemgetter("available_ts_ms")
end_key = itemgetter("end_ts_ms")


def _last(rows, count):
    return rows[count - 1] if count else None


def _before(rows, cutoff, asof, receipt, known):
    return min(
        bisect_left(rows, cutoff, key=receipt), bisect_right(rows, asof, key=known)
    )


def _span(rows, start, end, asof):
    # Closing receipt and proof availability are ordered. Starts may move
    # backwards for a merged proof or a late conflict on an old trade ID.
    left = bisect_right(rows, start, key=end_key)
    right = bisect_right(rows, asof, key=available_key)
    return [row for row in rows[left:right] if row["start_ts_ms"] < end]


def _carry(rows, start, count, key):
    first = bisect_right(rows, start, hi=count, key=key)
    return rows[max(first - 1, 0) : count]


class EvidenceIndex:
    """Append from the recorder after each successfully processed packet."""

    def __init__(self, recorder):
        self.recorder = recorder
        self.monitor = recorder.book.monitor
        self.transport = {pid: [] for pid in recorder.products}
        self.proofs = {pid: [] for pid in recorder.products}
        self.conflicts = {pid: [] for pid in recorder.products}
        self.trades = {pid: [] for pid in recorder.products}
        self._streams = (
            (self.monitor.transport, self.transport),
            (self.monitor.proofs, self.proofs),
            (self.monitor.conflicts, self.conflicts),
        )
        self._positions = [0] * len(self._streams)
        self._hash = hashlib.sha256()
        self._hash_checkpoints = [self._hash.copy()]
        self._last_prefix = None
        self._bin_sum = lru_cache(maxsize=8192)(self._sum_bin)
        self._decimal_signature = None
        self.source_sha256 = _sha(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        )

    def ingested(self, packet, trade):
        for i, (rows, index) in enumerate(self._streams):
            for position in range(self._positions[i], len(rows)):
                row = rows[position]
                index[row["product_id"]].append(row)
            self._positions[i] = len(rows)
        if trade is not None:
            self.trades[trade.product_id].append(trade)
        self._hash.update(_packet_line(packet))
        if len(self.recorder.packets) % CHECKPOINT_PACKETS == 0:
            self._hash_checkpoints.append(self._hash.copy())

    def prefix_at(self, asof):
        packets = self.recorder.packets
        count = bisect_right(packets, asof, key=available_ts)
        if self._last_prefix is not None and self._last_prefix[0] == count:
            digest = self._last_prefix[1]
        elif count == len(packets):
            digest = self._hash.hexdigest()
        else:
            block = count // CHECKPOINT_PACKETS
            prefix = self._hash_checkpoints[block].copy()
            for packet in packets[block * CHECKPOINT_PACKETS : count]:
                prefix.update(_packet_line(packet))
            digest = prefix.hexdigest()
        self._last_prefix = count, digest
        return count, digest, packets[count] if count < len(packets) else None

    def quote_records(self, product, cutoff, asof):
        book = self.recorder.book
        states = book.history[product]
        transport = self.transport[product]
        return (
            _last(states, _before(states, cutoff, asof, receipt_ts, available_ts)),
            _last(
                transport,
                _before(
                    transport,
                    cutoff,
                    asof,
                    receipt_key,
                    lambda row: book.completed[row["receipt_seq"]],
                ),
            ),
        )

    def book_window(self, product, start, end, asof):
        states = self.recorder.book.history[product]
        transport = self.transport[product]
        packets = self.recorder._packet_by_seq
        state_count = _before(
            states,
            end,
            asof,
            receipt_ts,
            lambda row: packets[row.receipt_seq].available_ts_ms,
        )
        transport_count = _before(
            transport,
            end,
            asof,
            receipt_key,
            lambda row: packets[row["receipt_seq"]].available_ts_ms,
        )
        return (
            _carry(states, start, state_count, receipt_ts),
            _carry(transport, start, transport_count, receipt_key),
        )

    def trade_window(self, product, start, end, asof):
        self.monitor._validate_assessment(product, start, end, asof)
        intervals = _span(self.proofs[product], start, end, asof)
        conflict = bool(_span(self.conflicts[product], start, end, asof))
        states = self.transport[product]
        latest = _last(states, bisect_right(states, asof, key=available_key))
        proof = self.monitor._assess_filtered(
            product, start, end, asof, latest, intervals, conflict
        )
        rows = self.trades[product]
        left, right = (
            bisect_left(rows, boundary, key=receipt_ts) for boundary in (start, end)
        )
        return proof, intervals, rows[left:right]

    def _sum_bin(self, product, left, right):
        return sum(
            (
                Decimal(t.price) * Decimal(t.size)
                for t in self.trades[product][left:right]
            ),
            Decimal(0),
        )

    def pace_bins(self, product, start, end, step):
        """Called only after the current as-of's entire window is certified."""
        context = getcontext()
        signature = (
            context.prec,
            context.rounding,
            context.Emin,
            context.Emax,
            context.clamp,
            tuple(context.traps.items()),
        )
        if signature != self._decimal_signature:
            self._bin_sum.cache_clear()
            self._decimal_signature = signature
        rows = self.trades[product]
        boundaries = [
            bisect_left(rows, timestamp, key=receipt_ts)
            for timestamp in range(start, end + 1, step)
        ]
        counts, amounts = [], []
        for left, right in zip(boundaries, boundaries[1:]):
            counts.append(right - left)
            amounts.append(self._bin_sum(product, left, right))
        return counts, amounts

===== BEGIN SOURCE 70 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_indexed_latency.py | sha256=82279c1e934c2d6caf5bd6e4191d2236700e69bb2b983a1cd13ad3e5499a7d96 =====
"""Measure indexed preparation on a continuously committed synthetic stream."""

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import time

from instant_coverage import WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT
from instant_delayed_decision import prepare_decision
from instant_input_evidence import DIRECTIONS, GRID_MS, _file_sha
from instant_source_pilot import LATER_ASOF_MS, PRODUCTS, WARMUP_MS, _summary, capture
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal, read_segment
from instant_warmup_capacity import PACKET_STOP, SyntheticSocket


COHORTS = 10


def _measure(operation, *args, **kwargs):
    started = time.perf_counter_ns()
    result = operation(*args, **kwargs)
    return result, (time.perf_counter_ns() - started) / 1000000


def _content(decision):
    # Compare the whole output except the actual processing timestamps, which
    # naturally differ because the oracle runs after the capture.
    row = json.loads(decision.feature_json)
    row.pop("computation_completed_ts_ms")
    row["baseline"].pop("computation_completed_ts_ms")
    return row, decision.anchor_quotes_json


def _batch_compare(recorder, measured):
    timings = []
    index = recorder._index
    try:
        # The recorder is sealed for ingest at this point. Use the same
        # immutable evidence and the unindexed lookup/calculation path.
        recorder._index = None
        for cohort in (measured[0], measured[-1]):
            for snapshot in cohort:
                batch, elapsed = _measure(
                    prepare_decision,
                    recorder,
                    snapshot.product_id,
                    snapshot.feature_cutoff_ts_ms,
                    snapshot.direction,
                    asof=snapshot.knowledge_asof_ts_ms,
                )
                if _content(snapshot) != _content(batch):
                    raise ValueError(
                        f"batch parity failed for {batch.product_id} "
                        f"{batch.direction} at {batch.feature_cutoff_ts_ms}"
                    )
                timings.append(elapsed)
    finally:
        recorder._index = index
    return timings


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    out = args.out_dir.resolve()
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    path = out / "indexed.sqlite"
    commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
    source = SyntheticSocket()
    next_cutoff = WARMUP_MS
    prepare_times, outbox_times, cohort_times, release_lags = [], [], [], []
    snapshots = []
    started = time.perf_counter()
    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        WARMUP_MS,
        WARMUP_MS + COHORTS * GRID_MS,
        max_packets=WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT,
        clock_ms=source.wall_ms,
        monotonic_ns=source.monotonic_ns,
        indexed=True,
    ) as journal:

        def after_commit(_first_ack_ts_ms):
            nonlocal next_cutoff
            if len(snapshots) == COHORTS or source.now < next_cutoff + LATER_ASOF_MS:
                return
            asof = next_cutoff + LATER_ASOF_MS
            release_lags.append(source.now - asof)
            cohort_started = time.perf_counter_ns()
            prepared = []
            for product in PRODUCTS:
                for direction in DIRECTIONS:
                    snapshot, elapsed = _measure(
                        prepare_decision,
                        journal.evidence,
                        product,
                        next_cutoff,
                        direction,
                        asof=asof,
                    )
                    prepare_times.append(elapsed)
                    prepared.append(snapshot)
                    receipt, elapsed = _measure(
                        journal.append_publication,
                        f"synthetic-{next_cutoff}-{product}-{direction}",
                        snapshot,
                    )
                    if receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms is None:
                        raise ValueError("diagnostic outbox entry was not acknowledged")
                    outbox_times.append(elapsed)
            cohort_times.append((time.perf_counter_ns() - cohort_started) / 1000000)
            snapshots.append(prepared)
            next_cutoff += GRID_MS
            print(
                f"Committed diagnostic cohort {len(snapshots)} of {COHORTS}.",
                flush=True,
            )

        run = capture(
            journal,
            lambda: source,
            monotonic=source.monotonic,
            packet_stop=PACKET_STOP,
            after_commit=after_commit,
        )
        capture_seconds = time.perf_counter() - started
        if not journal.evidence.failed:
            journal.finish()
        timings = (
            _batch_compare(journal.evidence, snapshots)
            if len(snapshots) == COHORTS and run["stop"] == "duration"
            else []
        )
        logical_bytes = journal.record_bytes
    report = dict(
        version="instant-indexed-latency-v1-draft",
        scope="synthetic_receipt_clock_real_serial_journal_and_computation",
        implementation_commit=commit,
        stop=run["stop"],
        connections=run["attempts"],
        simulated_ms=source.now,
        host_capture_seconds=round(capture_seconds, 3),
        planned_cohorts=COHORTS,
        committed_cohorts=len(snapshots),
        index_decision_preparation=_summary(prepare_times),
        local_outbox_write_and_ack=_summary(outbox_times),
        full_serial_cohort=_summary(cohort_times),
        synthetic_release_after_planned_asof=_summary(release_lags),
        batch_decision_preparation_after_capture=_summary(timings),
        exact_content_parity_cases=len(timings),
        callback_through_commit={
            key: data["callback_through_commit"]
            for key, data in run["latencies"].items()
        },
        logical_record_bytes=logical_bytes,
        journal_sha256=_file_sha(path),
        journal_bytes=path.stat().st_size,
        segment=read_segment(path),
        feed_gate_pass=False,
        fitting_allowed=False,
        external_publication_measured=False,
    )
    (out / "REPORT.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(
        f"Indexed latency run stopped: {run['stop']}. Report at {out / 'REPORT.json'}",
        flush=True,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 71 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_input_evidence.py | sha256=66ea9626b920a40b1ee968974bf2e2751bb4217d7f0ca051aedc1d376b1ed959 =====
"""Bounded input-evidence ledger and complete planned-slot accounting.

Measured processing is replay time, never historical knowledge. Components
retain their narrow scopes; this stage cannot emit positive study eligibility.
"""

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from decimal import Decimal
import argparse
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time

from instant_book import BookReplay
from instant_coverage import _integer


VERSION = "instant-input-evidence-v1-draft"
CONTRACT = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    / "docs/research/INSTANT_INPUT_EVIDENCE_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
)
GRID_MS = 5000
MAX_ROWS = 10000
BENCHMARK = "BTC-USD"
DIRECTIONS = ("up", "down")


def _json(value):
    return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)


def _sha(value):
    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()


def _packet_line(packet):
    return (_json(asdict(packet)) + "\n").encode("utf-8")


def _file_sha(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PacketEvidence:
    receipt_seq: int
    receipt_ts_ms: int
    processing_started_ts_ms: int
    processing_completed_ts_ms: int
    available_ts_ms: int
    envelope_sha256: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TradeEvidence:
    product_id: str
    trade_id: int
    receipt_seq: int
    receipt_ts_ms: int
    available_ts_ms: int
    connection_id: int | str
    continuity_epoch: int
    price: str
    size: str
    taker_side: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SlotEvidence:
    product_id: str
    direction: str
    cutoff_ts_ms: int
    assessment_completed_ts_ms: int
    inventory_sha256: str
    components_json: str
    unavailable_reasons: tuple[str, ...]

    def to_dict(self):
        result = asdict(self)
        result["components"] = json.loads(result.pop("components_json"))
        result["eligibility_status"] = "unavailable"
        result["study_eligible"] = False
        return result


class InputEvidenceRecorder:
    """Static declared inventory plus measured packet/trade processing times.

    The clock is measured by default. Injectable clocks are trusted synthetic
    test inputs, not evidence that old packets were processed historically.
    Frozen cutoff cohorts cannot be rewritten by later messages or failures.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        products,
        first_cutoff,
        stop_cutoff,
        *,
        clock_ms=None,
        packet_limit=None,
        indexed=False,
        extended_capacity=False,
    ):
        for value in (first_cutoff, stop_cutoff):
            _integer(value)
        if (
            not isinstance(products, (list, tuple))
            or not products
            or len(products) > 32
            or any(not isinstance(p, str) or not p for p in products)
            or len(set(products)) != len(products)
            or BENCHMARK not in products
            or first_cutoff < 30000
            or first_cutoff % GRID_MS
            or stop_cutoff % GRID_MS
            or stop_cutoff <= first_cutoff
            or (stop_cutoff - first_cutoff) // GRID_MS * len(products) * 2 > MAX_ROWS
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "bounded unique products including BTC and grid range required"
            )
        raw = CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        cfg = json.loads(raw)
        if (
            cfg["version"] != VERSION
            or cfg["grid_ms"] != GRID_MS
            or cfg["max_inventory_rows"] != MAX_ROWS
            or cfg["max_products"] != 32
            or cfg["max_packets"] != 100000
            or cfg["benchmark"] != BENCHMARK
            or any(
                cfg[key] is not False
                for key in (
                    "positive_eligibility_supported",
                    "directional_report_adapter_supported",
                    "durable_publication_verified",
                    "full_feed_verified",
                    "feed_gate_pass",
                    "study_frozen",
                    "fitting_allowed",
                    "live_integration",
                )
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("input-evidence contract and implementation differ")
        self.products = tuple(sorted(products))
        self.cutoffs = tuple(range(first_cutoff, stop_cutoff, GRID_MS))
        self.inventory = _json(
            dict(
                products=self.products,
                first_cutoff=first_cutoff,
                stop_cutoff=stop_cutoff,
                grid_ms=GRID_MS,
                directions=DIRECTIONS,
                benchmark=BENCHMARK,
            )
        )
        self.inventory_sha256 = _sha(self.inventory.encode("utf-8"))
        self.contract_sha256 = _sha(raw)
        self.source_sha256 = _sha(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        )
        self.clock = clock_ms or (lambda: time.time_ns() // 1000000)
        self.last_clock = None
        self.inventory_recorded_ts_ms = self._now()
        self.book = BookReplay(
            self.products,
            clock_ms=self._now,
            packet_limit=packet_limit,
            extended_capacity=extended_capacity,
        )
        self.packets = []
        self.trades = []
        self._packet_by_seq = {}
        self._cohorts = {}
        self.failed = False
        self.sealed = False
        self._index = None
        if indexed:
            from instant_evidence_index import EvidenceIndex

            self._index = EvidenceIndex(self)

    def _now(self):
        try:
            value = _integer(self.clock())
            if self.last_clock is not None and value < self.last_clock:
                raise ValueError("input-evidence processing clock regressed")
            self.last_clock = value
            return value
        except Exception:
            self.failed = True
            raise

    def _writable(self):
        if self.failed or self.sealed:
            raise ValueError("failed or sealed evidence recorder")

    def ingest(self, envelope):
        self._writable()
        try:
            started = self._now()
            # Detach from mutable caller buffers and retain a canonical hash.
            encoded = _json(envelope)
            env = json.loads(encoded)
            if not self.packets and env["receipt_seq"] != 1:
                raise ValueError(
                    "complete logger tape must start at receipt sequence 1"
                )
            old_count = len(self.book.monitor.seen)
            self.book.ingest(env)
            message = env["message"]
            trade = None
            if len(self.book.monitor.seen) > old_count:
                pid = message["product_id"]
                receipt, tid, connection, epoch = self.book.monitor.trades[pid][-1]
                price, size, side = self.book.monitor.seen[(pid, tid)][0]
                trade = dict(
                    product_id=pid,
                    trade_id=tid,
                    receipt_seq=env["receipt_seq"],
                    receipt_ts_ms=receipt,
                    connection_id=connection,
                    continuity_epoch=epoch,
                    price=str(price),
                    size=str(size),
                    taker_side={"buy": "sell", "sell": "buy"}.get(side),
                )
            finished = self._now()
            packet = PacketEvidence(
                env["receipt_seq"],
                env["receipt_ts_ms"],
                started,
                finished,
                max(finished, env["receipt_ts_ms"] + 1),
                _sha(encoded.encode("utf-8")),
            )
            self.packets.append(packet)
            self._packet_by_seq[packet.receipt_seq] = packet
            if trade:
                self.trades.append(
                    TradeEvidence(**trade, available_ts_ms=packet.available_ts_ms)
                )
            if self._index is not None:
                self._index.ingested(packet, self.trades[-1] if trade else None)
        except Exception:
            self.failed = True
            raise

    def _quote_at(self, product, cutoff, asof):
        self.book._validate_view(product, cutoff, asof)
        if self._index is None:
            state, transport = self.book._records_at(product, cutoff, asof)
        else:
            state, transport = self._index.quote_records(product, cutoff, asof)
        view = self.book._view_from_records(product, cutoff, asof, state, transport)
        used = ([state.receipt_seq] if state else []) + (
            [transport["receipt_seq"]] if transport else []
        )
        available = max(
            (self._packet_by_seq[s].available_ts_ms for s in used), default=None
        )
        quote_known = available is None or available <= asof
        quote_ready = view.book_state_available and quote_known
        quote = dict(
            available=quote_ready,
            reason=view.reason if quote_known else "quote_row_processing_incomplete",
            observation_ts_ms=view.observation_ts_ms,
            observation_age_ms=view.observation_age_ms,
            book_processing_available_ts_ms=view.available_ts_ms,
            bid=view.bid if quote_ready else None,
            ask=view.ask if quote_ready else None,
        )
        return quote, available if quote_ready else None

    def _prefix_at(self, asof):
        if self._index is not None:
            return self._index.prefix_at(asof)
        prefix = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, packet in enumerate(self.packets):
            if packet.available_ts_ms > asof:
                return i, prefix.hexdigest(), packet
            prefix.update(_packet_line(packet))
        return len(self.packets), prefix.hexdigest(), None

    def _book_window(self, product, start, end, asof):
        if self._index is not None:
            return self._index.book_window(product, start, end, asof)
        states = [
            state
            for state in self.book.history[product]
            if state.receipt_ts_ms < end
            and self._packet_by_seq[state.receipt_seq].available_ts_ms <= asof
        ]
        transport = [
            row
            for row in self.book.monitor.transport
            if row["product_id"] == product
            and row["receipt_ts_ms"] < end
            and self._packet_by_seq[row["receipt_seq"]].available_ts_ms <= asof
        ]
        return states, transport

    def _trade_window(self, product, start, cutoff, asof):
        if self._index is None:
            proof = self.book.monitor.assess(product, start, cutoff, asof=asof)
            intervals = [
                p
                for p in self.book.monitor.proofs
                if p["product_id"] == product
                and p["start_ts_ms"] < cutoff
                and p["end_ts_ms"] > start
                and p["available_ts_ms"] <= asof
            ]
            rows = [
                t
                for t in self.trades
                if t.product_id == product and start <= t.receipt_ts_ms < cutoff
            ]
        else:
            proof, intervals, rows = self._index.trade_window(
                product, start, cutoff, asof
            )
        known = [t for t in rows if t.available_ts_ms <= asof]
        proof_completion = max(
            (
                self._packet_by_seq[p["end_heartbeat_seq"]].available_ts_ms
                for p in intervals
            ),
            default=cutoff,
        )
        proof_rows_known = proof_completion <= asof and len(known) == len(rows)
        owners = {
            (p["connection_id"], p["continuity_epoch"])
            for p in intervals
            if p["verified"]
        }
        # Receipt milliseconds alone cannot place a trade on the safe side of
        # a barrier. Keep the first receipt, but never certify it using a proof
        # from the other epoch, even when the boundary timestamp is identical.
        continuity_ok = len(owners) == 1 and all(
            (t.connection_id, t.continuity_epoch) in owners for t in known
        )
        reason = proof["receipt_id_check_reason"]
        if reason is None:
            if not proof_rows_known:
                reason = "trade_row_processing_incomplete"
            elif not continuity_ok:
                reason = "trade_row_continuity_mismatch"
        return proof, known, reason, proof_completion if reason is None else None

    def _component(self, product, cutoff, asof):
        quote, _ = self._quote_at(product, cutoff, asof)
        proof, known, reason, _ = self._trade_window(
            product, cutoff - 30000, cutoff, asof
        )
        ids_ready = reason is None
        amounts = {
            side: sum(
                (
                    Decimal(t.price) * Decimal(t.size)
                    for t in known
                    if t.taker_side == side
                ),
                Decimal(0),
            )
            for side in ("buy", "sell", None)
        }
        return dict(
            feature_cutoff_ts_ms=cutoff,
            knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
            later_diagnostic=asof > cutoff,
            quote=quote,
            trades_30s=dict(
                receipt_id_check_passed=ids_ready,
                reason=reason,
                known_unique_match_count=len(known),
                verified_trade_count=len(known) if ids_ready else None,
                quote_volume_usd=str(sum(amounts.values())) if ids_ready else None,
                taker_buy_usd=str(amounts["buy"]) if ids_ready else None,
                taker_sell_usd=str(amounts["sell"]) if ids_ready else None,
                unknown_side_usd=str(amounts[None]) if ids_ready else None,
                flow_available=ids_ready
                and all(t.taker_side is not None for t in known),
                proof_available_ts_ms=proof["receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms"],
            ),
            full_feed_verified=False,
            interval_quote_continuity_verified=False,
        )

    def component_evidence(self, product, cutoff, *, asof=None):
        """Explicit later diagnostics cannot replace frozen cutoff cohorts."""
        if self.failed:
            raise ValueError("failed evidence recorder")
        if (
            product not in self.products
            or type(cutoff) is not int
            or cutoff not in self.cutoffs
        ):
            raise ValueError("product/cutoff outside declared inventory")
        asof = cutoff if asof is None else _integer(asof)
        if asof < cutoff or asof > self._now():
            raise ValueError(
                "diagnostic knowledge must be reached and not precede cutoff"
            )
        return self._component(product, cutoff, asof)

    def checkpoint(self, cutoff):
        if self.failed:
            raise ValueError("failed evidence recorder")
        if cutoff not in self.cutoffs or type(cutoff) is not int:
            raise ValueError("cutoff outside declared inventory")
        if cutoff in self._cohorts:
            return self._cohorts[cutoff]
        self._writable()
        if self._now() < cutoff:
            raise ValueError("cutoff has not been reached")
        try:
            components = {p: self._component(p, cutoff, cutoff) for p in self.products}
            # Encode before measuring the producer's own completion.
            payloads = {
                p: _json(dict(coin=components[p], benchmark=components[BENCHMARK]))
                for p in self.products
            }
            finished = self._now()
            rows = []
            for product in self.products:
                reasons = [
                    "baseline_evidence_not_implemented",
                    "feature_evidence_not_implemented",
                    "publication_policy_unfrozen",
                    "full_feed_evidence_not_verified",
                ]
                if self.inventory_recorded_ts_ms >= self.cutoffs[0]:
                    reasons.append("inventory_recorded_after_first_cutoff")
                if finished > cutoff:
                    reasons.append("assessment_completed_after_cutoff")
                for name, comp in (
                    ("coin", components[product]),
                    ("benchmark", components[BENCHMARK]),
                ):
                    if not comp["quote"]["available"]:
                        reasons.append(f"{name}:quote:{comp['quote']['reason']}")
                    if not comp["trades_30s"]["receipt_id_check_passed"]:
                        reasons.append(f"{name}:trades:{comp['trades_30s']['reason']}")
                rows.extend(
                    SlotEvidence(
                        product,
                        d,
                        cutoff,
                        finished,
                        self.inventory_sha256,
                        payloads[product],
                        tuple(reasons),
                    )
                    for d in DIRECTIONS
                )
            self._cohorts[cutoff] = tuple(rows)
            return self._cohorts[cutoff]
        except Exception:
            self.failed = True
            raise

    def seal(self):
        self._writable()
        if self._now() < self.cutoffs[-1]:
            raise ValueError(
                "cannot seal before all declared cutoffs have been reached"
            )
        for cutoff in self.cutoffs:
            self.checkpoint(cutoff)
        self.sealed = True
        return self.report()

    def report(self):
        if self.failed or not self.sealed:
            raise ValueError(
                "only a sealed complete inventory accounting may be reported"
            )
        rows = [r.to_dict() for cutoff in self.cutoffs for r in self._cohorts[cutoff]]
        return dict(
            version=VERSION,
            status="complete_inventory_accounting_only",
            inventory=json.loads(self.inventory),
            inventory_sha256=self.inventory_sha256,
            inventory_recorded_ts_ms=self.inventory_recorded_ts_ms,
            inventory_precedes_first_cutoff=self.inventory_recorded_ts_ms
            < self.cutoffs[0],
            expected_rows=len(self.products) * len(self.cutoffs) * 2,
            recorded_rows=len(rows),
            packet_count=len(self.packets),
            unique_live_matches=len(self.trades),
            rows=rows,
            implementation_sha256=self.source_sha256,
            contract_sha256=self.contract_sha256,
            book_implementation_sha256=self.book.source_sha256,
            monitor_implementation_sha256=self.book.monitor.source_sha256,
            scope="recorded_input_components_not_authenticated_full_feed_or_study_eligibility",
            processing_clock="measured_in_this_run_never_historical_receipt_backdating",
            full_feed_verified=False,
            positive_eligibility_supported=False,
            directional_report_adapter_supported=False,
            durable_publication_verified=False,
            study_frozen=False,
            feed_gate_pass=False,
            fitting_allowed=False,
            live_integration=False,
        )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("packets", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--inventory", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    source, plan_path, output = (
        args.packets.resolve(),
        args.inventory.resolve(),
        args.out_dir.resolve(),
    )
    if any(
        output == p.parent or p.parent in output.parents for p in (source, plan_path)
    ):
        raise ValueError("output must be outside both input directories")
    source_hash, plan_hash = (_file_sha(p) for p in (source, plan_path))
    plan = json.loads(plan_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    recorder = InputEvidenceRecorder(
        plan["products"], plan["first_cutoff"], plan["stop_cutoff"]
    )
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    try:
        opener = gzip.open if source.suffix == ".gz" else open
        with opener(source, "rt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            for line in stream:
                recorder.ingest(json.loads(line))
        report = recorder.seal()
        if (_file_sha(source), _file_sha(plan_path)) != (source_hash, plan_hash):
            raise ValueError("input changed during processing")
        report["input_sha256"] = source_hash
        report["inventory_file_sha256"] = plan_hash
        for name, rows in (
            ("packet_processing.jsonl", recorder.packets),
            ("trade_processing.jsonl", recorder.trades),
        ):
            with (output / name).open("x", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as target:
                for row in rows:
                    target.write(_json(asdict(row)) + "\n")
            report[name + "_sha256"] = _file_sha(output / name)
        (output / "REPORT.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        (output / "FAILURE.json").write_text(
            _json(dict(status="failed", reason=str(exc))), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        raise


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 72 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_publication.py | sha256=db1c7ede0db61f7a278edcfde90095b665d7410b64bb8c609ab8099da03702ab =====
"""Offline publication-clock diagnostics. No target labels or live decisions."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

from instant_coverage import _integer


VERSION = "instant-publication-clock-v1-draft"
CONTRACT = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    / "docs/research/INSTANT_PUBLICATION_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PublicationTiming:
    version: str
    publication_id: str
    product_id: str
    feature_cutoff_ts_ms: int
    inputs_available_ts_ms: int
    decision_completed_ts_ms: int
    published_ts_ms: int
    feature_age_at_publication_ms: int
    verification_lag_from_cutoff_ms: int
    decision_to_publication_ms: int
    legacy_cutoff_inputs_compatible: bool
    publication_target_amendment_required: bool
    max_feature_age_ms: int | None
    within_declared_feature_age: bool | None
    readiness_reason: str
    eligible_for_fitting: bool
    contract_sha256: str
    implementation_sha256: str


def publication_timing(
    *,
    publication_id,
    product_id,
    feature_cutoff,
    inputs_available,
    decision_completed,
    published,
    max_feature_age_ms=None
):
    """Record explicit clocks; a supplied age bound is not study approval.

    Caller clocks/provenance remain trusted. A later proof is representable
    here but must never be put into an old Sample as if known at its cutoff.
    This validates chronology only; it does not prove durable publication.
    """
    raw = CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
    cfg = json.loads(raw)
    if (
        cfg["version"] != VERSION
        or cfg["grid_ms"] != 5000
        or cfg["fitting_allowed"] is not False
    ):
        raise ValueError("publication contract and implementation differ")
    for value in (feature_cutoff, inputs_available, decision_completed, published):
        _integer(value)
    if (
        not isinstance(publication_id, str)
        or not publication_id
        or not isinstance(product_id, str)
        or not product_id
        or feature_cutoff % 5000
        or not feature_cutoff <= decision_completed <= published
        or not feature_cutoff <= inputs_available <= decision_completed
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "identity, grid cutoff and causal processing/publication clocks required"
        )
    if max_feature_age_ms is not None:
        _integer(max_feature_age_ms)
    age = published - feature_cutoff
    within = age <= max_feature_age_ms if max_feature_age_ms is not None else None
    return PublicationTiming(
        VERSION,
        publication_id,
        product_id,
        feature_cutoff,
        inputs_available,
        decision_completed,
        published,
        age,
        max(0, inputs_available - feature_cutoff),
        published - decision_completed,
        inputs_available <= feature_cutoff,
        published != feature_cutoff or inputs_available > feature_cutoff,
        max_feature_age_ms,
        within,
        (
            "publication_age_policy_unfrozen"
            if within is None
            else (
                "feature_window_too_old"
                if not within
                else "study_and_source_gates_unfrozen"
            )
        ),
        False,
        hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
        hashlib.sha256(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
    )

===== BEGIN SOURCE 73 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_queue_burst.py | sha256=299d44e4e047ac5b7800aae2581bb8e5dbecc370294872dcc52f6304c3cba92a =====
"""Compare two bounded queue sizes on a declared synthetic arrival burst."""

import argparse
from itertools import islice
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import subprocess
from threading import Event
import time

from instant_input_evidence import GRID_MS, _file_sha
from instant_real_indexed_pilot import MAX_QUEUE_CAPACITY, QUEUE_CAPACITY, QueuedSocket
from instant_source_pilot import PRODUCTS, capture, utc_ms
from instant_stream_journal import (
    MAX_BATCH_FRAMES,
    StreamJournal,
    iter_packets,
    iter_pending_ingress,
    read_segment,
)
from instant_warmup_capacity import frames


BURST_COUNT = 160
BURST_S = 0.100
TAIL_COUNT = 40
TAIL_S = 1
STALL_MS = (0, 450)
REPETITIONS = 3


def workload():
    boot = [message for _, message in islice(frames(), 5)]
    # The actual startup had ALCX's only full snapshot immediately before ACK.
    alcx = next(row for row in boot if row.get("product_id") == PRODUCTS[2])
    boot.remove(alcx)
    boot.insert(0, alcx)
    suffix = []
    for i, (_, message) in enumerate(islice(frames(), 5, 5 + BURST_COUNT + TAIL_COUNT)):
        if i < BURST_COUNT:
            delay = i * BURST_S / (BURST_COUNT - 1)
        else:
            delay = BURST_S + (i - BURST_COUNT + 1) * TAIL_S / TAIL_COUNT
        suffix.append((delay, message))
    return boot, suffix


class BurstSocket:
    def __init__(self, boot, suffix):
        self.boot = iter(boot)
        self.suffix = iter(suffix)
        self.release = Event()
        self.started = None
        self.burst_receipts_ns = []

    def send(self, raw):
        if json.loads(raw)["type"] != "subscribe":
            raise ValueError("expected the subscription command")

    def recv(self):
        initial = next(self.boot, None)
        if initial is not None:
            return json.dumps(initial, separators=(",", ":"))
        if not self.release.wait(timeout=0.2):
            raise TimeoutError("waiting for startup frames to commit")
        if self.started is None:
            self.started = time.perf_counter()
        item = next(self.suffix, None)
        if item is None:
            return ""
        delay, message = item
        time.sleep(max(0.0, self.started + delay - time.perf_counter()))
        if delay <= BURST_S:
            self.burst_receipts_ns.append(time.perf_counter_ns())
        return json.dumps(message, separators=(",", ":"))

    def abort(self):
        self.release.set()

    def close(self, timeout):
        pass


def experiment(path, capacity, stall_ms, *, batch_frames=1):
    boot, suffix = workload()
    source = BurstSocket(boot, suffix)
    queue = QueuedSocket(source, (TimeoutError,), max_frames=capacity)
    first = (utc_ms() // GRID_MS + 1) * GRID_MS
    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        first,
        first + GRID_MS,
        indexed=True,
        batch_frames=batch_frames,
        monotonic_ns=time.perf_counter_ns,
    ) as journal:

        def release_burst(_first_ack_ts_ms):
            if journal.packet_count == len(boot) + 1:
                source.release.set()
                if stall_ms:
                    time.sleep(stall_ms / 1000)

        result = capture(
            journal,
            lambda: queue,
            timeout_errors=(TimeoutError,),
            max_connections=1,
            duration_s=10,
            after_commit=release_burst,
            progress=lambda *args, **kwargs: None,
        )
        if not journal.evidence.failed:
            journal.finish()

    records = list(iter_packets(path))
    rows = [row["envelope"] for row in records]
    if any(iter_pending_ingress(path)):
        raise ValueError("completed trial left a pending batch")
    batched = [row for row in records if "ingress_commit_observed_ts_ms" in row]
    for row in batched:
        packet = row["processing"]
        if not (
            row["ingress_commit_observed_ts_ms"]
            <= packet["processing_started_ts_ms"]
            <= packet["processing_completed_ts_ms"]
            <= packet["available_ts_ms"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("invalid batch processing chronology")
    if batch_frames > 1 and len(batched) != len(rows) - 2:
        raise ValueError("missing batch commit observations")
    committed = [row["message"] for row in rows[1:-1]]
    # Match the socket's JSON representation, including lists on the wire.
    sent = json.loads(json.dumps(boot + [message for _, message in suffix]))
    if committed != sent[: len(committed)]:
        raise ValueError("journal differs from the source's ordered prefix")
    if len({row["connection_id"] for row in rows}) != 1:
        raise ValueError("one connection required")
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        if db.execute("PRAGMA quick_check").fetchall() != [("ok",)]:
            raise ValueError("journal quick check failed")
    latency = {
        kind: group["reader_to_writer"]
        for kind, group in result["latencies"].items()
        if "reader_to_writer" in group
    }
    through_commit = {
        kind: group["callback_through_commit"]
        for kind, group in result["latencies"].items()
    }
    return dict(
        capacity=capacity,
        batch_frames=batch_frames,
        batched_input_frames=len(batched),
        requested_stall_ms=stall_ms,
        stop=result["stop"],
        final_marker=rows[-1]["message"]["type"],
        source_frames=len(sent),
        synthetic_burst_frames_returned=len(source.burst_receipts_ns),
        synthetic_burst_return_span_ms=(
            (source.burst_receipts_ns[-1] - source.burst_receipts_ns[0]) / 1000000
            if source.burst_receipts_ns
            else None
        ),
        reader_received=queue.received,
        writer_delivered=queue.delivered,
        journal_committed_data=len(committed),
        observed_queue_high_water=queue.high_water,
        worst_measured_queue_ms=max(row["max_ms"] for row in latency.values()),
        reader_to_writer_by_kind=latency,
        worst_callback_through_commit_ms=max(
            row["max_ms"] for row in through_commit.values()
        ),
        worst_reader_through_commit_ms=max(
            group["reader_through_commit"]["max_ms"]
            for group in result["latencies"].values()
            if "reader_through_commit" in group
        ),
        digest=_file_sha(path),
        segment=read_segment(path),
    )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--batch-frames", type=int, choices=range(1, MAX_BATCH_FRAMES + 1), default=1
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    out = args.out_dir.resolve()
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
    reports = []
    for repeat in range(1, REPETITIONS + 1):
        for stall in STALL_MS:
            for capacity in (QUEUE_CAPACITY, MAX_QUEUE_CAPACITY):
                name = f"r{repeat}-stall{stall}-queue{capacity}"
                report = experiment(
                    out / f"{name}.sqlite",
                    capacity,
                    stall,
                    batch_frames=args.batch_frames,
                )
                reports.append(dict(name=name, **report))
                print(
                    f"{name}: {report['stop']}, {report['journal_committed_data']} committed data frames",
                    flush=True,
                )
    result = dict(
        version="instant-queue-burst-test-v1-draft",
        implementation_commit=commit,
        scope="synthetic_queued_socket_real_indexed_sqlite_writer",
        burst_frames=BURST_COUNT,
        burst_target_ms=BURST_S * 1000,
        tail_frames=TAIL_COUNT,
        tail_target_ms=TAIL_S * 1000,
        repetitions=REPETITIONS,
        journal_batch_frames=args.batch_frames,
        reports=reports,
        live_queue_profile=QUEUE_CAPACITY,
        fitting_allowed=False,
    )
    (out / "REPORT.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Run the real indexed diagnostic and observe the outbox from another process."""
