VERSION = "instant-real-indexed-pilot-v1"
QUEUE_CAPACITY = 128
MAX_QUEUE_CAPACITY = 256
DEFAULT_PROFILE = "single-128"
TIMING_PROFILE = "eligibility-timing-256"
TIMING_VERSION = "instant-eligibility-timing-pilot-v1"
TIMING_PRODUCTS = PRODUCTS + ("LINK-USD", "AVAX-USD")
TIMING_STEP_MS = 10000
TIMING_SOFT_RECORD_BYTES = 480 * 1024 * 1024
TIMING_DEADLINES_MS = {1500: 2500, LATER_ASOF_MS: 6000}
UTC_WINDOWS = {"asia": (0, 6), "europe": (7, 13), "us": (14, 20)}
PROFILES = {
    DEFAULT_PROFILE: (QUEUE_CAPACITY, 1),
    "batched-256": (MAX_QUEUE_CAPACITY, MAX_BATCH_FRAMES),
    TIMING_PROFILE: (MAX_QUEUE_CAPACITY, MAX_BATCH_FRAMES),
}
COHORTS = 10
POLL_S = 0.025
MAX_OBSERVER_S = 240


class QueuedSocket:
    """One socket reader timestamps frames; the owner thread alone writes."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        socket,
        timeout_errors,
        *,
        max_frames=QUEUE_CAPACITY,
        monotonic_ns=time.perf_counter_ns,
    ):
        if type(max_frames) is not int or not 1 <= max_frames <= MAX_QUEUE_CAPACITY:
            raise ValueError("reader queue size outside the supported range")
        self.socket = socket
        self.timeout_errors = timeout_errors
        self.queue = Queue(maxsize=max_frames)
        self.stopping, self.done = Event(), Event()
        self.thread = Thread(
            target=self._read, name="coinbase-diagnostic-reader", daemon=True
        )
        self.failure = None
        self.received = self.delivered = self.high_water = 0
        self._ns = monotonic_ns
        self._stats_lock = Lock()
        self._returns = deque(maxlen=MAX_QUEUE_CAPACITY + 1)
        self._writer = dict(phase="between_callbacks", started_ns=self._ns(), frames=0)
        self._last_writer = None
        self.overflow = None

    @contextmanager
    def writer_activity(self, phase, frames=0):
        """Observe the owner thread without timing/altering journal internals."""
        with self._stats_lock:
            self._writer = dict(phase=phase, started_ns=self._ns(), frames=frames)
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            with self._stats_lock:
                now = self._ns()
                self._last_writer = dict(
                    phase=phase,
                    frames=frames,
                    elapsed_ms=(now - self._writer["started_ns"]) / 1000000,
                )
                self._writer = dict(phase="between_callbacks", started_ns=now, frames=0)

    def _overflow_snapshot(self, reason, wall, now):
        # Called under _stats_lock at the failed enqueue, before shutdown/drain.
        with self.queue.mutex:
            depth = len(self.queue.queue)
            oldest = self.queue.queue[0].receipt_ns if depth else None
        arrivals = {}
        for window_ms in (100, 1000):
            boundary = now - window_ms * 1000000
            arrivals[str(window_ms)] = dict(
                returned=sum(stamp >= boundary for stamp in self._returns),
                lower_bound=(
                    len(self._returns) == self._returns.maxlen
                    and self._returns[0] >= boundary
                ),
            )
        self.overflow = dict(
            version="instant-reader-overflow-v1",
            reason=reason,
            receipt_ts_ms=wall,
            queue_capacity=self.queue.maxsize,
            queue_depth=depth,
            received=self.received,
            delivered=self.delivered,
            oldest_queued_age_ms=None if oldest is None else (now - oldest) / 1000000,
            retained_returns=len(self._returns),
            retained_return_span_ms=(now - self._returns[0]) / 1000000,
            arrival_windows_ms=arrivals,
            writer=dict(
                phase=self._writer["phase"],
                frames=self._writer["frames"],
                elapsed_ms=(now - self._writer["started_ns"]) / 1000000,
            ),
            last_completed_writer=deepcopy(self._last_writer),
        )

    def reader_report(self):
        with self._stats_lock:
            return dict(
                received=self.received,
                delivered=self.delivered,
                received_not_delivered=self.received - self.delivered,
                sampled_high_water=self.high_water,
                overflow=deepcopy(self.overflow),
            )

    def send(self, raw):
        self.socket.send(raw)
        self.thread.start()

    def _read(self):
        try:
            while not self.stopping.is_set():
                try:
                    raw = self.socket.recv()
                except self.timeout_errors:
                    continue
                wall, duration = utc_ms(), self._ns()
                if raw in ("", b""):
                    break
                with self._stats_lock:
                    self.received += 1
                    self._returns.append(duration)
                    if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES or (
                        isinstance(raw, str)
                        and len(raw.encode("utf-8", errors="surrogatepass"))
                        > MAX_FRAME_BYTES
                    ):
                        self._overflow_snapshot("frame_bytes_exceeded", wall, duration)
                        raise QueueOverflowError(
                            "reader frame exceeded the journal frame bound"
                        )
                    try:
                        self.queue.put_nowait(ReceivedFrame(raw, wall, duration))
                    except Full as exc:
                        self._overflow_snapshot("queue_full", wall, duration)
                        raise QueueOverflowError(
                            "socket reader queue reached its frame cap"
                        ) from exc
                    self.high_water = max(self.high_water, self.queue.qsize())
        except Exception as exc:
            if not self.stopping.is_set():
                self.failure = exc
                if self.overflow is not None:
                    # One bounded stderr record survives a still-blocked owner.
                    try:
                        print(
                            "READER_OVERFLOW " + json.dumps(self.overflow),
                            file=sys.stderr,
                            flush=True,
                        )
                    except OSError:
                        pass  # Logging must not replace the original failure.
        finally:
            self.done.set()

    def recv(self):
        if isinstance(self.failure, QueueOverflowError):
            raise self.failure
        try:
            frame = self.queue.get(timeout=0.5)
        except Empty as exc:
            if self.done.is_set():
                if self.failure is not None:
                    raise self.failure
                return ""
            raise TimeoutError("waiting for socket reader") from exc
        with self._stats_lock:
            self.delivered += 1
        return frame

    def recv_ready(self, limit):
        """Drain only already queued frames, stopping at a reported overflow."""
        frames = []
        for _ in range(limit):
            if isinstance(self.failure, QueueOverflowError):
                break
            try:
                frames.append(self.queue.get_nowait())
            except Empty:
                break
        with self._stats_lock:
            self.delivered += len(frames)
        return frames

    def close(self, timeout):
        self.stopping.set()
        if self.thread.ident is not None:
            self.thread.join(2)
        if self.thread.is_alive():
            try:
                self.socket.abort()
            except OSError:
                pass
            self.thread.join(2)
        self.socket.close(timeout=timeout)


def _observe(path, destination, stop_file, *, limit_s=100):
    """A separate process observes only acknowledged, digest-checked rows."""
    seen = set()
    until = time.monotonic() + limit_s
    with (
        closing(
            sqlite3.connect(
                path.resolve().as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=0.04
            )
        ) as db,
        destination.open("x", encoding="utf-8") as stream,
    ):
        while time.monotonic() < until:
            try:
                rows = db.execute(
                    "SELECT publication_id FROM publications WHERE local_commit_observed_ts_ms IS NOT NULL"
                ).fetchall()
                for (pub,) in rows:
                    if pub in seen:
                        continue
                    receipt, _ = _local_publication(db, pub)
                    seen_ts_ms = utc_ms()
                    record = dict(
                        publication_id=pub,
                        decision_sha256=receipt.decision_sha256,
                        commit_observed_ts_ms=receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms,
                        consumer_seen_ts_ms=seen_ts_ms,
                    )
                    stream.write(json.dumps(record, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
                    stream.flush()
                    seen.add(pub)
            except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:
                if "locked" not in str(exc):
                    raise
            if stop_file.exists():
                break
            time.sleep(POLL_S)


class DiagnosticCohorts:
    def __init__(
        self, journal, out, *, products=PRODUCTS, offsets=None, step_ms=GRID_MS
    ):
        self.journal = journal
        self.out = out
        self.products = products
        self.offsets = (LATER_ASOF_MS,) * COHORTS if offsets is None else offsets
        self.step_ms = step_ms
        self.first_cutoff = self.next_cutoff = None
        self.snapshots = {}
        self.observer = None
        self.rows = []
        self.elapsed = []

    def after_commit(self, first_ack_ts_ms):
        if self.next_cutoff is None:
            ready_at = first_ack_ts_ms + WARMUP_MS
            self.first_cutoff = self.next_cutoff = (
                (ready_at + GRID_MS - 1) // GRID_MS
            ) * GRID_MS
        if len(self.elapsed) == len(self.offsets):
            return
        now = utc_ms()
        if self.observer is None and now >= self.next_cutoff - GRID_MS:
            args = [
                sys.executable,
                str(Path(__file__).resolve()),
                "--observe",
                str(self.out),
            ]
            if len(self.offsets) > COHORTS:
                args += ["--observer-limit-s", str(MAX_OBSERVER_S)]
            self.observer = subprocess.Popen(
                args,
                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0),
            )
        asof = self.next_cutoff + self.offsets[len(self.elapsed)]
        if now < asof:
            return
        # A clock passing the planned as-of can coexist with queued packets.
        # Use the declared knowledge time and leave unavailable features null.
        started = time.perf_counter_ns()
        for product in self.products:
            for direction in DIRECTIONS:
                begin = time.perf_counter_ns()
                snapshot = prepare_decision(
                    self.journal.evidence,
                    product,
                    self.next_cutoff,
                    direction,
                    asof=asof,
                )
                prepared_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
                pub = f"diagnostic-{self.next_cutoff}-{product}-{direction}"
                receipt = self.journal.append_publication(pub, snapshot)
                committed_ns = time.perf_counter_ns()
                self.snapshots[pub] = snapshot
                features = json.loads(snapshot.feature_json)
                self.rows.append(
                    dict(
                        publication_id=pub,
                        decision_sha256=receipt.decision_sha256,
                        product_id=product,
                        direction=direction,
                        cutoff_ts_ms=self.next_cutoff,
                        knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
                        cohort_start_ts_ms=now,
                        decision_completed_ts_ms=snapshot.decision_completed_ts_ms,
                        commit_observed_ts_ms=receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms,
                        prepare_ms=(prepared_ns - begin) / 1000000,
                        outbox_ms=(committed_ns - prepared_ns) / 1000000,
                        readiness={
                            name: item.get("reason", "ready")
                            for name, item in features["features"].items()
                        },
                        evidence_available_ts_ms={
                            name: item.get("evidence_available_ts_ms")
                            for name, item in features["features"].items()
                        },
                        baseline={
                            name: features["baseline"][name].get("reason", "ready")
                            for name in ("volatility", "pace")
                        },
                    )
                )
        self.elapsed.append((time.perf_counter_ns() - started) / 1000000)
        self.next_cutoff += self.step_ms
        print(
            f"Committed live-source diagnostic cohort {len(self.elapsed)} of {len(self.offsets)}.",
            flush=True,
        )

    def stop_observer(self):
        if self.observer is None:
            return None
        (self.out / "observer.stop").touch()
        try:
            return self.observer.wait(timeout=6)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            self.observer.terminate()
            return self.observer.wait(timeout=6)


def _observation_report(path, rows):
    data = path / "observed.jsonl"
    lines = data.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if data.is_file() else []
    prepared = {row["publication_id"]: row for row in rows}
    commit_to_seen, cutoff_to_seen = [], []
    discrepancies = Counter()
    observed_ids = set()
    for line in lines:
        try:
            entry = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            discrepancies["unreadable_observation"] += 1
            continue
        pub = entry["publication_id"]
        if pub in observed_ids:
            discrepancies["duplicate"] += 1
            continue
        observed_ids.add(pub)
        row = prepared.get(pub)
        if row is None or any(
            entry[key] != row[key]
            for key in ("decision_sha256", "commit_observed_ts_ms")
        ):
            discrepancies["identity_or_receipt_mismatch"] += 1
            continue
        if entry["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] < entry["commit_observed_ts_ms"]:
            discrepancies["wall_clock_regression"] += 1
            continue
        commit_to_seen.append(
            entry["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] - entry["commit_observed_ts_ms"]
        )
        cutoff_to_seen.append(entry["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] - row["cutoff_ts_ms"])
        row["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] = entry["consumer_seen_ts_ms"]
    return dict(
        reader_poll_ms=POLL_S * 1000,
        commit_observed_to_reader_seen=_summary(commit_to_seen),
        cutoff_to_reader_seen=_summary(cutoff_to_seen),
        missing_or_invalid=len(rows) - len(commit_to_seen),
        discrepancies=dict(discrepancies),
    )


def _timing_readiness(cohorts):
    """Summarize both schedules against their full declared slot denominators."""
    recorder = cohorts.journal.evidence
    rows_by_cutoff = {}
    for row in cohorts.rows:
        rows_by_cutoff.setdefault(row["cutoff_ts_ms"], []).append(row)
    report = {}
    ages_by_offset = {offset: [] for offset in TIMING_DEADLINES_MS}
    for offset, deadline in TIMING_DEADLINES_MS.items():
        planned_cohorts = cohorts.offsets.count(offset)
        report[str(offset)] = dict(
            deadline_age_ms=deadline,
            planned_cohorts=planned_cohorts,
            products={
                product: dict(
                    planned_rows=planned_cohorts * len(DIRECTIONS),
                    committed_rows=0,
                    observed_rows=0,
                    observed_by_bound_rows=0,
                    complete_inputs_rows=0 if not recorder.failed else None,
                    complete_non_benchmark_observed_by_bound_rows=(
                        0 if not recorder.failed else None
                    ),
                )
                for product in cohorts.products
            },
            cutoff_comparisons=[],
        )
    for index, offset in enumerate(cohorts.offsets):
        result = report[str(offset)]
        cutoff = (
            None
            if cohorts.first_cutoff is None
            else cohorts.first_cutoff + index * cohorts.step_ms
        )
        rows = rows_by_cutoff.get(cutoff, ())
        for row in rows:
            product = result["products"][row["product_id"]]
            product["committed_rows"] += 1
            observed = row.get("consumer_seen_ts_ms")
            if observed is not None:
                age = observed - cutoff
                ages_by_offset[offset].append(age)
                product["observed_rows"] += 1
                product["observed_by_bound_rows"] += age <= TIMING_DEADLINES_MS[offset]
        on_time = dict.fromkeys(DIRECTIONS, None if recorder.failed else 0)
        summary = dict(
            cutoff_ts_ms=cutoff,
            committed_rows=len(rows),
            on_time_complete_non_benchmark_products_by_direction=on_time,
        )
        if rows and not recorder.failed:
            decisions, deliveries = [], {}
            for row in rows:
                snapshot = cohorts.snapshots[row["publication_id"]]
                decisions.append(snapshot)
                deliveries[(snapshot.product_id, snapshot.direction)] = LocalDelivery(
                    row["decision_sha256"],
                    row["commit_observed_ts_ms"],
                    row.get("consumer_seen_ts_ms"),
                )
            assessment = assess_cohort(
                recorder,
                cutoff,
                asof=cutoff + offset,
                decisions=decisions,
                local_deliveries=deliveries,
                deadline_age_ms=TIMING_DEADLINES_MS[offset],
            )
            summary["eligibility"] = assessment
            for row in assessment["rows"]:
                product = result["products"][row["product_id"]]
                product["complete_inputs_rows"] += row["input_status"] == "ready"
                on_time_candidate = (
                    row["complete_vector_candidate"]
                    and row["delivery"]["status"] == "within_bound"
                )
                product[
                    "complete_non_benchmark_observed_by_bound_rows"
                ] += on_time_candidate
                on_time[row["direction"]] += on_time_candidate
        result["cutoff_comparisons"].append(summary)
    for offset, ages in ages_by_offset.items():
        report[str(offset)]["cutoff_to_consumer"] = _summary(ages)
    return report


def _timing_options(parser, args, now):
    timing = args.profile == TIMING_PROFILE
    if not timing and (args.window is not None or args.startup_smoke):
        parser.error("window and startup smoke require the eligibility timing profile")
    if not timing:
        return PRODUCTS, (LATER_ASOF_MS,) * COHORTS, GRID_MS, DURATION_S
    if args.startup_smoke:
        if args.window is not None:
            parser.error("startup smoke is separate from the declared windows")
        return TIMING_PRODUCTS, (), TIMING_STEP_MS, 60
    if args.window is None:
        parser.error("the eligibility timing profile requires a declared UTC window")
    start, end = UTC_WINDOWS[args.window]
    seconds = now.hour * 3600 + now.minute * 60 + now.second
    # Leave a minute for the socket and subscription before the timed session.
    if not start * 3600 <= seconds <= end * 3600 - DURATION_S - 60:
        parser.error("start early enough to finish the attempt inside its UTC window")
    pair = (LATER_ASOF_MS, 1500) if args.window == "europe" else (1500, LATER_ASOF_MS)
    return TIMING_PRODUCTS, pair * COHORTS, TIMING_STEP_MS, DURATION_S


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    paths = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    paths.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path)
    paths.add_argument("--observe", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--profile", choices=PROFILES, default=DEFAULT_PROFILE)
    parser.add_argument("--window", choices=UTC_WINDOWS)
    parser.add_argument("--startup-smoke", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--observer-limit-s", type=int, choices=(100, MAX_OBSERVER_S))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.observe is not None:
        out = args.observe.resolve()
        _observe(
            out / "capture.sqlite",
            out / "observed.jsonl",
            out / "observer.stop",
            limit_s=args.observer_limit_s or 100,
        )
        return
    if args.observer_limit_s is not None:
        parser.error("observer lifetime is only for the read process")

    import websocket

    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    products, offsets, step_ms, duration_s = _timing_options(parser, args, now)
    timing = args.profile == TIMING_PROFILE
    extended = timing and not args.startup_smoke
    packet_limit = TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT if extended else WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT
    record_limit = TIMING_RECORD_LIMIT_BYTES if extended else MAX_RECORD_BYTES
    out = args.out_dir.resolve()
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    db = out / "capture.sqlite"
    queue_frames, batch_frames = PROFILES[args.profile]
    first = (utc_ms() // GRID_MS + 1) * GRID_MS
    plan = dict(
        version=TIMING_VERSION if timing else VERSION,
        implementation_commit=subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True
        ).strip(),
        started_utc=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        endpoint=URL,
        products=products,
        channels=CHANNELS,
        profile=args.profile,
        duration_s=duration_s,
        max_connections=1,
        packet_stop=packet_limit - 10,
        byte_stop=TIMING_SOFT_RECORD_BYTES if extended else SOFT_RECORD_BYTES,
        max_reader_queue_frames=queue_frames,
        max_batch_frames=batch_frames,
        warmup_ms=WARMUP_MS,
        cohort_count=len(offsets),
        cohort_evidence_offset_ms=None if timing else LATER_ASOF_MS,
        local_consumer_poll_ms=POLL_S * 1000,
    )
    if timing:
        plan.update(
            window_utc=args.window,
            startup_smoke=args.startup_smoke,
            extended_capacity=extended,
            cohort_step_ms=step_ms,
            cohort_evidence_offsets_ms=offsets,
            diagnostic_deadlines_ms=TIMING_DEADLINES_MS,
            observer_limit_s=MAX_OBSERVER_S if offsets else None,
        )
    (out / "PLAN.json").write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    sockets = []

    def connect():
        ws = websocket.create_connection(URL, timeout=5, enable_multithread=True)
        ws.settimeout(1)
        socket = QueuedSocket(
            ws, (websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException,), max_frames=queue_frames
        )
        sockets.append(socket)
        return socket

    with StreamJournal(
        db,
        products,
        first,
        first + (69 * 60000 if timing else 4320000),
        max_packets=packet_limit,
        max_record_bytes=record_limit,
        indexed=True,
        extended_capacity=extended,
        batch_frames=batch_frames,
        monotonic_ns=time.perf_counter_ns,
    ) as journal:
        cohorts = DiagnosticCohorts(
            journal, out, products=products, offsets=offsets, step_ms=step_ms
        )
        try:
            result = capture(
                journal,
                connect,
                timeout_errors=(TimeoutError,),
                transport_errors=(OSError, websocket.WebSocketException),
                duration_s=plan["duration_s"],
                packet_stop=plan["packet_stop"],
                byte_stop=plan["byte_stop"],
                after_commit=cohorts.after_commit,
                max_connections=plan["max_connections"],
                products=products,
            )
        finally:
            observer_exit = cohorts.stop_observer()
        result["coverage"] = None
        if not journal.evidence.failed:
            journal.finish()
            if (
                result["first_ack_ts_ms"] is not None
                and result["last_reader_receipt_ts_ms"] is not None
            ):
                result["coverage"] = _coverage(
                    journal.evidence,
                    result["first_ack_ts_ms"],
                    result["last_reader_receipt_ts_ms"],
                    products=products,
                )
    result.update(plan)
    result["segment"] = read_segment(db)
    result["recovered_pending_ingress_frames"] = sum(
        1 for _ in iter_pending_ingress(db)
    )
    result["journal_sha256"] = _file_sha(db)
    result["journal_bytes"] = db.stat().st_size
    result["reader"] = [socket.reader_report() for socket in sockets]
    result["diagnostic_cohorts"] = dict(
        planned=len(offsets), complete=len(cohorts.elapsed)
    )
    result["cohort_serial"] = _summary(cohorts.elapsed)
    result["decision_preparation"] = _summary(
        [row["prepare_ms"] for row in cohorts.rows]
    )
    result["outbox_commit"] = _summary([row["outbox_ms"] for row in cohorts.rows])
    result["local_reader"] = _observation_report(out, cohorts.rows)
    result["local_reader"]["process_exit_code"] = observer_exit
    result["diagnostic_rows"] = cohorts.rows
    if timing and not args.startup_smoke:
        result["timing_readiness"] = _timing_readiness(cohorts)
    result["feed_gate_pass"] = False
    result["fitting_allowed"] = False
    result["public_delivery_measured"] = False
    (out / "REPORT.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(
        f"Indexed real pilot stopped: {result['stop']}. Report at {out / 'REPORT.json'}",
        flush=True,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 75 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_samples.py | sha256=0d3f07c032b5d3e48c1746033cb35f34241c1bcbed9836fb55045f193405b3bd =====
"""Pair legacy offline ticker snapshots at one immutable knowledge cutoff."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

from instant_targets import Sample


VERSION = "instant-paired-sample-v1-draft"
CONTRACT = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    / "docs/research/INSTANT_PAIRED_SAMPLE_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PairedSample:
    version: str
    product_id: str
    ts_ms: int
    sample: Sample | None
    unavailable_reasons: tuple[str, ...]
    coin_flow_available: bool
    producer_recorder_sha256: str
    input_provenance_sha256: str
    adapter_sha256: str
    adapter_contract_sha256: str


def paired_sample(store, product, cutoff, *, benchmark="BTC-USD"):
    """No retrospective-asof override. Unavailable rows stay explicit and absent.

    Uses ReplayStore's existing evidence assertions and receipt quote TTL. It
    does not upgrade their trust or accept coverage-monitor proofs as quotes.
    """
    raw = CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
    cfg = json.loads(raw)
    if (
        cfg["version"] != VERSION
        or cfg["grid_ms"] != 5000
        or cfg["retrospective_override_allowed"] is not False
    ):
        raise ValueError("paired-sample contract and implementation differ")
    coin = store.snapshot(product, cutoff)
    btc = coin if product == benchmark else store.snapshot(benchmark, cutoff)
    reasons = tuple(
        f"{name}:{row['reason']}"
        for name, row in (("coin", coin), ("benchmark", btc))
        if row["reason"]
    )
    known = max(coin["available_ts_ms"], btc["available_ts_ms"])
    if known > cutoff:
        reasons += ("input_available_after_cutoff",)
    sample = (
        None
        if reasons
        else Sample(
            product_id=product,
            ts_ms=cutoff,
            bid=coin["bid"],
            ask=coin["ask"],
            btc_bid=btc["bid"],
            btc_ask=btc["ask"],
            quote_ts_ms=coin["quote_ts_ms"],
            btc_quote_ts_ms=btc["quote_ts_ms"],
            available_ts_ms=known,
            trade_count=coin["trade_count"],
            quote_volume_usd=coin["quote_volume_usd"],
            coverage_verified=coin["coverage_verified"] and btc["coverage_verified"],
            trade_count_at_bucket_start=coin["trade_count_at_bucket_start"],
        )
    )
    provenance = store.provenance()
    return PairedSample(
        VERSION,
        product,
        cutoff,
        sample,
        reasons,
        coin["flow_available"],
        provenance["producer_recorder_sha256"],
        hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(
                provenance, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False
            ).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
        hashlib.sha256(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
        hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
    )

===== BEGIN SOURCE 76 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_source_pilot.py | sha256=58d3a5f78fdff9dd8e01771c21484633d46e56954e4db38727f46eabc232b688 =====
"""Single-socket Coinbase Exchange source and latency pilot."""

import argparse
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from contextlib import nullcontext
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import time

from instant_coverage import MAX_PACKETS, WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT
from instant_input_evidence import _file_sha
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal, read_segment
from instant_timed_features import _book_pressure, build_timed_features


VERSION = "instant-source-pilot-v2-draft"
URL = "wss://ws-feed.exchange.coinbase.com"
PRODUCTS = ("BTC-USD", "SOL-USD", "ALCX-USD", "AURORA-USD")
CHANNELS = ("level2_batch", "matches", "heartbeat", "ticker")
DURATION_S = 67 * 60
WARMUP_MS = 62 * 60000
PROBE_MS = 30000
LATER_ASOF_MS = 5000
ACK_TIMEOUT_S = 20
IDLE_TIMEOUT_S = 15
MAX_CONNECTIONS = 2
SOFT_PACKETS = 99990
SOFT_RECORD_BYTES = 240 * 1024 * 1024


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReceivedFrame:
    raw: str | bytes
    receipt_ts_ms: int
    receipt_ns: int


class QueueOverflowError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def utc_ms():
    return time.time_ns() // 1000000


def _summary(values):
    if not values:
        return {"n": 0}
    ordered = sorted(values)
    return dict(
        n=len(ordered),
        p50_ms=round(ordered[math.ceil(len(ordered) * 0.5) - 1], 3),
        p95_ms=round(ordered[math.ceil(len(ordered) * 0.95) - 1], 3),
        p99_ms=round(ordered[math.ceil(len(ordered) * 0.99) - 1], 3),
        max_ms=round(ordered[-1], 3),
    )


def _exchange_ms(value):
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return None
    try:
        parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value)
        return parsed.timestamp() * 1000 if parsed.tzinfo else None
    except (ValueError, OverflowError, OSError):
        return None


class PilotCallbacks:
    """Socket callbacks run on the same thread as the journal writer."""

    def __init__(self, journal, *, duration_ns=time.perf_counter_ns, products=PRODUCTS):
        self.journal = journal
        self.products = tuple(products)
        if set(self.products) != set(journal.evidence.products):
            raise ValueError("subscription roster must match journal inventory")
        self.duration_ns = duration_ns
        self.connection = None
        self.ack = None
        self.last_reader_receipt_ts_ms = None
        self.frames = Counter()
        self.metrics = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
        self.sides = defaultdict(dict)

    def on_open(self, socket):
        self.ack = None
        self.connection = self.journal.open_connection()
        socket.send(
            json.dumps(
                dict(type="subscribe", product_ids=self.products, channels=CHANNELS)
            )
        )

    def on_message(self, raw):
        started = self.duration_ns()
        received = None
        if isinstance(raw, ReceivedFrame):
            received = raw.receipt_ts_ms, raw.receipt_ns
            raw = raw.raw
        receipt = self.journal.receive(self.connection, raw, received=received)
        elapsed = (self.duration_ns() - started) / 1000000
        self._account(receipt, raw, received, started, elapsed)

    def on_batch(self, frames):
        started = self.duration_ns()
        stamped = [
            (frame.raw, (frame.receipt_ts_ms, frame.receipt_ns)) for frame in frames
        ]
        receipts = self.journal.receive_batch(self.connection, stamped)
        elapsed = (self.duration_ns() - started) / 1000000
        for receipt, (raw, received) in zip(receipts, stamped):
            self._account(receipt, raw, received, started, elapsed)

    def _account(self, receipt, raw, received, started, elapsed):
        kind, product = receipt.message_type, receipt.product_id
        group = f"{kind}:{product}" if product in self.products else kind
        self.frames[group] += 1
        metrics = self.metrics[group]
        metrics["callback_through_commit"].append(elapsed)
        if received is not None:
            self.last_reader_receipt_ts_ms = receipt.receipt_ts_ms
            queue_ms = (started - received[1]) / 1000000
            metrics["reader_to_writer"].append(queue_ms)
            metrics["reader_through_commit"].append(queue_ms + elapsed)
        metrics["receipt_to_processing"].append(
            receipt.processing_completed_ts_ms - receipt.receipt_ts_ms
        )
        metrics["processing_to_commit_observation"].append(
            receipt.commit_observed_ts_ms - receipt.processing_completed_ts_ms
        )
        exchange = _exchange_ms(receipt.exchange_time)
        if exchange is not None:
            metrics["exchange_stamp_to_callback"].append(
                receipt.receipt_ts_ms - exchange
            )
        if kind in ("match", "ticker") and product in self.products:
            message = json.loads(raw)
            trade_id, side = message.get("trade_id"), message.get("side")
            if type(trade_id) is int and side in ("buy", "sell"):
                self.sides[(product, trade_id)].setdefault(kind, side)
        if kind == "subscriptions":
            ticker_products = {
                product
                for entry in json.loads(raw)["channels"]
                if entry["name"] == "ticker"
                for product in entry["product_ids"]
            }
            confirmed = set()
            for state in reversed(self.journal.evidence.book.monitor.transport):
                if state["receipt_seq"] < receipt.receipt_seq:
                    break
                if (
                    state["receipt_seq"] == receipt.receipt_seq
                    and state["reason"] == "awaiting_heartbeat"
                ):
                    confirmed.add(state["product_id"])
            if set(self.products) <= ticker_products & confirmed and self.ack is None:
                self.ack = receipt.receipt_ts_ms

    def on_close(self, *, reason="socket_closed"):
        if self.journal.connection_active:
            self.journal.end_connection(self.connection, reason=reason)
        self.connection = None

    def report(self):
        side_pairs = {pid: Counter() for pid in self.products}
        for (pid, _), sides in self.sides.items():
            if {"match", "ticker"} <= sides.keys():
                side_pairs[pid]["paired"] += 1
                side_pairs[pid][
                    "opposite" if sides["match"] != sides["ticker"] else "same"
                ] += 1
        return dict(
            frames=dict(self.frames),
            latencies={
                group: {
                    metric: {
                        **_summary(values),
                        "negative_n": sum(value < 0 for value in values),
                    }
                    for metric, values in by_metric.items()
                }
                for group, by_metric in self.metrics.items()
            },
            ticker_match_sides={pid: dict(side_pairs[pid]) for pid in self.products},
        )


def capture(
    journal,
    connect,
    *,
    monotonic=time.monotonic,
    duration_ns=time.perf_counter_ns,
    timeout_errors=(TimeoutError,),
    transport_errors=(OSError,),
    duration_s=DURATION_S,
    packet_stop=SOFT_PACKETS,
    byte_stop=SOFT_RECORD_BYTES,
    progress=print,
    after_commit=None,
    max_connections=MAX_CONNECTIONS,
    products=PRODUCTS,
):
    """Run the callback path and give the post-commit hook the first ACK receipt."""
    callbacks = PilotCallbacks(journal, duration_ns=duration_ns, products=products)
    first_ack = deadline = None
    last_progress = monotonic()
    stop = "transport_unavailable"
    attempts = []
    for _ in range(max_connections):
        if deadline is not None and monotonic() >= deadline:
            stop = "duration"
            break
        socket = None
        attempt = {}
        attempts.append(attempt)
        close_reason = "socket_closed"
        try:
            socket = connect()
            callbacks.on_open(socket)
            attempt["connection_id"] = callbacks.connection
            opened = last_frame = monotonic()
            while True:
                now = monotonic()
                if deadline is not None and now >= deadline:
                    stop = "duration"
                    break
                if callbacks.ack is None and now - opened >= ACK_TIMEOUT_S:
                    stop = "subscription_timeout"
                    close_reason = "transport_error"
                    break
                if now - last_frame >= IDLE_TIMEOUT_S:
                    stop = "transport_idle"
                    close_reason = "transport_error"
                    break
                try:
                    raw = socket.recv()
                except timeout_errors:
                    continue
                if raw in ("", b""):
                    stop = "remote_close"
                    break
                last_frame = monotonic()
                activity = getattr(
                    socket, "writer_activity", lambda *args: nullcontext()
                )
                if journal.batch_frames > 1:
                    extra = (
                        min(journal.batch_frames, packet_stop - journal.packet_count)
                        - 1
                    )
                    frames = (raw, *socket.recv_ready(max(0, extra)))
                    with activity("journal_callback", len(frames)):
                        callbacks.on_batch(frames)
                else:
                    with activity("journal_callback", 1):
                        callbacks.on_message(raw)
                if not journal.connection_active:
                    stop = "exchange_or_source_error"
                    break
                if callbacks.ack is not None:
                    attempt.setdefault("ack_ts_ms", callbacks.ack)
                    if first_ack is None:
                        first_ack = callbacks.ack
                        deadline = monotonic() + duration_s
                        progress(
                            f"Subscriptions acknowledged for {len(callbacks.products)} products.",
                            flush=True,
                        )
                if (
                    journal.packet_count >= packet_stop
                    or journal.record_bytes >= byte_stop
                ):
                    stop = (
                        "packet_budget"
                        if journal.packet_count >= packet_stop
                        else "byte_budget"
                    )
                    break
                if after_commit is not None and first_ack is not None:
                    with activity("after_commit"):
                        after_commit(first_ack)
                if monotonic() - last_progress >= 60:
                    progress(
                        f"{journal.packet_count} packets committed, {journal.record_bytes} logical bytes.",
                        flush=True,
                    )
                    last_progress = monotonic()
        except QueueOverflowError as exc:
            stop = "queue_overflow"
            attempt["error"] = str(exc)
            close_reason = "queue_overflow"
        except transport_errors as exc:
            stop = "transport_error"
            attempt["error"] = str(exc)
            close_reason = "transport_error"
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            stop = "interrupted"
        except Exception as exc:
            stop = "journal_failure" if journal.evidence.failed else "callback_failure"
            attempt["error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        finally:
            try:
                callbacks.on_close(reason=close_reason)
            except Exception as exc:
                stop = "journal_failure"
                attempt["close_error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            finally:
                if socket is not None:
                    try:
                        socket.close(timeout=1)
                    except transport_errors as exc:
                        attempt["socket_close_error"] = str(exc)
        attempt["stop"] = stop
        if first_ack is None or stop not in (
            "remote_close",
            "transport_idle",
            "transport_error",
        ):
            break
    return dict(
        stop=stop,
        first_ack_ts_ms=first_ack,
        attempts=attempts,
        last_reader_receipt_ts_ms=callbacks.last_reader_receipt_ts_ms,
        **callbacks.report(),
    )


def _coverage(recorder, first_ack, stop_ts, *, products=PRODUCTS):
    probes = list(
        range(
            ((first_ack + PROBE_MS - 1) // PROBE_MS + 1) * PROBE_MS,
            stop_ts - LATER_ASOF_MS + 1,
            PROBE_MS,
        )
    )
    by_product = {}
    for product in products:
        raw = [p for p in recorder.book.monitor.proofs if p["product_id"] == product]
        proofs = Counter(
            (
                "reconciled"
                if p.get("supersedes_end_heartbeat_seqs")
                else "strict_verified" if p["verified"] else p["reason"]
            )
            for p in raw
        )
        views = {}
        for lag in (0, LATER_ASOF_MS):
            quotes, matches, pressure = Counter(), Counter(), Counter()
            quote_ages, proof_lags = [], []
            for cutoff in probes:
                asof = cutoff + lag
                quote, _ = recorder._quote_at(product, cutoff, asof)
                quotes["ready" if quote["available"] else quote["reason"]] += 1
                if quote["available"]:
                    quote_ages.append(quote["observation_age_ms"])
                _, _, reason, available = recorder._trade_window(
                    product, cutoff - PROBE_MS, cutoff, asof
                )
                matches[reason or "ready"] += 1
                if available is not None:
                    proof_lags.append(available - cutoff)
                book = _book_pressure(
                    recorder, product, cutoff - PROBE_MS, cutoff, asof, 1
                )
                pressure[book.get("reason", "ready")] += 1
            views[str(lag)] = dict(
                quote=dict(quotes),
                trade_window=dict(matches),
                sampled_book_window=dict(pressure),
                ready_quote_age=_summary(quote_ages),
                ready_proof_lag_from_cutoff=_summary(proof_lags),
            )
        by_product[product] = dict(
            probes=len(probes),
            proof_counts=dict(proofs),
            asof_offset_ms=views,
            first_receipt_matches=sum(t.product_id == product for t in recorder.trades),
        )
    warm_cutoff = next((t for t in probes if t >= first_ack + WARMUP_MS), None)
    warmup = dict(first_post_warmup_cutoff_ts_ms=warm_cutoff, readiness={})
    if warm_cutoff is not None:
        for product in products:
            started = time.perf_counter_ns()
            row = build_timed_features(
                recorder, product, warm_cutoff, "up", asof=warm_cutoff + LATER_ASOF_MS
            )
            warmup["readiness"][product] = dict(
                features={
                    key: value.get("reason", "ready")
                    for key, value in row["features"].items()
                },
                baseline={
                    name: row["baseline"][name].get("reason", "ready")
                    for name in ("volatility", "pace")
                },
                post_capture_calculation_ms=round(
                    (time.perf_counter_ns() - started) / 1000000, 3
                ),
            )
    return dict(by_product=by_product, warmup=warmup)


def main():
    import websocket

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--warmup-capacity", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    max_packets = WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT if args.warmup_capacity else MAX_PACKETS
    packet_stop = max_packets - (MAX_PACKETS - SOFT_PACKETS)
    duration_clock = time.get_clock_info("perf_counter")
    out = args.out_dir.resolve()
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    db_path = out / "capture.sqlite"
    first_cutoff = (utc_ms() // 5000 + 1) * 5000
    commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
    metadata = dict(
        version=VERSION,
        code_commit=commit,
        started_utc=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        products=PRODUCTS,
        channels=CHANNELS,
        endpoint=URL,
        seconds=DURATION_S,
        packet_stop=packet_stop,
        max_packets=max_packets,
        callback_duration_clock=duration_clock.implementation,
        callback_duration_clock_resolution_ns=duration_clock.resolution * 1e9,
        exchange_stamp_to_callback_scope="signed_wall_clock_difference",
        exchange_clock_offset_applied=False,
        logical_byte_stop=SOFT_RECORD_BYTES,
        max_connections=MAX_CONNECTIONS,
        websocket_client=websocket.__version__,
    )
    (out / "PLAN.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(metadata, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )

    def connect():
        socket = websocket.create_connection(URL, timeout=5, enable_multithread=False)
        socket.settimeout(2)
        return socket

    monotonic_start = time.monotonic()
    with StreamJournal(
        db_path, PRODUCTS, first_cutoff, first_cutoff + 4320000, max_packets=max_packets
    ) as journal:
        result = capture(
            journal,
            connect,
            packet_stop=packet_stop,
            timeout_errors=(websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException,),
            transport_errors=(OSError, websocket.WebSocketException),
        )
        stop_ts = utc_ms()
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - monotonic_start
        result["stop_ts_ms"] = stop_ts
        result["elapsed_seconds"] = round(elapsed, 3)
        result["logical_record_bytes"] = journal.record_bytes
        result["coverage"] = None
        if not journal.evidence.failed:
            journal.finish()
            if result["first_ack_ts_ms"] is not None:
                result["coverage"] = _coverage(
                    journal.evidence, result["first_ack_ts_ms"], stop_ts
                )
    result.update(metadata)
    result["segment"] = read_segment(db_path)
    result["journal_sha256"] = _file_sha(db_path)
    result["journal_bytes"] = db_path.stat().st_size
    result["full_feed_verified"] = False
    result["feed_gate_pass"] = False
    result["fitting_allowed"] = False
    result["external_publication_measured"] = False
    (out / "REPORT.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(
        f"Pilot stopped: {result['stop']}. Report at {out / 'REPORT.json'}", flush=True
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 77 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_stream_journal.py | sha256=3b24834f2dfb0ceb622448aef5295d241977eced984b5f6b02367666ecb3139c =====
"""Bounded, single-writer journal for prospective instant-radar inputs."""

from contextlib import closing
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
from threading import get_ident
import time
from uuid import uuid4

from instant_coverage import (
    MAX_PACKETS,
    TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT,
    WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT,
    _integer,
)
from instant_delayed_decision import _check_prefix
from instant_input_evidence import InputEvidenceRecorder, _json, _sha


VERSION = "instant-stream-journal-v1-draft"
MAX_RECORD_BYTES = 256 * 1024 * 1024
TIMING_RECORD_LIMIT_BYTES = 512 * 1024 * 1024
MAX_FRAME_BYTES = 1024 * 1024
MAX_BATCH_FRAMES = 16
END_EVENTS = {
    "socket_closed": "connection_closed",
    "transport_error": "audit_transport_error",
    "queue_overflow": "queue_overflow",
}

SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE segment (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
    manifest TEXT NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE packets (
    receipt_seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    body TEXT NOT NULL,
    digest TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE pending_ingress (
    receipt_seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    body TEXT NOT NULL,
    digest TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE publications (
    publication_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    decision_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
    body TEXT NOT NULL,
    digest TEXT NOT NULL,
    local_commit_observed_ts_ms INTEGER
);
"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JournalReceipt:
    receipt_seq: int
    receipt_ts_ms: int
    processing_completed_ts_ms: int
    commit_observed_ts_ms: int
    message_type: str
    product_id: str | None
    exchange_time: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LocalPublication:
    publication_id: str
    decision_sha256: str
    local_commit_observed_ts_ms: int | None


def _encoded(value):
    body = _json(value)
    return body, _sha(body.encode("utf-8"))


def _decoded(body, digest):
    if _sha(body.encode("utf-8")) != digest:
        raise ValueError("journal record digest mismatch")
    return json.loads(body)


def _read_connection(path):
    return sqlite3.connect(Path(path).resolve().as_uri() + "?mode=rw", uri=True)


def _local_publication(db, publication_id):
    found = db.execute(
        """
        SELECT decision_sha256, local_commit_observed_ts_ms, body, digest
        FROM publications WHERE publication_id = ?
        """,
        (publication_id,),
    ).fetchone()
    if found is None:
        return None
    decision_sha, observed, body, digest = found
    record = _decoded(body, digest)
    actual = _sha(_json(record["decision"]).encode("utf-8"))
    if record["publication_id"] != publication_id or decision_sha != actual:
        raise ValueError("journal publication identity mismatch")
    if observed is not None and observed < record["commit_requested_ts_ms"]:
        raise ValueError("journal publication clock mismatch")
    return LocalPublication(publication_id, decision_sha, observed), record


class StreamJournal:
    """A fresh segment consumes frames from one caller-owned socket at a time.

    The synchronous API provides backpressure at this boundary. Each call must
    finish before the producer dispatches the next frame or lifecycle event.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        path,
        products,
        first_cutoff,
        stop_cutoff,
        *,
        max_packets=MAX_PACKETS,
        max_record_bytes=MAX_RECORD_BYTES,
        clock_ms=None,
        monotonic_ns=None,
        indexed=False,
        batch_frames=1,
        extended_capacity=False,
    ):
        packet_ceiling = (
            TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT if extended_capacity else WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT
        )
        byte_ceiling = (
            TIMING_RECORD_LIMIT_BYTES if extended_capacity else MAX_RECORD_BYTES
        )
        if not 1 <= _integer(max_packets) <= packet_ceiling or not (
            1 <= _integer(max_record_bytes) <= byte_ceiling
        ):
            raise ValueError("journal bounds exceed the supported range")
        if not 1 <= _integer(batch_frames) <= MAX_BATCH_FRAMES:
            raise ValueError("journal batch size outside the supported range")
        self.batch_frames = batch_frames
        self.evidence = InputEvidenceRecorder(
            products,
            first_cutoff,
            stop_cutoff,
            clock_ms=clock_ms,
            packet_limit=max_packets,
            indexed=indexed,
            extended_capacity=extended_capacity,
        )
        self._monotonic = monotonic_ns or time.monotonic_ns
        self._max_packets = max_packets
        self._max_record_bytes = max_record_bytes
        self._bytes = self._packet_count = self._connection_count = 0
        self._active = None
        self._status = "recording"
        self._owner_thread = get_ident()
        self._segment_id = uuid4().hex

        path = Path(path).resolve()
        with path.open("xb"):
            pass
        self._db = sqlite3.connect(path)
        try:
            mode = self._db.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = DELETE").fetchone()[0]
            self._db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = EXTRA")
            sync = self._db.execute("PRAGMA synchronous").fetchone()[0]
            if (mode, sync) != ("delete", 3):
                raise RuntimeError("journal durability settings unavailable")
            self._db.executescript(SCHEMA)
            manifest = dict(
                version=VERSION,
                segment_id=self._segment_id,
                inventory=json.loads(self.evidence.inventory),
                inventory_sha256=self.evidence.inventory_sha256,
                inventory_recorded_ts_ms=self.evidence.inventory_recorded_ts_ms,
                input_implementation_sha256=self.evidence.source_sha256,
                input_contract_sha256=self.evidence.contract_sha256,
                book_implementation_sha256=self.evidence.book.source_sha256,
                book_contract_sha256=self.evidence.book.contract_sha256,
                monitor_implementation_sha256=self.evidence.book.monitor.source_sha256,
                monitor_contract_sha256=self.evidence.book.monitor.contract_sha256,
                journal_implementation_sha256=_sha(
                    Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
                ),
                index_implementation_sha256=(
                    self.evidence._index.source_sha256 if indexed else None
                ),
                max_packets=max_packets,
                max_record_bytes=max_record_bytes,
                max_frame_bytes=MAX_FRAME_BYTES,
                batch_frames=batch_frames,
                journal_mode=mode,
                synchronous=sync,
            )
            if extended_capacity:
                manifest["extended_capacity"] = True
            with self._db:
                self._db.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO segment VALUES (1, ?, 'recording')", (_json(manifest),)
                )
        except BaseException:
            self._db.close()
            raise

    @property
    def packet_count(self):
        return self._packet_count

    @property
    def record_bytes(self):
        return self._bytes

    @property
    def connection_active(self):
        return self._active is not None

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc):
        self.close()

    def close(self):
        self._db.close()
        self._status = "closed_handle"

    def _writable(self):
        if get_ident() != self._owner_thread:
            raise ValueError("journal writes require the owner thread")
        if self._status != "recording" or self.evidence.failed:
            raise ValueError("journal segment is not writable")

    def _stop(self, status):
        self._status = status
        with self._db:
            self._db.execute("UPDATE segment SET status = ? WHERE id = 1", (status,))

    def _fail(self, status):
        self.evidence.failed = True
        self._stop(status)

    def _check_bytes(self, size):
        if self._bytes + size > self._max_record_bytes:
            self._fail("record_byte_limit")
            raise ValueError("journal record byte limit exceeded")

    def _envelope(self, seq, connection_id, message, receipt, mono):
        return dict(
            connection_id=connection_id,
            receipt_seq=seq,
            receipt_ts_ms=receipt,
            receipt_monotonic_ns=mono,
            source="ws",
            message=message,
        )

    def _process(self, envelope, ingress_observed=None):
        previous_trade_count = len(self.evidence.trades)
        try:
            self.evidence.ingest(envelope)
            packet = self.evidence.packets[-1]
            trade = (
                asdict(self.evidence.trades[-1])
                if len(self.evidence.trades) > previous_trade_count
                else None
            )
            record = dict(envelope=envelope, processing=asdict(packet), trade=trade)
            if ingress_observed is not None:
                record["ingress_commit_observed_ts_ms"] = ingress_observed
            body, digest = _encoded(record)
        except Exception:
            self._fail("packet_processing_failed")
            raise
        return packet.receipt_seq, body, digest

    def _persist(self, rows, *, pending=False):
        size = sum(len(body.encode("utf-8")) for _, body, _ in rows)
        self._check_bytes(size)
        try:
            with self._db:
                self._db.executemany("INSERT INTO packets VALUES (?, ?, ?)", rows)
                if pending:
                    self._db.execute("DELETE FROM pending_ingress")
            observed = self.evidence._now()
        except Exception:
            # A commit error may be ambiguous. Only a reopened journal can
            # determine which transaction committed.
            self.evidence.failed = True
            self._status = "write_failed"
            raise
        self._bytes += size
        self._packet_count = rows[-1][0]
        return observed

    def _receipt(self, envelope, packet, observed):
        message = envelope["message"]
        return JournalReceipt(
            packet.receipt_seq,
            packet.receipt_ts_ms,
            packet.processing_completed_ts_ms,
            observed,
            message["type"],
            message.get("product_id"),
            message.get("time"),
        )

    def _check_packet_budget(self, additional):
        if self._packet_count + additional > self._max_packets:
            self._fail("packet_limit")
            raise ValueError("journal packet limit exceeded")

    def _record(self, connection_id, message, receipt, mono):
        self._writable()
        self._check_packet_budget(1)
        envelope = self._envelope(
            self._packet_count + 1, connection_id, message, receipt, mono
        )
        observed = self._persist([self._process(envelope)])
        return self._receipt(envelope, self.evidence.packets[-1], observed)

    def _check_connection(self, connection_id):
        self._writable()
        if self._active is None or connection_id != self._active:
            raise ValueError("frame does not belong to the active connection")

    def open_connection(self):
        """Call once after opening a fresh transport instance."""
        self._writable()
        if self._active is not None:
            raise ValueError("close the active connection before opening another")
        self._connection_count += 1
        connection_id = f"{self._segment_id}/{self._connection_count}"
        self._record(
            connection_id,
            {"type": "connection_opened"},
            self.evidence._now(),
            self._monotonic(),
        )
        self._active = connection_id
        return connection_id

    def _decode_frame(self, raw):
        if not isinstance(raw, (str, bytes)):
            raise TypeError("expected a WebSocket text frame")
        if len(raw) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
            self._fail("frame_limit")
            raise ValueError("WebSocket frame byte limit exceeded")
        encoded = (
            raw.encode("utf-8", errors="surrogatepass") if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
        )
        if len(encoded) > MAX_FRAME_BYTES:
            self._fail("frame_limit")
            raise ValueError("WebSocket frame byte limit exceeded")
        try:
            message = json.loads(encoded.decode("utf-8"))
            if not isinstance(message, dict) or not isinstance(
                message.get("type"), str
            ):
                raise ValueError("expected an object with a message type")
            _json(message)
        except (ValueError, RecursionError):
            return (
                dict(
                    type="audit_parse_error",
                    frame_bytes=len(encoded),
                    frame_sha256=_sha(encoded),
                ),
                True,
            )
        return message, False

    @staticmethod
    def _ends_connection(message):
        kind = message["type"]
        return kind in (
            "error",
            "queue_overflow",
            "connection_closed",
        ) or kind.startswith("audit_")

    def receive(self, connection_id, raw, *, received=None):
        """Commit a frame, preserving a measured reader stamp when supplied."""
        self._check_connection(connection_id)
        receipt, mono = (
            (self.evidence._now(), self._monotonic())
            if received is None
            else tuple(map(_integer, received))
        )
        message, invalid = self._decode_frame(raw)
        result = self._record(connection_id, message, receipt, mono)
        if self._ends_connection(message):
            self._active = None
        if invalid:
            raise ValueError("invalid WebSocket frame; reopen the connection")
        return result

    def receive_batch(self, connection_id, frames):
        """Commit bounded FIFO ingress before processing it into decision evidence."""
        self._check_connection(connection_id)
        if self.batch_frames == 1 or not 1 <= len(frames) <= self.batch_frames:
            raise ValueError("batch requires an enabled journal and bounded frames")
        envelopes = []
        for raw, received in frames:
            receipt, mono = map(_integer, received)
            message, invalid = self._decode_frame(raw)
            envelopes.append(
                self._envelope(
                    self._packet_count + len(envelopes) + 1,
                    connection_id,
                    message,
                    receipt,
                    mono,
                )
            )
            if self._ends_connection(message):
                break
        self._check_packet_budget(len(envelopes))
        ingress = [
            (envelope["receipt_seq"], *_encoded(envelope)) for envelope in envelopes
        ]
        self._check_bytes(sum(len(body.encode("utf-8")) for _, body, _ in ingress))
        try:
            with self._db:
                self._db.executemany(
                    "INSERT INTO pending_ingress VALUES (?, ?, ?)", ingress
                )
            ingress_observed = self.evidence._now()
        except Exception:
            self.evidence.failed = True
            self._status = "write_failed"
            raise
        rows = [self._process(envelope, ingress_observed) for envelope in envelopes]
        observed = self._persist(rows, pending=True)
        packets = self.evidence.packets[-len(envelopes) :]
        if self._ends_connection(envelopes[-1]["message"]):
            self._active = None
        if invalid:
            raise ValueError("invalid WebSocket frame; reopen the connection")
        return tuple(
            self._receipt(envelope, packet, observed)
            for envelope, packet in zip(envelopes, packets, strict=True)
        )

    def end_connection(self, connection_id, *, reason="socket_closed"):
        self._check_connection(connection_id)
        if reason not in END_EVENTS:
            raise ValueError("unknown connection end reason")
        receipt = self._record(
            connection_id,
            {"type": END_EVENTS[reason]},
            self.evidence._now(),
            self._monotonic(),
        )
        self._active = None
        return receipt

    def finish(self):
        """Close this segment explicitly after its last socket is closed."""
        self._writable()
        if self._active is not None:
            raise ValueError("close the active connection before finishing")
        self._stop("finished")

    def _acknowledge(self, publication_id, observed):
        with self._db:
            self._db.execute(
                """
                UPDATE publications SET local_commit_observed_ts_ms = ?
                WHERE publication_id = ? AND local_commit_observed_ts_ms IS NULL
                """,
                (observed, publication_id),
            )

    def append_publication(self, publication_id, decision):
        """Commit a local outbox entry and then persist its commit observation."""
        self._writable()
        if not isinstance(publication_id, str) or not 1 <= len(publication_id) <= 128:
            raise ValueError("a bounded publication ID is required")
        decision_sha = decision.sha256
        existing = _local_publication(self._db, publication_id)
        if existing is not None:
            receipt, _ = existing
            if receipt.decision_sha256 != decision_sha:
                raise ValueError("publication ID already refers to another decision")
            return receipt
        _check_prefix(self.evidence, decision, json.loads(decision.feature_json))
        requested = self.evidence._now()
        if requested < decision.decision_completed_ts_ms:
            raise ValueError("decision preparation has not completed")
        body, digest = _encoded(
            dict(
                publication_id=publication_id,
                decision=asdict(decision),
                commit_requested_ts_ms=requested,
            )
        )
        size = len(body.encode("utf-8"))
        self._check_bytes(size)
        try:
            with self._db:
                self._db.execute(
                    "INSERT INTO publications VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, NULL)",
                    (publication_id, decision_sha, body, digest),
                )
            self._bytes += size
            observed = self.evidence._now()
            self._acknowledge(publication_id, observed)
        except Exception:
            self.evidence.failed = True
            self._status = "write_failed"
            raise
        return LocalPublication(publication_id, decision_sha, observed)


def read_segment(path):
    """Recover committed metadata and counts, including an unfinished prefix."""
    with closing(_read_connection(path)) as db:
        body, status, packets, publications = db.execute(
            """
            SELECT manifest, status, (SELECT count(*) FROM packets),
                (SELECT count(*) FROM publications) FROM segment WHERE id = 1
            """
        ).fetchone()
        manifest = json.loads(body)
        if manifest["version"] != VERSION:
            raise ValueError("unsupported journal version")
        return dict(
            manifest=manifest, status=status, packets=packets, publications=publications
        )


def iter_packets(path):
    """Yield committed envelopes and original processing evidence in order."""
    with closing(_read_connection(path)) as db:
        for expected, (seq, body, digest) in enumerate(
            db.execute(
                "SELECT receipt_seq, body, digest FROM packets ORDER BY receipt_seq"
            ),
            1,
        ):
            row = _decoded(body, digest)
            if (
                not expected
                == seq
                == row["envelope"]["receipt_seq"]
                == row["processing"]["receipt_seq"]
            ):
                raise ValueError("journal packet sequence mismatch")
            yield row


def iter_pending_ingress(path):
    """Recover durable input after the processed prefix without inventing evidence."""
    with closing(_read_connection(path)) as db:
        manifest = json.loads(db.execute("SELECT manifest FROM segment").fetchone()[0])
        if manifest.get("batch_frames", 1) == 1:
            return
        db.execute("BEGIN")
        last_seq = db.execute(
            "SELECT coalesce(max(receipt_seq), 0) FROM packets"
        ).fetchone()[0]
        for expected, (seq, body, digest) in enumerate(
            db.execute(
                "SELECT receipt_seq, body, digest FROM pending_ingress ORDER BY receipt_seq"
            ),
            last_seq + 1,
        ):
            envelope = _decoded(body, digest)
            if expected != seq or seq != envelope["receipt_seq"]:
                raise ValueError("pending journal ingress sequence mismatch")
            yield envelope


def read_local_publication(path, publication_id):
    """Recover a local entry and its possibly absent commit observation."""
    with closing(_read_connection(path)) as db:
        return _local_publication(db, publication_id)
