"""Prospective evaluation labels only. Never import into live ranking code.

No I/O occurs on import. Inputs are immutable receipt-time snapshots; late
recovery must not replace them. Baselines must be causal at the anchor.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path


CONTRACT_PATH = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    / "docs/research/INSTANT_TARGET_CONTRACT_V1.json"
)
V2_CONTRACT_PATH = CONTRACT_PATH.with_name("INSTANT_TARGET_CONTRACT_V2_DRAFT.json")


def load_contract(path=None):
    raw = Path(path or CONTRACT_PATH).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
    return json.loads(raw), hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Sample:
    """State available at ts_ms; counts/volume cover [ts_ms-5000, ts_ms)."""

    product_id: str
    ts_ms: int
    bid: float | None
    ask: float | None
    btc_bid: float | None
    btc_ask: float | None
    quote_ts_ms: int
    btc_quote_ts_ms: int
    available_ts_ms: int
    trade_count: int
    quote_volume_usd: float
    coverage_verified: bool
    price_source: str = "mid"
    btc_price_source: str = "mid"
    trade_count_at_bucket_start: int | None = None

    @property
    def mid(self):
        return (self.bid + self.ask) / 2

    @property
    def btc_mid(self):
        return (self.btc_bid + self.btc_ask) / 2

    @property
    def spread_bps(self):
        return (self.ask - self.bid) / self.mid * 10000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Baseline:
    sigma_1m: float
    beta: float
    return_count: int
    end_ts_ms: int
    available_ts_ms: int
    beta_available_ts_ms: int
    beta_fallback: bool
    pace_trade_median_30s: float
    pace_quote_usd_median_30s: float
    pace_window_count: int
    pace_end_ts_ms: int
    pace_available_ts_ms: int


def finite(value):
    return (
        isinstance(value, (float, int))
        and not isinstance(value, bool)
        and math.isfinite(value)
    )


class TargetEvaluator:
    def __init__(self, samples, contract_path=None):
        self.cfg, self.contract_sha256 = load_contract(contract_path)
        if self.cfg["version"] not in ("instant-target-v1", "instant-target-v2-draft"):
            raise ValueError("unsupported target contract")
        self.is_v2 = self.cfg["version"] == "instant-target-v2-draft"
        self.implementation_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(
            Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        self.rows = {}
        self.product_id = None
        previous = None
        for row in samples:
            if (
                not isinstance(row.ts_ms, int)
                or isinstance(row.ts_ms, bool)
                or row.ts_ms % self.cfg["grid_ms"]
            ):
                raise ValueError("sample timestamps must lie on the 5s grid")
            if previous is not None and row.ts_ms <= previous:
                raise ValueError("samples must be strictly increasing and unique")
            if self.product_id is not None and row.product_id != self.product_id:
                raise ValueError("one product per evaluator")
            self.product_id = row.product_id
            self.rows[row.ts_ms] = row
            previous = row.ts_ms
        if not self.rows:
            raise ValueError("samples are required")

    def _quality(self, start, end):
        for ts in range(start, end + 1, self.cfg["grid_ms"]):
            row = self.rows.get(ts)
            if row is None:
                return "missing_grid_point"
            if row.coverage_verified is not True:
                return "unverified_coverage"
            if any(
                type(value) is not int
                for value in (row.available_ts_ms, row.quote_ts_ms, row.btc_quote_ts_ms)
            ):
                return "invalid_timestamp"
            if row.available_ts_ms > ts:
                return "future_receipt"
            if row.price_source != "mid" or row.btc_price_source != "mid":
                return "price_fallback"
            for bid, ask, observed in (
                (row.bid, row.ask, row.quote_ts_ms),
                (row.btc_bid, row.btc_ask, row.btc_quote_ts_ms),
            ):
                if not finite(bid) or not finite(ask) or bid <= 0 or ask < bid:
                    return "invalid_quote"
                if not 0 <= ts - observed <= self.cfg["max_quote_age_ms"]:
                    return "stale_or_future_quote"
            if type(row.trade_count) is not int or row.trade_count < 0:
                return "invalid_trade_count"
            if not finite(row.quote_volume_usd) or row.quote_volume_usd < 0:
                return "invalid_volume"
        return None

    def _baseline_quality(self, baseline, anchor):
        if baseline is None:
            return "missing_baseline"
        if (
            any(
                type(value) is not int
                for value in (
                    baseline.return_count,
                    baseline.end_ts_ms,
                    baseline.available_ts_ms,
                    baseline.beta_available_ts_ms,
                )
            )
            or type(baseline.beta_fallback) is not bool
        ):
            return "invalid_baseline_metadata"
        if (
            not finite(baseline.sigma_1m)
            or baseline.sigma_1m < 0
            or not finite(baseline.beta)
        ):
            return "invalid_baseline"
        if baseline.return_count < self.cfg["minimum_baseline_returns"]:
            return "baseline_warming"
        baseline_cutoff = anchor
        if self.is_v2:
            # Reviewed from Claude d711832. Apply only in the explicit v2
            # draft so the pinned v1 definition and outputs remain reproducible.
            baseline_cutoff -= (
                self.cfg["onset"]["velocity_window_seconds"] * 1000
                + self.cfg["grid_ms"]
            )
        if baseline.end_ts_ms != baseline_cutoff // 60000 * 60000:
            return "baseline_window_misaligned"
        if baseline.available_ts_ms > anchor or baseline.beta_available_ts_ms > anchor:
            return "future_baseline"
        return None

    def _residual(self, start, end, beta, direction):
        a, b = self.rows[start], self.rows[end]
        return direction * (
            math.log(b.mid / a.mid) - beta * math.log(b.btc_mid / a.btc_mid)
        )

    def evaluate(self, anchor, baseline, direction, horizon_seconds, asof_ts_ms):
        """Grade every eligible grid anchor, including no-move observations.

        Status 'ready' means a complete label, not a successful impulse.
        All returned outcome/onset fields are evaluation-only.
        """
        if type(horizon_seconds) is not int or type(asof_ts_ms) is not int:
            raise ValueError("horizon and as-of timestamp must be integers")
        if (
            direction not in ("up", "down")
            or horizon_seconds not in self.cfg["outcome_horizons_seconds"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("unsupported direction or horizon")
        if type(anchor) is not int or anchor % self.cfg["grid_ms"]:
            raise ValueError("anchor must lie on the 5s grid")
        sign = 1 if direction == "up" else -1
        end = anchor + horizon_seconds * 1000
        result = dict(
            contract_version=self.cfg["version"],
            contract_sha256=self.contract_sha256,
            evaluation_only=True,
            product_id=self.product_id,
            anchor_ts_ms=anchor,
            horizon_seconds=horizon_seconds,
            direction=direction,
            label_maturity_ts_ms=end,
            status="pending",
            reason="horizon_not_mature",
            verified_forward=None,
            verified_onset=None,
        )
        if self.is_v2:
            result.update(
                evaluator_sha256=self.implementation_sha256,
                primary_pointwise_target="verified_forward",
                physical_onset_defined=False,
            )
        if asof_ts_ms < end:
            return result
        reason = self._baseline_quality(baseline, anchor) or self._quality(anchor, end)
        if reason:
            return {**result, "status": "unavailable", "reason": reason}
        boundary_count = self.rows[
            anchor + self.cfg["grid_ms"]
        ].trade_count_at_bucket_start
        if (
            type(boundary_count) is not int
            or not 0
            <= boundary_count
            <= self.rows[anchor + self.cfg["grid_ms"]].trade_count
        ):
            return {
                **result,
                "status": "unavailable",
                "reason": "unknown_boundary_trade_count",
            }
        sigma = max(baseline.sigma_1m, self.cfg["sigma_floor_1m"])
        path = [
            (ts, self._residual(anchor, ts, baseline.beta, sign))
            for ts in range(anchor, end + 1, self.cfg["grid_ms"])
        ]
        peak_ts, peak = max(path, key=lambda pair: pair[1])  # first tie wins
        terminal = path[-1][1]
        adverse = min(value for _, value in path)
        retention = terminal / peak if peak > 0 else None
        first, last = self.rows[anchor], self.rows[end]
        rules = self.cfg["impulse"]
        threshold = max(
            rules["terminal_sigma_multiple"] * sigma * math.sqrt(horizon_seconds / 60),
            rules["minimum_terminal_log_return"],
            rules["entry_spread_multiple"] * first.spread_bps / 10000,
        )
        trades = sum(self.rows[ts].trade_count for ts, _ in path[1:]) - boundary_count
        tolerance = self.cfg["comparison_tolerance"]
        verified = (
            terminal + tolerance >= threshold
            and retention is not None
            and retention + tolerance >= rules["minimum_peak_retention"]
            and trades >= rules["minimum_forward_matches"]
        )
        gross_bps = sign * (last.mid / first.mid - 1) * 10000
        spread_cost = (first.spread_bps + last.spread_bps) / 2
        result.update(
            status="ready",
            reason=None,
            verified_forward=verified,
            sigma_1m=sigma,
            sigma_floor_applied=baseline.sigma_1m < sigma,
            beta=baseline.beta,
            beta_fallback=baseline.beta_fallback,
            terminal_residual_log=terminal,
            required_terminal_log=threshold,
            peak_residual_log=peak,
            peak_ts_ms=peak_ts,
            adverse_residual_log=adverse,
            retention=retention,
            forward_trade_count=trades,
            gross_coin_return_bps=gross_bps,
            cost_scenarios=[
                dict(
                    fee_bps_per_side=fee,
                    net_coin_return_bps=gross_bps - spread_cost - 2 * fee,
                )
                for fee in self.cfg["costs"]["fee_bps_per_side_scenarios"]
            ],
        )
        onset = self.cfg["onset"]
        velocity_ms = onset["velocity_window_seconds"] * 1000
        step = self.cfg["grid_ms"]
        if self.is_v2:
            reference = anchor - velocity_ms
            context_reason = self._quality(reference, anchor)
            result.update(
                pre_window_reference_ts_ms=reference,
                pre_window_reference_is_physical_origin=False,
                pre_window_reference_status=(
                    "unavailable" if context_reason else "ready"
                ),
                pre_window_reference_reason=context_reason,
            )
            if not context_reason:
                reference_path = [
                    (ts, self._residual(reference, ts, baseline.beta, sign))
                    for ts in range(reference, end + 1, step)
                ]
                reference_peak_ts, reference_peak = max(
                    reference_path, key=lambda pair: pair[1]
                )
                result.update(
                    pre_window_peak_ts_ms=reference_peak_ts,
                    pre_window_peak_residual_log=reference_peak,
                )
        reason = self._quality(anchor - velocity_ms - step, anchor)
        if any(
            type(value) is not int
            for value in (
                baseline.pace_window_count,
                baseline.pace_end_ts_ms,
                baseline.pace_available_ts_ms,
            )
        ):
            return {
                **result,
                "onset_status": "unavailable",
                "onset_reason": "invalid_pace_metadata",
            }
        if baseline.pace_window_count < onset["minimum_prior_pace_windows"]:
            reason = reason or "pace_warming"
        if (
            baseline.pace_end_ts_ms != anchor - velocity_ms
            or baseline.pace_available_ts_ms > anchor
        ):
            reason = reason or "future_pace_baseline"
        medians = (baseline.pace_trade_median_30s, baseline.pace_quote_usd_median_30s)
        if not all(finite(value) and value >= 0 for value in medians) or not any(
            value > 0 for value in medians
        ):
            reason = reason or "pace_unavailable"
        if reason:
            return {**result, "onset_status": "unavailable", "onset_reason": reason}
        current_velocity = self._residual(
            anchor - velocity_ms, anchor, baseline.beta, sign
        ) / (sigma / math.sqrt(2))
        previous_velocity = self._residual(
            anchor - velocity_ms - step, anchor - step, baseline.beta, sign
        ) / (sigma / math.sqrt(2))
        recent = [
            self.rows[ts] for ts in range(anchor - velocity_ms + step, anchor + 1, step)
        ]
        recent_trades = sum(row.trade_count for row in recent)
        recent_volume = sum(row.quote_volume_usd for row in recent)
        pace = max(
            (
                value / median
                for value, median in zip((recent_trades, recent_volume), medians)
                if median > 0
            ),
            default=0,
        )
        candidate = (
            previous_velocity < onset["velocity_crossing_sigma"] - tolerance
            and current_velocity + tolerance >= onset["velocity_crossing_sigma"]
            and recent_trades >= onset["minimum_matches_30s"]
            and pace > onset["pace_multiple_strictly_greater_than"] + tolerance
        )
        breakout_ts = next(
            (
                ts
                for ts, value in path[1:]
                if ts <= anchor + onset["breakout_deadline_seconds"] * 1000
                and value + tolerance >= onset["breakout_sigma"] * sigma
            ),
            None,
        )
        no_retrace = adverse >= -onset["no_retrace_tolerance_log"]
        result.update(
            onset_status="ready",
            onset_reason=None,
            onset_candidate=candidate,
            previous_velocity_sigma=previous_velocity,
            velocity_sigma=current_velocity,
            pace_multiple=pace,
            recent_trade_count=recent_trades,
            breakout_ts_ms=breakout_ts,
            no_retrace=no_retrace,
            verified_onset=bool(
                candidate and verified and breakout_ts is not None and no_retrace
            ),
        )
        return result

    def alert_timing(self, label, publish_ts_ms):
        """Retrospective event association; never a live stage or input."""
        if self.is_v2:
            return self._v2_alert_timing(label, publish_ts_ms, strict_trigger=True)
        if type(publish_ts_ms) is not int:
            raise ValueError("publication timestamp must be an integer")
        if (
            label.get("contract_sha256") != self.contract_sha256
            or label.get("product_id") != self.product_id
            or not label.get("verified_onset")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "a verified onset from this contract and product is required"
            )
        anchor = label["anchor_ts_ms"]
        rules = self.cfg["events"]
        if (
            not anchor - rules["prediction_lead_limit_seconds"] * 1000
            <= publish_ts_ms
            <= label["label_maturity_ts_ms"]
        ):
            return dict(evaluation_only=True, timing="outside_event_window")
        # Backward as-of lookup only. Never use the next grid point at publication.
        ts = publish_ts_ms // self.cfg["grid_ms"] * self.cfg["grid_ms"]
        reason = self._quality(ts, ts)
        if reason:
            return dict(evaluation_only=True, timing="unavailable", reason=reason)
        row = self.rows[ts]
        if (
            max(publish_ts_ms - row.quote_ts_ms, publish_ts_ms - row.btc_quote_ts_ms)
            > self.cfg["max_quote_age_ms"]
        ):
            return dict(
                evaluation_only=True, timing="unavailable", reason="stale_alert_quote"
            )
        sign = 1 if label["direction"] == "up" else -1
        consumed = (
            self._residual(anchor, ts, label["beta"], sign) / label["peak_residual_log"]
        )
        timing = (
            "pre_onset_prediction"
            if publish_ts_ms < anchor
            else (
                "early_detection"
                if publish_ts_ms <= label["peak_ts_ms"]
                and consumed
                < rules["early_detection_consumed_fraction_strictly_below"]
                - self.cfg["comparison_tolerance"]
                else "late_detection"
            )
        )
        return dict(
            evaluation_only=True,
            timing=timing,
            detection_delay_seconds=(publish_ts_ms - anchor) / 1000,
            lead_to_peak_seconds=(label["peak_ts_ms"] - publish_ts_ms) / 1000,
            consumed_fraction=consumed,
            price_snapshot_ts_ms=ts,
        )

    def forward_alert_timing(self, label, publish_ts_ms):
        """v2 forward-window association, without inferring a physical onset."""
        if not self.is_v2:
            raise ValueError("forward-window timing requires the explicit v2 draft")
        return self._v2_alert_timing(label, publish_ts_ms, strict_trigger=False)

    def _v2_alert_timing(self, label, publish_ts_ms, strict_trigger):
        if type(publish_ts_ms) is not int:
            raise ValueError("publication timestamp must be an integer")
        required = "verified_onset" if strict_trigger else "verified_forward"
        if (
            label.get("contract_sha256") != self.contract_sha256
            or label.get("evaluator_sha256") != self.implementation_sha256
            or label.get("product_id") != self.product_id
            or label.get("status") != "ready"
            or label.get(required) is not True
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "a complete verified label from this evaluator is required"
            )
        peak = label.get("peak_residual_log")
        if not finite(peak) or peak <= 0:
            raise ValueError("a positive finite forward peak is required")
        anchor = label["anchor_ts_ms"]
        lead_ms = self.cfg["events"]["prediction_lead_limit_seconds"] * 1000
        if not anchor - lead_ms <= publish_ts_ms <= label["label_maturity_ts_ms"]:
            return dict(evaluation_only=True, timing="outside_event_window")
        ts = publish_ts_ms // self.cfg["grid_ms"] * self.cfg["grid_ms"]
        reason = self._quality(ts, ts)
        if reason:
            return dict(evaluation_only=True, timing="unavailable", reason=reason)
        row = self.rows[ts]
        if (
            max(publish_ts_ms - row.quote_ts_ms, publish_ts_ms - row.btc_quote_ts_ms)
            > self.cfg["max_quote_age_ms"]
        ):
            return dict(
                evaluation_only=True, timing="unavailable", reason="stale_alert_quote"
            )
        sign = 1 if label["direction"] == "up" else -1
        post_fraction = self._residual(anchor, ts, label["beta"], sign) / peak
        output = dict(
            evaluation_only=True,
            physical_onset_defined=False,
            post_anchor_consumed_fraction=post_fraction,
            post_anchor_reference_ts_ms=anchor,
            offset_from_anchor_seconds=(publish_ts_ms - anchor) / 1000,
            lead_to_forward_peak_seconds=(label["peak_ts_ms"] - publish_ts_ms) / 1000,
            price_snapshot_ts_ms=ts,
            pre_window_consumed_fraction=None,
            pre_window_reference_ts_ms=label.get("pre_window_reference_ts_ms"),
            pre_window_reference_is_physical_origin=False,
        )
        reference_peak = label.get("pre_window_peak_residual_log")
        if (
            label.get("pre_window_reference_status") == "ready"
            and finite(reference_peak)
            and reference_peak > 0
        ):
            output["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] = (
                self._residual(
                    label["pre_window_reference_ts_ms"], ts, label["beta"], sign
                )
                / reference_peak
            )
            output["lead_to_pre_window_peak_seconds"] = (
                label["pre_window_peak_ts_ms"] - publish_ts_ms
            ) / 1000
        if strict_trigger:
            fraction = output["pre_window_consumed_fraction"]
            if fraction is None:
                return dict(
                    evaluation_only=True,
                    timing="unavailable",
                    reason="pre_window_reference_unavailable",
                )
            early = (
                publish_ts_ms <= label["pre_window_peak_ts_ms"]
                and fraction
                < self.cfg["events"]["early_detection_consumed_fraction_strictly_below"]
                - self.cfg["comparison_tolerance"]
            )
            output["timing"] = (
                "before_activity_trigger"
                if publish_ts_ms < anchor
                else (
                    "early_after_activity_trigger"
                    if early
                    else "late_after_activity_trigger"
                )
            )
            output["association_kind"] = "strict_activity_trigger"
        else:
            output["timing"] = (
                "before_forward_window_anchor"
                if publish_ts_ms < anchor
                else (
                    "within_forward_window_before_peak"
                    if publish_ts_ms <= label["peak_ts_ms"]
                    else "within_forward_window_after_peak"
                )
            )
            output["association_kind"] = "forward_window_not_physical_impulse"
        return output


def event_catalog(labels, *, target="verified_onset"):
    """Earliest verified event owns [onset,onset+H), per product/direction/H.

    This retrospective deduplication is separate from the live 15m per-coin
    alert cooldown. Unverified/pending rows never suppress another event.
    """
    labels = list(labels)
    if target not in ("verified_onset", "verified_forward"):
        raise ValueError("unsupported catalog target")
    selected, ends = [], {}
    hashes = {row["contract_sha256"] for row in labels}
    if len(hashes) > 1:
        raise ValueError("cannot mix target contracts")
    implementations = {row.get("evaluator_sha256") for row in labels}
    if len(implementations) > 1:
        raise ValueError("cannot mix evaluator implementations")
    for row in sorted(
        labels,
        key=lambda row: (
            row["anchor_ts_ms"],
            row["product_id"],
            row["direction"],
            row["horizon_seconds"],
        ),
    ):
        key = row["product_id"], row["direction"], row["horizon_seconds"]
        if (
            row.get(target) is True
            and row.get("status") == "ready"
            and row["anchor_ts_ms"] >= ends.get(key, -math.inf)
        ):
            selected.append(row)
            ends[key] = row["label_maturity_ts_ms"]
    return selected


def forward_event_catalog(labels):
    """Catalog forward windows independently of activity/no-retrace gates.

    These are deduplicated qualifying forecast windows, not a definition of
    physical move onset or a completed operational missed-move denominator.
    """
    labels = list(labels)
    if any(row.get("contract_version") != "instant-target-v2-draft" for row in labels):
        raise ValueError(
            "forward-window catalog requires labels from the explicit v2 draft"
        )
    return [
        {**row, "catalog_kind": "forward_window_not_physical_impulse"}
        for row in event_catalog(labels, target="verified_forward")
    ]

===== BEGIN SOURCE 79 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_timed_baseline.py | sha256=f7a753d37c139d8f44bb6e94905dd809d6d1ffa95653363b9f6637f101586a43 =====
"""Compute offline baseline components from the timed input ledger."""

from decimal import Decimal, DecimalException
import math
from pathlib import Path
import statistics

from instant_coverage import _integer
from instant_input_evidence import BENCHMARK, GRID_MS, _sha


VERSION = "instant-timed-baseline-v1-draft"
MINUTE_MS = 60000
PACE_MS = 30000
RETURN_COUNT = 60
PACE_COUNT = 120


def _volatility(recorder, product, start, end, asof):
    if start < GRID_MS:
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason="insufficient_history")
    if end > asof:
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason="window_not_closed")

    availability, returns = [], {}
    for pid in dict.fromkeys((product, BENCHMARK)):
        # Include the bucket preceding the first close, as in micro_baseline.
        _, _, reason, available = recorder._trade_window(
            pid, start - GRID_MS, end, asof
        )
        if reason:
            return dict(status="unavailable", reason=reason, product_id=pid)
        availability.append(available)
        closes = []
        for cutoff in range(start, end + 1, MINUTE_MS):
            quote, available = recorder._quote_at(pid, cutoff, asof)
            if not quote["available"]:
                return dict(
                    status="unavailable",
                    reason=quote["reason"],
                    product_id=pid,
                    missing_close_ts_ms=cutoff,
                )
            bid, ask = float(quote["bid"]), float(quote["ask"])
            closes.append(math.log(bid + (ask - bid) / 2))
            availability.append(available)
        returns[pid] = [b - a for a, b in zip(closes, closes[1:])]

    coin, btc = returns[product], returns[BENCHMARK]
    coin_mean, btc_mean = statistics.fmean(coin), statistics.fmean(btc)
    variance = sum((value - btc_mean) ** 2 for value in btc)
    fallback = variance == 0
    beta = (
        1.0
        if fallback
        else (
            sum((a - coin_mean) * (b - btc_mean) for a, b in zip(coin, btc)) / variance
        )
    )
    sigma = statistics.stdev(coin)
    if not math.isfinite(beta) or not math.isfinite(sigma):
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason="nonfinite_baseline")
    return dict(
        status="ready",
        inputs_available_ts_ms=max(availability),
        return_count=len(coin),
        sigma_1m=sigma,
        beta=beta,
        beta_fallback=fallback,
    )


def _pace(recorder, product, start, end, asof):
    if start < 0:
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason="insufficient_history")
    if end > asof:
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason="window_not_closed")
    # A single certified chain covers the entire interval, including every
    # empty subwindow. Receipts are then assigned to half-open 30s bins.
    _, trades, reason, available = recorder._trade_window(product, start, end, asof)
    if reason:
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason=reason, product_id=product)
    counts, amounts = [0] * PACE_COUNT, [Decimal(0)] * PACE_COUNT
    try:
        if recorder._index is None:
            for trade in trades:
                index = (trade.receipt_ts_ms - start) // PACE_MS
                counts[index] += 1
                amounts[index] += Decimal(trade.price) * Decimal(trade.size)
        else:
            counts, amounts = recorder._index.pace_bins(product, start, end, PACE_MS)
        median = statistics.median(amounts)
    except DecimalException:
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason="invalid_quote_amount")
    if not median.is_finite():
        return dict(status="unavailable", reason="invalid_quote_amount")
    return dict(
        status="ready",
        inputs_available_ts_ms=available,
        window_count=PACE_COUNT,
        pace_trade_median_30s=statistics.median(counts),
        pace_quote_usd_median_30s=str(median),
    )


def build_timed_baseline(recorder, product, cutoff, *, asof):
    """Assess independent volatility and pace components for a declared slot.

    Windows end before cutoff, so a baseline may finish ahead of the slot.
    The evidence as-of is an input limit and completion uses the recorder clock.
    """
    _integer(cutoff)
    _integer(asof)
    if recorder.failed:
        raise ValueError("failed evidence recorder")
    if product not in recorder.products or cutoff not in recorder.cutoffs:
        raise ValueError("product/cutoff outside declared inventory")
    if asof < recorder.inventory_recorded_ts_ms:
        raise ValueError("inventory was not yet recorded at baseline evidence time")
    if asof > recorder._now():
        raise ValueError("baseline evidence time has not been reached")

    sigma_end = (cutoff - 35000) // MINUTE_MS * MINUTE_MS
    sigma_start = sigma_end - RETURN_COUNT * MINUTE_MS
    pace_end = cutoff - PACE_MS
    pace_start = pace_end - PACE_COUNT * PACE_MS
    volatility = _volatility(recorder, product, sigma_start, sigma_end, asof)
    pace = _pace(recorder, product, pace_start, pace_end, asof)
    known_packets, prefix, _ = recorder._prefix_at(asof)

    result = dict(
        version=VERSION,
        product_id=product,
        benchmark=BENCHMARK,
        feature_cutoff_ts_ms=cutoff,
        knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
        sigma_start_ts_ms=sigma_start,
        sigma_end_ts_ms=sigma_end,
        pace_start_ts_ms=pace_start,
        pace_end_ts_ms=pace_end,
        volatility=volatility,
        pace=pace,
        inventory_sha256=recorder.inventory_sha256,
        inventory_recorded_ts_ms=recorder.inventory_recorded_ts_ms,
        known_packet_count=known_packets,
        known_packet_prefix_sha256=prefix,
        input_implementation_sha256=recorder.source_sha256,
        input_contract_sha256=recorder.contract_sha256,
        book_implementation_sha256=recorder.book.source_sha256,
        book_contract_sha256=recorder.book.contract_sha256,
        monitor_implementation_sha256=recorder.book.monitor.source_sha256,
        monitor_contract_sha256=recorder.book.monitor.contract_sha256,
        builder_sha256=_sha(Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")),
        scope="offline_baseline_components",
        fitting_allowed=False,
    )
    result["computation_completed_ts_ms"] = recorder._now()
    return result

===== BEGIN SOURCE 80 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_timed_features.py | sha256=4a100cf629e460bae69f75c7bedb624c3835274c38af227c9aa3dab8b2e2eb0b =====
"""Offline F1–F5 evidence from declared, receipt-time observations."""

from decimal import Decimal, DecimalException
import math
from pathlib import Path

from instant_book import MAX_QUOTE_AGE_MS
from instant_coverage import MAX_HEARTBEAT_GAP_MS
from instant_input_evidence import BENCHMARK, DIRECTIONS, _sha
from instant_timed_baseline import MINUTE_MS, PACE_MS, build_timed_baseline


VERSION = "instant-timed-features-v1-draft"
SLOW_MS = 180000
SIGMA_FLOOR = 0.0015
CANDLE_COUNT = 15
MIN_TRADED_CANDLES = 10
MIN_MATCHES = 5


def _missing(reason, **details):
    return dict(status="unavailable", reason=reason, **details)


def _value(value, *known, **details):
    if not math.isfinite(value):
        return _missing("nonfinite_feature", **details)
    return dict(
        status="ready", value=value, evidence_available_ts_ms=max(known), **details
    )


def _price_point(recorder, product, cutoff, asof):
    if cutoff < 0:
        return _missing(
            "insufficient_history", product_id=product, endpoint_ts_ms=cutoff
        )
    quote, known = recorder._quote_at(product, cutoff, asof)
    if not quote["available"]:
        return _missing(quote["reason"], product_id=product, endpoint_ts_ms=cutoff)
    bid, ask = float(quote["bid"]), float(quote["ask"])
    mid = bid + (ask - bid) / 2
    return _value(math.log(mid), known, spread_bps=(ask - bid) / mid * 10000)


def _move(start, stop):
    for point in (start, stop):
        if point["status"] != "ready":
            return point
    return _value(
        stop["value"] - start["value"],
        start["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
        stop["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
    )


def _participation(recorder, product, start, end, asof, pace, sign):
    if start < 0:
        return _missing("insufficient_history"), {}
    _, trades, reason, known = recorder._trade_window(product, start, end, asof)
    if reason:
        return _missing(reason), {}

    counts = dict(verified_match_count=len(trades))
    try:
        amounts = {"buy": Decimal(0), "sell": Decimal(0), None: Decimal(0)}
        for trade in trades:
            amounts[trade.taker_side] += Decimal(trade.price) * Decimal(trade.size)
        total = sum(amounts.values())
        if not total.is_finite():
            return _missing("invalid_quote_amount"), counts
        counts["verified_quote_usd"] = str(total)
        counts["unknown_side_usd"] = str(amounts[None])
        if pace["status"] != "ready":
            return (
                _missing("pace_baseline_unavailable", baseline_reason=pace["reason"]),
                counts,
            )
        median = Decimal(pace["pace_quote_usd_median_30s"])
        if median <= 0:
            return _missing("nonpositive_pace_median"), counts
        ratio = float(total / median)
        if not math.isfinite(ratio) or (total > 0 and ratio == 0):
            return _missing("invalid_volume_pace"), counts
        counts["unsigned_volume_pace"] = ratio
        if len(trades) < MIN_MATCHES:
            return _missing("too_few_matches"), counts
        if any(trade.taker_side is None for trade in trades):
            return _missing("unknown_match_side"), counts
        if total <= 0:
            return _missing("nonpositive_window_quote_amount"), counts
        imbalance = float((amounts["buy"] - amounts["sell"]) / total)
    except DecimalException:
        return _missing("invalid_quote_amount"), counts
    return (
        _value(
            sign * imbalance * math.log1p(ratio), known, pace["inputs_available_ts_ms"]
        ),
        counts,
    )


def _book_pressure(recorder, product, start, end, asof, sign):
    if start < 0:
        return _missing("insufficient_history")
    # Each stream is already receipt ordered. Overwriting the start key keeps
    # the last carried state; overwriting a same-ms key keeps the last event.
    book_rows, transport_rows = recorder._book_window(product, start, end, asof)
    states = {max(start, state.receipt_ts_ms): state for state in book_rows}
    transport = {max(start, row["receipt_ts_ms"]): row for row in transport_rows}
    boundaries = sorted({start, end, *states, *transport})
    state = heartbeat = None
    known = end
    integral = 0.0
    for left, right in zip(boundaries, boundaries[1:]):
        state = states.get(left, state)
        heartbeat = transport.get(left, heartbeat)
        if state is None or state.best is None or state.reason:
            return _missing("book_state_unavailable", segment_start_ts_ms=left)
        if (
            heartbeat is None
            or heartbeat["reason"] != "heartbeat_observed"
            or state.connection_id != heartbeat["connection_id"]
        ):
            return _missing("heartbeat_unavailable", segment_start_ts_ms=left)
        if right - state.observation_ts_ms > MAX_QUOTE_AGE_MS:
            return _missing(
                "stale_book_span",
                first_invalid_ts_ms=max(
                    left, state.observation_ts_ms + MAX_QUOTE_AGE_MS
                ),
            )
        if right - heartbeat["receipt_ts_ms"] > MAX_HEARTBEAT_GAP_MS:
            return _missing(
                "stale_heartbeat_span",
                first_invalid_ts_ms=max(
                    left, heartbeat["receipt_ts_ms"] + MAX_HEARTBEAT_GAP_MS
                ),
            )
        bid_size, ask_size = map(Decimal, state.best[2:])
        pressure = float((bid_size - ask_size) / (bid_size + ask_size))
        integral += pressure * (right - left)
        known = max(
            known,
            recorder._packet_by_seq[state.receipt_seq].available_ts_ms,
            recorder._packet_by_seq[heartbeat["receipt_seq"]].available_ts_ms,
        )
    return _value(sign * integral / (end - start), known)


def _candle_range(recorder, product, start, end, asof):
    if start < 0:
        return _missing("insufficient_history")
    _, trades, reason, known = recorder._trade_window(product, start, end, asof)
    if reason:
        return _missing(reason)
    candles = {}
    for trade in trades:
        bucket = trade.receipt_ts_ms // MINUTE_MS
        price = Decimal(trade.price)
        low, high = candles.get(bucket, (price, price))
        candles[bucket] = min(low, price), max(high, price)
    counts = dict(
        traded_candles=len(candles), verified_empty_candles=CANDLE_COUNT - len(candles)
    )
    if len(candles) < MIN_TRADED_CANDLES:
        return _missing("too_few_traded_candles", **counts)
    low = min(candle[0] for candle in candles.values())
    high = max(candle[1] for candle in candles.values())
    try:
        log_range = float(high.ln() - low.ln())
    except DecimalException:
        return _missing("invalid_candle_range", **counts)
    return _value(log_range, known, **counts)


def _scaled_velocity(move, baseline, sigma, sign):
    if move["status"] != "ready":
        return move
    if baseline["status"] != "ready":
        return _missing(
            "volatility_baseline_unavailable", baseline_reason=baseline["reason"]
        )
    return _value(
        sign * move["value"] / sigma,
        move["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
        baseline["inputs_available_ts_ms"],
    )


def _acceleration(velocity, prior, sigma, sign):
    for component in (velocity, prior):
        if component["status"] != "ready":
            return component
    return _value(
        velocity["value"] - sign * prior["value"] / (5 * sigma),
        velocity["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
        prior["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
    )


def _compression(velocity, candles, sigma):
    for component in (velocity, candles):
        if component["status"] != "ready":
            return component
    expansion = max(velocity["value"], 0)
    return _value(
        expansion / (1 + candles["value"] / (sigma * math.sqrt(CANDLE_COUNT))),
        velocity["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
        candles["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
    )


def _market_relative(coin_move, btc_move, baseline, sigma, sign):
    for component in (coin_move, btc_move):
        if component["status"] != "ready":
            return component
    if baseline["status"] != "ready":
        return _missing(
            "volatility_baseline_unavailable", baseline_reason=baseline["reason"]
        )
    return _value(
        sign * (coin_move["value"] - baseline["beta"] * btc_move["value"]) / sigma,
        coin_move["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
        btc_move["evidence_available_ts_ms"],
        baseline["inputs_available_ts_ms"],
        beta_fallback=baseline["beta_fallback"],
    )


def build_timed_features(recorder, product, cutoff, direction, *, asof):
    """Calculate a single offline feature row with explicit evidence and clocks."""
    if direction not in DIRECTIONS:
        raise ValueError("direction must be up or down")
    if type(cutoff) is not int or type(asof) is not int or asof < cutoff:
        raise ValueError("feature as-of must be at or after the integer cutoff")
    baseline = build_timed_baseline(recorder, product, cutoff, asof=asof)
    vol, pace = baseline["volatility"], baseline["pace"]
    sigma = max(vol["sigma_1m"], SIGMA_FLOOR) if vol["status"] == "ready" else None
    sign = 1 if direction == "up" else -1
    slow, fast = cutoff - SLOW_MS, cutoff - PACE_MS

    start, middle, end = (
        _price_point(recorder, product, t, asof) for t in (slow, fast, cutoff)
    )
    recent, prior = _move(middle, end), _move(start, middle)
    coin = _move(start, end)
    btc = (
        coin
        if product == BENCHMARK
        else _move(
            _price_point(recorder, BENCHMARK, slow, asof),
            _price_point(recorder, BENCHMARK, cutoff, asof),
        )
    )
    candle_end = fast // MINUTE_MS * MINUTE_MS
    candle_start = candle_end - CANDLE_COUNT * MINUTE_MS
    candles = _candle_range(recorder, product, candle_start, candle_end, asof)
    velocity = _scaled_velocity(recent, vol, sigma, sign)
    flow, activity = _participation(recorder, product, fast, cutoff, asof, pace, sign)
    features = {
        "F1": _acceleration(velocity, prior, sigma, sign),
        "F2": flow,
        "F3": _book_pressure(recorder, product, fast, cutoff, asof, sign),
        "F4": _compression(velocity, candles, sigma),
        "F5": _market_relative(coin, btc, vol, sigma, sign),
    }

    row = dict(
        version=VERSION,
        product_id=product,
        direction=direction,
        feature_cutoff_ts_ms=cutoff,
        knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
        baseline=baseline,
        sigma_used=sigma,
        candle_start_ts_ms=candle_start,
        candle_end_ts_ms=candle_end,
        features=features,
        controls=dict(
            current_velocity_sigma=velocity,
            unsigned_activity=activity,
            cutoff_spread_bps=end.get("spread_bps"),
            candle_range=candles,
            expansion=(
                max(velocity["value"], 0) if velocity["status"] == "ready" else None
            ),
            raw_signed_180s=sign * coin["value"] if coin["status"] == "ready" else None,
        ),
        builder_sha256=_sha(Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")),
        scope="offline_feature_components",
        fitting_allowed=False,
    )
    row["computation_completed_ts_ms"] = recorder._now()
    row["later_diagnostic"] = row["computation_completed_ts_ms"] > cutoff
    return row

===== BEGIN SOURCE 81 | scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_warmup_capacity.py | sha256=a0c4a75a32fce097488a6f53dd34295cc9acb10695424ff540309ee3922f03f1 =====
"""Run one continuous synthetic 67-minute stream through the real journal."""

import argparse
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import time

from instant_coverage import WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT
from instant_input_evidence import _file_sha
from instant_source_pilot import (
    CHANNELS,
    DURATION_S,
    LATER_ASOF_MS,
    PRODUCTS,
    SOFT_RECORD_BYTES,
    WARMUP_MS,
    capture,
)
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal, read_segment
from instant_timed_features import build_timed_features


# Depths observed in the initial four snapshots, with synthetic prices/sizes.
DEPTHS = dict(zip(PRODUCTS, ((17558, 22137), (2848, 10094), (49, 564), (104, 617))))
BOOK_FRAMES = dict(zip(PRODUCTS, (17, 12, 1, 12)))
CHANGES = dict(zip(PRODUCTS, (5, 3, 4, 4)))
PACKET_STOP = WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT - 10


def _price(level):
    return f"{1000 + level / 100:.2f}"


def frames():
    yield 0, dict(
        type="subscriptions",
        channels=[
            dict(
                name="level2_50" if name == "level2_batch" else name,
                product_ids=PRODUCTS,
            )
            for name in CHANNELS
        ],
    )
    for product, (bids, asks) in DEPTHS.items():
        yield 0, dict(
            type="snapshot",
            product_id=product,
            bids=[[_price(-i), "1.12345678"] for i in range(1, bids + 1)],
            asks=[[_price(i), "1.12345678"] for i in range(1, asks + 1)],
        )
    watermarks = Counter({product: 10000 for product in PRODUCTS})
    for second in range(DURATION_S + 1):
        timestamp = datetime.fromtimestamp(second, timezone.utc).isoformat()
        batch = [
            dict(
                type="heartbeat",
                product_id=pid,
                last_trade_id=watermarks[pid],
                time=timestamp,
            )
            for pid in PRODUCTS
        ]
        for product, count in BOOK_FRAMES.items():
            for i in range(count):
                size = "1.12345678" if (i + second) % 2 else "2.12345678"
                batch.append(
                    dict(
                        type="l2update",
                        product_id=product,
                        time=timestamp,
                        changes=[
                            ["buy", _price(-level), size]
                            for level in range(1, CHANGES[product] + 1)
                        ],
                    )
                )
        participants = [PRODUCTS[0]] * 5 + [PRODUCTS[1]] * 2
        if second % 71 == 0:
            participants[-1] = PRODUCTS[2]
        if second % 109 == 0:
            participants[-2] = PRODUCTS[3]
        for pid in participants:
            watermarks[pid] += 1
            common = dict(
                product_id=pid,
                time=timestamp,
                trade_id=watermarks[pid],
                sequence=second * 60,
                price="1000.00",
            )
            batch.append(
                dict(
                    type="match",
                    **common,
                    side="sell",
                    size="0.12345678",
                    maker_order_id="11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111",
                    taker_order_id="22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222",
                )
            )
            batch.append(
                dict(
                    type="ticker",
                    **common,
                    side="buy",
                    last_size="0.12345678",
                    best_bid="999.99",
                    best_ask="1000.01",
                    best_bid_size="1.12345678",
                    best_ask_size="1.12345678",
                    open_24h="998.12",
                    high_24h="1010.12",
                    low_24h="992.12",
                    volume_24h="10000.12345678",
                    volume_30d="200000.12345678",
                )
            )
        for index, message in enumerate(batch, 1):
            yield second * 1000 + index * 1000 // (len(batch) + 1), message


class SyntheticSocket:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 0
        self._frames = frames()

    def wall_ms(self):
        return self.now

    def monotonic(self):
        return self.now / 1000

    def monotonic_ns(self):
        return self.now * 1000000

    def send(self, raw):
        assert json.loads(raw) == dict(
            type="subscribe", product_ids=list(PRODUCTS), channels=list(CHANNELS)
        )

    def recv(self):
        self.now, message = next(self._frames)
        return json.dumps(message, separators=(",", ":"))

    def close(self, timeout):
        self._frames.close()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    out = args.out_dir.resolve()
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    path = out / "synthetic.sqlite"
    commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
    source = SyntheticSocket()
    start = time.perf_counter()
    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        WARMUP_MS,
        WARMUP_MS + 5000,
        max_packets=WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT,
        clock_ms=source.wall_ms,
        monotonic_ns=source.monotonic_ns,
    ) as journal:
        run = capture(
            journal,
            lambda: source,
            monotonic=source.monotonic,
            duration_s=DURATION_S,
            packet_stop=PACKET_STOP,
            byte_stop=SOFT_RECORD_BYTES,
        )
        seconds = time.perf_counter() - start
        readiness = {}
        if not journal.evidence.failed:
            journal.finish()
            if source.now >= WARMUP_MS + LATER_ASOF_MS:
                for product in PRODUCTS:
                    started = time.perf_counter()
                    row = build_timed_features(
                        journal.evidence,
                        product,
                        WARMUP_MS,
                        "up",
                        asof=WARMUP_MS + LATER_ASOF_MS,
                    )
                    readiness[product] = dict(
                        baseline={
                            k: row["baseline"][k].get("reason", "ready")
                            for k in ("volatility", "pace")
                        },
                        features={
                            k: v.get("reason", "ready")
                            for k, v in row["features"].items()
                        },
                        calculation_ms=round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000, 3),
                    )
        logical_bytes = journal.record_bytes
    runtime_clock = time.get_clock_info("perf_counter")
    report = dict(
        version="instant-warmup-capacity-v1-draft",
        scope="synthetic_frames_virtual_receipt_clock_real_sqlite_commits",
        code_commit=commit,
        expected_virtual_seconds=DURATION_S,
        observed_virtual_ms=source.now,
        host_capture_seconds=round(seconds, 3),
        stop=run["stop"],
        connections=run["attempts"],
        frames=run["frames"],
        callback_through_commit={
            key: metrics["callback_through_commit"]
            for key, metrics in run["latencies"].items()
        },
        duration_clock=runtime_clock.implementation,
        duration_resolution_ns=runtime_clock.resolution * 1e9,
        snapshot_depths=DEPTHS,
        warmup_readiness=readiness,
        logical_record_bytes=logical_bytes,
        journal_bytes=path.stat().st_size,
        journal_sha256=_file_sha(path),
        segment=read_segment(path),
        feed_gate_pass=False,
        fitting_allowed=False,
    )
    (out / "REPORT.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(report, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(
        f"Capacity run stopped: {run['stop']}. Report at {out / 'REPORT.json'}",
        flush=True,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 82 | scripts/scoring_experiment/verify_instant_timing_attempt.py | sha256=c26ebe0ed1f1cab264c3d9d9fba27a22dbd0f937c00a1bc7de51844fbb6c95cc =====
"""Verify a finished indexed pilot from its journal and local reader receipts."""

import argparse
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from contextlib import closing
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3

from instant_delayed_eligibility import BASELINES, FEATURES, _component_reasons
from instant_input_evidence import BENCHMARK, DIRECTIONS, GRID_MS, _file_sha, _json
from instant_source_pilot import _summary
from instant_stream_journal import _decoded, _local_publication


def _require(condition, detail):
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(detail)


def _index(rows, key):
    indexed = {}
    for row in rows:
        identity = row[key]
        _require(identity not in indexed, f"duplicate {key}: {identity}")
        indexed[identity] = row
    return indexed


def _verify_packets(db, asofs, plan):
    waiting = iter(sorted(set(asofs)))
    next_asof = next(waiting, None)
    prefixes = {}
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    previous_available = count = batched = 0
    first_ack = None
    roster = set(plan["products"])
    for seq, body, row_sha in db.execute(
        "SELECT receipt_seq, body, digest FROM packets ORDER BY receipt_seq"
    ):
        row = _decoded(body, row_sha)
        packet, envelope = row["processing"], row["envelope"]
        receipt, start, end, available = (
            packet[key]
            for key in (
                "receipt_ts_ms",
                "processing_started_ts_ms",
                "processing_completed_ts_ms",
                "available_ts_ms",
            )
        )
        _require(
            count + 1 == seq == packet["receipt_seq"] == envelope["receipt_seq"]
            and receipt == envelope["receipt_ts_ms"]
            and receipt <= start <= end
            and available == max(end, receipt + 1)
            and available >= previous_available
            and packet["envelope_sha256"]
            == hashlib.sha256(_json(envelope).encode()).hexdigest(),
            f"packet identity, digest or processing clock differs at {seq}",
        )
        if "ingress_commit_observed_ts_ms" in row:
            _require(
                receipt <= row["ingress_commit_observed_ts_ms"] <= start,
                f"batch ingress clock differs at {seq}",
            )
            batched += 1
        message = envelope["message"]
        if first_ack is None and message["type"] == "subscriptions":
            channels = defaultdict(set)
            for item in message["channels"]:
                name = item["name"]
                if name == "level2_50":
                    name = "level2_batch"
                channels[name].update(item["product_ids"])
            if all(roster <= channels[name] for name in plan["channels"]):
                first_ack = receipt
        while next_asof is not None and next_asof < available:
            prefixes[next_asof] = count, digest.hexdigest()
            next_asof = next(waiting, None)
        digest.update((_json(packet) + "\n").encode("utf-8"))
        previous_available = available
        count += 1
    while next_asof is not None:
        prefixes[next_asof] = count, digest.hexdigest()
        next_asof = next(waiting, None)
    return count, batched, prefixes, first_ack


def _readiness(decision, manifest, prefixes):
    features = json.loads(decision["feature_json"])
    baseline = features["baseline"]
    asof = decision["knowledge_asof_ts_ms"]
    _require(
        (baseline["known_packet_count"], baseline["known_packet_prefix_sha256"])
        == prefixes[asof],
        "decision prefix differs from the journal",
    )
    for key in ("inventory_sha256", "inventory_recorded_ts_ms"):
        _require(
            baseline[key] == manifest[key], f"decision {key} differs from the journal"
        )
    for source in ("input", "book", "monitor"):
        for label in ("implementation", "contract"):
            key = f"{source}_{label}_sha256"
            _require(
                baseline[key] == manifest[key],
                f"decision {key} differs from the journal",
            )
    anchors = json.loads(decision["anchor_quotes_json"])
    product = decision["product_id"]
    feature_reasons = _component_reasons(
        features["features"], FEATURES, asof, "evidence_available_ts_ms"
    )
    baseline_reasons = _component_reasons(
        baseline, BASELINES, asof, "inputs_available_ts_ms"
    )
    return (
        not feature_reasons
        and not baseline_reasons
        and all(anchors[pid]["available"] is True for pid in (product, BENCHMARK))
    )


def _schedule(plan, ack):
    offsets = plan.get("cohort_evidence_offsets_ms")
    if offsets is None:
        offsets = [plan["cohort_evidence_offset_ms"]] * plan["cohort_count"]
    _require(
        len(offsets) == plan["cohort_count"],
        "declared cohort count differs from its schedule",
    )
    if ack is None:
        return offsets, {}
    ready_at = ack + plan["warmup_ms"]
    first = ((ready_at + GRID_MS - 1) // GRID_MS) * GRID_MS
    step = plan.get("cohort_step_ms", GRID_MS)
    return offsets, {first + i * step: offset for i, offset in enumerate(offsets)}


def _count_two(candidates, offset):
    return {
        direction: sum(
            count >= 2
            for (wait, _, side), count in candidates.items()
            if wait == offset and side == direction
        )
        for direction in DIRECTIONS
    }


def verify(attempt, *, plan_sha256=None):
    plan_path = attempt / "PLAN.json"
    report_path = attempt / "REPORT.json"
    _require(report_path.is_file(), "capture has no final report")
    plan = json.loads(plan_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    report = json.loads(report_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    journal_path = attempt / "capture.sqlite"
    actual_plan_sha = _file_sha(plan_path)
    _require(
        plan_sha256 is None or actual_plan_sha == plan_sha256,
        "plan digest differs from the checkpoint",
    )
    _require(
        all(report.get(k) == v for k, v in plan.items()),
        "report and capture plan differ",
    )
    journal_sha = _file_sha(journal_path)
    _require(
        journal_sha == report["journal_sha256"],
        "journal digest differs from the report",
    )

    observer_path = attempt / "observed.jsonl"
    observed = _index(
        (
            (
                json.loads(line)
                for line in observer_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
            )
            if observer_path.is_file()
            else ()
        ),
        "publication_id",
    )
    reported = _index(report["diagnostic_rows"], "publication_id")
    with closing(
        sqlite3.connect(
            journal_path.resolve().as_uri() + "?mode=ro&immutable=1", uri=True
        )
    ) as db:
        _require(
            db.execute("PRAGMA quick_check").fetchall() == [("ok",)],
            "SQLite quick check failed",
        )
        manifest_json, status = db.execute(
            "SELECT manifest, status FROM segment WHERE id = 1"
        ).fetchone()
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_json)
        _require(
            manifest == report["segment"]["manifest"]
            and status == report["segment"]["status"],
            "journal segment differs from the report",
        )
        _require(
            sorted(plan["products"]) == sorted(manifest["inventory"]["products"]),
            "journal roster differs from the plan",
        )
        publications = dict(
            (pub, _local_publication(db, pub))
            for (pub,) in db.execute("SELECT publication_id FROM publications")
        )
        pending = db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM pending_ingress").fetchone()[0]
        packets, batched, prefixes, first_ack = _verify_packets(
            db,
            (
                record["decision"]["knowledge_asof_ts_ms"]
                for _, record in publications.values()
            ),
            plan,
        )

    _require(
        packets == report["segment"]["packets"]
        and len(publications) == report["segment"]["publications"]
        and pending == report["recovered_pending_ingress_frames"],
        "journal counts differ from the report",
    )
    _require(
        reported.keys() <= publications.keys()
        and observed.keys() <= publications.keys(),
        "report or observation refers to an unknown publication",
    )
    _require(
        first_ack == report["first_ack_ts_ms"],
        "subscription acknowledgment differs from the report",
    )
    offsets, schedule = _schedule(plan, first_ack)
    arms = {}
    ages = defaultdict(list)
    ready_candidates, on_time_candidates = Counter(), Counter()
    for offset, count in Counter(offsets).items():
        bound = plan.get("diagnostic_deadlines_ms", {}).get(str(offset))
        arms[str(offset)] = dict(
            planned_cohorts=count,
            consumer_age_bound_ms=bound,
            products={
                product: dict(
                    planned=count * len(DIRECTIONS),
                    journaled=0,
                    complete_inputs=0,
                    observed=0,
                    within_bound=0 if bound is not None else None,
                    complete_and_within_bound=0 if bound is not None else None,
                )
                for product in plan["products"]
            },
        )
    slots = set()
    for pub, (receipt, record) in publications.items():
        decision = record["decision"]
        cutoff, asof, completed = (
            decision[key]
            for key in (
                "feature_cutoff_ts_ms",
                "knowledge_asof_ts_ms",
                "decision_completed_ts_ms",
            )
        )
        product, direction = decision["product_id"], decision["direction"]
        offset = schedule.get(cutoff)
        slot = cutoff, product, direction
        commit = receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms
        _require(
            offset is not None
            and asof == cutoff + offset
            and product in plan["products"]
            and direction in DIRECTIONS
            and slot not in slots
            and asof
            <= decision["feature_computation_completed_ts_ms"]
            <= completed
            <= record["commit_requested_ts_ms"],
            f"publication slot or decision clock differs from the plan: {pub}",
        )
        slots.add(slot)
        expected = dict(
            cutoff_ts_ms=cutoff,
            knowledge_asof_ts_ms=asof,
            decision_completed_ts_ms=completed,
            product_id=product,
            direction=direction,
            decision_sha256=receipt.decision_sha256,
            commit_observed_ts_ms=commit,
        )
        if pub in reported:
            _require(
                all(reported[pub][k] == v for k, v in expected.items()),
                f"reported decision differs from the journal: {pub}",
            )
        arm = arms[str(offset)]
        stats = arm["products"][product]
        stats["journaled"] += 1
        ready = _readiness(decision, manifest, prefixes)
        stats["complete_inputs"] += ready
        candidate = ready and product != BENCHMARK
        if candidate:
            ready_candidates[offset, cutoff, direction] += 1
        if pub not in observed:
            _require(
                reported.get(pub, {}).get("consumer_seen_ts_ms") is None,
                f"reported consumer clock has no observation: {pub}",
            )
            continue
        observation = observed[pub]
        seen = observation["consumer_seen_ts_ms"]
        _require(
            observation["decision_sha256"] == receipt.decision_sha256
            and observation["commit_observed_ts_ms"] == commit
            and commit is not None
            and commit <= seen
            and (pub not in reported or reported[pub]["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] == seen),
            f"consumer observation differs from the journal or report: {pub}",
        )
        age = seen - cutoff
        stats["observed"] += 1
        ages[offset].append(age)
        bound = arm["consumer_age_bound_ms"]
        if bound is not None and age <= bound:
            stats["within_bound"] += 1
            stats["complete_and_within_bound"] += ready
            if candidate:
                on_time_candidates[offset, cutoff, direction] += 1

    for arm, stats in arms.items():
        offset = int(arm)
        stats["cutoff_to_consumer_ms"] = _summary(ages[offset])
        stats["cutoffs_with_two_ready_candidates_by_direction"] = _count_two(
            ready_candidates, offset
        )
        stats["cutoffs_with_two_on_time_candidates_by_direction"] = (
            _count_two(on_time_candidates, offset)
            if stats["consumer_age_bound_ms"] is not None
            else dict.fromkeys(DIRECTIONS)
        )
    return dict(
        stop=report["stop"],
        segment_status=status,
        plan_sha256=actual_plan_sha,
        journal_sha256=journal_sha,
        sqlite_quick_check="ok",
        packet_records_verified=packets,
        batch_ingress_clocks_verified=batched,
        pending_ingress_frames=pending,
        publication_prefixes_verified=len(publications),
        journal_only_publications=len(publications.keys() - reported.keys()),
        unacknowledged_publications=sum(
            r.local_commit_observed_ts_ms is None for r, _ in publications.values()
        ),
        local_observations_verified=len(observed),
        schedule_results=arms,
    )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("attempt", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--plan-sha256")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(verify(args.attempt, plan_sha256=args.plan_sha256), indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
