===== BEGIN SOURCE 83 | tests/test_instant_association.py | sha256=0cd3d21442ace3d25cc80a105015b04203778d5d26c5905466b2bee675752abe =====
"""Association/exposure reporting probes: synthetic only, no fitting or live code."""

import copy
import json
import sys

import pytest

from test_instant_targets import ROOT, target

sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment"))
import instant_association as assoc
import reproduce_instant_study_choices as repro


B = 3_600_000  # Base cutoff; hand oracles use offsets from here in ms.
PRODUCT = "A-USD"


def label(
    offset,
    *,
    verified=True,
    status="ready",
    product=PRODUCT,
    direction="up",
    horizon=300,
    peak=60_000,
    reason=None,
):
    anchor = B + offset
    ready = status == "ready"
    return dict(
        contract_version="instant-target-v2-draft",
        contract_sha256="contract",
        evaluator_sha256="evaluator",
        product_id=product,
        anchor_ts_ms=anchor,
        direction=direction,
        horizon_seconds=horizon,
        status=status,
        reason=reason,
        verified_forward=verified if ready else None,
        label_maturity_ts_ms=anchor + horizon * 1000,
        peak_ts_ms=anchor + peak if ready else None,
    )


def alert(pid, published, *, anchor=None, inputs=None, product=PRODUCT, direction="up"):
    anchor = B + (published if anchor is None else anchor)
    return dict(
        publication_id=pid,
        product_id=product,
        direction=direction,
        anchor_ts_ms=anchor,
        inputs_available_ts_ms=anchor if inputs is None else B + inputs,
        published_ts_ms=B + published,
    )


def synthetic_alert(pid, anchor, published=None):
    """Absolute clocks for labels graded by the real v2 evaluator."""
    published = anchor if published is None else published
    return dict(
        publication_id=pid,
        product_id="SYNTHETIC-USD",
        direction="up",
        anchor_ts_ms=anchor,
        inputs_available_ts_ms=anchor,
        published_ts_ms=published,
    )


def slot(product, offset, **flags):
    base = dict(
        universe_member=True,
        warmup_complete=True,
        health_verified=True,
        candidate_eligible=True,
    )
    return dict(product_id=product, ts_ms=B + offset, **{**base, **flags})


ASOF = B + 3_000_000


def test_duplicate_ranker_slots_are_rejected_instead_of_collapsed():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate ranker slot"):
        assoc.exposure_report(
            [],
            [slot(PRODUCT, 0)],
            asof_ts_ms=ASOF,
            ranker_slots={"ranker": [(PRODUCT, B), (PRODUCT, B)]},
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("horizon", [300.0, 900.0])
def test_horizon_identity_requires_integer_even_with_valid_integer_maturity(horizon):
    row = label(0, horizon=horizon)
    row["label_maturity_ts_ms"] = int(row["label_maturity_ts_ms"])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unsupported label"):
        assoc.association_report([row], [], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)


def oracle_labels(direction, horizon):
    """Three verified windows spaced one horizon apart, with censor context."""
    h = horizon * 1000
    return [
        label(0, direction=direction, horizon=horizon),
        label(h - 5000, verified=False, direction=direction, horizon=horizon),
        label(h, direction=direction, horizon=horizon),
        label(
            2 * h - 5000,
            status="unavailable",
            reason="missing_grid_point",
            direction=direction,
            horizon=horizon,
        ),
        label(2 * h, direction=direction, horizon=horizon),
    ]


def oracle_alerts(direction, horizon):
    h = horizon * 1000
    return [
        alert("too_early", -185_000, anchor=-185_000, direction=direction),
        alert("edge", -180_000, anchor=-180_000, direction=direction),
        alert("between", 10_000, direction=direction),
        alert("tie_b", h + 60_000, direction=direction),
        alert("tie_a", h + 60_000, direction=direction),
    ]


# 1. Hand-calculated matching oracle across both directions and horizons ----------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("horizon", [300, 900])
def test_matching_oracle_by_hand(direction, horizon):
    out = assoc.association_report(
        oracle_labels(direction, horizon),
        oracle_alerts(direction, horizon),
        asof_ts_ms=ASOF,
    )
    table = out["horizons"][str(horizon)]["forward_window_coverage"]
    h = horizon * 1000
    assert table["window_count"] == 3
    assert [
        (w["anchor_ts_ms"] - B, w["covering_publication_id"])
        for w in table["covered_windows"]
    ] == [
        (0, "edge"),
        (h, "tie_a"),
    ]
    assert [w["anchor_ts_ms"] - B for w in table["uncovered_windows"]] == [2 * h]
    assert table["uncovered_forward_window_rate"] == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
    assert table["conditional_catalog_coverage_rate"] == pytest.approx(2 / 3)
    assert table["alerts_without_forward_window"] == ["too_early", "between", "tie_b"]
    assert table["physical_missed_move_rate"] is None
    censored = {
        w["anchor_ts_ms"] - B: w["left_censor_reason"]
        for w in table["covered_windows"] + table["uncovered_windows"]
    }
    assert censored == {0: "previous_anchor_missing", h: None, 2 * h: "unavailable"}
    assert table["left_censored_window_count"] == 2
    other = out["horizons"][str(900 if horizon == 300 else 300)]
    assert other["forward_window_coverage"]["window_count"] == 0
    assert other["forward_window_coverage"]["uncovered_forward_window_rate"] is None
    assert out["physical_onset_defined"] is False
    assert out["operational_missed_move_rates_enabled"] is False
    json.dumps(out, allow_nan=False)


def test_identical_timestamps_are_ordered_by_publication_id_only():
    swapped = [
        {
            **a,
            "publication_id": {"tie_a": "tie_b", "tie_b": "tie_a"}.get(
                a["publication_id"], a["publication_id"]
            ),
        }
        for a in oracle_alerts("up", 300)
    ]
    out = assoc.association_report(oracle_labels("up", 300), swapped, asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    covered = out["horizons"]["300"]["forward_window_coverage"]["covered_windows"]
    assert covered[1]["covering_publication_id"] == "tie_a"


def test_one_alert_covers_at_most_one_window_per_horizon_but_both_horizons():
    labels = [
        label(0, peak=200_000),
        label(300_000),
        label(0, horizon=900, peak=200_000),
    ]
    out = assoc.association_report(labels, [alert("only", 150_000)], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    five = out["horizons"]["300"]["forward_window_coverage"]
    assert [w["covering_publication_id"] for w in five["covered_windows"]] == ["only"]
    assert [w["anchor_ts_ms"] - B for w in five["uncovered_windows"]] == [300_000]
    fifteen = out["horizons"]["900"]["forward_window_coverage"]
    assert [w["covering_publication_id"] for w in fifteen["covered_windows"]] == [
        "only"
    ]
    assert fifteen["uncovered_count"] == 0


def test_empty_inputs_keep_denominators_explicit():
    out = assoc.report([], [], [], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    for horizon in ("300", "900"):
        primary = out["association"]["horizons"][horizon]["primary_pointwise"]
        coverage = out["association"]["horizons"][horizon]["forward_window_coverage"]
        assert primary["alert_count"] == 0 and primary["graded_fraction"] is None
        assert primary["success_rate_among_graded"] is None
        assert coverage["uncovered_forward_window_rate"] is None
        assert (
            out["exposure"]["horizons"][horizon]["gradable_fraction_of_eligible"]
            is None
        )
    assert out["exposure"]["decision_eligible_product_hours"] == 0
    assert out["exposure"]["universe_size_first"] is None
    assert out["association"]["target_contract_sha256"] is None


def test_absent_pending_and_unavailable_labels_stay_ungradable_and_visible():
    labels = [
        label(0),
        label(5000, status="pending", reason="horizon_not_mature"),
        label(10_000, status="unavailable", reason="unverified_coverage"),
        label(15_000, verified=False),
    ]
    alerts = [alert(f"p{i}", i * 5000) for i in range(5)]
    primary = assoc.association_report(labels, alerts, asof_ts_ms=ASOF)["horizons"][
        "300"
    ]["primary_pointwise"]
    assert (
        primary["success_count"],
        primary["failure_count"],
        primary["ungradable_count"],
    ) == (1, 1, 3)
    assert primary["ungradable_reasons"] == {
        "label_missing": 1,
        "pending:horizon_not_mature": 1,
        "unavailable:unverified_coverage": 1,
    }
    assert primary["graded_fraction"] == pytest.approx(2 / 5)
    assert primary["success_rate_among_graded"] == pytest.approx(1 / 2)
    assert [row["verified_forward"] for row in primary["outcomes"]] == [
        True,
        None,
        None,
        False,
        None,
    ]


# 2. Association never forgives a failed pointwise prediction -----------------------


def grade_labels(engine, offsets, horizon):
    return [repro.grade(engine, offset, horizon) for offset in offsets]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("horizon,impulse_start", [(300, 340), (900, 940)])
def test_later_move_is_covered_but_the_alert_stays_a_pointwise_failure(
    horizon, impulse_start
):
    engine = repro.engine(
        lambda seconds: 0.022 * min(max((seconds - impulse_start) / 30, 0), 1)
    )
    labels = grade_labels(engine, range(0, 121, 5), horizon)
    out = assoc.association_report(
        labels,
        [synthetic_alert("radar", repro.ANCHOR)],
        asof_ts_ms=repro.ANCHOR + 2_000_000,
    )
    table = out["horizons"][str(horizon)]
    (row,) = table["primary_pointwise"]["outcomes"]
    assert row["primary_outcome"] == "failure" and row["verified_forward"] is False
    assert table["forward_window_coverage"]["covered_count"] == 1
    assert row["covers_forward_window_anchor_ts_ms"] > repro.ANCHOR
    assert out["failed_alerts_that_cover_a_forward_window"] == 1
    assert table["primary_pointwise"]["failure_count"] == 1
    assert table["primary_pointwise"]["success_count"] == 0


def test_publication_at_a_completed_window_endpoint_is_still_a_failure():
    engine = repro.engine(
        lambda seconds: 0.002 + 0.02 * min(seconds / 300, 1) if seconds >= 0 else 0
    )
    labels = grade_labels(engine, range(0, 301, 5), 300)
    out = assoc.association_report(
        labels,
        [synthetic_alert("endpoint", repro.ANCHOR + 300_000)],
        asof_ts_ms=repro.ANCHOR + 2_000_000,
    )
    table = out["horizons"]["300"]
    (row,) = table["primary_pointwise"]["outcomes"]
    (window,) = table["forward_window_coverage"]["covered_windows"]
    assert window["anchor_ts_ms"] == repro.ANCHOR
    assert (
        window["forward_peak_ts_ms"] == repro.ANCHOR + 300_000 == row["published_ts_ms"]
    )
    assert row["primary_outcome"] == "failure"
    assert out["failed_alerts_that_cover_a_forward_window"] == 1


def test_real_v2_labels_round_trip_with_provenance():
    engine = repro.engine(lambda seconds: 0.002 + 0.02 * min(max(seconds / 300, 0), 1))
    labels = grade_labels(engine, range(0, 61, 5), 300)
    out = assoc.association_report(
        labels,
        [synthetic_alert("own", repro.ANCHOR)],
        asof_ts_ms=repro.ANCHOR + 1_000_000,
    )
    assert out["target_contract_sha256"] == engine.contract_sha256
    assert out["target_evaluator_sha256"] == engine.implementation_sha256
    primary = out["horizons"]["300"]["primary_pointwise"]
    assert primary["success_count"] == 1
    assert out["horizons"]["300"]["forward_window_coverage"]["covered_count"] == 1


# 3. Clocks and provenance are validated, never coerced -------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "edit",
    [
        lambda a: {**a, "inputs_available_ts_ms": a["anchor_ts_ms"] - 5000},
        lambda a: {**a, "published_ts_ms": a["inputs_available_ts_ms"] - 1},
        lambda a: {**a, "published_ts_ms": ASOF + 1},
        lambda a: {**a, "anchor_ts_ms": a["anchor_ts_ms"] + 1},
        lambda a: {**a, "direction": "sideways"},
        lambda a: {**a, "publication_id": ""},
        lambda a: {**a, "published_ts_ms": True},
        lambda a: {k: v for k, v in a.items() if k != "inputs_available_ts_ms"},
    ],
)
def test_malformed_or_non_causal_alerts_are_rejected(edit):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        assoc.association_report([label(0)], [edit(alert("x", 0))], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)


def test_duplicate_publication_identity_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate publication"):
        assoc.association_report([], [alert("x", 0), alert("x", 5000)], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)


def test_late_inputs_are_retained_not_backdated():
    out = assoc.association_report(
        [label(0)], [alert("late", 7000, anchor=0, inputs=6000)], asof_ts_ms=ASOF
    )
    (row,) = out["horizons"]["300"]["primary_pointwise"]["outcomes"]
    assert row["anchor_ts_ms"] == B and row["inputs_available_ts_ms"] == B + 6000
    assert row["published_ts_ms"] == B + 7000
    assert (row["input_lag_ms"], row["publication_delay_ms"]) == (6000, 7000)
    assert row["inputs_known_at_anchor"] is False
    assert row["primary_outcome"] == "success"  # graded at the declared cutoff anchor


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "edit",
    [
        lambda l: {**l, "contract_version": "instant-target-v1"},
        lambda l: {**l, "verified_forward": None},
        lambda l: {**l, "verified_forward": 1},
        lambda l: {**l, "status": "pending", "verified_forward": False},
        lambda l: {**l, "status": "done"},
        lambda l: {**l, "label_maturity_ts_ms": l["anchor_ts_ms"] + 1000},
        lambda l: {**l, "peak_ts_ms": l["label_maturity_ts_ms"] + 5000},
        lambda l: {**l, "peak_ts_ms": None},
        lambda l: {
            **l,
            "anchor_ts_ms": l["anchor_ts_ms"] + 1,
            "label_maturity_ts_ms": l["label_maturity_ts_ms"] + 1,
        },
        lambda l: {
            **l,
            "horizon_seconds": 600,
            "label_maturity_ts_ms": l["anchor_ts_ms"] + 600_000,
        },
        lambda l: {**l, "product_id": ""},
        lambda l: {k: v for k, v in l.items() if k != "evaluator_sha256"},
    ],
)
def test_malformed_labels_are_rejected(edit):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        assoc.association_report([edit(label(0))], [], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)


def test_mixed_provenance_and_duplicate_labels_are_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="mix"):
        assoc.association_report(
            [label(0), {**label(5000), "evaluator_sha256": "other"}],
            [],
            asof_ts_ms=ASOF,
        )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="mix"):
        assoc.association_report(
            [label(0), {**label(5000), "contract_sha256": "other"}], [], asof_ts_ms=ASOF
        )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate label"):
        assoc.association_report(
            [label(0), label(0, verified=False)], [], asof_ts_ms=ASOF
        )


def test_ready_label_cannot_mature_after_asof():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="as-of"):
        assoc.association_report([label(0)], [], asof_ts_ms=B + 299_999)


def test_inputs_are_not_mutated():
    labels, alerts = oracle_labels("up", 300), oracle_alerts("up", 300)
    before = copy.deepcopy((labels, alerts))
    assoc.association_report(labels, alerts, asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    assert (labels, alerts) == before


# 4. Exposure oracle by hand -----------------------------------------------------------


def oracle_slots():
    return [
        slot(PRODUCT, 0),
        slot(PRODUCT, 5000),
        slot(PRODUCT, 10_000, health_verified=False),
        slot("B-USD", 0, universe_member=False),
        slot("B-USD", 5000),
        slot("B-USD", 10_000, warmup_complete=False, candidate_eligible=False),
    ]


def oracle_exposure_labels():
    return [
        label(0, direction="up"),
        label(0, direction="down", verified=False),
        label(5000, direction="up"),  # A-USD at 5000 lacks a down label.
        label(5000, direction="up", product="B-USD"),
        label(
            5000,
            direction="down",
            product="B-USD",
            status="unavailable",
            reason="stale_or_future_quote",
        ),
    ]


def test_exposure_oracle_by_hand():
    out = assoc.exposure_report(
        oracle_exposure_labels(),
        oracle_slots(),
        asof_ts_ms=ASOF,
        ranker_slots={
            "r1": [
                (PRODUCT, B),
                (PRODUCT, B + 5000),
                ("B-USD", B + 5000),
                ("B-USD", B),
            ],
            "r2": [(PRODUCT, B), ("B-USD", B + 5000)],
        },
    )
    hour = 5 / 3600
    assert out["recorded_slot_count"] == 6
    assert out["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 3
    assert out["decision_eligible_product_hours"] == pytest.approx(3 * hour)
    assert out["board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product"] == pytest.approx(
        2 * hour
    )
    assert out["board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_verified_health"] == pytest.approx(
        3 * hour
    )
    assert out["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"] == {
        "universe_member": 1,
        "warmup_complete": 1,
        "health_verified": 1,
        "candidate_eligible": 0,
    }
    assert out["universe_size_by_cutoff"] == [[B, 1], [B + 5000, 2], [B + 10_000, 2]]
    five = out["horizons"]["300"]
    assert five["gradable_slot_count"] == 1
    assert five["gradable_product_hours"] == pytest.approx(hour)
    assert five["ungradable_reasons"] == {
        "label_missing:down": 1,
        "unavailable:down:stale_or_future_quote": 1,
    }
    assert five["gradable_fraction_of_eligible"] == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
    assert five["comparison"]["identical_complete_row_count"] == 1
    assert five["comparison"]["natural_ranker_coverage"] == {
        "r1": dict(eligible_scored_slot_count=3, gradable_scored_slot_count=1),
        "r2": dict(eligible_scored_slot_count=2, gradable_scored_slot_count=1),
    }
    fifteen = out["horizons"]["900"]
    assert fifteen["gradable_slot_count"] == 0
    assert fifteen["ungradable_reasons"] == {"label_missing:up": 3}


def test_missing_future_data_does_not_erase_the_decision_universe():
    with_labels = assoc.exposure_report(
        oracle_exposure_labels(), oracle_slots(), asof_ts_ms=ASOF
    )
    without = assoc.exposure_report([], oracle_slots(), asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    for key in (
        "decision_eligible_slot_count",
        "decision_eligible_product_hours",
        "board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product",
        "universe_size_by_cutoff",
        "exclusions_by_first_failed_flag",
    ):
        assert with_labels[key] == without[key]
    assert without["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 0
    assert without["horizons"]["300"]["ungradable_reasons"] == {"label_missing:up": 3}


def test_immature_trailing_horizon_is_visible_not_graded():
    asof = B + 5000 + 300_000  # Slot at 5000 matures exactly now at 5m; 15m does not.
    out = assoc.exposure_report(
        [label(0, direction=d) for d in ("up", "down")]
        + [label(5000, direction=d) for d in ("up", "down")],
        [slot(PRODUCT, 0), slot(PRODUCT, 5000)],
        asof_ts_ms=asof,
    )
    assert out["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 2
    assert out["horizons"]["900"]["ungradable_reasons"] == {
        "immature_trailing_horizon": 2
    }
    assert out["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 2


def test_shrinking_universe_stays_visible():
    slots = [
        slot(p, t, universe_member=(t == 0 or p == PRODUCT))
        for p in (PRODUCT, "B-USD", "C-USD")
        for t in (0, 5000, 10_000)
    ]
    out = assoc.exposure_report([], slots, asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    assert [size for _, size in out["universe_size_by_cutoff"]] == [3, 1, 1]
    assert (out["universe_size_first"], out["universe_size_last"]) == (3, 1)
    assert out["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"]["universe_member"] == 4


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "edit",
    [
        lambda s: {**s, "health_verified": None},
        lambda s: {**s, "warmup_complete": 1},
        lambda s: {k: v for k, v in s.items() if k != "candidate_eligible"},
        lambda s: {**s, "ts_ms": s["ts_ms"] + 1},
        lambda s: {**s, "ts_ms": ASOF + 5000},
        lambda s: {**s, "product_id": ""},
    ],
)
def test_malformed_eligibility_is_rejected_never_coerced(edit):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        assoc.exposure_report([], [edit(slot(PRODUCT, 0))], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)


def test_duplicate_slots_and_unknown_ranker_slots_are_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate or overlapping"):
        assoc.exposure_report(
            [],
            [slot(PRODUCT, 0), slot(PRODUCT, 0, health_verified=False)],
            asof_ts_ms=ASOF,
        )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ranker slots"):
        assoc.exposure_report(
            [],
            [slot(PRODUCT, 0)],
            asof_ts_ms=ASOF,
            ranker_slots={"r": [(PRODUCT, B + 5000)]},
        )

    prov = assoc.provenance()

    assert cfg["physical_onset_defined"] is False

    assert (

        == target.load_contract(target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH)[0]["events"][
            "prediction_lead_limit_seconds"
        ]
    )

"""Hand-computed books, atomic batches, lifecycle/knowledge and publication clocks."""

from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError
from pathlib import Path
import sys

import pytest

sys.path.insert(
    0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts/scoring_experiment")
)
from instant_book import BookReplay
from instant_publication import publication_timing


PID = "TEST-USD"


class Feed:
    def __init__(self, products=(PID,)):
        self.now = 0
        self.seq = 0
        self.book = BookReplay(products, clock_ms=lambda: self.now)
        self.products = products

    def send(self, ts, kind, *, pid=PID, connection=1, source="ws", lag=0, **fields):
        self.seq += 1
        self.now = ts + lag
        env = dict(
            receipt_seq=self.seq,
            receipt_ts_ms=ts,
            receipt_monotonic_ns=ts * 1000000,
            connection_id=connection,
            source=source,
            message=dict(type=kind, product_id=pid, **fields),
        )
        self.book.ingest(env)

    def ack(self, ts, *, products=None, connection=1):
        self.send(
            ts,
            "subscriptions",
            connection=connection,
            channels=[
                dict(
                    name=c,
                    product_ids=list(self.products if products is None else products),
                )
                for c in ("level2_batch", "matches", "heartbeat")
            ],
        )

    def hb(self, ts, *, pid=PID, connection=1):
        self.send(ts, "heartbeat", pid=pid, connection=connection, last_trade_id=10)

    def snapshot(self, ts, **kw):
        fields = dict(
            bids=[["99", "2"], ["98", "7"]], asks=[["101", "1"], ["102", "8"]]
        )
        fields.update(kw)
        self.send(ts, "snapshot", **fields)

    def update(self, ts, changes, **kw):
        self.send(ts, "l2update", changes=changes, **kw)


def ready():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(100)
    feed.hb(1000)
    feed.snapshot(1200)
    feed.hb(4500)
    return feed


def test_hand_computed_snapshot_is_sorted_has_sizes_and_preserves_source_scope():
    feed = ready()
    view = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    assert view.book_state_available and view.mid == 100
    assert (view.bid, view.ask, view.bid_size, view.ask_size) == ("99", "101", "2", "1")
    assert view.sampled_pressure == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
    assert view.observation_age_ms == 3800 and view.available_ts_ms == 1201
    assert not view.full_feed_verified and not view.target_sample_adapter_supported
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        view.bid = "100"
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        feed.book.history[PID][-1].reason = "fake"


def test_absolute_updates_zero_deletion_and_repeated_price_last_wins():
    feed = ready()
    feed.update(
        4600,
        [
            ["buy", "99", "3"],
            ["buy", "99", "5"],
            ["sell", "101", "0"],
            ["sell", "101.5", "4"],
        ],
    )
    view = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    assert (view.bid_size, view.ask, view.ask_size) == ("5", "101.5", "4")
    assert view.sampled_pressure == pytest.approx(1 / 9)
    assert view.mid == 100.25


def test_whole_batch_is_atomic_when_an_intermediate_state_would_cross():
    feed = ready()
    feed.update(
        4600,
        [
            ["buy", "102", "3"],
            ["sell", "101", "0"],
            ["sell", "102", "0"],
            ["sell", "103", "4"],
        ],
    )
    view = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    assert view.book_state_available and view.bid == "102" and view.ask == "103"
    # Only the final packet state is observable; no intrabatch peak is created.
    assert len([r for r in feed.book.history[PID] if r.receipt_ts_ms == 4600]) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "changes",
    [
        [["buy", "105", "3"]],
        [["buy", "101", "3"]],
        [["sell", "101", "0"], ["sell", "102", "0"]],
        [["buy", "99", "3"], ["invalid", "100", "2"]],
        [["buy", "NaN", "1"]],
        [["sell", "101", "-1"]],
        [],
        [["buy", "99", "Infinity"]],
        [["buy", "99", True]],
        [["buy", "1e9999", "1"]],
        [["buy", "1e-9999", "1"]],
    ],
)
def test_invalid_batch_discards_book_and_requires_a_new_snapshot(changes):
    feed = ready()
    before = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    feed.update(5100, changes)
    feed.hb(9000)
    failed = feed.book.view(PID, 10000)
    assert failed.reason == "invalid_book_update" and failed.bid is None
    feed.update(9100, [["buy", "99", "2"]])
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 10000).reason == "awaiting_snapshot"
    feed.snapshot(9200)
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 10000).book_state_available
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000) == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "fields",
    [
        dict(bids=[]),
        dict(asks=[]),
        dict(bids=[["99", "0"]]),
        dict(bids=[["99", "2"], ["99.0", "2"]]),
        dict(bids=[["101", "2"]]),
        dict(asks=[["98", "1"]]),
        dict(bids=[["-1", "2"]]),
        dict(asks=[["101", None]]),
    ],
)
def test_invalid_snapshots_do_not_create_usable_quotes(fields):
    feed = ready()
    feed.snapshot(4600, **fields)
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000).reason == "invalid_book_snapshot"


def test_observation_whole_book_and_bbo_change_clocks_are_distinct():
    feed = ready()
    feed.update(4600, [["buy", "98", "8"]])
    view = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    assert view.observation_ts_ms == view.book_change_ts_ms == 4600
    assert view.bbo_change_ts_ms == 1200
    feed.update(
        4700, [["buy", "98", "8"]]
    )  # Actual no-op observation renews observation only.
    view = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    assert (view.observation_ts_ms, view.book_change_ts_ms, view.bbo_change_ts_ms) == (
        4700,
        4600,
        1200,
    )
    feed.update(4800, [["buy", "99", "4"]])
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000).bbo_change_ts_ms == 4800


def test_heartbeats_do_not_refresh_book_quote_age():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(100)
    feed.hb(1000)
    feed.hb(5000)
    feed.snapshot(5000)
    for ts in (9000, 13000, 14999):
        feed.hb(ts)
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 15000).book_state_available  # Exact 10s allowed.
    feed.hb(19000)
    view = feed.book.view(PID, 20000)
    assert view.reason == "stale_book_observation" and view.observation_ts_ms == 5000
    assert view.mid is None and view.observation_age_ms == 15000


def test_same_cutoff_packet_is_excluded_even_with_later_knowledge():
    feed = ready()
    early = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    feed.update(5000, [["buy", "99", "4"]])
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000) == early
    later = feed.book.view(PID, 5000, asof=6000)
    assert later.bid_size == "2"


def test_later_processing_and_retro_view_do_not_rewrite_earlier_knowledge():
    feed = ready()
    before = feed.book.view(PID, 5000)
    feed.update(4600, [["buy", "99", "4"]], lag=1000)
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000) == before
    retrospective = feed.book.view(PID, 5000, asof=5600)
    assert retrospective.bid_size == "4" and retrospective.available_ts_ms == 5600
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000) == before


def test_availability_is_measured_after_book_computation():
    feed = ready()
    clock = iter(
        (4600, 4700, 5500)
    )  # Monitor start, monitor completion, book completion.
    feed.book.monitor.clock_ms = lambda: next(clock)
    feed.update(4600, [["buy", "99", "4"]])
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000).bid_size == "2"
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000, asof=5500).available_ts_ms == 5500

    feed.snapshot(9400)

def timing(**kw):
    args = dict(
        publication_id="alert-1",
        product_id=PID,
        feature_cutoff=10000,
        inputs_available=10060,
        decision_completed=10080,
        published=10100,
    )
    args.update(kw)
    return publication_timing(**args)


def test_late_proof_is_recorded_without_backdating_or_approving_fit():
    row = timing()
    assert row.feature_age_at_publication_ms == 100
    assert row.verification_lag_from_cutoff_ms == 60
    assert row.decision_to_publication_ms == 20

    assert row.within_declared_feature_age is None
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        row.feature_cutoff_ts_ms = 10100

===== BEGIN SOURCE 85 | tests/test_instant_book_review.py | sha256=8bd39356022bb9bbba041ec5d7f8d245b7cada10dd2e3e6e596309bad205fcec =====
"""Independent adversarial review probes for instant_book / instant_publication.

Claude review of Codex 8c51878 (inspected at tip 3ad47b3). Every expected value
below is hand-calculated from the contract, not read back from the module.
Probes marked DEFECT failed on the unmodified code; controls passed on it.
Synthetic inputs establish engineering behaviour only, not predictive value.
"""

from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment"))
import instant_book  # noqa: E402
from instant_book import BookReplay  # noqa: E402
from instant_publication import publication_timing  # noqa: E402

A, B = "AAA-USD", "BBB-USD"
CHANNELS = ("level2_batch", "matches", "heartbeat")


class Tape:
    """Independent envelope writer; the clock is a trusted synthetic input."""

    def __init__(self, products=(A,)):
        self.products, self.seq, self.now = products, 0, 0
        self.clocks = None
        self.book = BookReplay(list(products), clock_ms=self._clock)

    def _clock(self):
        return next(self.clocks) if self.clocks else self.now

    def send(self, ts, kind, *, pid=A, conn=1, source="ws", clocks=None, **fields):
        self.seq += 1
        self.now = ts
        self.clocks = iter(clocks) if clocks else None
        self.book.ingest(
            dict(
                receipt_seq=self.seq,
                receipt_ts_ms=ts,
                receipt_monotonic_ns=ts * 1000000,
                connection_id=conn,
                source=source,
                message=dict(type=kind, product_id=pid, **fields),
            )
        )
        self.clocks = None

    def ack(self, ts, products=None, conn=1, channels=CHANNELS):
        ids = list(self.products if products is None else products)
        self.send(
            ts,
            "subscriptions",
            conn=conn,
            channels=[dict(name=c, product_ids=ids) for c in channels],
        )

    def hb(self, ts, pid=A, conn=1, **kw):
        self.send(ts, "heartbeat", pid=pid, conn=conn, last_trade_id=5, **kw)

    def snap(self, ts, pid=A, conn=1, bids=None, asks=None):
        self.send(
            ts,
            "snapshot",
            pid=pid,
            conn=conn,
            bids=bids or [["100.5", "3"], ["100", "4"]],
            asks=asks or [["101", "1"], ["101.5", "2"]],
        )

    def upd(self, ts, changes, pid=A, conn=1, **kw):
        self.send(ts, "l2update", pid=pid, conn=conn, changes=changes, **kw)

    def view(self, cutoff, pid=A, asof=None):
        return self.book.view(pid, cutoff, asof=asof)


def live():
    t = Tape()
    t.ack(10)
    t.hb(500)
    t.snap(600)
    t.hb(1500)
    return t


def quote(v):
    return (v.bid, v.ask, v.bid_size, v.ask_size)


# 1. Hand-calculated book and pressure oracles ------------------------------


def test_oracle_sequence_absolute_replace_zero_delete_repeat_and_deep_levels():
    t = live()
    v = t.view(5000)
    # bid 100.5x3 / ask 101x1: mid 100.75, pressure (3-1)/(3+1).
    assert quote(v) == ("100.5", "101", "3", "1")
    assert v.mid == 100.75 and v.sampled_pressure == 0.5

    t.upd(1600, [["buy", "100.5", "0"], ["buy", "100.75", "6"], ["sell", "101", "5"]])
    t.hb(2500)
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert quote(v) == ("100.75", "101", "6", "5")
    assert v.mid == 100.875 and v.sampled_pressure == pytest.approx(1 / 11)

    t.upd(2600, [["sell", "101", "2"]])  # Absolute replacement, not 5 + 2.
    assert t.view(5000).ask_size == "2"
    assert t.view(5000).sampled_pressure == pytest.approx(0.5)  # (6-2)/8

    t.upd(
        2700, [["buy", "100.75", "1"], ["buy", "100.75", "0"], ["buy", "100.75", "9"]]
    )
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert v.bid_size == "9" and v.sampled_pressure == pytest.approx(7 / 11)
    assert (v.book_change_ts_ms, v.bbo_change_ts_ms) == (2700, 2700)

    t.upd(2800, [["sell", "150", "3"]])  # Deep ask: book changes, BBO does not.
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert (v.observation_ts_ms, v.book_change_ts_ms, v.bbo_change_ts_ms) == (
        2800,
        2800,
        2700,
    )

    t.upd(2900, [["sell", "101", "0"]])  # Delete best ask: next level surfaces.
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert (v.ask, v.ask_size, v.mid) == ("101.5", "2", 101.125)
    assert v.sampled_pressure == pytest.approx(7 / 11)  # (9-2)/(9+2)


def test_transient_cross_removed_in_same_batch_is_observation_only():
    t = live()
    t.upd(
        1600,
        [
            ["buy", "102", "1"],
            ["sell", "100", "1"],
            ["buy", "102", "0"],
            ["sell", "100", "0"],
        ],
    )
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert v.book_state_available and quote(v) == ("100.5", "101", "3", "1")
    assert (v.observation_ts_ms, v.book_change_ts_ms, v.bbo_change_ts_ms) == (
        1600,
        600,
        600,
    )
    assert [r.receipt_ts_ms for r in t.book.history[A]].count(1600) == 1


def test_deleting_an_absent_level_is_an_observation_not_a_change():
    t = live()
    t.upd(1600, [["buy", "50", "0"]])
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert (v.observation_ts_ms, v.book_change_ts_ms) == (1600, 600)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "changes",
    [
        [["buy", "101", "1"]],  # final locked
        [["sell", "100.5", "1"]],  # final locked from the ask side
        [["buy", "101.5", "1"], ["sell", "101", "0"]],  # final crossed at depth
        [["buy", "100.5", "0"], ["buy", "100", "0"]],  # bid side exhausted
    ],
)
def test_final_locked_crossed_or_missing_side_is_unavailable(changes):
    t = live()
    t.upd(1600, changes)
    assert t.view(5000).reason == "invalid_book_update"


# B2 DEFECT: a representation-only size change moved the BBO clock.


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "changes",
    [
        [["buy", "100.5", "3.00"]],
        [["buy", "100.5", "0"], ["buy", "100.50", "3"]],
    ],
)
def test_numerically_identical_bbo_does_not_move_change_clocks(changes):
    t = live()
    t.upd(1600, changes)
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert v.book_state_available and v.observation_ts_ms == 1600
    # No price or quantity changed, so neither change clock may move, and a
    # BBO change can never be newer than the whole-book change.
    assert (v.book_change_ts_ms, v.bbo_change_ts_ms) == (600, 600)


def test_control_real_bbo_size_change_moves_both_clocks():
    t = live()
    t.upd(1600, [["buy", "100.5", "3.5"]])
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert (v.book_change_ts_ms, v.bbo_change_ts_ms, v.bid_size) == (1600, 1600, "3.5")


# 2. Sessions, acknowledgements, ignored inputs, failures ------------------


def test_multi_product_ack_and_failure_isolation():
    t = Tape((A, B))
    t.ack(10, products=(A,))
    t.hb(500)
    t.hb(500, pid=B)
    t.snap(600)
    t.snap(600, pid=B)
    t.hb(1500)
    t.hb(1500, pid=B)
    assert t.view(5000).book_state_available
    assert t.view(5000, pid=B).reason == "subscription_unconfirmed"
    # An invalid batch on A does not touch B, and vice versa.
    t.ack(1600)
    t.snap(1700, pid=B)
    t.hb(2000, pid=B)
    t.upd(2100, [["buy", "200", "1"]])
    assert t.view(5000).reason == "invalid_book_update"
    assert t.view(5000, pid=B).book_state_available


def test_ack_missing_level2_batch_channel_does_not_confirm_book():
    t = Tape()
    t.ack(10, channels=("level2", "matches", "heartbeat"))
    t.hb(500)
    t.snap(600)
    t.hb(1500)
    assert t.view(5000).reason == "subscription_unconfirmed"


def test_rest_input_with_other_connection_neither_resets_nor_replaces_book():
    t = live()
    before = t.view(5000)
    t.send(
        1600, "snapshot", source="rest", conn=99, bids=[["1", "1"]], asks=[["2", "1"]]
    )
    t.send(
        1700,
        "snapshot",
        source="rest_recovery",
        conn=98,
        bids=[["1", "1"]],
        asks=[["2", "1"]],
    )
    t.send(1800, "ticker", best_bid="1", best_ask="2")
    assert t.view(5000) == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "kind", ["queue_overflow", "audit_parse_error", "audit_transport_error", "error"]
)
def test_failure_marker_invalidates_every_product_and_needs_ack_then_snapshot(kind):
    t = Tape((A, B))
    t.ack(10)
    for ts, step in ((500, t.hb), (600, t.snap), (1500, t.hb)):
        step(ts, pid=A)
        step(ts, pid=B)
    t.send(1600, kind, pid=None)
    for ts, step in ((2000, t.hb), (2100, t.snap)):  # Snapshot without ack.
        step(ts, pid=A)
        step(ts, pid=B)
    for pid in (A, B):
        assert t.view(5000, pid=pid).reason == "subscription_unconfirmed"
    t.ack(2200)
    t.hb(2300)
    assert t.view(5000).reason == "awaiting_snapshot"
    t.snap(2400)
    assert t.view(5000).book_state_available
    assert t.view(5000, pid=B).reason == "awaiting_snapshot"


def test_same_millisecond_heartbeat_collision_fails_closed_until_next_beat():
    t = live()
    t.hb(1500)
    assert t.view(5000).reason == "heartbeat_unavailable"
    t.hb(2500)
    assert t.view(5000).book_state_available


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "second,reason", [(6500, None), (6501, "heartbeat_gap_requires_snapshot")]
)
def test_heartbeat_gap_boundary_is_strictly_greater_than_five_seconds(second, reason):
    t = live()
    t.upd(6000, [["buy", "100.5", "4"]])
    t.hb(second)
    assert t.view(10000).reason == reason


# 3. Receipt vs processing completion, TTL and as-of ------------------------


def test_receipt_one_ms_before_cutoff_is_included_receipt_at_cutoff_excluded():
    t = live()
    t.upd(4999, [["buy", "100.5", "7"]])
    t.upd(5000, [["buy", "100.5", "8"]])
    assert t.view(5000).bid_size == "7"
    assert t.view(5000, asof=999999).bid_size == "7"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("beat,ok", [(5000, True), (4999, False)])
def test_heartbeat_freshness_boundary_is_exactly_five_seconds(beat, ok):
    t = Tape()
    t.ack(10)
    t.hb(beat - 1000)
    t.snap(beat - 900)
    t.hb(beat)
    v = t.view(10000)
    assert v.book_state_available is ok
    assert v.reason == (None if ok else "stale_heartbeat")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("obs,reason", [(5000, None), (4999, "stale_book_observation")])
def test_observation_ttl_is_exactly_ten_seconds_and_heartbeats_do_not_renew(
    obs, reason
):
    t = Tape()
    t.ack(10)
    t.hb(obs - 100)
    t.snap(obs)
    for ts in range(obs + 900, 15000, 1000):
        t.hb(ts)
    v = t.view(15000)
    assert v.reason == reason and v.observation_ts_ms == obs


# B1 DEFECT: a heartbeat was usable before the book finished processing it.
# The monitor stamps its transport record at monitor completion; the book's
# heartbeat-gap invalidation from the SAME packet is stamped later, at book
# completion. In between, the gap heartbeat freshened a book it invalidates.


def test_gap_heartbeat_cannot_freshen_a_book_before_its_invalidation_is_known():
    t = live()  # Last heartbeat 1500; book observed at 600 (age 9400 at 10000).
    # Gap 8100ms > 5000. Clocks: monitor start, monitor done, book done.
    t.hb(9600, clocks=(9600, 10000, 10500))
    v = t.view(10000)  # asof 10000: monitor done, book not done.
    assert not v.book_state_available and v.bid is None
    assert v.reason == "stale_heartbeat"  # Latest fully processed beat: 1500.
    assert t.view(10000, asof=10500).reason == "heartbeat_gap_requires_snapshot"


def test_control_without_the_gap_heartbeat_the_view_is_stale():
    assert live().view(10000).reason == "stale_heartbeat"


def test_control_ordinary_heartbeat_is_still_usable_while_the_next_one_is_in_flight():
    t = live()
    t.upd(6000, [["buy", "100.5", "4"]])
    t.hb(6400)
    t.hb(9600, clocks=(9600, 10000, 10500))
    assert t.view(10000).book_state_available  # Beat 6400 is 3600ms old.
    assert t.view(10000, asof=10500).book_state_available


def test_late_failure_is_visible_only_after_processing_and_never_rewrites_earlier():
    t = live()
    t.upd(3000, [["buy", "100.5", "4"]])
    t.hb(3500)
    before = t.view(5000)
    t.send(4000, "connection_closed", pid=None, clocks=(4000, 4001, 7000))
    later = t.view(5000, asof=7000)
    assert later.reason == "connection_closed" and later.bid is None
    assert before.book_state_available and before.bid_size == "4"


def test_one_packet_has_one_knowledge_time_for_transport_and_book():
    # Same root cause as B1, in the fail-closed direction: on the pinned code
    # the marker's transport record vetoed the view at 4001 although the book
    # had not yet processed the marker (available 7000). Both now share 7000.
    t = live()
    t.upd(3000, [["buy", "100.5", "4"]])
    t.hb(3500)
    before = t.view(5000)
    t.send(4000, "connection_closed", pid=None, clocks=(4000, 4001, 7000))
    assert t.view(5000) == before
    assert t.view(5000, asof=6999).book_state_available
    assert not t.view(5000, asof=7000).book_state_available


def test_queued_packet_is_not_certified_absent_by_an_older_quote():
    # Documented scope: the view is the last fully processed state, not a
    # queue/completeness certificate. This control pins that behaviour.
    t = live()
    t.upd(4000, [["buy", "100.5", "9"]], clocks=(4000, 4001, 6000))
    v = t.view(5000)
    assert v.bid_size == "3" and v.available_ts_ms == 601
    assert not v.full_feed_verified


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "clocks,match",
    [((2000, 2001, 1999), "regressed"), ((2000, 2001, 2000.5), "integer")],
)
def test_bad_book_processing_clock_fails_the_artifact(clocks, match):
    t = live()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=match):
        t.hb(2000, clocks=clocks)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="failed book"):
        t.view(5000)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="failed"):
        t.hb(2500)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "cutoff,asof",
    [(4999, None), (5000, 4999), (True, None), (5000, True), (-5000, None)],
)
def test_query_requires_grid_cutoff_and_causal_integer_asof(cutoff, asof):
    t = live()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        t.view(cutoff, asof=asof)


def test_results_are_frozen_and_carry_provenance():
    t = live()
    v = t.view(5000)
    for attr in ("bid", "reason", "knowledge_asof_ts_ms"):
        with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
            setattr(v, attr, None)
    src = (ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_book.py").read_text("utf-8")
    con = (ROOT / "docs/research/INSTANT_BOOK_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json").read_text(
        "utf-8"
    )
    assert v.implementation_sha256 == hashlib.sha256(src.encode()).hexdigest()
    assert v.contract_sha256 == hashlib.sha256(con.encode()).hexdigest()
    assert not v.full_feed_verified and not v.target_sample_adapter_supported


# 4. Publication chronology -------------------------------------------------


def clocks(**kw):
    args = dict(
        publication_id="p",
        product_id=A,
        feature_cutoff=20000,
        inputs_available=20040,
        decision_completed=20070,
        published=20150,
    )
    args.update(kw)
    return publication_timing(**args)


def test_publication_hand_calculated_ages_and_anchor_flags():
    row = clocks()
    assert (
        row.feature_age_at_publication_ms,
        row.verification_lag_from_cutoff_ms,
        row.decision_to_publication_ms,
    ) == (150, 40, 80)
    assert not row.legacy_cutoff_inputs_compatible
    assert row.publication_target_amendment_required
    assert row.readiness_reason == "publication_age_policy_unfrozen"
    assert not row.eligible_for_fitting


def test_inputs_at_cutoff_but_late_publication_still_needs_amendment():
    row = clocks(inputs_available=20000)
    assert row.legacy_cutoff_inputs_compatible
    assert row.publication_target_amendment_required


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "kw",
    [
        dict(inputs_available=20000, decision_completed=20000, published=20000),
        dict(decision_completed=20040),
        dict(published=20070),
    ],
)
def test_publication_equal_neighbouring_clocks_are_allowed(kw):
    assert clocks(**kw).published_ts_ms >= clocks(**kw).feature_cutoff_ts_ms


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "bound,within,reason",
    [
        (149, False, "feature_window_too_old"),
        (150, True, "study_and_source_gates_unfrozen"),
        (0, False, "feature_window_too_old"),
    ],
)
def test_publication_age_bound_is_exact_and_never_approves_fitting(
    bound, within, reason
):
    row = clocks(max_feature_age_ms=bound)
    assert row.within_declared_feature_age is within and row.readiness_reason == reason
    assert not row.eligible_for_fitting


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "kw",
    [
        dict(publication_id=7),
        dict(product_id=None),
        dict(inputs_available=19999),
        dict(decision_completed=20039),
        dict(published=20069),
        dict(feature_cutoff=20000.0),
        dict(max_feature_age_ms=1.5),
        dict(
            feature_cutoff=-5000, inputs_available=0, decision_completed=0, published=0
        ),
    ],
)
def test_publication_rejects_malformed_or_noncausal_metadata(kw):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        clocks(**kw)


def test_publication_is_keyword_only_frozen_and_hashes_its_sources():
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        publication_timing("p", A, 20000, 20000, 20000, 20000)
    row = clocks()
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        row.published_ts_ms = 1
    src = (ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_publication.py").read_text(
        "utf-8"
    )
    assert row.implementation_sha256 == hashlib.sha256(src.encode()).hexdigest()


# 5. Offline imports ------------------------------------------------------


def test_modules_import_without_network_or_backend_dependencies():
    code = (
        "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[1]);"
        "import instant_book, instant_publication;"
        "bad = {'socket', 'ssl', 'http', 'urllib.request', 'requests', 'websocket',"
        " 'websockets', 'aiohttp', 'backend', 'app'} & set(sys.modules);"
        "print(sorted(bad)); sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)"
    )
    run = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-I", "-c", code, str(ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment")],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    assert run.returncode == 0, run.stdout + run.stderr


def test_book_level_cap_is_inherited_not_raised():
    assert instant_book.MAX_LEVELS == 100000 and instant_book.MAX_QUOTE_AGE_MS == 10000

"""Adversarial causal timing, ID completeness, session and offline CLI probes."""

import dataclasses
import gzip
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

sys.path.insert(
    0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts/scoring_experiment")
)
from instant_coverage import CoverageMonitor
import instant_coverage
from instant_samples import paired_sample
from instant_targets import TargetEvaluator
from micro_replay import ReplayStore


PID = "TEST-USD"


class Feed:
    def __init__(self, *, products=(PID,), quote="ticker", lag=0):
        self.now = 0
        self.seq = 0
        self.lag = lag
        self.monitor = CoverageMonitor(
            products, quote_channel=quote, clock_ms=lambda: self.now
        )
        self.products = products
        self.quote = quote
        self.events = []

    def send(self, ts, kind, *, pid=PID, connection=1, source="ws", **fields):
        self.seq += 1
        self.now = ts + self.lag
        event = dict(
            receipt_seq=self.seq,
            receipt_ts_ms=ts,
            receipt_monotonic_ns=ts * 1000000,
            connection_id=connection,
            source=source,
            message=dict(type=kind, product_id=pid, **fields),
        )
        self.events.append(event)
        self.monitor.ingest(event)
        return event

    def ack(self, ts, *, products=None, connection=1, channels=None):
        return self.send(
            ts,
            "subscriptions",
            connection=connection,
            channels=(
                channels
                if channels is not None
                else [
                    dict(name=c, product_ids=list(products or self.products))
                    for c in (self.quote, "matches", "heartbeat")
                ]
            ),
        )

    def hb(self, ts, last, **kw):
        return self.send(ts, "heartbeat", last_trade_id=last, **kw)

    def trade(self, ts, tid, **kw):
        return self.send(
            ts, "match", trade_id=tid, price="100", size="2", side="sell", **kw
        )


def check(feed, start=1000, end=2000, asof=2001):
    return feed.monitor.assess(PID, start, end, asof=asof)


def simple(*, lag=0):
    feed = Feed(lag=lag)
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.trade(1500, 11)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    return feed


def test_actual_proof_time_never_backdates_and_same_ms_receipt_is_excluded():
    feed = simple(lag=100)
    early = check(feed, asof=2000)
    assert early["receipt_id_check_passed"] is False
    assert check(feed, asof=2099) == {**early, "knowledge_asof_ts_ms": 2099}
    later = check(feed, asof=2100)
    assert later["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert later["receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms"] == 2100
    assert check(feed, asof=2000) == early
    no_lag = simple()
    assert not check(no_lag, asof=2000)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert check(no_lag)["receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms"] == 2001


def test_connection_health_is_not_completeness_or_quote_proof():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Missing execution even though the transport is alive.
    result = check(feed)
    assert result["transport_observed_ok"]
    assert result["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"
    assert not result["quote_coverage_verified"]


def test_healthy_no_trade_intervals_can_be_verified_without_fake_trades():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 10)
    feed.hb(3000, 10)
    assert check(feed, end=3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert feed.monitor.proofs[0]["observed_unique_trade_count"] == 0
    assert not check(feed, start=999, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "kind,source",
    [
        ("last_match", "ws"),
        ("ticker", "ws"),
        ("match", "rest"),
        ("match", "rest_recovery"),
    ],
)
def test_non_live_executions_never_repair_missing_ids(kind, source):
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.send(
        1500,
        kind,
        source=source,
        trade_id=11,
        price="100",
        size="2",
        side="sell",
        last_size="9999",
    )
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    assert check(feed)["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"
    assert not feed.monitor.seen


def test_duplicate_decimal_representation_is_not_a_second_trade():
    feed = simple()
    feed.send(2100, "match", trade_id=11, price="100.00", size="2.0", side="sell")
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert len(feed.monitor.seen) == 1
    assert check(feed, end=3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_conflicting_duplicate_vetoes_only_when_the_conflict_is_known():
    feed = simple()
    original = check(feed)
    feed.send(2100, "match", trade_id=11, price="100.01", size="2", side="sell")
    assert check(feed, asof=2100)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert check(feed, asof=2101)["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "conflicting_trade_id"
    assert check(feed) == original


def test_late_match_repairs_only_through_a_later_known_merged_proof():
    # Amended by coverage contract amendment 1. Before it, a late match left
    # both intervals failed permanently. The failed records and earlier
    # knowledge are still untouched; repair exists only from the closing proof.
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    old = check(feed)
    assert old["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"
    feed.trade(2100, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    reasons = [p["reason"] for p in feed.monitor.proofs if not p["verified"]]
    assert reasons == ["missing_live_match_ids", "receipt_ids_outside_watermarks"]
    assert check(feed) == old
    repaired = check(feed, asof=3001)
    assert repaired["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert repaired["receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms"] == 3001
    assert repaired["receipt_id_check_merged_proofs"] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("when", [1000, 2000])
def test_receipt_boundary_ambiguity_is_not_hidden_by_heartbeat_sequence(when):
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    if when == 1000:
        feed.trade(1000, 10)  # Received before the lower HB at the same ms.
        feed.hb(1000, 10)
        feed.hb(2000, 10)
    else:
        feed.hb(1000, 10)
        feed.trade(2000, 11)  # Excluded from [1000,2000), even before the HB.
        feed.hb(2000, 11)
    assert not check(feed)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "cause", ["heartbeat_gap", "watermark_regression", "id_span_bound"]
)
def test_bad_heartbeat_pairs_are_bounded_and_fresh_pairs_can_resume(cause):
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    end = 7000 if cause == "heartbeat_gap" else 2000
    last = {"heartbeat_gap": 10, "watermark_regression": 9, "id_span_bound": 100011}[
        cause
    ]
    feed.hb(end, last)
    assert check(feed, end=end, asof=end + 1)["receipt_id_check_reason"] == cause
    feed.hb(end + 1000, last)
    assert check(feed, start=end, end=end + 1000, asof=end + 1001)[
        "receipt_id_check_passed"
    ]


def test_missing_subscription_is_per_product_and_full_ack_replaces_membership():
    feed = Feed(products=(PID, "BTC-USD"))
    feed.ack(900, products=("BTC-USD",))
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 10)
    assert check(feed)["transport_reason"] == "subscription_unconfirmed"
    assert not feed.monitor.proofs
    feed.ack(2100)
    feed.hb(2200, 10)
    feed.ack(2300, products=("BTC-USD",))
    feed.ack(2400)
    feed.hb(2500, 10)
    assert not feed.monitor.proofs  # No bridge through the lost subscription.
    feed.hb(3000, 10)
    assert check(feed, start=2500, end=3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert not check(feed, start=2200, end=3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ["connection_closed", "error", "queue_overflow"])
def test_disconnect_or_queue_failure_requires_new_ack_and_watermark(kind):
    feed = simple()
    old = check(feed)
    feed.send(2100, kind)
    assert check(feed, asof=2101)["transport_reason"] == kind
    feed.hb(2200, 11)
    assert len(feed.monitor.proofs) == 1
    feed.ack(2300)
    feed.hb(2400, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert check(feed, start=2400, end=3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert check(feed) == old


def test_connection_change_without_close_never_reuses_old_ack_or_watermark():
    feed = simple()
    feed.hb(2100, 11, connection=2)
    assert check(feed, asof=2101)["transport_reason"] == "subscription_unconfirmed"
    feed.ack(2200, connection=2)
    feed.hb(2300, 11, connection=2)
    feed.hb(3000, 11, connection=2)
    assert not check(feed, end=3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert check(feed, start=2300, end=3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "fault",
    [
        "seq",
        "wall",
        "mono",
        "missing_mono",
        "processing",
        "future_receipt",
        "source",
        "amount",
    ],
)
def test_invalid_logger_or_clock_fails_the_artifact_closed(fault):
    feed = simple()
    feed.now = 2100
    event = dict(
        receipt_seq=5,
        receipt_ts_ms=2100,
        receipt_monotonic_ns=2100000000,
        connection_id=1,
        message=dict(type="match", product_id=PID, trade_id=12, price="100", size="1"),
    )
    if fault == "seq":
        event["receipt_seq"] = 6
    if fault == "wall":
        event["receipt_ts_ms"] = 1999
    if fault == "mono":
        event["receipt_monotonic_ns"] = 0
    if fault == "missing_mono":
        event.pop("receipt_monotonic_ns")
    if fault == "processing":
        feed.now = 1999
    if fault == "future_receipt":
        event["receipt_ts_ms"] = 2101
    if fault == "source":
        event["source"] = "maybe_ws"
    if fault == "amount":
        event["message"]["price"] = "NaN"
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, KeyError)):
        feed.monitor.ingest(event)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="failed monitor"):
        check(feed)


def test_heartbeat_liveness_expires_without_claiming_historical_trade_loss():
    feed = simple()
    result = check(feed, asof=7001)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert result["transport_reason"] == "stale_heartbeat"


def test_same_timestamp_heartbeat_cannot_erase_the_old_watermark():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(1000, 11)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    assert check(feed)["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"


def test_book_subscription_does_not_fabricate_book_quality():
    feed = Feed(quote="level2_batch")
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 10)
    assert check(feed)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert not check(feed)["quote_coverage_verified"]
    assert not check(feed)["full_feed_verified"]


def test_availability_uses_completion_not_the_start_of_processing():
    feed = simple()
    clock = iter((3000, 3500))
    feed.monitor.clock_ms = lambda: next(clock)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert not check(feed, end=3000, asof=3499)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert check(feed, end=3000, asof=3500)["receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms"] == 3500


def test_packet_bound_never_accepts_a_truncated_prefix(monkeypatch):
    feed = simple()
    monkeypatch.setattr(instant_coverage, "MAX_PACKETS", 4)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="packet bound"):
        feed.hb(3000, 11)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="failed monitor"):
        check(feed)


def test_mid_processing_clock_regression_fails_closed():
    feed = simple()

    feed.monitor.clock_ms = lambda: next(clock)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="completion clock"):
        feed.hb(3000, 11)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="failed monitor"):
        check(feed)


def test_cli_is_offline_preserves_source_and_uses_real_processing_time(tmp_path):
    feed = simple()
    source_dir = tmp_path / "source"
    source_dir.mkdir()
    source = source_dir / "ws.jsonl.gz"
    with gzip.open(source, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        for event in feed.events:
            stream.write(json.dumps(event) + "\n")
    before = source.read_bytes()
    script = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
        / "scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_coverage.py"
    )
    output = tmp_path / "new-result"
    command = [
        sys.executable,
        str(script),
        str(source),
        "--products",
        PID,
        "--out-dir",
        str(output),
    ]
    result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    report = json.loads((output / "REPORT.json").read_text())
    assert report["packet_count"] == 4 and report["unique_ws_matches"] == 1
    assert report["verified_proofs"] == 1 and not report["feed_gate_pass"]
    assert report["first_proof_available_ts_ms"] > 1000000
    assert source.read_bytes() == before
    assert subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True).returncode != 0
    bad = command[:-1] + [str(source_dir / "inside")]
    assert subprocess.run(bad, capture_output=True).returncode != 0
    assert not (source_dir / "inside").exists()


def test_paired_samples_are_immutable_keep_real_zero_and_exact_boundary_counts(
    tmp_path,
):
    store = ReplayStore(tmp_path / "sample.sqlite", create=True)
    try:
        feed = Feed(products=(PID, "BTC-USD"))
        for product in (PID, "BTC-USD"):
            feed.send(100001, "ticker", pid=product, best_bid="99", best_ask="101")
        feed.trade(105000, 11)
        for event in feed.events:
            store.ingest(event)
        for product in (PID, "BTC-USD"):
            store.add_health(
                product=product,
                start=100000,
                end=110000,
                available=110000,
                verified=True,
                evidence="synthetic timely assertion",
            )
        store.finalize()
        early = paired_sample(store, PID, 105000)
        assert (
            early.sample is None
            and "coin:unverified_coverage" in early.unavailable_reasons
        )
        row = paired_sample(store, PID, 110000)
        assert (
            row.sample.trade_count == 1 and row.sample.trade_count_at_bucket_start == 1
        )
        assert row.sample.quote_ts_ms == 100001
        zero = paired_sample(store, "BTC-USD", 110000)
        assert zero.sample.trade_count == 0 and zero.sample.quote_volume_usd == 0
        with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
            row.sample.ts_ms = 115000
        with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
            row.ts_ms = 115000
        assert TargetEvaluator([row.sample]).rows[110000] == row.sample
        assert paired_sample(store, PID, 105000) == early
        assert (
            len(row.producer_recorder_sha256) == len(row.input_provenance_sha256) == 64
        )
    finally:
        store.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "fault", ["stale_coin", "stale_btc", "missing_btc", "late_health", "invalid_quote"]
)
def test_paired_sample_never_falls_back_to_last_trade_or_later_audit(tmp_path, fault):
    store = ReplayStore(tmp_path / "unavailable.sqlite", create=True)
    try:
        feed = Feed()
        if fault == "stale_coin":
            feed.send(90000, "ticker", best_bid=99, best_ask=101)
        if fault == "stale_btc":
            feed.send(90000, "ticker", pid="BTC-USD", best_bid=99, best_ask=101)
        if fault not in ("missing_btc", "stale_btc"):
            feed.send(109000, "ticker", pid="BTC-USD", best_bid=99, best_ask=101)
        if fault != "stale_coin":
            feed.send(
                109001,
                "ticker",
                best_bid=102 if fault == "invalid_quote" else 99,
                best_ask=101,
            )
        feed.trade(109999, 11)  # A fresh execution never substitutes for missing L1.
        for event in feed.events:
            store.ingest(event)
        for product in (PID, "BTC-USD"):
            store.add_health(
                product=product,
                start=85000,
                end=110000,
                available=110001 if fault == "late_health" else 110000,
                verified=True,
                evidence="synthetic",
            )
        store.finalize()
        result = paired_sample(store, PID, 110000)
        assert result.sample is None and result.unavailable_reasons
        if fault == "late_health":
            assert store.snapshot(PID, 110000, asof=110001)["mid"] == 100
            assert paired_sample(store, PID, 110000) == result
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_unknown_side_does_not_hide_an_otherwise_valid_target_sample(tmp_path):
    store = ReplayStore(tmp_path / "side.sqlite", create=True)
    try:
        feed = Feed()
        for product in (PID, "BTC-USD"):
            feed.send(109000, "ticker", pid=product, best_bid=99, best_ask=101)
        feed.send(109001, "match", trade_id=11, price=100, size=2, side="unknown")
        for event in feed.events:
            store.ingest(event)
        for product in (PID, "BTC-USD"):
            store.add_health(
                product=product,
                start=100000,
                end=110000,
                available=110000,
                verified=True,
                evidence="synthetic",
            )
        store.finalize()
        result = paired_sample(store, PID, 110000)
        assert result.sample.trade_count == 1 and result.sample.quote_volume_usd == 200
        assert result.coin_flow_available is False
        assert not result.unavailable_reasons
    finally:
        store.close()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 87 | tests/test_instant_coverage_barriers.py | sha256=31d257aa611c501cb82d732613b0cdf4264e31129acc976231e03068131cebd8 =====
"""Continuity barriers for amendment-1 reconciliation and assess chaining.

Codex's reproduced false pass against bfab9c8 comes first. Written
independently of the d26c57d fix on this branch (26 of 30 failed on bfab9c8)
and kept as a second probe set against the same implementation. An audit, queue or
transport failure, a connection change or a lost required subscription resets
continuity. No merged proof or assessment chain may cross that reset, even when
every event shares one millisecond, the connection ID is reused and watermarks
touch. Synthetic only; nothing here reads prices, returns or labels.
"""

import pytest

from test_instant_coverage import Feed, PID, check
from instant_coverage import MERGED_SCOPE

FAILURES = (
    "audit_parse_error",
    "audit_transport_error",
    "audit_unknown_loss",
    "queue_overflow",
    "connection_closed",
    "error",
)


def merged(feed):
    return [p for p in feed.monitor.proofs if p["scope"] == MERGED_SCOPE]


def intervals(feed):
    return [p for p in feed.monitor.proofs if p["scope"] != MERGED_SCOPE]


def _fail(feed, ts, kind):
    feed.send(ts, kind, pid=None)
    feed.ack(ts)


def _drop_and_readd(feed, ts):
    feed.ack(ts, channels=[dict(name="matches", product_ids=[])])
    feed.ack(ts)


def _drop_one_channel_membership(feed, ts):
    channels = [
        dict(name=c, product_ids=[] if c == "heartbeat" else [PID])
        for c in ("ticker", "matches", "heartbeat")
    ]
    feed.ack(ts, channels=channels)
    feed.ack(ts)


BARRIERS = [pytest.param(lambda f, ts, k=k: _fail(f, ts, k), id=k) for k in FAILURES]
BARRIERS += [
    pytest.param(_drop_and_readd, id="lost_all_membership"),
    pytest.param(_drop_one_channel_membership, id="lost_heartbeat_membership"),
]


def codex_counterexample(barrier=lambda f, ts: _fail(f, ts, "audit_parse_error")):
    """docs/research/RECONCILIATION_BOUNDARY_REVIEW_20261104.md, verbatim timing."""
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Missing ID 11.
    barrier(feed, 2000)  # Continuity must reset here.
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # New baseline, same millisecond, same connection.
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)  # ID 11 is outside the new interval's watermarks.
    return feed


def test_codex_audit_failure_at_one_millisecond_is_never_merged_across():
    feed = codex_counterexample()
    assert [p["reason"] for p in intervals(feed)] == [
        "missing_live_match_ids",
        "receipt_ids_outside_watermarks",
    ]
    assert merged(feed) == []
    result = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is False
    assert result["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("barrier", BARRIERS)
def test_no_merge_across_any_barrier_at_a_shared_millisecond(barrier):
    feed = codex_counterexample(barrier)
    assert len(intervals(feed)) == 2 and merged(feed) == []
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    # The reconstruction that bfab9c8 merged: same connection, touching time
    # and watermarks, exact ID equality over the union. Only the epoch differs.
    assert first["connection_id"] == second["connection_id"] == 1
    assert first["end_ts_ms"] == second["start_ts_ms"] == 2000
    assert first["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] == second["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
    for start, end in ((1000, 3000), (1000, 2000), (2000, 3000)):
        assert not check(feed, start, end, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("barrier", BARRIERS)
def test_assess_never_chains_verified_intervals_across_a_barrier(barrier):
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 10)  # Verified, no trades.
    barrier(feed, 2000)
    feed.hb(2000, 10)
    feed.hb(3000, 10)  # Verified, no trades.
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    assert first["verified"] and second["verified"]
    assert first["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] == second["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
    joined = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)
    assert joined["receipt_id_check_passed"] is False
    assert joined["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"
    # Each side is still usable on its own.
    assert check(feed, 1000, 2000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert check(feed, 2000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("barrier", BARRIERS)
def test_valid_races_reconcile_on_each_side_of_a_barrier(barrier):
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Old epoch race: heartbeat overtakes match 11.
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    barrier(feed, 3000)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    feed.hb(4000, 12)  # New epoch race: heartbeat overtakes match 12.
    feed.trade(4500, 12)
    feed.hb(5000, 12)
    spans = [(p["start_ts_ms"], p["end_ts_ms"]) for p in merged(feed)]
    assert spans == [(1000, 3000), (3000, 5000)]
    old, new = merged(feed)
    assert old["available_ts_ms"] == 3001 and new["available_ts_ms"] == 5001
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert check(feed, 3000, 5000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 5000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_healthy_repeated_acknowledgements_do_not_break_merges_or_chains():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.ack(2000)  # Same full membership: no reset.
    feed.ack(2200)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    feed.hb(4000, 11)
    assert len(merged(feed)) == 1
    assert check(feed, 1000, 4000, 4001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_ordinary_race_without_a_barrier_still_reconciles():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert len(merged(feed)) == 1
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_epoch_advances_at_the_reset_and_rows_stay_immutable():
    feed = codex_counterexample()
    marker = next(
        e["receipt_seq"]
        for e in feed.events
        if e["message"]["type"] == "audit_parse_error"
    )
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    # The marker and the acknowledgement that re-adds membership both advance
    # the epoch; the second interval lies strictly after both.
    assert second["continuity_epoch"] >= first["continuity_epoch"] + 2
    assert second["end_heartbeat_seq"] > marker
    healthy = Feed()
    healthy.ack(900)
    healthy.hb(1000, 10)
    healthy.hb(2000, 10)
    healthy.ack(2000)  # Same full membership: the epoch is retained.
    healthy.hb(3000, 10)
    assert len({p["continuity_epoch"] for p in healthy.monitor.proofs}) == 1
    # Original failed rows, availability and trade attribution are unchanged.
    assert (first["available_ts_ms"], second["available_ts_ms"]) == (2001, 3001)
    assert [t[:3] for t in feed.monitor.trades[PID]] == [(2500, 11, 1)]
    assert feed.monitor.times[PID] == [2500]


def test_earlier_knowledge_is_not_rewritten_by_a_later_barrier():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)  # Merged [1000,3000), known at 3001.
    before = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)
    rows = [dict(p) for p in feed.monitor.proofs]
    _fail(feed, 3000, "audit_transport_error")
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    feed.hb(4000, 11)
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001) == before
    assert before["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert [dict(p) for p in feed.monitor.proofs[: len(rows)]] == rows


def test_barrier_on_another_connection_id_also_separates_epochs():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.ack(2000, connection=2)
    feed.send(2000, "audit_parse_error", pid=None, connection=2)
    feed.ack(2000, connection=2)
    feed.hb(2000, 11, connection=2)
    feed.trade(2500, 11, connection=2)
    feed.hb(3000, 11, connection=2)
    assert merged(feed) == []
