===== BEGIN SOURCE 88 | tests/test_instant_coverage_continuity.py | sha256=81ec75d7f77a30dc077ba586b70ba4698fb25aba69ffb022be3229f90e3b9a06 =====
"""Continuity epochs: no merge or assessment chain crosses a lifecycle barrier.

Codex's counterexample (docs/research/RECONCILIATION_BOUNDARY_REVIEW_20261104.md
on codex/instant-radar-20261028) first, then every barrier kind at a shared
millisecond on one reused connection ID, valid recovery on each side, healthy
repeated acknowledgements, ordinary races without a barrier and known-at
immutability. Synthetic only.
"""

import copy
import random

import pytest

from test_instant_coverage import PID, Feed, check
from instant_coverage import MERGED_SCOPE


def merged(feed):
    return [p for p in feed.monitor.proofs if p["scope"] == MERGED_SCOPE]


def intervals(feed):
    return [p for p in feed.monitor.proofs if p["scope"] != MERGED_SCOPE]


def started():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    return feed


def _audit(feed, ts):
    feed.send(ts, "audit_parse_error")


def _transport(feed, ts):
    feed.send(ts, "error")


def _closed(feed, ts):
    feed.send(ts, "connection_closed")


def _queue(feed, ts):
    feed.send(ts, "queue_overflow")


def _lost_subscription(feed, ts):
    # Membership in a required channel is lost and re-added, same connection ID.
    feed.ack(ts, channels=[dict(name="matches", product_ids=[])])


BARRIERS = dict(
    audit_parse_error=_audit,
    transport_error=_transport,
    connection_closed=_closed,
    queue_overflow=_queue,
    lost_subscription=_lost_subscription,
)


def _across(barrier):
    """Codex's tape, with `barrier` in place of the audit failure."""
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # First interval fails: ID 11 missing.
    BARRIERS[barrier](feed, 2000)
    feed.ack(2000)  # New complete acknowledgement, same connection ID.
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # New baseline.
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)  # Second interval: ID 11 outside its watermarks.
    return feed


def test_codex_counterexample_audit_failure_at_a_shared_millisecond():
    feed = _across("audit_parse_error")
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    assert (first["end_ts_ms"], first["upper_trade_id_inclusive"]) == (
        second["start_ts_ms"],
        second["lower_trade_id_exclusive"],
    )
    assert first["connection_id"] == second["connection_id"] == 1
    assert merged(feed) == []
    result = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is False
    assert result["receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms"] is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("barrier", sorted(BARRIERS))
def test_no_merge_across_any_barrier_at_a_shared_millisecond(barrier):
    feed = _across(barrier)
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    assert first["continuity_epoch"] < second["continuity_epoch"]
    assert merged(feed) == []
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    # Each side alone keeps its own honest failure.
    assert (
        check(feed, 1000, 2000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_reason"]
        == "missing_live_match_ids"
    )
    assert (
        check(feed, 2000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_reason"]
        == "receipt_ids_outside_watermarks"
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("barrier", sorted(BARRIERS))
def test_assess_never_joins_valid_intervals_across_a_barrier(barrier):
    feed = started()
    feed.trade(1500, 11)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Verified.
    BARRIERS[barrier](feed, 2000)
    feed.ack(2000)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 12)
    feed.hb(3000, 12)  # Verified, touching time and watermark, same ID.
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    assert first["verified"] and second["verified"]
    assert first["end_ts_ms"] == second["start_ts_ms"]
    assert first["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] == second["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
    joined = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)
    assert joined["receipt_id_check_passed"] is False
    assert joined["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"
    # Valid recovery on each side is still usable on its own.
    assert check(feed, 1000, 2000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert check(feed, 2000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize("barrier", sorted(BARRIERS))
def test_races_reconcile_on_each_side_of_a_barrier(barrier):
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)  # Race before: reconciles within epoch.
    BARRIERS[barrier](feed, 3000)
    feed.ack(3000)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    feed.hb(4000, 12)
    feed.trade(4500, 12)
    feed.hb(5000, 12)  # Race after: reconciles within the new epoch.
    spans = [(p["start_ts_ms"], p["end_ts_ms"]) for p in merged(feed)]
    assert spans == [(1000, 3000), (3000, 5000)]
    epochs = [p["continuity_epoch"] for p in merged(feed)]
    assert epochs[0] < epochs[1]
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert check(feed, 3000, 5000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 5000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_trade_received_before_a_barrier_is_not_counted_after_it():
    """Attribution follows receipt order: the pre-barrier receipt stays there."""
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 10)  # Verified empty.
    feed.trade(2000, 11)  # Received at 2000, before the barrier.
    _audit(feed, 2000)
    feed.ack(2000)
    feed.hb(2000, 10)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert intervals(feed)[-1]["observed_unique_trade_count"] == 0
    assert intervals(feed)[-1]["reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"
    assert feed.monitor.trades[PID][0][:2] == (2000, 11)
    assert feed.monitor.seen[(PID, 11)][1] == 2000


def test_healthy_repeated_acknowledgement_is_not_a_barrier():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.ack(2000)  # Same full membership: continuity is retained.
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    assert first["continuity_epoch"] == second["continuity_epoch"]
    assert [p["merged_proof_count"] for p in merged(feed)] == [2]
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


def test_acknowledgement_for_other_products_only_is_not_a_barrier():
    other = "OTHER-USD"
    feed = Feed(products=(PID, other))
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    # Loses OTHER only; TEST-USD keeps full membership.
    feed.ack(
        2000,
        channels=[
            dict(name="ticker", product_ids=[PID, other]),
            dict(name="matches", product_ids=[PID]),
            dict(name="heartbeat", product_ids=[PID, other]),
        ],
    )
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert [p["merged_proof_count"] for p in merged(feed)] == [2]
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


def test_ordinary_same_millisecond_race_without_a_barrier_reconciles():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2000, 11)  # Same millisecond, after the heartbeat.
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert [p["merged_proof_count"] for p in merged(feed)] == [2]
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


def test_barrier_does_not_change_known_at_answers_or_records():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    before_records = copy.deepcopy(feed.monitor.proofs)
    before = [check(feed, 1000, 2000, asof) for asof in (2000, 2001, 2500)]
    _audit(feed, 2000)
    feed.ack(2000)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert feed.monitor.proofs[: len(before_records)] == before_records
    assert [check(feed, 1000, 2000, asof) for asof in (2000, 2001, 2500)] == before
    assert all(p["available_ts_ms"] >= p["end_ts_ms"] + 1 for p in intervals(feed))


def test_epoch_is_per_product_for_a_lost_subscription():
    other = "OTHER-USD"
    feed = Feed(products=(PID, other))
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(1000, 50, pid=other)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.hb(2000, 51, pid=other)
    feed.ack(
        2000,
        channels=[
            dict(name="ticker", product_ids=[PID, other]),
            dict(name="matches", product_ids=[other]),
            dict(name="heartbeat", product_ids=[PID, other]),
        ],
    )
    feed.ack(2000)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 51, pid=other)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 51, pid=other)
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert feed.monitor.assess(other, 1000, 3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def _random_barrier_tape(seed):
    """Safety property: no proof or passing chain contains a barrier receipt."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    feed = started()
    now, watermark, barriers = 1000, 10, []
    for _ in range(rng.randint(6, 30)):
        now += rng.choice((0, 500, 1000))
        if rng.random() < 0.2:
            name = rng.choice(sorted(BARRIERS))
            BARRIERS[name](feed, now)
            barriers.append((feed.seq, now))
            feed.ack(now)
            feed.hb(now, watermark)
            continue
        for _ in range(rng.choice((0, 1, 2))):
            watermark += 1
            if rng.random() < 0.7:
                feed.trade(now, watermark)
        feed.hb(now, watermark)
    for proof in feed.monitor.proofs:
        inside = [
            seq
            for seq, _ in barriers
            if proof["start_heartbeat_seq"] < seq < proof["end_heartbeat_seq"]
        ]
        assert inside == [], proof
    edges = sorted(
        {p["start_ts_ms"] for p in feed.monitor.proofs}
        | {p["end_ts_ms"] for p in feed.monitor.proofs}
    )
    passes = 0
    for start in edges:
        for end in edges:
            if (
                end > start
                and check(feed, start, end, now + 1)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
            ):
                passes += 1
                assert not any(start < ts < end for _, ts in barriers)
    return passes


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(200))
def test_randomized_barriers_never_join_epochs(seed):
    _random_barrier_tape(seed)


def test_randomized_barrier_tapes_still_certify_within_epochs():
    assert sum(_random_barrier_tape(seed) for seed in range(200)) > 500


def test_contract_records_continuity_barriers_with_limits_unchanged():
    import json

    import instant_coverage

    cfg = json.loads(instant_coverage.CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    merge = cfg["reconciliation"]
    assert "continuity_barriers" in merge
    assert (merge["max_merged_proofs"], merge["max_merged_span_ms"]) == (4, 10000)
    assert cfg["feed_gate_pass"] is False
    assert cfg["live_integration"] is False

===== BEGIN SOURCE 89 | tests/test_instant_coverage_reconciliation.py | sha256=a5aa4a3d84a540caf157fc461572ca6ae6b61cb217cd98afcafe43ee044c8d64 =====
"""Coverage contract amendment 1: bounded merged reconciliation. Synthetic only."""

import copy
import gzip
import json
import random
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from test_instant_coverage import Feed, PID, check
import instant_coverage
from instant_coverage import MERGED_SCOPE, CoverageMonitor


def started(lag=0, first_id=10):
    feed = Feed(lag=lag)
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, first_id)
    return feed


def merged(feed):
    return [p for p in feed.monitor.proofs if p["scope"] == MERGED_SCOPE]


def intervals(feed):
    return [p for p in feed.monitor.proofs if p["scope"] != MERGED_SCOPE]


def overtaken():
    """A heartbeat names trade 11 before the match arrives; nothing is lost."""
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    return feed


def test_heartbeat_overtaking_its_match_reconciles_with_exact_ids():
    feed = overtaken()
    assert [p["reason"] for p in intervals(feed)] == [
        "missing_live_match_ids",
        "receipt_ids_outside_watermarks",
    ]
    proof = merged(feed)[0]
    assert (proof["start_ts_ms"], proof["end_ts_ms"]) == (1000, 3000)
    assert proof["lower_trade_id_exclusive"] == 10
    assert proof["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] == 11
    assert proof["observed_unique_trade_count"] == proof["expected_trade_count"] == 1
    assert proof["merged_proof_count"] == 2
    result = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert result["receipt_id_check_merged_proofs"] == 1


def test_match_arriving_before_a_lagging_heartbeat_reconciles():
    feed = started()
    feed.trade(1500, 11)
    feed.hb(2000, 10)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert [p["reason"] for p in intervals(feed)] == [
        "receipt_ids_outside_watermarks",
        "missing_live_match_ids",
    ]
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


def test_truly_lost_id_still_fails_even_with_a_race_beside_it():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 12)  # Names 11 and 12.
    feed.trade(2500, 12)  # 11 never arrives.
    feed.hb(3000, 12)
    assert merged(feed) == []
    result = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is False
    assert result["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"


def test_extra_unexpected_id_never_reconciles():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2400, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 99)  # Outside every watermark in the run.
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert merged(feed) == []
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_wrong_id_cannot_stand_in_for_a_lost_one_with_a_matching_count():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Names 11, which is lost.
    feed.trade(2500, 99)  # One ID arrives, so the counts agree but the sets do not.
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert merged(feed) == []
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_merged_proof_is_known_only_after_the_closing_heartbeat_finishes():
    feed = started(lag=100)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    before_close = check(feed, 1000, 2000, 2100)
    assert before_close["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    # Later knowledge cannot change what was known earlier.
    assert check(feed, 1000, 2000, 2100) == before_close
    assert (
        check(feed, 1000, 2000, 3099)["receipt_id_check_reason"]
        == "missing_live_match_ids"
    )
    result = check(feed, 1000, 2000, 3100)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert result["receipt_id_check_available_ts_ms"] == 3100  # Not 2100.


def test_failed_records_stay_unmodified_and_trade_attribution_does_not_move():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    failed = copy.deepcopy(intervals(feed))
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    assert intervals(feed)[0] == failed[0]
    assert intervals(feed)[0]["verified"] is False
    assert feed.monitor.trades[PID] == [(2500, 11, 1, feed.monitor.epochs[PID])]
    assert feed.monitor.seen[(PID, 11)][1] == 2500


def _race_of(size):
    """`size` adjacent failed proofs: IDs named one per second, all received last."""
    feed = started()
    last = 10 + size - 1
    for step, trade_id in enumerate(range(11, last + 1)):
        feed.hb(2000 + step * 1000, trade_id)
    close = 1000 + size * 1000
    for trade_id in range(11, last + 1):
        feed.trade(close - 500, trade_id)
    feed.hb(close, last)
    return feed, close


@pytest.mark.parametrize("size", [2, 3, 4])
def test_runs_up_to_the_proof_cap_reconcile(size):
    feed, close = _race_of(size)
    assert [p["merged_proof_count"] for p in merged(feed)] == [size]
    assert check(feed, 1000, close, close + 1)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


def test_run_beyond_the_proof_cap_stays_vetoed():
    assert instant_coverage.MAX_MERGED_PROOFS == 4
    feed, close = _race_of(5)
    assert merged(feed) == []
    assert len(intervals(feed)) == 5
    assert not any(p["verified"] for p in intervals(feed))
    assert not check(feed, 1000, close, close + 1)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "step, reconciled",
    [(3000, True), (5000, False)],
)
def test_span_limit_is_enforced_separately_from_the_proof_cap(step, reconciled):
    """Two 3s intervals span 6s (allowed); three 5s intervals span 15s (refused)."""
    assert instant_coverage.MAX_MERGED_SPAN_MS == 10000
    feed = started()
    count = 2 if reconciled else 3
    for index in range(count - 1):
        feed.hb(1000 + (index + 1) * step, 11 + index)
    close = 1000 + count * step
    for trade_id in range(11, 11 + count - 1):
        feed.trade(close - 100, trade_id)
    feed.hb(close, 11 + count - 2)
    assert bool(merged(feed)) is reconciled
    assert len(intervals(feed)) == count


def test_id_span_limit_applies_to_the_merged_span(monkeypatch):
    feed = started()
    monkeypatch.setattr(instant_coverage, "MAX_ID_SPAN", 3)
    feed.hb(2000, 13)  # Delta 3: within the per-pair bound, missing.
    for trade_id in (11, 12, 13, 14, 15):
        feed.trade(2500, trade_id)
    feed.hb(3000, 15)  # Merged delta 5 exceeds it.
    assert intervals(feed)[0]["reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"
    assert merged(feed) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "cause, reason",
    [
        ("gap", "heartbeat_gap"),
        ("regression", "watermark_regression"),
    ],
)
def test_structural_failures_never_join_a_run(cause, reason):
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Missing.
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    if cause == "gap":
        feed.hb(7001, 11)
    else:
        feed.hb(3000, 9)
    assert intervals(feed)[-1]["reason"] == reason
    assert merged(feed) == []
    # A later race next to the structural failure cannot reach across it.
    close = 8000 if cause == "gap" else 4000
    feed.hb(close, intervals(feed)[-1]["upper_trade_id_inclusive"])
    assert merged(feed) == []


def test_verified_interval_breaks_a_run():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Missing: trade 11 is lost.
    feed.hb(3000, 11)  # Verified empty interval.
    feed.hb(4000, 12)  # Missing.
    feed.trade(4500, 12)
    feed.trade(4600, 11)  # Too late: separated by a verified interval.
    feed.hb(5000, 12)
    assert all(
        p["start_ts_ms"] >= 3000 for p in merged(feed)
    ), "must not merge across the verified interval"
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 2000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_merging_never_crosses_a_connection():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Missing on connection 1.
    feed.ack(2100, connection=2)
    feed.hb(2200, 11, connection=2)
    feed.trade(2300, 11, connection=2)
    feed.hb(2400, 11, connection=2)  # Outside on connection 2.
    assert merged(feed) == []
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 2000, 2401)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_merging_never_crosses_a_connection_even_at_a_shared_millisecond():
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Missing on connection 1.
    feed.ack(2000, connection=2)
    feed.hb(2000, 11, connection=2)
    feed.trade(2500, 11, connection=2)
    feed.hb(3000, 11, connection=2)  # Outside on connection 2.
    assert [p["connection_id"] for p in intervals(feed)] == [1, 2]
    assert merged(feed) == []


def _lose_subscription(feed, ts):
    feed.ack(ts, channels=[dict(name="matches", product_ids=[])])
    feed.ack(ts)


def test_merging_never_spans_a_subscription_gap():
    """Same connection and continuous watermarks, but unobserved receipt time."""
    feed = started()
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Missing.
    _lose_subscription(feed, 2100)
    feed.hb(2300, 11)  # New baseline after the gap.
    feed.trade(2400, 11)
    feed.hb(2500, 11)  # Outside.
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    assert first["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] == second["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
    assert first["end_ts_ms"] != second["start_ts_ms"]
    assert merged(feed) == []


def test_merging_requires_continuous_watermarks():
    """A same-ms baseline reset skips IDs, even when every ID was received."""
    feed = started()
    for trade_id in (11, 12, 13, 14, 16, 17, 18, 19, 20):
        feed.trade(1500, trade_id)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)  # Outside.
    _lose_subscription(feed, 2000)
    feed.hb(2000, 20)  # New baseline jumps past 12-20.
    feed.trade(2500, 15)
    feed.hb(3000, 20)  # Outside.
    first, second = intervals(feed)
    assert (first["end_ts_ms"], first["reason"]) == (2000, second["reason"])
    assert first["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] != second["lower_trade_id_exclusive"]
    assert merged(feed) == []


def test_known_conflicting_duplicate_still_vetoes_a_reconciled_span():
    feed = overtaken()
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    feed.send(3100, "match", trade_id=11, price="100", size="3", side="sell")
    result = check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3101)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "conflicting_trade_id"
    assert check(feed, 1000, 3000, 3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_two_consecutive_races_stay_separate_and_join_continuously():
    feed = overtaken()
    feed.hb(4000, 12)
    feed.trade(4500, 12)
    feed.hb(5000, 12)
    assert [
        (p["start_ts_ms"], p["end_ts_ms"], p["merged_proof_count"])
        for p in merged(feed)
    ] == [(1000, 3000, 2), (3000, 5000, 2)]
    result = check(feed, 1000, 5000, 5001)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert result["receipt_id_check_merged_proofs"] == 2


def test_a_failed_proof_cannot_be_claimed_by_two_merged_proofs():
    feed = overtaken()
    seen = [s for p in merged(feed) for s in p["supersedes_end_heartbeat_seqs"]]
    feed.hb(4000, 12)
    feed.hb(5000, 12)  # Fresh failures after the merged pair.
    claimed = [s for p in merged(feed) for s in p["supersedes_end_heartbeat_seqs"]]
    assert claimed == seen
    assert len(claimed) == len(set(claimed))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(200))
def test_randomized_tape_never_certifies_a_session_with_a_lost_id(seed):
    """Safety property: a whole-session pass implies every named ID was received."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    feed = started()
    now, watermark = 1000, 10
    pending, delivered, lost = [], set(), set()
    for _ in range(rng.randint(6, 40)):
        for _ in range(rng.choice((0, 0, 1, 1, 2))):
            watermark += 1
            if rng.random() < 0.03:
                lost.add(watermark)
            else:
                pending.append(watermark)
        now += 1000
        late = [t for t in pending if rng.random() < 0.2]
        for offset, trade_id in enumerate(t for t in pending if t not in late):
            feed.trade(now - 500 + offset, trade_id)
            delivered.add(trade_id)
        pending = late
        feed.hb(now, watermark)
    for offset, trade_id in enumerate(pending):
        feed.trade(now + 100 + offset, trade_id)
        delivered.add(trade_id)
    now += 1000
    feed.hb(now, watermark)
    result = check(feed, 1000, now, now + 1)
    if result["receipt_id_check_passed"]:
        assert not lost
        assert delivered == set(range(11, watermark + 1))
    elif not lost:
        # Without a loss, only a limit or the disjoint-merge rule may veto, which
        # always leaves a failed interval on record. A clean tape must pass.
        assert any(not p["verified"] for p in intervals(feed))
    claimed = [s for p in merged(feed) for s in p["supersedes_end_heartbeat_seqs"]]
    assert len(claimed) == len(set(claimed))
    for proof in merged(feed):
        assert proof["merged_proof_count"] <= instant_coverage.MAX_MERGED_PROOFS
        span = proof["end_ts_ms"] - proof["start_ts_ms"]
        assert span <= instant_coverage.MAX_MERGED_SPAN_MS
        received = {
            tid
            for ts, tid, *_ in feed.monitor.trades[PID]
            if proof["start_ts_ms"] <= ts < proof["end_ts_ms"]
        }
        expected = range(
            proof["lower_trade_id_exclusive"] + 1,
            proof["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] + 1,
        )
        assert received == set(expected)


def test_race_nested_around_a_merged_pair_stays_vetoed_by_design():
    """All IDs arrive, but merges stay disjoint: a deliberate fail-closed veto."""
    feed = started()
    feed.trade(1500, 11)
    feed.hb(2000, 12)  # Names 11, 12; 12 is late until the last interval.
    feed.trade(2500, 13)
    feed.hb(3000, 14)  # Names 13, 14; 14 is late by one interval.
    feed.trade(3500, 14)
    feed.hb(4000, 14)  # Closes the inner pair.
    assert [p["merged_proof_count"] for p in merged(feed)] == [2]
    feed.trade(4500, 12)
    feed.hb(5000, 14)  # Only the whole four-proof span reconciles now.
    assert len(intervals(feed)) == 4
    assert [p["merged_proof_count"] for p in merged(feed)] == [2]
    assert not check(feed, 1000, 5000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    # The inner pair remains proven on its own.
    assert check(feed, 2000, 4000, 5001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]


def test_products_reconcile_independently():
    other = "OTHER-USD"
    feed = Feed(products=(PID, other))
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.hb(1000, 50, pid=other)
    feed.hb(2000, 11)
    feed.hb(2000, 51, pid=other)  # 51 is lost.
    feed.trade(2500, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 11)
    feed.hb(3000, 51, pid=other)
    assert feed.monitor.assess(PID, 1000, 3000, asof=3001)["receipt_id_check_passed"]
    assert not feed.monitor.assess(other, 1000, 3000, asof=3001)[
        "receipt_id_check_passed"
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "constant, value",
    [
        ("MAX_MERGED_PROOFS", 5),
        ("MAX_MERGED_SPAN_MS", 20000),
        ("MERGEABLE_REASONS", ("missing_live_match_ids",)),
    ],
)
def test_implementation_refuses_limits_that_differ_from_the_contract(
    monkeypatch, constant, value
):
    monkeypatch.setattr(instant_coverage, constant, value)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="contract and implementation differ"):
        CoverageMonitor((PID,))


def test_contract_states_the_limits_and_stays_gated():
    cfg = json.loads(instant_coverage.CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    merge = cfg["reconciliation"]
    assert merge["max_merged_proofs"] == 4
    assert merge["max_merged_span_ms"] == 10000
    assert "heartbeat_gap" in merge["never_merges"]
    assert cfg["version"].endswith("amendment-1")
    assert cfg["feed_gate_pass"] is False
    assert cfg["fitting_allowed"] is False
    assert cfg["live_integration"] is False


def test_cli_report_separates_interval_merged_and_unreconciled_counts(tmp_path):
    feed = overtaken()
    feed.hb(4000, 12)  # 12 is lost: this failure stays unreconciled.
    source = tmp_path / "in" / "packets.jsonl.gz"
    source.parent.mkdir()
    with gzip.open(source, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        for event in feed.events:
            stream.write(json.dumps(event) + "\n")
    output = tmp_path / "out"
    script = Path(instant_coverage.__file__)
    subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-B", str(script), str(source)]
        + ["--products", PID, "--out-dir", str(output)],
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
    )
    report = json.loads((output / "REPORT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert report["proof_count"] == 3
    assert report["verified_proofs"] == 0
    assert report["merged_proofs"] == 1
    assert report["failed_proofs_reconciled"] == 2
    assert report["failed_proofs_unreconciled"] == 1
    assert report["feed_gate_pass"] is False

"""Independent Claude review probes for the coverage monitor and paired samples (4ed9045).

Synthetic only; no fitting, new capture, live hook or outcome evaluation.
"""

import pytest

from test_instant_coverage import Feed, PID, check, simple
from micro_replay import ReplayStore
from instant_samples import paired_sample


def two_sessions_touching(close=False):
    """Trades 12-20 execute while no session can see them; the sessions share one ms."""
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.trade(1200, 11)
    feed.hb(1500, 11)
    if close:
        feed.send(1500, "connection_closed")
    feed.ack(1500, connection=2)
    feed.hb(1500, 20, connection=2)
    feed.hb(2000, 20, connection=2)
    return feed


@pytest.mark.parametrize("close", [False, True])
def test_proofs_join_only_when_connection_and_watermark_continue(close):
    feed = two_sessions_touching(close)
    proofs = feed.monitor.proofs
    assert [(p["end_ts_ms"], p["verified"]) for p in proofs] == [
        (1500, True),
        (2000, True),
    ]
    assert proofs[0]["upper_trade_id_inclusive"] == 11
    assert proofs[1]["lower_trade_id_exclusive"] == 20
    result = check(feed)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is False
    assert result["receipt_id_check_reason"] == "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"
    # Each session's own interval is still individually proven.
    assert check(feed, 1000, 1500, 2001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    assert check(feed, 1500, 2000, 2001)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


def test_contiguous_heartbeats_on_one_connection_still_join():
    feed = simple()
    feed.hb(2500, 11)
    assert check(feed, 1000, 2500, 2501)["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True


def test_old_session_receipt_cannot_satisfy_new_session_id_check():
    """The contract scopes the check to one connection, including at a shared ms."""
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack(900)
    feed.hb(1000, 10)
    feed.trade(1500, 11)  # Received only on the old connection.
    feed.ack(1500, connection=2)
    feed.hb(1500, 10, connection=2)
    feed.hb(2000, 11, connection=2)
    proof = feed.monitor.proofs[-1]
    assert proof["connection_id"] == 2
    assert proof["verified"] is False
    assert proof["reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "marker, fields",
    [
        ("audit_transport_error", dict(error="reset", error_type="OSError")),
        ("audit_parse_error", dict(raw="{not json")),
    ],
)
def test_real_recorder_failure_markers_invalidate_transport(marker, fields):
    """scripts/coinbase_feed_audit.py writes these names; they carry no product_id."""
    feed = simple()
    feed.send(2100, marker, pid=None, **fields)
    result = feed.monitor.assess(PID, 1000, 2000, asof=2101)
    assert result["transport_observed_ok"] is False
    assert result["transport_reason"] == marker
    # Evidence already completed is not rewritten, but no new pair can straddle it.
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] is True
    feed.hb(2500, 11)
    assert not any(p["end_ts_ms"] == 2500 for p in feed.monitor.proofs)
    assert feed.monitor.assess(PID, 2000, 2500, asof=2501)[
        "receipt_id_check_reason"
    ] == ("trade_coverage_not_yet_proven")


def test_real_recorder_failure_markers_require_a_new_ack_and_baseline():
    feed = simple()
    feed.send(2100, "audit_transport_error", pid=None, error="x", error_type="E")
    feed.ack(2200)
    feed.hb(2300, 11)
    feed.hb(2800, 11)
    result = feed.monitor.assess(PID, 2300, 2800, asof=2801)
    assert result["receipt_id_check_passed"] and result["transport_observed_ok"]


def store_with_health(tmp_path):
    """One coin + BTC store with verified health known by the 10,000ms cutoff."""
    store = ReplayStore(tmp_path / "replay.sqlite", create=True)
    seq = 0
    for pid in (PID, "BTC-USD"):
        seq += 1
        store.ingest(
            dict(
                receipt_seq=seq,
                receipt_ts_ms=9000,
                receipt_monotonic_ns=seq,
                connection_id=1,
                message=dict(
                    type="ticker",
                    product_id=pid,
                    best_bid="100",
                    best_ask="101",
                    best_bid_size="1",
                    best_ask_size="1",
                ),
            )
        )
        store.add_health(
            product=pid,
            start=5000,
            end=10000,
            available=10000,
            verified=True,
            evidence="synthetic known-at-cutoff assertion",
        )
    return store


def test_late_negative_evidence_cannot_change_the_decision_time_sample(tmp_path):
    """Documents the open grading question: correct for inputs, unresolved for labels."""
    store = store_with_health(tmp_path)
    store.add_health(
        product=PID,
        start=5000,
        end=10000,
        available=10500,
        verified=False,
        evidence="synthetic later discovery of bad coverage",
    )
    store.finalize(dict(input_sha256={}, recorder_sha256="x", feed_gate_pass=False))
    paired = paired_sample(store, PID, 10000)
    assert paired.sample is not None
    assert paired.sample.coverage_verified is True
    assert store.coverage(PID, 5000, 10000, 10500) == (False, 10500)
    store.close()


def test_sample_availability_reflects_health_not_row_processing(tmp_path):
    """The store records no processing time, so the row's own availability is not measured."""
    store = store_with_health(tmp_path)
    store.finalize(dict(input_sha256={}, recorder_sha256="x", feed_gate_pass=False))
    paired = paired_sample(store, PID, 10000)
    assert paired.sample.available_ts_ms == 10000
    columns = {row[1] for row in store.db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(quotes)")}
    assert not any("process" in c or "available" in c for c in columns)
    store.close()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 91 | tests/test_instant_delayed_decision.py | sha256=f1b82e7ac390775392dcb51db1f1c46f6b0c86c1cab2270357a681d79abd6922 =====
"""Synthetic late-decision and independent grading-clock tests."""

from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError
import json
import math

import pytest

from test_instant_timed_features import CUTOFF, COIN, BTC, feature_tape
from instant_delayed_decision import (
    grade_publication,
    observe_publication,
    prepare_decision,
)


def start(tape, *, asof=CUTOFF + 180, direction="up"):
    decision = prepare_decision(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, direction, asof=asof)
    tape.now = CUTOFF + 260
    publication = observe_publication(
        tape.rec, decision, publication_id="synthetic-1", published=CUTOFF + 230
    )
    return decision, publication


def outcome(tape, horizon=300, *, blank_btc_close=False):
    end = CUTOFF + horizon * 1000
    watermark = tape.rec.trades[-1].trade_id
    ids = []
    for ts in range(CUTOFF, end, 5000):
        if len(ids) < 3:
            watermark += 1
            ids.append(watermark)
            tape.trade(ts + 1000, watermark)
        mid = 110 + 40 * (ts + 5000 - CUTOFF) / (end - CUTOFF)
        tape.send(
            ts + 2000,
            "snapshot",
            COIN,
            bids=[[str(mid - 1), "3"]],
            asks=[[str(mid + 1), "1"]],
        )
        if not blank_btc_close or ts not in (end - 5000, end - 10000):
            tape.send(
                ts + 2000, "snapshot", BTC, bids=[["50999", "3"]], asks=[["51001", "1"]]
            )
        tape.hb(ts + 5000, watermark)
        tape.hb(ts + 5000, 10, BTC)
    # Exact right boundary also contributes no forward match.
    tape.trade(end, watermark + 1)
    tape.now = end + 1
    return end, ids


def test_freezes_actual_feature_timing_and_binds_observed_publication():
    tape = feature_tape()
    decision, publication = start(tape)
    row = json.loads(decision.feature_json)
    assert row["features"]["F2"]["evidence_available_ts_ms"] == CUTOFF + 180
    assert decision.knowledge_asof_ts_ms == CUTOFF + 180
    assert decision.feature_computation_completed_ts_ms == CUTOFF + 180
    assert decision.decision_completed_ts_ms == CUTOFF + 180
    assert publication.timing.published_ts_ms == CUTOFF + 230
    assert publication.timing.feature_age_at_publication_ms == 230
    assert publication.timing.legacy_cutoff_inputs_compatible is False
    assert publication.observation_recorded_ts_ms == CUTOFF + 260
    assert publication.durable_publication_verified is False
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        decision.feature_json = "{}"
    row["features"].clear()
    assert json.loads(decision.feature_json)["features"]["F2"]["status"] == "ready"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("published", "error"),
    [(CUTOFF + 179, "causal"), (CUTOFF + 181, "has not been reached")],
)
def test_rejects_impossible_publication_clock(published, error):
    tape = feature_tape()
    decision = prepare_decision(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, "up", asof=CUTOFF + 180)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=error):
        observe_publication(tape.rec, decision, publication_id="x", published=published)


def test_rejects_a_different_tape_with_the_same_plan():
    decision, publication = start(feature_tape())
    other = feature_tape(unknown_side=True)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="evidence prefix differ"):
        observe_publication(
            other.rec, decision, publication_id="x", published=CUTOFF + 180
        )
    other.now = CUTOFF + 1000
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="evidence prefix differ"):
        grade_publication(other.rec, publication, 300, asof=other.now)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("horizon", "direction", "success"), [(300, "up", True), (900, "down", False)]
)
def test_maturity_horizon_direction_and_open_forward_trade_bounds(
    horizon, direction, success
):
    tape = feature_tape()
    decision, publication = start(tape, direction=direction)
    end, _ = outcome(tape, horizon)
    pending = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, horizon, asof=end - 1)
    assert pending["status"] == "pending"
    assert pending["cutoff_verified_forward"] is None
    closed = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, horizon, asof=end)
    assert closed["status"] == "unavailable"
    assert closed["cutoff_verified_forward"] is None
    grade = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, horizon, asof=end + 1)
    assert grade["status"] == "ready"
    assert grade["cutoff_verified_forward"] is success
    assert grade["forward_match_count"] == 3
    assert grade["terminal_residual_log"] == pytest.approx(
        math.log(150 / 110) * (1 if direction == "up" else -1)
    )
    assert grade["horizon_end_ts_ms"] == CUTOFF + horizon * 1000
    assert grade["decision_sha256"] == decision.sha256


@pytest.mark.parametrize("decision_conflict", [False, True])
def test_late_conflict_vetoes_grading_without_rewriting_the_decision(decision_conflict):
    tape = feature_tape()
    decision, publication = start(tape)
    frozen = decision.sha256
    end, ids = outcome(tape)
    trade_id = (
        next(t.trade_id for t in tape.rec.trades if t.receipt_ts_ms == CUTOFF - 30000)
        if decision_conflict
        else ids[0]
    )
    assert grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, 300, asof=end + 1)[
        "cutoff_verified_forward"
    ]
    ts = end + 2000
    tape.trade(ts, trade_id, size="250", steps=[ts, ts, ts, ts, ts + 90])
    partial = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, 300, asof=ts + 1)
    assert partial["reason"] == "grading_payload_processing_incomplete"
    later = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, 300, asof=ts + 90)
    assert later["status"] == "unavailable"
    assert later["reason"] == "conflicting_trade_id"
    assert later["evidence_scope"] == (
        "decision_trade_window" if decision_conflict else "outcome_trade_window"
    )
    assert later["cutoff_verified_forward"] is None
    prior = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, 300, asof=end + 1)
    assert prior["cutoff_verified_forward"] is True
    assert decision.sha256 == frozen
    assert (
        later["grading_packet_prefix_sha256"] != prior["grading_packet_prefix_sha256"]
    )


def test_later_evidence_cannot_fill_a_feature_missing_from_the_decision():
    tape = feature_tape()
    decision, publication = start(tape, asof=CUTOFF + 1)
    end, _ = outcome(tape)
    grade = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, 300, asof=end + 1)
    assert grade["reason"] == "decision_features_unavailable"
    assert grade["missing_features"] == ["F2"]
    assert (
        json.loads(decision.feature_json)["features"]["F2"]["status"] == "unavailable"
    )
    assert grade["cutoff_verified_forward"] is None


def test_missing_benchmark_close_is_ungradable():
    tape = feature_tape()
    _, publication = start(tape)
    end, _ = outcome(tape, blank_btc_close=True)
    grade = grade_publication(tape.rec, publication, 300, asof=end + 1)
    assert grade["reason"] == "stale_book_observation"
    assert grade["missing_quote_ts_ms"] == end
    assert grade["product_with_missing_evidence"] == BTC
    assert grade["cutoff_verified_forward"] is None

===== BEGIN SOURCE 92 | tests/test_instant_delayed_eligibility.py | sha256=bb87a4fe25b84df80df38735d60cc830f4c2ad6236f7a7b01334d75353e5852d =====
"""Delayed readiness is scoped, keeps missing rows, and does not imply delivery."""

from dataclasses import replace

import pytest

from test_instant_timed_features import BTC, COIN, CUTOFF, feature_tape
from instant_delayed_decision import prepare_decision
from instant_delayed_eligibility import LocalDelivery, assess_cohort


def test_proof_arrival_changes_a_new_view_without_rewriting_an_earlier_decision():
    tape = feature_tape()
    early = CUTOFF + 1
    late = CUTOFF + 180
    before = prepare_decision(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, "up", asof=early)
    ready = prepare_decision(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, "up", asof=late)
    control = prepare_decision(tape.rec, BTC, CUTOFF, "up", asof=late)

    def assess(asof, decisions, **kwargs):
        cohort = assess_cohort(
            tape.rec, CUTOFF, asof=asof, decisions=decisions, **kwargs
        )
        assert cohort["planned_rows"] == 4
        assert not cohort["study_eligible"]
        return {(r["product_id"], r["direction"]): r for r in cohort["rows"]}

    old = assess(early, [before])
    assert (
        old[(COIN, "up")]["feature_reasons"]["F2"] == "trade_row_processing_incomplete"
    )
    assert not old[(COIN, "up")]["complete_vector_candidate"]
    observation = LocalDelivery(ready.sha256, CUTOFF + 240, CUTOFF + 260)
    new = assess(
        late,
        [ready, control],
        local_deliveries={(COIN, "up"): observation},
        deadline_age_ms=250,
    )
    coin = new[(COIN, "up")]
    assert coin["complete_vector_candidate"]
    assert coin["delivery"] == dict(
        status="after_bound", commit_age_ms=240, consumer_age_ms=260
    )
    assert not new[(BTC, "up")]["complete_vector_candidate"]
    assert new[(BTC, "up")]["role"] == "benchmark"
    assert new[(COIN, "down")]["input_status"] == "decision_missing"
    assert new[(BTC, "down")]["delivery"]["status"] == "not_measured"
    tape.now += 5000
    tape.hb(tape.now, 10, BTC)
    assert assess(early, [before]) == old


def test_rejects_duplicate_decisions_and_unbound_delivery():
    tape = feature_tape()
    asof = CUTOFF + 180
    decision = prepare_decision(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, "up", asof=asof)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicated"):
        assess_cohort(tape.rec, CUTOFF, asof=asof, decisions=[decision, decision])
    wrong = LocalDelivery("wrong-digest", CUTOFF + 200, CUTOFF + 210)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not match decision digest"):
        assess_cohort(
            tape.rec,
            CUTOFF,
            asof=asof,
            decisions=[decision],
            local_deliveries={(COIN, "up"): wrong},
        )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="precedes"):
        assess_cohort(
            tape.rec,
            CUTOFF,
            asof=asof,
            decisions=[decision],
            local_deliveries={
                (COIN, "up"): replace(
                    wrong,
                    decision_sha256=decision.sha256,
                    consumer_seen_ts_ms=CUTOFF + 199,
                )
            },
        )

===== BEGIN SOURCE 93 | tests/test_instant_diagnostics.py | sha256=4a05924a3ca50bd6ba1459bac6f6f2410059e85d5e23bfe8c6b258c53f597749 =====
"""Reporting diagnostics preserve label semantics and show missingness explicitly."""

import copy
from dataclasses import replace
import sys

import pytest

from test_instant_targets import ROOT, ANCHOR, target, samples
from test_instant_targets_v2_review import v2_base, wick_rows

sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment"))
import instant_diagnostics as diag


def grade(rows=None):
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(
        rows or samples(), contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH
    )
    return engine, engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)


def test_wick_is_retained_and_forward_peak_gets_a_separate_name():
    engine, label = grade(wick_rows("up"))
    before = copy.deepcopy(label)
    original = engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)
    report = diag.alert_report(engine, label, ANCHOR, strict_trigger=True)
    assert all(report[k] == v for k, v in original.items())
    assert report["timing"] == "late_after_activity_trigger"
    assert report["whole_window_peak_precedes_anchor"] is True
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] == pytest.approx(0.004 / 0.045)
    assert report["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction"] == pytest.approx(
        0.004 / 0.024
    )
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason"] is None
    assert report["diagnostic_version"] == "instant-diagnostics-v1-draft"
    assert label == before


def test_missing_reference_reason_is_preserved_without_erasing_forward_outcome():
    engine, label = grade([r for r in samples() if r.ts_ms != ANCHOR - 30000])
    report = diag.alert_report(engine, label, ANCHOR)
    assert label["verified_forward"] is True
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] is None
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason"] == "missing_grid_point"
    assert (
        report["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction_reason"]
        == "missing_grid_point"
    )


def test_nonpositive_reference_peak_is_diagnostic_not_false_outcome():
    engine, label = grade(
        samples(residual=lambda t: 0.03 if t < 0 else 0.02 * min(t / 300, 1))
    )
    report = diag.alert_report(engine, label, ANCHOR)
    assert label["verified_forward"] is True
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] is None
    assert (
        report["pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason"] == "nonpositive_pre_window_peak"
    )
    assert (
        report["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction_reason"]
        == "nonpositive_pre_window_peak"
    )


def test_fraction_is_not_clamped_and_stale_publication_keeps_its_reason():
    engine, label = grade(wick_rows("up"))
    assert (
        diag.alert_report(engine, label, ANCHOR - 10000)[
            "pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction"
        ]
        > 1
    )
    report = diag.alert_report(engine, label, ANCHOR + 901000)
    assert report["timing"] == "outside_event_window"
    assert report["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction"] is None


def test_report_rejects_wrong_contract_and_forged_evaluator():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        diag.alert_report(target.TargetEvaluator(samples()), {}, ANCHOR)
    engine, label = grade()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        diag.alert_report(engine, {**label, "evaluator_sha256": "other"}, ANCHOR)


def test_unavailable_publication_has_explicit_null_fields_even_in_strict_mode():
    rows = [r for r in wick_rows("up") if r.ts_ms != ANCHOR - 100000]
    engine, label = grade(rows)
    report = diag.alert_report(engine, label, ANCHOR - 100000, strict_trigger=True)
    assert report["timing"] == "unavailable"
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] is None
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason"] == "missing_grid_point"
    assert report["pre_window_to_forward_peak_consumed_fraction"] is None


def test_reference_and_publication_failures_are_reported_independently():
    rows = [
        replace(r, price_source="last_trade") if r.ts_ms == ANCHOR - 15000 else r
        for r in samples()
        if r.ts_ms != ANCHOR - 100000
    ]
    engine, label = grade(rows)
    report = diag.alert_report(engine, label, ANCHOR - 100000)
    assert report["timing"] == "unavailable"
    assert report["pre_window_consumed_fraction_reason"] == "price_fallback"
    assert report["pre_window_reference_unavailable_reason"] == "price_fallback"
    assert report["publication_unavailable_reason"] == "missing_grid_point"


def test_catalog_gap_flag_does_not_change_selection_and_rejects_duplicates():
    _, label = grade()
    selected = target.forward_event_catalog([label])
    missing = diag.forward_catalog_report([label])[0]
    assert missing["anchor_ts_ms"] == selected[0]["anchor_ts_ms"]
    assert missing["left_censored"] is True
    previous = {
        **label,
        "anchor_ts_ms": ANCHOR - 5000,
        "verified_forward": False,
        "status": "ready",
    }
    assert diag.forward_catalog_report([previous, label])[0]["left_censored"] is False
    previous["status"] = "unavailable"
    assert (
        diag.forward_catalog_report([previous, label])[0]["left_censor_reason"]
        == "unavailable"
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate"):
        diag.forward_catalog_report([label, label])

===== BEGIN SOURCE 94 | tests/test_instant_directional_exposure.py | sha256=2a5287538ccba0564cf28c19df517565313b0d947ce8cb746aa8f0a9b6d23ba2 =====
"""Hand-calculated directional denominators and causal eligibility boundaries."""

import copy
import json

import pytest

from test_instant_association import ASOF, B, PRODUCT, label, slot
import instant_association as assoc
import instant_directional_exposure as directional


def eligibility(product=PRODUCT, offset=0, **board_flags):
    return [
        dict(
            slot(product, offset, **board_flags.get(direction, {})),
            direction=direction,
            available_ts_ms=B + offset,
        )
        for direction in assoc.DIRECTIONS
    ]


def report(labels, records=None, **kwargs):
    return directional.exposure_report(
        labels,
        eligibility() if records is None else records,
        asof_ts_ms=kwargs.pop("asof_ts_ms", ASOF),
        **kwargs,
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", assoc.DIRECTIONS)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("horizon", assoc.HORIZONS)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("other_status", ["missing", "pending", "unavailable"])
def test_other_direction_missingness_never_removes_a_ready_board_row(
    direction, horizon, other_status
):
    other = "down" if direction == "up" else "up"
    rows = [label(0, direction=direction, horizon=horizon, verified=False)]
    if other_status != "missing":
        rows.append(label(0, direction=other, horizon=horizon, status=other_status))
    out = report(rows)
    own, opposite = out["boards"][direction], out["boards"][other]
    assert own["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 1
    assert opposite["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 1
    assert own["horizons"][str(horizon)]["gradable_slot_count"] == 1
    assert opposite["horizons"][str(horizon)]["gradable_slot_count"] == 0
    # The existing joint comparator retains its explicitly stricter definition.
    joint = assoc.exposure_report(rows, [slot(PRODUCT, 0)], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    assert joint["horizons"][str(horizon)]["gradable_slot_count"] == 0


def test_product_hours_wall_clock_unions_and_directional_flags_by_hand():
    records = eligibility()
    records += eligibility("B-USD", up={"candidate_eligible": False})
    records += eligibility(offset=5000, down={"warmup_complete": False})
    out = report([], records)
    up, down = out["boards"]["up"], out["boards"]["down"]
    # Up: A@0, A@5. Down: A@0, B@0. Two slots per board.
    assert up["decision_eligible_product_hours"] == pytest.approx(10 / 3600)
    assert down["decision_eligible_product_hours"] == pytest.approx(10 / 3600)
    assert up["board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product"] == pytest.approx(
        10 / 3600
    )
    assert down["board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product"] == pytest.approx(
        5 / 3600
    )
    assert out["wall_clock_hours_with_either_board_eligible"] == pytest.approx(
        10 / 3600
    )
    assert out["universe_size_by_cutoff"] == [[B, 2], [B + 5000, 1]]
    assert up["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"]["candidate_eligible"] == 1
    assert down["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"]["warmup_complete"] == 1
    assert up["horizons"]["300"]["ungradable_reasons"] == {"label_missing": 2}


def test_future_label_changes_do_not_change_decision_exposure():
    before = report([])
    after = report([label(0), label(0, direction="down", verified=False)])
    assert before["universe_size_by_cutoff"] == after["universe_size_by_cutoff"]
    for direction in assoc.DIRECTIONS:
        for key in (
            "recorded_slot_count",
            "decision_eligible_slot_count",
            "decision_eligible_product_hours",
            "board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product",
            "exclusions_by_first_failed_flag",
        ):
            assert before["boards"][direction][key] == after["boards"][direction][key]


def test_identical_ranker_rows_keep_direction_and_natural_coverage_separate():
    rows = [label(offset) for offset in (0, 5000)]
    rows += [label(0, direction="down")]
    records = eligibility() + eligibility(offset=5000)
    out = report(
        rows,
        records,
        ranker_slots={
            "A": [(PRODUCT, "up", B), (PRODUCT, "up", B + 5000)],
            "B": [(PRODUCT, "up", B + 5000), (PRODUCT, "down", B)],
        },
    )
    up = out["boards"]["up"]["horizons"]["300"]["comparison"]
    down = out["boards"]["down"]["horizons"]["300"]["comparison"]
    assert up["identical_complete_rows"] == [[PRODUCT, B + 5000]]
    assert down["identical_complete_rows"] == []
    assert up["natural_ranker_coverage"]["A"]["eligible_scored_slot_count"] == 2
    assert up["natural_ranker_coverage"]["B"]["gradable_scored_slot_count"] == 1
    assert down["natural_ranker_coverage"]["B"]["gradable_scored_slot_count"] == 1


def test_maturity_boundary_is_separate_for_each_horizon():
    rows = [label(0), label(0, horizon=900, status="pending")]
    out = report(rows, asof_ts_ms=B + 300_000)
    horizons = out["boards"]["up"]["horizons"]
    assert horizons["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 1
    assert horizons["900"]["ungradable_reasons"] == {"immature_trailing_horizon": 1}
    assert out["boards"]["up"]["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 1


def test_own_direction_unavailable_retains_reason_without_becoming_failure():
    out = report([label(0, status="unavailable", reason="missing_benchmark")])
    h = out["boards"]["up"]["horizons"]["300"]
    assert h["gradable_slot_count"] == 0
    assert h["ungradable_reasons"] == {"unavailable:missing_benchmark": 1}
    assert h["gradable_fraction_of_eligible"] == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("available", [B - 1, B])
def test_asserted_eligibility_known_by_cutoff_is_accepted(available):
    records = eligibility()
    records[0]["available_ts_ms"] = available
    assert report([], records)["boards"]["up"]["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "field,value", [("available_ts_ms", B + 1), ("ts_ms", ASOF + 5000)]
)
def test_future_eligibility_is_rejected_even_if_labels_are_ready(field, value):
    records = eligibility()
    records[0][field] = value
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="known by its cutoff"):
        report([label(0)], records)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("flag", assoc.ELIGIBILITY_FLAGS)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [None, 0, "true"])
def test_unknown_and_coerced_boolean_flags_are_rejected(flag, value):
    records = eligibility()
    records[0][flag] = value
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="explicit boolean"):
        report([], records)


def test_missing_board_record_is_not_silently_counted_as_excluded():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="both directional"):
        report([], eligibility()[:1])


def test_universe_membership_is_shared_even_when_candidate_flags_differ():
    records = eligibility()
    records[0]["universe_member"] = False
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="share the declared decision universe"):
        report([], records)


def test_duplicate_records_and_duplicate_ranker_rows_are_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate directional eligibility"):
        report([], eligibility() * 2)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate directional ranker"):
        report([], ranker_slots={"A": [(PRODUCT, "up", B)] * 2})


def test_rankers_cannot_add_unrecorded_rows():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="recorded eligibility"):
        report([], ranker_slots={"A": [(PRODUCT, "up", B + 5000)]})


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "field", ["product_id", "direction", "ts_ms", "available_ts_ms"]
)
def test_missing_identity_or_clock_is_rejected(field):
    records = eligibility()
    del records[0][field]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="identity, clocks"):
        report([], records)


def test_ready_labels_cannot_restore_excluded_slots_and_first_flag_is_reported():
    records = eligibility(
        up={"warmup_complete": False, "candidate_eligible": False},
        down={"health_verified": False},
    )
    out = report([label(0), label(0, direction="down")], records)
    assert out["universe_size_by_cutoff"] == [[B, 1]]
    up, down = out["boards"]["up"], out["boards"]["down"]
    assert up["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"]["warmup_complete"] == 1
    assert up["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"]["candidate_eligible"] == 0
    assert down["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"]["health_verified"] == 1
    for board in (up, down):
        assert board["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 0
        assert board["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "change", ["version", "provenance", "duplicate", "float_horizon"]
)
def test_label_validation_remains_identical_to_the_association_contract(change):
    rows = [label(0), label(0, direction="down")]
    if change == "version":
        rows[0]["contract_version"] = "instant-target-v1"
    elif change == "provenance":
        rows[0]["evaluator_sha256"] = "other"
    elif change == "duplicate":
        rows += [copy.deepcopy(rows[0])]
    else:
        rows[0]["horizon_seconds"] = 300.0
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        report(rows)


def test_inputs_are_unchanged_and_report_is_json_serializable():
    rows, records = [label(0)], eligibility()
    frozen = copy.deepcopy((rows, records))
    out = report(rows, records)
    assert (rows, records) == frozen
    json.dumps(out, allow_nan=False)
    assert out["eligibility_producer_implemented"] is False
    assert out["legacy_joint_report_changed"] is False
    assert out["feed_gate_pass"] is False


def test_no_eligible_rows_has_null_graded_fraction_and_zero_exposure():
    out = report([], [])
    assert out["universe_size_by_cutoff"] == []
    assert out["wall_clock_hours_with_either_board_eligible"] == 0
    for board in out["boards"].values():
        assert board["decision_eligible_product_hours"] == 0
        assert board["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_fraction_of_eligible"] is None

===== BEGIN SOURCE 95 | tests/test_instant_directional_exposure_review.py | sha256=f0ae85b338e37debd57043a695dfa9ff3b005db87e6ea7e59520212260e47f7e =====
"""Independent review probes for the opt-in directional exposure report.

Claude review of Codex 9da4987 at tip f561706. Every expected number below is
computed by hand from the directional contract before running the code. The
reporter consumes caller assertions; nothing here authenticates eligibility or
proves the submitted universe is complete, and the probes say so explicitly.
Synthetic only: no market data, outcomes, fitting or live change.
"""

import copy
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from test_instant_association import ASOF, B, label, slot
import instant_association as assoc
import instant_directional_exposure as directional

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
A, Bp, C = "A-USD", "B-USD", "C-USD"
T0, T1, T2, T3 = B, B + 5000, B + 10000, B + 15000
HOUR = 3_600_000


def rec(product, ts, direction, available=None, **flags):
    row = dict(slot(product, ts - B, **flags), direction=direction)
    row["available_ts_ms"] = ts if available is None else available
    return row


def pair(product, ts, up=None, down=None):
    return [
        rec(product, ts, "up", **(up or {})),
        rec(product, ts, "down", **(down or {})),
    ]


def run(labels, records, asof=ASOF, **kw):
    return directional.exposure_report(labels, records, asof_ts_ms=asof, **kw)


def lab(product, ts, direction, horizon=300, **kw):
    return label(ts - B, product=product, direction=direction, horizon=horizon, **kw)


# --- Hand-built universe: three products, four cutoffs, both boards -------
# Up eligible:   A@T0, A@T1, B@T1, C@T3            -> 4 slots, cutoffs {T0,T1,T3}
# Down eligible: B@T0, B@T2, C@T2                  -> 3 slots, cutoffs {T0,T2}
# Either board:  {T0,T1,T2,T3}                     -> 4 cutoffs, not 3 + 2 = 5
# C@T0 is outside the universe on both boards.
OFF = {"candidate_eligible": False}
WARM = {"warmup_complete": False}
SICK = {"health_verified": False}
OUT = {"universe_member": False}


def universe():
    return (
        pair(A, T0, down=OFF)
        + pair(Bp, T0, up=WARM)
        + pair(C, T0, up=OUT, down=OUT)
        + pair(A, T1, down=SICK)
        + pair(Bp, T1, down=SICK)
        + pair(C, T1, up=SICK, down=SICK)
        + pair(A, T2, up=OFF, down=WARM)
        + pair(Bp, T2, up=OFF)
        + pair(C, T2, up=OFF)
        + pair(A, T3, up=WARM, down=OFF)
        + pair(Bp, T3, up=WARM, down=OFF)
        + pair(C, T3, down=OFF)
    )


def labels_300_900():
    return [
        lab(A, T0, "up"),  # ready success
        lab(A, T1, "up", verified=False),  # ready failure, still gradable
        lab(Bp, T1, "up", status="pending"),
        # C@T3 up: missing on purpose.
        lab(A, T0, "up", horizon=900),
        lab(A, T1, "up", horizon=900, status="unavailable", reason="x"),
        lab(Bp, T0, "down", verified=False),
        lab(Bp, T2, "down"),
        lab(C, T2, "down", status="pending"),
    ]


def test_hand_oracle_product_hours_board_unions_and_either_board_union():
    out = run([], universe())
    up, down = out["boards"]["up"], out["boards"]["down"]
    assert up["recorded_slot_count"] == down["recorded_slot_count"] == 12
    assert up["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 4
    assert down["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 3
    assert up["decision_eligible_product_hours"] == pytest.approx(20000 / HOUR)
    assert down["decision_eligible_product_hours"] == pytest.approx(15000 / HOUR)
    assert up["board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product"] == pytest.approx(
        15000 / HOUR
    )
    assert down["board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_eligible_product"] == pytest.approx(
        10000 / HOUR
    )
    either = out["wall_clock_hours_with_either_board_eligible"]
    assert either == pytest.approx(20000 / HOUR)
    assert either < (15000 + 10000) / HOUR  # A union, never a sum.
    assert out["universe_size_by_cutoff"] == [[T0, 2], [T1, 3], [T2, 3], [T3, 3]]
    assert up["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"] == dict(
        universe_member=1, warmup_complete=3, health_verified=1, candidate_eligible=3
    )
    assert down["exclusions_by_first_failed_flag"] == dict(
        universe_member=1, warmup_complete=1, health_verified=3, candidate_eligible=4
    )


def test_hand_oracle_ranker_complete_row_intersections_per_board_and_horizon():
    rankers = {
        "R1": [
            (A, "up", T0),
            (A, "up", T1),
            (Bp, "up", T1),
            (C, "up", T3),
            (Bp, "down", T0),
        ],
        "R2": [
            (A, "up", T1),
            (C, "up", T3),
            (Bp, "up", T0),
            (Bp, "down", T0),
            (Bp, "down", T2),
            (C, "down", T2),
        ],
        "R3": [(A, "up", T0), (A, "up", T1)],
    }
    out = run(labels_300_900(), universe(), ranker_slots=rankers)
    up, down = out["boards"]["up"]["horizons"], out["boards"]["down"]["horizons"]
    # Up board gradable: 300s {A@T0, A@T1}; 900s {A@T0}.
    assert up["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 2
    assert up["300"]["ungradable_reasons"] == {
        "label_missing": 1,
        "pending:no_reason": 1,
    }
    assert up["900"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 1
    assert up["900"]["ungradable_reasons"] == {"label_missing": 2, "unavailable:x": 1}
    # Shared up rows R1 ∩ R2 ∩ R3 = {A@T1}.
    assert up["300"]["comparison"]["identical_complete_rows"] == [[A, T1]]
    assert up["900"]["comparison"]["identical_complete_rows"] == []
    cov = up["300"]["comparison"]["natural_ranker_coverage"]
    assert cov["R1"] == dict(eligible_scored_slot_count=4, gradable_scored_slot_count=2)
    assert cov["R2"] == dict(eligible_scored_slot_count=2, gradable_scored_slot_count=1)
    assert cov["R3"] == dict(eligible_scored_slot_count=2, gradable_scored_slot_count=2)
    # Down board: R3 scored nothing there, so no row is scored by every ranker.
    assert down["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 2
    assert down["300"]["comparison"]["identical_complete_row_count"] == 0
    dcov = down["300"]["comparison"]["natural_ranker_coverage"]
    assert dcov["R1"] == dict(
        eligible_scored_slot_count=1, gradable_scored_slot_count=1
    )
    assert dcov["R2"] == dict(
        eligible_scored_slot_count=3, gradable_scored_slot_count=2
    )
    assert dcov["R3"] == dict(
        eligible_scored_slot_count=0, gradable_scored_slot_count=0
    )


def test_without_rankers_the_complete_row_table_is_every_gradable_row():
    out = run(labels_300_900(), universe())
    rows = out["boards"]["up"]["horizons"]["300"]["comparison"][
        "identical_complete_rows"
    ]
    assert rows == [[A, T0], [A, T1]]
    empty = run(labels_300_900(), universe(), ranker_slots={"Z": []})
    for board in empty["boards"].values():
        for h in board["horizons"].values():
            assert h["comparison"]["identical_complete_row_count"] == 0


# --- Own-direction gradability --------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("other_board", [{}, OFF, OUT])
def test_opposite_board_state_or_missing_label_never_removes_a_ready_row(other_board):
    records = (
        pair(A, T0, down=other_board)
        if other_board is not OUT
        else pair(A, T0, up=OUT, down=OUT)
    )
    out = run([lab(A, T0, "up", verified=False)], records)
    up = out["boards"]["up"]
    expected = 0 if other_board is OUT else 1
    assert up["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == expected
    assert up["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == expected
    # The legacy joint report still demands both direction labels.
    joint = assoc.exposure_report(
        [lab(A, T0, "up", verified=False)], [slot(A, 0)], asof_ts_ms=ASOF
    )
    assert joint["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 0


def test_both_boards_graded_independently_with_opposite_outcomes():
    out = run([lab(A, T0, "up"), lab(A, T0, "down", verified=False)], pair(A, T0))
    for direction in assoc.DIRECTIONS:
        assert out["boards"][direction]["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 1


def test_report_exposes_no_outcome_counts_so_unavailable_cannot_become_negative():
    out = run(
        [
            lab(A, T0, "up", status="unavailable", reason="missing_benchmark"),
            lab(A, T0, "down", verified=False),
        ],
        pair(A, T0),
    )
    text = json.dumps(out)
    for word in ("success", "failure", "negative", "verified_forward"):
        assert word not in text
    assert out["boards"]["up"]["horizons"]["300"]["ungradable_reasons"] == {
        "unavailable:missing_benchmark": 1
    }
    assert out["boards"]["down"]["horizons"]["300"]["gradable_slot_count"] == 1


def test_exposure_is_identical_with_no_labels_and_with_every_label_ready():
    full = []
    for product in (A, Bp, C):
        for ts in (T0, T1, T2, T3):
            for direction in assoc.DIRECTIONS:
                for horizon in assoc.HORIZONS:
                    full.append(
                        lab(product, ts, direction, horizon, verified=(ts == T0))
                    )
    before, after = run([], universe()), run(full, universe())
    strip = lambda board: {k: v for k, v in board.items() if k != "horizons"}
    for direction in assoc.DIRECTIONS:
        assert strip(before["boards"][direction]) == strip(after["boards"][direction])
    for key in (
        "universe_size_by_cutoff",
        "wall_clock_hours_with_either_board_eligible",
    ):
        assert before[key] == after[key]


# --- Horizon maturity boundaries ------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "asof,mature300,mature900",
    [
        (T0 + 299_999, False, False),
        (T0 + 300_000, True, False),
        (T0 + 899_999, True, False),
        (T0 + 900_000, True, True),
    ],
)
def test_each_horizon_matures_exactly_at_anchor_plus_horizon(
    asof, mature300, mature900
):
    rows = [lab(A, T0, "up", h, status="pending") for h in assoc.HORIZONS]
    out = run(rows, pair(A, T0), asof=asof)
    horizons = out["boards"]["up"]["horizons"]
    for h, mature in (("300", mature300), ("900", mature900)):
        reason = "pending:no_reason" if mature else "immature_trailing_horizon"
        assert horizons[h]["ungradable_reasons"] == {reason: 1}
    assert out["boards"]["up"]["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 1

    }

def test_ranker_may_score_an_ineligible_recorded_slot_without_adding_exposure():
    out = run(
        [lab(A, T0, "up")], pair(A, T0, up=OFF), ranker_slots={"R": [(A, "up", T0)]}
    )

def test_mixed_label_provenance_is_rejected_even_for_an_irrelevant_slot():
    rows = [lab(A, T0, "up"), lab(C, T3, "down", status="pending")]
    rows[1]["contract_sha256"] = "other-contract"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="provenance"):
        run(rows, pair(A, T0))
    rows[1]["contract_sha256"] = rows[0]["contract_sha256"]
    rows[1]["evaluator_sha256"] = "other-evaluator"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="provenance"):
        run(rows, pair(A, T0))


def test_inputs_including_rankers_are_not_mutated():
    rows, records = labels_300_900(), universe()
    rankers = {"R": [(A, "up", T0), [A, "down", T0]]}
    frozen = copy.deepcopy((rows, records, rankers))
    run(rows, records, ranker_slots=rankers)
    assert (rows, records, rankers) == frozen

            assert h["comparison"]["identical_complete_rows"] == []


# --- Legacy reporter and provenance ---------------------------------------


def test_directional_report_embeds_the_unchanged_association_provenance():
    out = run([lab(A, T0, "up")], pair(A, T0))
    assert out["association_dependency"] == assoc.provenance()
    src = (
        ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_directional_exposure.py"
    ).read_text("utf-8")
    con = (
        ROOT / "docs/research/INSTANT_DIRECTIONAL_EXPOSURE_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
    ).read_text("utf-8")
    assert out["implementation_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(src.encode()).hexdigest()
    assert out["contract_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(con.encode()).hexdigest()
    assert out["target_contract_sha256"] == "contract"
    assert out["opposite_direction_label_required"] is False
    assert out["legacy_joint_report_changed"] is False


def test_legacy_joint_reporter_output_shape_is_untouched():
    joint = assoc.exposure_report([], [slot(A, 0)], asof_ts_ms=ASOF)
    assert (
        "boards" not in joint
        and "board_wall_clock_hours_with_any_verified_health" in joint
    )
    assert joint["horizons"]["300"]["comparison"][
        "identical_complete_rows_definition"
    ] == ("decision_eligible_and_gradable_and_scored_by_every_ranker")


# --- The limitation, stated as executable facts ---------------------------


def test_reporter_accepts_unverifiable_assertions_and_says_so():
    """It records caller claims; it cannot authenticate them."""
    records = pair(A, T0)
    for r in records:
        r["available_ts_ms"] = 0  # Claims eligibility was known an hour early.
    out = run([], records)
    assert out["boards"]["up"]["decision_eligible_slot_count"] == 1
    assert (
        out["eligibility_source"] == "caller_assertions_not_authenticated_feed_evidence"
    )
    assert out["eligibility_producer_implemented"] is False


def test_reporter_cannot_see_a_product_omitted_from_both_boards():
    out = run([], pair(A, T0) + pair(Bp, T0))
    assert out["universe_size_by_cutoff"] == [[T0, 2]]  # C is simply absent.
    assert out["boards"]["up"]["recorded_slot_count"] == 2
    cfg = json.loads(directional.CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert cfg["universe_completeness"].startswith("not_authenticated")

===== BEGIN SOURCE 96 | tests/test_instant_evidence_index.py | sha256=26dcdc75d1e959b5169a7c5886c68f2f16a7a930103ed6d48e066b7d112e90a9 =====
"""Compare indexed inputs and decisions against the batch evidence path."""

import pytest

from test_instant_input_evidence_review import BTC, COIN, Tape, healthy
from test_instant_timed_features import CUTOFF, feature_tape
from instant_delayed_decision import prepare_decision
from instant_evidence_index import CHECKPOINT_PACKETS
from instant_input_evidence import InputEvidenceRecorder
from instant_timed_features import _book_pressure


class IndexedTape(Tape):
    def __init__(self, products=(COIN, BTC), first=30000, stop=40000, start_clock=0):
        super().__init__(products, first, stop, start_clock)
        self.rec = InputEvidenceRecorder(
            products, first, stop, clock_ms=self._clock, indexed=True
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("unknown_side", [False, True])
def test_feature_rows_and_exact_prefixes_match_at_both_knowledge_times(unknown_side):
    batch = feature_tape(unknown_side=unknown_side)
    fast = feature_tape(unknown_side=unknown_side, tape_type=IndexedTape)
    for asof in (CUTOFF + 1, CUTOFF + 180, CUTOFF):
        for direction in ("up", "down"):
            assert prepare_decision(
                fast.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, direction, asof=asof
            ) == prepare_decision(batch.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, direction, asof=asof)

    # Probe prefix edges and the same-time first-pending packet, including
    # a non-current prefix after the incremental hash advanced past it.
    for seq in (
        1,
        CHECKPOINT_PACKETS,
        2 * CHECKPOINT_PACKETS + 1,
        len(batch.rec.packets),
    ):
        at = batch.rec.packets[seq - 1].available_ts_ms
        for timestamp in (at - 1, at):
            assert fast.rec._prefix_at(timestamp) == batch.rec._prefix_at(timestamp)


def test_delayed_book_payload_and_same_ms_barrier_have_batch_parity():
    batch, fast = (
        healthy(cls(first=30000, stop=50000), through=30000)
        for cls in (Tape, IndexedTape)
    )
    for tape in (batch, fast):
        tape.send(30001, "audit_transport_error")
        tape.ack(30001)
        tape.hb(30001, 10)
        tape.snap(30001)
        tape.hb(35000, 10, steps=[35000, 35000, 35000, 35000, 35150])
    for asof in (35001, 35150):
        assert fast.rec._quote_at(COIN, 35000, asof) == batch.rec._quote_at(
            COIN, 35000, asof
        )
        assert fast.rec._trade_window(
            COIN, 25000, 35000, asof
        ) == batch.rec._trade_window(COIN, 25000, 35000, asof)
        assert _book_pressure(fast.rec, COIN, 30000, 35000, asof, 1) == _book_pressure(
            batch.rec, COIN, 30000, 35000, asof, 1
        )


def test_late_merge_and_conflict_are_queried_by_end_and_knowledge_time():
    batch, fast = Tape(), IndexedTape()
    for tape in (batch, fast):
        tape.ack()
        tape.hb(0, 10)
        tape.hb(1000, 11)
        tape.trade(1100, 11)
        tape.hb(2000, 11)
        tape.trade(2400, 11, size="99")
    for asof in (1001, 2001, 2401):
        left = fast.rec._trade_window(COIN, 0, 1000, asof)
        right = batch.rec._trade_window(COIN, 0, 1000, asof)
        assert left == right
    assert fast.rec._trade_window(COIN, 0, 2000, 2001)[2] is None
    assert fast.rec._trade_window(COIN, 1100, 2000, 2401)[2] == "conflicting_trade_id"
