===== BEGIN SOURCE 97 | tests/test_instant_input_evidence.py | sha256=240ef221589d8a6f44833b0cef831c58d5c806e67092c6474befd61daeaedec0 =====
"""Planned-row accounting, measured pipeline completion and scoped evidence."""

from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

from test_instant_book import PID
from instant_input_evidence import InputEvidenceRecorder
import instant_input_evidence as evidence


BTC = "BTC-USD"


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 0
        self.steps = []

    def __call__(self):
        if self.steps:
            self.now = self.steps.pop(0)
        return self.now


class Feed:
    def __init__(self, first=30000, stop=40000):
        self.clock = Clock()
        self.recorder = InputEvidenceRecorder(
            (PID, BTC), first, stop, clock_ms=self.clock
        )
        self.seq = 0
        self.events = []
        self.ids = {PID: 10, BTC: 10}

    def send(self, ts, kind, pid=PID, lag=0, **fields):
        self.clock.now = ts + lag
        self.seq += 1
        event = dict(
            receipt_seq=self.seq,
            receipt_ts_ms=ts,
            receipt_monotonic_ns=ts * 1000000,
            connection_id=1,
            message=dict(type=kind, product_id=pid, **fields),
        )
        self.events.append(event)
        self.recorder.ingest(event)
        return event

    def ack(self, products=(PID, BTC)):
        self.send(
            0,
            "subscriptions",
            channels=[
                dict(name=c, product_ids=list(products))
                for c in ("matches", "heartbeat", "level2_batch")
            ],
        )

    def hb(self, ts, pid=PID):
        self.send(ts, "heartbeat", pid, last_trade_id=self.ids[pid])

    def snapshot(self, ts, pid=PID, **kw):
        self.send(ts, "snapshot", pid, bids=[["99", "2"]], asks=[["101", "1"]], **kw)

    def trade(self, ts, pid=PID, side="sell", **kw):
        self.ids[pid] += 1
        return self.send(
            ts,
            "match",
            pid,
            trade_id=self.ids[pid],
            price="100",
            size="2",
            side=side,
            **kw,
        )


def ready(*, side=None):
    feed = Feed()
    feed.ack()
    for p in (PID, BTC):
        feed.hb(0, p)
    for p in (PID, BTC):
        feed.snapshot(10, p)
    for ts in range(1000, 30001, 1000):
        if ts == 1000 and side is not None:
            feed.trade(500, side=side)
        if ts == 26000:
            for p in (PID, BTC):
                feed.snapshot(25500, p)
        for p in (PID, BTC):
            feed.hb(ts, p)
    feed.clock.now = 30001
    return feed


def test_inventory_accounts_for_products_directions_and_every_cutoff_without_packets():
    f = Feed()
    f.clock.now = 35000
    report = f.recorder.seal()
    assert report["expected_rows"] == report["recorded_rows"] == 8
    assert {
        (r["product_id"], r["direction"], r["cutoff_ts_ms"]) for r in report["rows"]
    } == {(p, d, t) for p in (PID, BTC) for d in ("up", "down") for t in (30000, 35000)}
    assert report["packet_count"] == 0
    assert all(
        r["eligibility_status"] == "unavailable" and not r["study_eligible"]
        for r in report["rows"]
    )
    assert all(
        "baseline_evidence_not_implemented" in r["unavailable_reasons"]
        for r in report["rows"]
    )


def test_a_missing_product_is_retained_in_both_boards():
    f = Feed()
    f.ack((BTC,))
    f.hb(1000, BTC)
    f.snapshot(2000, BTC)
    f.clock.now = 30000
    rows = f.recorder.checkpoint(30000)
    missing = [r.to_dict() for r in rows if r.product_id == PID]
    assert len(missing) == 2
    assert all(r["components"]["coin"]["quote"]["available"] is False for r in missing)


def test_processing_completion_is_measured_after_book_and_trade_construction():
    f = Feed()
    f.ack()
    # Producer start, monitor start, monitor finish, book finish, producer finish.
    f.clock.steps = [100, 101, 102, 103, 110]
    f.trade(100)
    packet, trade = f.recorder.packets[-1], f.recorder.trades[-1]
    assert packet.processing_started_ts_ms == 100
    assert packet.processing_completed_ts_ms == trade.available_ts_ms == 110
    assert packet.available_ts_ms > packet.receipt_ts_ms
    assert f.recorder.book.completed[packet.receipt_seq] == 103
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        trade.available_ts_ms = 100


def test_healthy_empty_is_zero_only_after_proof_completion_not_at_cutoff():
    f = ready()
    early = f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000)
    later = f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000, asof=30001)
    assert early["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] is None
    assert later["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] == 0
    assert later["trades_30s"]["quote_volume_usd"] == "0"
    assert later["later_diagnostic"] is True
    assert later["knowledge_asof_ts_ms"] == 30001
    assert later["full_feed_verified"] is False
    assert later["interval_quote_continuity_verified"] is False


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "side,expected_buy,expected_sell", [("sell", "200", "0"), ("buy", "0", "200")]
)
def test_match_maker_side_is_inverted_with_exact_decimal_amounts(
    side, expected_buy, expected_sell
):
    f = ready(side=side)
    view = f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000, asof=30001)["trades_30s"]
    assert view["verified_trade_count"] == 1
    assert view["quote_volume_usd"] == "200"
    assert (view["taker_buy_usd"], view["taker_sell_usd"]) == (
        expected_buy,
        expected_sell,
    )
    assert view["flow_available"] is True


def test_unknown_side_keeps_verified_count_but_signed_flow_unavailable():
    f = ready(side="unknown")
    view = f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000, asof=30001)["trades_30s"]
    assert view["verified_trade_count"] == 1
    assert view["unknown_side_usd"] == "200"
    assert view["flow_available"] is False


def test_later_evidence_and_caller_mutation_cannot_rewrite_frozen_cohort():
    f = ready()
    cohort = f.recorder.checkpoint(30000)
    before = [r.to_dict() for r in cohort]
    f.send(30002, "audit_transport_error")
    assert f.recorder.checkpoint(30000) is cohort
    assert [r.to_dict() for r in cohort] == before
    edited = cohort[0].to_dict()
    edited["components"]["coin"]["quote"]["available"] = "forged"
    assert [r.to_dict() for r in cohort] == before
    assert all(
        "assessment_completed_after_cutoff" in r.unavailable_reasons for r in cohort
    )
    assert all(
        r.to_dict()["components"]["coin"]["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] is None
        for r in cohort
    )


def test_assessment_completion_is_measured_and_not_labelled_as_input_availability():
    f = ready()
    f.clock.steps = [30001, 30020]
    cohort = f.recorder.checkpoint(30000)
    assert all(r.assessment_completed_ts_ms == 30020 for r in cohort)
    assert all(
        r.to_dict()["components"]["coin"]["knowledge_asof_ts_ms"] == 30000
        for r in cohort
    )


def test_receipt_at_cutoff_is_excluded_and_row_completion_cannot_be_backdated():
    f = ready()
    f.trade(30000)
    f.clock.now = 30001
    assert (
        f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000, asof=30001)["trades_30s"][
            "known_unique_match_count"
        ]
        == 0
    )


def test_lost_id_is_not_converted_to_zero():
    f = ready()
    # The following window includes an undelivered ID and no book refresh.
    f.ids[PID] += 1
    f.hb(31000)
    f.hb(35000)
    f.clock.now = 35001
    view = f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 35000, asof=35001)
    assert view["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] is None
    assert view["trades_30s"]["quote_volume_usd"] is None


def test_source_ticker_and_rest_do_not_create_trade_rows():
    f = Feed()
    event = f.send(100, "ticker", last_size="1000", best_bid="99", best_ask="101")
    event["message"]["last_size"] = "forged"
    f.send(200, "last_match", trade_id=11, price="100", size="2", side="sell")
    f.clock.now = 300
    f.seq += 1
    recovery = dict(
        receipt_seq=f.seq,
        receipt_ts_ms=300,
        receipt_monotonic_ns=300000000,
        connection_id=1,
        source="rest_recovery",
        message=dict(type="match", product_id=PID, trade_id=12, price="100", size="2"),
    )
    f.recorder.ingest(recovery)
    assert len(f.recorder.packets) == 3 and not f.recorder.trades
    assert (
        f.recorder.packets[0].envelope_sha256
        != hashlib.sha256(evidence._json(event).encode()).hexdigest()
    )


def test_first_receipt_deduplication_and_conflicts_preserve_original_processing():
    f = ready(side="sell")
    original = f.recorder.trades[0]
    f.send(30001, "match", trade_id=11, price="100.0", size="2.0", side="sell")
    f.send(30002, "match", trade_id=11, price="101", size="2", side="sell")
    assert f.recorder.trades == [original]
    f.clock.now = 30003
    assert (
        f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000, asof=30003)["trades_30s"]["reason"]
        == "conflicting_trade_id"
    )


def test_quote_age_is_not_refreshed_by_heartbeat_and_benchmark_is_separate():
    f = ready()
    f.hb(35000)
    f.hb(35000, BTC)
    f.clock.now = 35001
    # Last snapshot was 25,500. At 35,000 it is still inside the 10s TTL.
    assert (
        f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 35000, asof=35001)["quote"][
            "observation_age_ms"
        ]
        == 9500
    )
    f.send(35001, "l2update", BTC, changes=[["sell", "101", "0"], ["sell", "102", "0"]])
    f.clock.now = 35002
    assert (
        f.recorder.component_evidence(BTC, 35000, asof=35002)["quote"]["available"]
        is True
    )  # update at/after cutoff excluded


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change", ["sequence", "clock", "packet_bound"])
def test_failed_or_truncated_tapes_cannot_be_reported_or_sealed(change, monkeypatch):
    f = Feed()
    f.ack()
    if change == "sequence":
        f.seq += 1
    elif change == "clock":
        f.clock.steps = [-1]
    else:
        import instant_coverage

        monkeypatch.setattr(instant_coverage, "MAX_PACKETS", 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        f.hb(1000)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        f.recorder.seal()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        f.recorder.report()

    assert row["components"]["benchmark"]["quote"]["available"] is False

    f = ready()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="outside declared"):
        f.recorder.checkpoint(40000)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="knowledge must be reached"):
        f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000, asof=40000)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="cutoff has not been reached"):
        f.recorder.checkpoint(35000)


def test_receipt_at_an_epoch_boundary_is_not_certified_by_the_next_epoch():
    f = Feed()
    f.ack()
    f.hb(0)
    f.trade(0)  # Actual first receipt stays at 0, before the failure.
    f.send(0, "audit_parse_error")
    f.ack()
    f.ids[PID] = 10
    f.hb(0)
    f.hb(0, BTC)
    for p in (PID, BTC):
        f.snapshot(1, p)
    for ts in range(1000, 30001, 1000):
        if ts == 26000:
            for p in (PID, BTC):
                f.snapshot(25500, p)
        for p in (PID, BTC):
            f.hb(ts, p)
    f.clock.now = 30001
    component = f.recorder.component_evidence(PID, 30000, asof=30001)["trades_30s"]
    assert component["known_unique_match_count"] == 1  # No receipt reassignment.
    assert component["verified_trade_count"] is None
    assert component["reason"] == "trade_row_continuity_mismatch"


def test_cli_round_trip_retains_planned_rows_and_processing_ledgers(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / "input" / "packets.jsonl.gz"
    source.parent.mkdir()
    f = Feed(stop=35000)
    f.ack()
    f.trade(100)
    with gzip.open(source, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        for event in f.events:
            stream.write(json.dumps(event) + "\n")
    plan = source.parent / "inventory.json"
    plan.write_text(
        json.dumps(dict(products=[PID, BTC], first_cutoff=30000, stop_cutoff=35000)),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    output = tmp_path / "output"
    before = source.read_bytes(), plan.read_bytes()
    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(Path(evidence.__file__)),
        str(source),
        "--inventory",
        str(plan),
        "--out-dir",
        str(output),
    ]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)
    report = json.loads((output / "REPORT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert report["recorded_rows"] == report["expected_rows"] == 4
    assert report["unique_live_matches"] == 1
    assert report["inventory_precedes_first_cutoff"] is False
    for name in ("packet_processing.jsonl", "trade_processing.jsonl"):
        assert (
            hashlib.sha256((output / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            == report[name + "_sha256"]
        )
    assert (source.read_bytes(), plan.read_bytes()) == before
    assert subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True).returncode != 0

===== BEGIN SOURCE 98 | tests/test_instant_input_evidence_review.py | sha256=ab0517b7e605e59ad337671c0ff6dddb717cb2fe076f6f64b89dfb35aa4b63e3 =====
"""Independent review probes for input-evidence stage 1 (Codex 8607a36).

Claude review at tip fee55a7. The tape helper here is written from the
recorder's contract, not copied from the implementation's own tests, and sets
watermarks, connections and processing clocks explicitly. Everything is
synthetic. Nothing here authenticates a feed, proves a healthy capture or
produces positive eligibility, and the last section says so executably.
"""

from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError
import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

sys.path.insert(
    0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts/scoring_experiment")
)
import instant_input_evidence as evidence  # noqa: E402
from instant_input_evidence import InputEvidenceRecorder  # noqa: E402

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
COIN, BTC, ZZZ = "ALT-USD", "BTC-USD", "ZZZ-USD"
NOT_IMPLEMENTED = {
    "baseline_evidence_not_implemented",
    "feature_evidence_not_implemented",
    "publication_policy_unfrozen",
    "full_feed_evidence_not_verified",
}


class Tape:
    """Explicit watermarks, connections and a five-step per-packet clock."""

    def __init__(self, products=(COIN, BTC), first=30000, stop=40000, start_clock=0):
        self.now, self.steps, self.seq, self.events = start_clock, [], 0, []
        self.rec = InputEvidenceRecorder(
            list(products), first, stop, clock_ms=self._clock
        )

    def _clock(self):
        if self.steps:
            self.now = self.steps.pop(0)
        return self.now

    def send(self, ts, kind, pid=COIN, conn=1, source=None, steps=None, **fields):
        self.now = max(self.now, ts)
        self.steps = list(steps or [])
        self.seq += 1
        env = dict(
            receipt_seq=self.seq,
            receipt_ts_ms=ts,
            receipt_monotonic_ns=ts * 1000000,
            connection_id=conn,
            message=dict(type=kind, product_id=pid, **fields),
        )
        if source:
            env["source"] = source
        self.events.append(env)
        self.rec.ingest(env)
        return env

    def ack(self, ts=0, products=(COIN, BTC), conn=1):
        self.send(
            ts,
            "subscriptions",
            conn=conn,
            channels=[
                dict(name=c, product_ids=list(products))
                for c in ("matches", "heartbeat", "level2_batch")
            ],
        )

    def hb(self, ts, last, pid=COIN, conn=1, **kw):
        self.send(ts, "heartbeat", pid, conn=conn, last_trade_id=last, **kw)

    def snap(self, ts, pid=COIN, conn=1):
        self.send(
            ts, "snapshot", pid, conn=conn, bids=[["99", "2"]], asks=[["101", "1"]]
        )

    def trade(
        self, ts, tid, pid=COIN, conn=1, side="sell", price="100", size="2", **kw
    ):
        return self.send(
            ts,
            "match",
            pid,
            conn=conn,
            trade_id=tid,
            price=price,
            size=size,
            side=side,
            **kw,
        )

    def comp(self, cutoff, pid=COIN, asof=None):
        return self.rec.component_evidence(pid, cutoff, asof=asof)


def healthy(tape, through=30000, products=(COIN, BTC), watermark=10, snapshots=True):
    """Heartbeats every second with a fixed watermark; fresh snapshots every 5s."""
    tape.ack(0, products)
    for ts in range(0, through + 1, 1000):
        for p in products:
            tape.hb(ts, watermark, p)
        if snapshots and ts % 5000 == 0 and ts < through:
            for p in products:
                tape.snap(ts + 10, p)
    return tape


def rows_of(cohort, pid):
    return [r.to_dict() for r in cohort if r.product_id == pid]


# --- Inventory ----------------------------------------------------------------


def test_inventory_is_the_full_cross_product_and_canonical_across_order():
    a = Tape((BTC, COIN))
    b = Tape((COIN, BTC))
    assert a.rec.inventory_sha256 == b.rec.inventory_sha256
    a.now = 40000
    report = a.rec.seal()
    keys = {
        (r["product_id"], r["direction"], r["cutoff_ts_ms"]) for r in report["rows"]
    }
    assert keys == {
        (p, d, t) for p in (COIN, BTC) for d in ("up", "down") for t in (30000, 35000)
    }
    assert report["expected_rows"] == report["recorded_rows"] == 8
    assert report["inventory"]["products"] == [COIN, BTC]  # sorted, order-independent


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "declared,precedes", [(29999, True), (30000, False), (10_000_000, False)]
)
def test_declaration_time_is_measured_separately_from_the_plan_hash(declared, precedes):
    reference = Tape().rec.inventory_sha256
    tape = Tape(start_clock=declared)
    assert tape.rec.inventory_sha256 == reference
    assert tape.rec.inventory_recorded_ts_ms == declared
    tape.now = max(declared, 40000)
    report = tape.rec.seal()
    assert report["inventory_precedes_first_cutoff"] is precedes
    flagged = all(
        "inventory_recorded_after_first_cutoff" in r["unavailable_reasons"]
        for r in report["rows"]
    )
    assert flagged is (not precedes)


def test_planned_product_with_no_packets_keeps_all_rows_with_explicit_reasons():
    tape = healthy(Tape((COIN, BTC, ZZZ)), products=(COIN, BTC))
    cohort = tape.rec.checkpoint(30000)
    zzz = rows_of(cohort, ZZZ)
    assert len(zzz) == 2 and {r["direction"] for r in zzz} == {"up", "down"}
    for row in zzz:
        assert row["components"]["coin"]["quote"]["available"] is False
        assert row["components"]["coin"]["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] is None
        assert any(x.startswith("coin:quote:") for x in row["unavailable_reasons"])
        assert any(x.startswith("coin:trades:") for x in row["unavailable_reasons"])
        assert row["components"]["benchmark"]["quote"]["available"] is True


def test_completeness_is_relative_to_the_plan_not_the_tape():
    tape = healthy(
        Tape((COIN, BTC)), products=(COIN, BTC, ZZZ)
    )  # ZZZ on the wire, not in the plan.
    tape.now = 40000
    report = tape.rec.seal()
    assert report["expected_rows"] == report["recorded_rows"] == 8
    assert not any(r["product_id"] == ZZZ for r in report["rows"])
    assert "ZZZ" not in json.dumps(report)  # Nothing flags the unplanned product.


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "products,first,stop",
    [
        ((COIN, BTC), 30000, 30000 + 5000 * 2501),  # 10,004 rows
        ((COIN, BTC), 25000, 35000),
        ((COIN, BTC), 30000, 35001),
        ((COIN, BTC), 35000, 30000),
        ((COIN, ""), 30000, 35000),
        ((COIN, 1, BTC), 30000, 35000),
        (tuple(f"P{i}-USD" for i in range(32)) + (BTC,), 30000, 35000),
    ],
)
def test_inventory_bounds_and_identities_are_enforced(products, first, stop):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        InputEvidenceRecorder(list(products), first, stop)


def test_inventory_row_cap_is_inclusive():
    rec = InputEvidenceRecorder(
        [COIN, BTC], 30000, 30000 + 5000 * 2500
    )  # exactly 10,000
    assert len(rec.cutoffs) * 2 * 2 == 10000


# --- Payload completion versus receipt, book and monitor completion -------------


def test_late_merged_proof_is_known_at_producer_completion_and_never_backdated():
    tape = healthy(Tape(), through=29000)
    tape.hb(29500, 11)  # Overtakes match 11: interval [29000,29500) fails.
    tape.trade(29600, 11)
    # Closing heartbeat: producer 30000, monitor 30001-30002, book 30003, producer done 30500.
    tape.hb(30000, 11, steps=[30000, 30001, 30002, 30003, 30500])
    tape.hb(30000, 10, BTC)
    frozen = rows_of(tape.rec.checkpoint(30000), COIN)[0]["components"]["coin"][
        "trades_30s"
    ]
    assert (
        frozen["reason"] == "missing_live_match_ids"
        and frozen["verified_trade_count"] is None
    )
    assert frozen["known_unique_match_count"] == 1
    monitor_only = tape.comp(30000, asof=30100)["trades_30s"]
    assert (
        monitor_only["proof_available_ts_ms"] == 30002
    )  # Monitor has the merged proof.
    assert monitor_only["reason"] == "trade_row_processing_incomplete"
    assert monitor_only["verified_trade_count"] is None
    done = tape.comp(30000, asof=30500)["trades_30s"]
    assert done["receipt_id_check_passed"] and done["verified_trade_count"] == 1
    assert done["quote_volume_usd"] == "200" and done["taker_buy_usd"] == "200"
    assert (
        rows_of(tape.rec.checkpoint(30000), COIN)[0]["components"]["coin"]["trades_30s"]
        == frozen
    )


def test_trade_row_completion_always_precedes_its_closing_proof():
    """Sequential ingest: a row's payload completes before the proof closing it.

    So the producer's len(known) == len(rows) guard is defensive; the reachable
    gate is the closing heartbeat's own producer completion (probe above).
    """
    tape = healthy(Tape(), through=29000)
    tape.trade(29500, 11, steps=[29500, 29501, 29502, 29503, 30200])
    tape.hb(30000, 11, steps=[30200, 30200, 30200, 30200, 30201])
    tape.hb(30000, 10, BTC)
    tape.now = 30201
    trade, closing = tape.rec.trades[-1], tape.rec.packets[-2]
    assert trade.available_ts_ms == 30200 <= closing.available_ts_ms
    early = tape.comp(30000, asof=30150)["trades_30s"]
    assert early["known_unique_match_count"] == 0
    assert early["verified_trade_count"] is None
    assert early["reason"] == "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"  # Proof unknown first.
    late = tape.comp(30000, asof=30201)["trades_30s"]
    assert late["known_unique_match_count"] == 1 and late["verified_trade_count"] == 1


def test_heartbeat_payload_completion_gates_the_quote_even_when_book_is_done():
    tape = healthy(Tape(), through=29000)
    # Book and monitor finish within the cutoff; the producer finishes after it.
    tape.hb(29999, 10, steps=[29999, 29999, 29999, 29999, 30001])
    tape.hb(29999, 10, BTC, steps=[30001, 30001, 30001, 30001, 30001])
    assert tape.rec.book.view(COIN, 30000).book_state_available
    at_cutoff = tape.comp(30000)["quote"]
    assert at_cutoff["available"] is False and at_cutoff["bid"] is None
    assert at_cutoff["reason"] == "quote_row_processing_incomplete"
    later = tape.comp(30000, asof=30001)["quote"]
    assert later["available"] is True and later["bid"] == "99"
    assert later["book_processing_available_ts_ms"] == 25011  # the snapshot state


def test_receipt_time_never_stands_in_for_completion():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.ack()
    tape.trade(100, 11, steps=[100, 100, 100, 100, 100])
    packet, trade = tape.rec.packets[-1], tape.rec.trades[-1]
    assert packet.processing_completed_ts_ms == 100
    assert packet.available_ts_ms == trade.available_ts_ms == 101  # receipt + 1 floor
    assert (
        packet.envelope_sha256
        == hashlib.sha256(evidence._json(tape.events[-1]).encode()).hexdigest()
    )


# --- Deduplication, sides, epochs ------------------------------------------------


def test_first_receipt_wins_across_connections_and_a_conflict_vetoes():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.ack()
    tape.hb(0, 10)
    tape.trade(1000, 11, conn=1, steps=[1000, 1000, 1000, 1000, 1000])
    tape.ack(1000, conn=2)
    tape.trade(1000, 11, conn=2)  # Same signature on a new connection.
    tape.trade(
        1500, 11, conn=2, price="100.00", size="2.0"
    )  # Same amounts, new spelling.
    assert len(tape.rec.trades) == 1
    first = tape.rec.trades[0]
    assert (first.connection_id, first.receipt_ts_ms, first.available_ts_ms) == (
        1,
        1000,
        1001,
    )
    tape.trade(2000, 11, conn=2, price="101")  # Conflicting duplicate.
    assert len(tape.rec.trades) == 1
    tape.now = 30000
    view = tape.comp(30000)["trades_30s"]
    assert (
        view["reason"] == "conflicting_trade_id"
        and view["verified_trade_count"] is None
    )


def test_mixed_known_and_unknown_sides_keep_amounts_separate_and_block_flow():
    tape = healthy(Tape(), through=29000)
    tape.trade(29100, 11, side="sell")  # taker buy
    tape.trade(29200, 12, side="weird")
    tape.hb(30000, 12)
    tape.hb(30000, 10, BTC)
    tape.now = 30001
    view = tape.comp(30000, asof=30001)["trades_30s"]
    assert view["verified_trade_count"] == 2
    assert (
        view["taker_buy_usd"],
        view["taker_sell_usd"],
        view["unknown_side_usd"],
    ) == ("200", "0", "200")
    assert view["quote_volume_usd"] == "400" and view["flow_available"] is False


def test_shared_millisecond_connection_change_cannot_certify_the_old_receipt():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.ack(0, conn=1)
    tape.hb(0, 10, conn=1)
    tape.trade(0, 11, conn=1)  # First receipt at 0 on connection 1.
    tape.ack(0, conn=2)
    for p in (COIN, BTC):
        tape.hb(0, 11 if p == COIN else 10, p, conn=2)
    for ts in range(1000, 30001, 1000):
        for p in (COIN, BTC):
            tape.hb(ts, 11 if p == COIN else 10, p, conn=2)
    tape.now = 30001
    view = tape.comp(30000, asof=30001)["trades_30s"]
    assert view["known_unique_match_count"] == 1
    assert tape.rec.trades[0].connection_id == 1  # Receipt attribution unchanged.
    assert (
        view["reason"] == "trade_row_continuity_mismatch"
        and view["verified_trade_count"] is None
    )


def test_proof_in_one_epoch_and_trade_in_another_at_a_shared_millisecond_fails():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.ack(0)
    tape.hb(0, 10)
    tape.trade(0, 11)  # Receipt 0, epoch 0, exactly at the window start.
    tape.send(0, "queue_overflow", pid=None)
    tape.ack(0)
    tape.hb(0, 11)
    tape.hb(0, 10, BTC)
    for ts in range(1000, 30001, 1000):
        tape.hb(ts, 11)
        tape.hb(ts, 10, BTC)
    tape.now = 30001
    view = tape.comp(30000, asof=30001)["trades_30s"]
    assert (
        view["known_unique_match_count"] == 1 and view["verified_trade_count"] is None
    )
    assert view["reason"] == "trade_row_continuity_mismatch"


# --- Verified zeros, quotes, benchmark ---------------------------------------------


def test_verified_zero_needs_a_proof_and_quote_is_an_independent_component():
    tape = healthy(Tape(), snapshots=False)  # Heartbeats only: no book ever.
    tape.now = 30001
    comp = tape.comp(30000, asof=30001)
    assert comp["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] == 0
    assert comp["trades_30s"]["quote_volume_usd"] == "0"
    assert comp["quote"]["available"] is False and comp["quote"]["bid"] is None
    at_cutoff = tape.comp(30000)["trades_30s"]
    assert at_cutoff["verified_trade_count"] is None  # Closing beat known at 30001.
    assert at_cutoff["reason"] == "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"


def test_stale_quote_keeps_original_observation_and_is_not_zero_volume():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.ack()
    for p in (COIN, BTC):
        tape.hb(0, 10, p)
    for p in (COIN, BTC):
        tape.snap(10, p)
    for ts in range(1000, 30001, 1000):
        for p in (COIN, BTC):
            tape.hb(ts, 10, p)
    tape.now = 30001
    comp = tape.comp(30000, asof=30001)
    assert comp["quote"]["reason"] == "stale_book_observation"
    assert comp["quote"]["observation_ts_ms"] == 10
    assert comp["quote"]["observation_age_ms"] == 29990
    assert comp["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] == 0  # Trades proven; quote not.


def test_missing_benchmark_marks_every_row_including_btc_own_rows():
    tape = healthy(Tape(), products=(COIN,))
    cohort = tape.rec.checkpoint(30000)
    for row in (r.to_dict() for r in cohort):
        assert any(x.startswith("benchmark:quote:") for x in row["unavailable_reasons"])
        assert any(
            x.startswith("benchmark:trades:") for x in row["unavailable_reasons"]
        )
    coin = rows_of(cohort, COIN)[0]["components"]
    assert coin["coin"]["quote"]["available"] is True
    assert coin["benchmark"]["quote"]["available"] is False
    btc = rows_of(cohort, BTC)[0]["components"]
    assert btc["coin"] == btc["benchmark"]


# --- Cohort immutability and timing -------------------------------------------------


def test_cohort_is_frozen_identical_after_seal_and_decoded_copies_are_independent():
    tape = healthy(Tape())
    cohort = tape.rec.checkpoint(30000)
    before = [r.to_dict() for r in cohort]
    with pytest.raises(FrozenInstanceError):
        cohort[0].unavailable_reasons = ()
    copy = cohort[0].to_dict()
    copy["components"]["coin"]["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] = 99
    assert [r.to_dict() for r in cohort] == before
    tape.trade(31000, 11)
    tape.send(31500, "audit_transport_error", pid=None)
    tape.now = 40000
    report = tape.rec.seal()
    assert tape.rec.checkpoint(30000) is cohort
    assert report["rows"][: len(before)] == before


def test_late_checkpoint_never_adds_evidence_beyond_cutoff_knowledge():
    prompt = healthy(Tape())
    prompt.now = 30001
    early = [r.to_dict()["components"] for r in prompt.rec.checkpoint(30000)]
    late = healthy(Tape())
    late.trade(31000, 11)
    for ts in range(31000, 36001, 1000):
        late.hb(ts, 11)
        late.hb(ts, 10, BTC)
    late.now = 36001
    later = [r.to_dict()["components"] for r in late.rec.checkpoint(30000)]
    assert early == later
    assert early[0]["coin"]["trades_30s"]["reason"] == "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"


def test_assessment_time_is_measured_and_separate_from_knowledge():
    tape = healthy(Tape())
    tape.steps = [30000, 30007]
    cohort = tape.rec.checkpoint(30000)
    assert {r.assessment_completed_ts_ms for r in cohort} == {30007}
    row = cohort[0].to_dict()
    assert row["components"]["coin"]["knowledge_asof_ts_ms"] == 30000
    assert "assessment_completed_after_cutoff" in row["unavailable_reasons"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "cutoff,asof",
    [
        (30000.0, None),
        (True, None),
        (32500, None),
        (30000, 29999),
        (30000, 30000.0),
        (30000, True),
    ],
)
def test_component_queries_need_a_planned_int_cutoff_and_causal_int_asof(cutoff, asof):
    tape = healthy(Tape())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        tape.rec.component_evidence(COIN, cutoff, asof=asof)


# --- Clock, sequence, cap failures --------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [1.5, True, -1, "30000"])
def test_non_integer_or_negative_clock_fails_closed(bad):
    tape = Tape()
    tape.ack()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        tape.hb(1000, 10, steps=[bad])
    assert tape.rec.failed
    for call in (lambda: tape.rec.checkpoint(30000), tape.rec.seal, tape.rec.report):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            call()


def test_tape_must_start_at_sequence_one_and_stay_contiguous():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.seq = 1  # First packet would be seq 2.
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="sequence 1"):
        tape.ack()
    assert tape.rec.failed
    tape = Tape()
    tape.ack()
    tape.seq += 1
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        tape.hb(1000, 10)
    assert tape.rec.failed and len(tape.rec.packets) == 1


def test_packet_cap_is_inherited_from_the_monitor(monkeypatch):
    import instant_coverage

    tape = Tape()  # Construct first: the cap is checked against the contract.
    monkeypatch.setattr(instant_coverage, "MAX_PACKETS", 2)
    tape.ack()
    tape.hb(0, 10)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="packet bound"):
        tape.hb(1000, 10)
    assert tape.rec.failed


# --- CLI -----------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _cli(source, plan, output):
    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(Path(evidence.__file__)),
            str(source),
            "--inventory",
            str(plan),
            "--out-dir",
            str(output),
        ],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )


def _write_tape(path, events):
    with gzip.open(path, "wt", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        for event in events:
            stream.write(json.dumps(event) + "\n")


def test_cli_reports_plan_hash_all_rows_unavailable_and_refuses_bad_layouts(tmp_path):
    inputs = tmp_path / "in"
    inputs.mkdir()
    tape = healthy(Tape(stop=35000))
    source, plan = inputs / "tape.jsonl.gz", inputs / "plan.json"
    _write_tape(source, tape.events)
    plan.write_text(
        json.dumps(dict(products=[BTC, COIN], first_cutoff=30000, stop_cutoff=35000)),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    assert _cli(source, plan, inputs / "nested").returncode != 0
    assert not (inputs / "nested").exists()
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    run = _cli(source, plan, out)
    assert run.returncode == 0, run.stderr
    report = json.loads((out / "REPORT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert (
        report["inventory_file_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(plan.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    )
    assert report["input_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    assert report["recorded_rows"] == report["expected_rows"] == 4
    assert report["inventory_precedes_first_cutoff"] is False
    assert all(r["eligibility_status"] == "unavailable" for r in report["rows"])
    assert all(not r["study_eligible"] for r in report["rows"])
    assert not (out / "FAILURE.json").exists()
    packets = (out / "packet_processing.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
    assert len(packets) == len(tape.events)


def test_cli_leaves_a_failure_record_and_no_report_for_a_broken_tape(tmp_path):
    inputs = tmp_path / "in"
    inputs.mkdir()
    tape = healthy(Tape(stop=35000))
    events = tape.events[:5] + tape.events[6:]  # Sequence gap.
    source, plan = inputs / "tape.jsonl.gz", inputs / "plan.json"
    _write_tape(source, events)
    plan.write_text(
        json.dumps(dict(products=[COIN, BTC], first_cutoff=30000, stop_cutoff=35000)),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    assert _cli(source, plan, out).returncode != 0
    failure = json.loads((out / "FAILURE.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert failure["status"] == "failed" and "sequence" in failure["reason"]
    assert not (out / "REPORT.json").exists()


# --- Scope, stated executably ----------------------------------------------------------


def test_a_fully_healthy_tape_still_has_no_positive_eligibility_path():
    tape = healthy(Tape())
    tape.now = 40000
    for ts in range(31000, 40001, 1000):
        for p in (COIN, BTC):
            tape.hb(ts, 10, p)
        if ts % 5000 == 0 and ts < 40000:
            for p in (COIN, BTC):
                tape.snap(ts + 10, p)
    report = tape.rec.seal()
    for row in report["rows"]:
        assert (
            row["eligibility_status"] == "unavailable"
            and row["study_eligible"] is False
        )
        assert NOT_IMPLEMENTED <= set(row["unavailable_reasons"])
    for flag in (
        "full_feed_verified",
        "positive_eligibility_supported",
        "directional_report_adapter_supported",
        "durable_publication_verified",
        "study_frozen",
        "feed_gate_pass",
        "fitting_allowed",
        "live_integration",
    ):
        assert report[flag] is False
    text = json.dumps(report)
    assert 'eligible": true' not in text.lower()


def test_empty_tape_accounts_for_every_row_but_every_component_is_unavailable():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.now = 40000
    report = tape.rec.seal()
    assert report["packet_count"] == 0 and report["recorded_rows"] == 8
    for row in report["rows"]:
        reasons = row["unavailable_reasons"]
        assert any(x.startswith("coin:quote:") for x in reasons)
        assert any(x.startswith("coin:trades:") for x in reasons)
        assert row["components"]["coin"]["trades_30s"]["verified_trade_count"] is None


def test_module_imports_offline_and_touches_no_report_or_target_code():
    code = (
        "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[1]); import instant_input_evidence;"
        "bad = {'instant_directional_exposure','instant_association','instant_targets',"
        " 'instant_diagnostics','micro_replay','socket','ssl','http','urllib.request',"
        " 'requests','websocket','websockets','backend'} & set(sys.modules);"
        "print(sorted(bad)); sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)"
    )
    run = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-I", "-c", code, str(ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment")],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    assert run.returncode == 0, run.stdout + run.stderr


def test_contract_and_sources_are_hashed_into_every_report():
    tape = Tape()
    tape.now = 40000
    report = tape.rec.seal()
    src = (ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_input_evidence.py").read_text(
        "utf-8"
    )
    con = (
        ROOT / "docs/research/INSTANT_INPUT_EVIDENCE_CONTRACT_V1_DRAFT.json"
    ).read_text("utf-8")
    assert report["implementation_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(src.encode()).hexdigest()
    assert report["contract_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(con.encode()).hexdigest()
    assert report["book_implementation_sha256"] == tape.rec.book.source_sha256
    assert (
        report["monitor_implementation_sha256"] == tape.rec.book.monitor.source_sha256
    )
    assert (
        report["inventory_sha256"]
        == hashlib.sha256(tape.rec.inventory.encode()).hexdigest()
    )

"""Contract counterexamples for the November 6 static methodology review.

These synthetic checks preserve existing clocks. They do not implement a
delayed-decision adapter, select a lag threshold or test predictive value.
"""

from dataclasses import replace
import sys

import pytest

from test_instant_targets import ANCHOR, ROOT, baseline, samples, target

sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment"))
import instant_directional_exposure as directional
from instant_publication import publication_timing


@pytest.mark.parametrize("contract", [target.CONTRACT_PATH, target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
def test_sample_availability_cannot_be_redefined_as_just_receipt(contract, direction):
    rows = samples(direction=direction)
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(rows, contract_path=contract)
    base = baseline(
        end_ts_ms=(
            (ANCHOR - 35000) // 60000 * 60000
            if contract == target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH
            else ANCHOR
        )
    )
    assert (
        engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, base, direction, 300, ANCHOR + 300000)["status"]
        == "ready"
    )
    # Even grading much later cannot make the anchor's delayed input known then.
    late = [
        replace(r, available_ts_ms=ANCHOR + 180) if r.ts_ms == ANCHOR else r
        for r in rows
    ]
    result = target.TargetEvaluator(late, contract_path=contract).evaluate(
        ANCHOR, base, direction, 300, ANCHOR + 900000
    )
    assert result["status"] == "unavailable"
    assert result["reason"] == "future_receipt"

===== BEGIN SOURCE 100 | tests/test_instant_pre_ack_book.py | sha256=3d5c3201de5c07d858cc3d22e29249ceb4a60e91846e76cdbac37c9a056129d1 =====
"""Causal handling of a recorder-owned socket's pre-acknowledgment book."""

import pytest

from test_instant_book import Feed, PID


def test_same_ms_snapshot_before_ack_is_activated_without_backdating():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.send(1000, "connection_opened")
    feed.snapshot(4900)
    feed.send(
        4900,
        "subscriptions",
        lag=200,
        channels=[
            dict(name=c, product_ids=[PID])
            for c in ("level2_50", "matches", "heartbeat")
        ],
    )
    feed.send(4950, "heartbeat", lag=250, last_trade_id=10)
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000).reason == "subscription_unconfirmed"
    after_ack = feed.book.view(PID, 5000, asof=5100)
    assert after_ack.reason == "heartbeat_unavailable"
    quote = feed.book.view(PID, 5000, asof=5200)
    assert quote.book_state_available and quote.mid == 100
    assert quote.observation_ts_ms == 4900
    assert quote.available_ts_ms == 5100
    assert feed.book.history[PID][-1].receipt_seq == 3


def test_pre_ack_update_keeps_its_observation_time_but_activates_at_ack():
    feed = Feed()
    feed.send(1000, "connection_opened")
    feed.snapshot(1500)
    feed.update(2700, [["buy", "99", "3"]])
    feed.ack(5000)
    feed.hb(8000)
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000, asof=8500).reason == "subscription_unconfirmed"
    quote = feed.book.view(PID, 10000)
    assert quote.book_state_available and quote.bid_size == "3"
    assert quote.observation_ts_ms == 2700 and quote.observation_age_ms == 7300
    assert feed.book.history[PID][-1].receipt_ts_ms == 5000


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "barrier", ["queue_overflow", "connection_change", "partial_ack", "invalid_update"]
)
def test_staged_snapshot_cannot_cross_a_loss_or_missing_membership(barrier):
    feed = Feed()
    feed.send(1000, "connection_opened")
    feed.snapshot(1500)
    conn = 1
    if barrier == "queue_overflow":
        feed.send(1600, "queue_overflow")
    elif barrier == "connection_change":
        conn = 2
        feed.send(1600, "connection_opened", connection=conn)
    elif barrier == "partial_ack":
        feed.ack(1600, products=())
    else:
        feed.update(1600, [["buy", "103", "1"]])
    feed.ack(2000, connection=conn)
    feed.hb(3000, connection=conn)
    assert feed.book.view(PID, 5000).reason == "awaiting_snapshot"

===== BEGIN SOURCE 101 | tests/test_instant_reader_disconnect.py | sha256=fb455859d4e04836acd0a936787acbd8107f3fb28e8d4e7d5e2a23def739aa37 =====
"""Offline reader termination/queued-prefix probes; no live feed or DNS requests."""

import json
import sqlite3

import pytest
import websocket

from test_instant_source_pilot import acknowledgement
from test_instant_stream_journal import Clock
from instant_real_indexed_pilot import QueuedSocket
from instant_source_pilot import PRODUCTS, WARMUP_MS, capture
from instant_stream_journal import (
    StreamJournal,
    iter_packets,
    iter_pending_ingress,
    read_segment,
)


class TerminalSource:
    def __init__(self, clock, messages):
        self.clock = clock
        self.messages = iter(messages)
        self.closed = False
        self.terminal_ms = None

    def send(self, raw):
        assert json.loads(raw)["type"] == "subscribe"

    def recv(self):
        self.clock.now += 10
        message = next(self.messages, "")
        if isinstance(message, Exception):
            if not isinstance(message, websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException):
                self.terminal_ms = self.clock.now
            raise message
        if message == "":
            self.terminal_ms = self.clock.now
            return ""
        return json.dumps(message)

    def close(self, timeout):
        self.closed = True


class FinishedReader(QueuedSocket):
    def send(self, raw):
        super().send(raw)
        # Deterministically observe the queued prefix and terminal state before
        # the owner can dequeue anything; no scheduler-speed assumptions.
        assert self.done.wait(5), "synthetic reader did not terminate"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("batch_frames", [1, 16])
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "ending, expected_stop, expected_marker",
    [
        (
            websocket.WebSocketConnectionClosedException(
                "Connection to remote host was lost."
            ),
            "transport_error",
            "audit_transport_error",
        ),
        (
            ConnectionResetError(10054, "connection reset"),
            "transport_error",
            "audit_transport_error",
        ),
        ("", "remote_close", "connection_closed"),
    ],
    ids=["websocket-lost", "socket-reset", "empty-return"],
)
def test_transport_termination_drains_exact_prefix_then_preserves_barrier(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys, batch_frames, ending, expected_stop, expected_marker
):
    clock = Clock()
    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock)
    messages = [acknowledgement()] + [
        dict(type="ticker", product_id="BTC-USD", diagnostic_index=i) for i in range(32)
    ]
    source = TerminalSource(clock, [*messages, ending])
    socket = FinishedReader(
        source,
        (websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException,),
        max_frames=256,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
    )
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    connections = []

    def connect():
        connections.append(True)
        return socket

    def after_commit(_):
        # A delayed owner sees the error later than the actual reader detects it.
        clock.now += 100

    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        35000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        indexed=True,
        batch_frames=batch_frames,
    ) as journal:
        result = capture(
            journal,
            connect,
            monotonic=lambda: clock.now / 1000,
            duration_ns=clock.mono,
            timeout_errors=(TimeoutError,),
            transport_errors=(OSError, websocket.WebSocketException),
            max_connections=1,
            after_commit=after_commit,
            progress=lambda *args, **kwargs: None,
        )
        journal.finish()
    assert result["stop"] == expected_stop
    assert len(connections) == 1
    attempt = result["attempts"][0]
    if isinstance(ending, Exception):
        assert socket.failure is ending
        assert attempt["error"] == str(ending)
        # This is a diagnostic limitation, not evidence of a new connection bug:
        # the reader keeps an exception object but the report only saves text.
        assert "error_type" not in attempt and "errno" not in attempt
    else:
        assert socket.failure is None and "error" not in attempt
    assert socket.reader_report() == dict(
        received=33,
        delivered=33,
        received_not_delivered=0,
        sampled_high_water=33,
        overflow=None,
    )
    packets = list(iter_packets(path))
    assert [row["envelope"]["message"] for row in packets[1:-1]] == json.loads(
        json.dumps(messages)
    )
    assert packets[-1]["envelope"]["message"] == {"type": expected_marker}
    assert packets[-1]["envelope"]["receipt_ts_ms"] > source.terminal_ms
    assert result["last_reader_receipt_ts_ms"] == 330
    assert result["last_reader_receipt_ts_ms"] - result["first_ack_ts_ms"] < WARMUP_MS
    assert len({row["envelope"]["connection_id"] for row in packets}) == 1
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "finished"
    assert not list(iter_pending_ingress(path))
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute("PRAGMA quick_check").fetchone() == ("ok",)
        assert db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM publications").fetchone() == (0,)
    assert source.closed
    # Transport loss has no immediate stderr event in the current reader.
    assert capsys.readouterr().err == ""


def test_socket_timeout_is_retried_without_poisoning_later_data(monkeypatch):
    clock = Clock()
    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock)
    source = TerminalSource(
        clock, [websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException("idle"), acknowledgement(), ""]
    )
    socket = FinishedReader(
        source, (websocket.WebSocketTimeoutException,), monotonic_ns=clock.mono
    )
    try:
        socket.send('{"type":"subscribe"}')
        assert socket.failure is None
        assert json.loads(socket.recv().raw) == json.loads(
            json.dumps(acknowledgement())
        )
        assert socket.recv() == ""
        assert socket.reader_report()["received"] == 1
    finally:
        socket.close(timeout=1)


def test_immediate_disconnect_is_rethrown_after_empty_queue_without_new_frames(
    monkeypatch,
):
    clock = Clock()
    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock)
    ending = websocket.WebSocketConnectionClosedException(
        "Connection to remote host was lost."
    )
    socket = FinishedReader(
        TerminalSource(clock, [ending]), (), monotonic_ns=clock.mono
    )
    try:
        socket.send('{"type":"subscribe"}')
        with pytest.raises(websocket.WebSocketConnectionClosedException) as caught:
            socket.recv()
        assert caught.value is ending
        assert socket.recv_ready(16) == []
        assert socket.reader_report()["received"] == 0
        assert socket.reader_report()["overflow"] is None
    finally:
        socket.close(timeout=1)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "wire_bytes", [b"", b"\x88\x02\x03\xe8"], ids=["stream-eof", "websocket-close-1000"]
)
def test_pinned_websocket_library_distinguishes_stream_eof_and_close_frame(wire_bytes):
    class Wire:
        def __init__(self, data):
            self.data = data

        def recv(self, size):
            result, self.data = self.data[:size], self.data[size:]
            return result

        def send(self, data):
            return len(data)

        def gettimeout(self):
            return 1

        def close(self):
            pass

    ws = websocket.WebSocket()
    ws.sock = Wire(wire_bytes)
    ws.connected = True
    try:
        if wire_bytes:
            # recv() intentionally discards the protocol close code/reason.
            assert ws.recv() == ""
        else:
            with pytest.raises(
                websocket.WebSocketConnectionClosedException,
                match="Connection to remote host was lost",
            ):
                ws.recv()
    finally:
        ws.shutdown()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 102 | tests/test_instant_reader_overflow.py | sha256=ad2c0c79d3e2d4c42a7a509307ab82bf1ec5c642177800f33505bd1be1b516b1 =====
"""Offline, event-gated queue overloads; no network or scheduler-speed assertions."""

import json
import sqlite3
from threading import Event

import pytest

from test_instant_source_pilot import acknowledgement
from instant_real_indexed_pilot import QueuedSocket
from instant_source_pilot import PRODUCTS, QueueOverflowError, capture
from instant_stream_journal import (
    MAX_FRAME_BYTES,
    StreamJournal,
    iter_packets,
    iter_pending_ingress,
)


class ArrivalClock:
    """Only the synthetic socket advances elapsed time; no real sleeps."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.ns = 1_000_000_000

    def mono(self):
        return self.ns

    def wall(self):
        return self.ns // 1_000_000


class GatedFrames:
    def __init__(self, clock, count, interval_ns, *, ack=False):
        self.clock, self.count, self.interval_ns = clock, count, interval_ns
        self.ack = ack
        self.release = Event()
        self.closed = False

    def send(self, raw):
        pass

    def recv(self):
        if self.ack:
            self.ack = False
            return json.dumps(acknowledgement())
        assert self.release.wait(5), "writer never released the synthetic source"
        if not self.count:
            return ""
        self.count -= 1
        self.clock.ns += self.interval_ns
        return '{"type":"ticker","product_id":"BTC-USD"}'

    def abort(self):
        self.release.set()

    def close(self, timeout):
        self.closed = True


def legacy_counts(report):
    return {
        key: report[key]
        for key in (
            "received",
            "delivered",
            "received_not_delivered",
            "sampled_high_water",
        )
    }


def test_burst_without_writer_and_slow_arrivals_during_stall_share_old_counts(
    monkeypatch, capsys
):
    reports = []
    for phase, interval_ns in (
        ("between_callbacks", 100_000),
        ("journal_callback", 20_000_000),
    ):
        clock = ArrivalClock()
        monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock.wall)
        source = GatedFrames(clock, 257, interval_ns)
        socket = QueuedSocket(
            source, (TimeoutError,), max_frames=256, monotonic_ns=clock.mono
        )
        try:
            # For the stall case this labels the blocked owner. The integration
            # test below also stalls the real journal's persistence operation.
            with socket.writer_activity(phase, 1 if phase == "journal_callback" else 0):
                socket.send("{}")
                source.release.set()
                assert socket.done.wait(5)
                reports.append(socket.reader_report())
            with pytest.raises(QueueOverflowError, match="frame cap"):
                socket.recv()
            assert socket.recv_ready(16) == []
        finally:
            socket.close(timeout=1)
        assert source.closed
    burst, stall = reports
    assert (
        legacy_counts(burst)
        == legacy_counts(stall)
        == dict(
            received=257,
            delivered=0,
            received_not_delivered=257,
            sampled_high_water=256,
        )
    )
    burst, stall = burst["overflow"], stall["overflow"]
    assert burst["arrival_windows_ms"]["100"] == {"returned": 257, "lower_bound": True}
    assert burst["retained_return_span_ms"] == pytest.approx(25.6)
    assert burst["writer"]["phase"] == "between_callbacks"
    assert stall["arrival_windows_ms"]["100"] == {"returned": 6, "lower_bound": False}
    assert stall["retained_return_span_ms"] == 5120
    assert stall["writer"] == {
        "phase": "journal_callback",
        "frames": 1,
        "elapsed_ms": 5140,
    }
    assert stall["oldest_queued_age_ms"] == 5120
    logs = [
        json.loads(line.removeprefix("READER_OVERFLOW "))
        for line in capsys.readouterr().err.splitlines()
    ]
    assert logs == [burst, stall]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("capacity", [4, 256])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("interval_ns", [100_000, 20_000_000], ids=["burst", "paced"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("phase", ["journal_callback", "after_commit"])
def test_real_capture_freezes_failure_context_before_stalled_owner_recovers(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys, capacity, interval_ns, phase
):
    clock = ArrivalClock()
    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock.wall)
    source = GatedFrames(clock, capacity + 1, interval_ns, ack=True)
    socket = QueuedSocket(
        source, (TimeoutError,), max_frames=capacity, monotonic_ns=clock.mono
    )
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    frozen = []

    def stall_owner():
        source.release.set()
        assert socket.done.wait(5), "reader failed to fill bounded queue"
        # Snapshot and stderr must already exist before the journal/hook resumes.
        frozen.append(socket.reader_report()["overflow"])
        assert frozen[-1]["writer"]["phase"] == phase
        log = capsys.readouterr().err.strip()
        assert json.loads(log.removeprefix("READER_OVERFLOW ")) == frozen[-1]
        clock.ns += 100_000_000  # Recovery must not rewrite failure-time clocks.

    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        35000,
        clock_ms=clock.wall,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        indexed=True,
        batch_frames=16,
    ) as journal:
        persist = journal._persist

        def blocked_persist(rows, *, pending=False):
            if pending and phase == "journal_callback":
                stall_owner()
            return persist(rows, pending=pending)

        monkeypatch.setattr(journal, "_persist", blocked_persist)
        result = capture(
            journal,
            lambda: socket,
            monotonic=lambda: clock.ns / 1e9,
            duration_ns=clock.mono,
            max_connections=1,
            after_commit=(lambda _: stall_owner()) if phase == "after_commit" else None,
            progress=lambda *args, **kwargs: None,
        )
        assert result["stop"] == "queue_overflow"
        journal.finish()
    report = socket.reader_report()
    assert report["overflow"] == frozen[0]
    assert report["overflow"]["writer"]["frames"] == (
        1 if phase == "journal_callback" else 0
    )
    assert report["overflow"]["queue_depth"] == capacity
    assert (
        report["overflow"]["received"] == capacity + 2
    )  # ACK, queued prefix, rejected frame
    assert report["overflow"]["delivered"] == 1
    assert report["received_not_delivered"] == capacity + 1
    assert result["attempts"][0]["error"] == "socket reader queue reached its frame cap"
    legacy_callback_ms = result["latencies"]["subscriptions"][
        "callback_through_commit"
    ]["max_ms"]
    if phase == "journal_callback":
        assert legacy_callback_ms == pytest.approx(
            (capacity + 1) * interval_ns / 1e6 + 100, abs=0.001
        )
    else:
        # The old callback metric entirely misses a stalled post-commit hook.
        assert legacy_callback_ms == 0
    packets = list(iter_packets(path))
    assert [row["envelope"]["message"]["type"] for row in packets] == [
        "connection_opened",
        "subscriptions",
        "queue_overflow",
    ]
    assert not list(iter_pending_ingress(path))
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute("PRAGMA quick_check").fetchone() == ("ok",)
    # A caller mutating its report cannot change the retained evidence.
    report["overflow"]["writer"]["phase"] = "changed"
    assert socket.reader_report()["overflow"] == frozen[0]


def test_oversize_is_distinct_from_queue_saturation(monkeypatch, capsys):
    clock = ArrivalClock()
    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", lambda: 123)
    source = GatedFrames(clock, 0, 0)
    source.recv = lambda: "x" * (MAX_FRAME_BYTES + 1)
    socket = QueuedSocket(source, (TimeoutError,), monotonic_ns=clock.mono)
    try:
        socket.send("{}")
        assert socket.done.wait(5)
        with pytest.raises(QueueOverflowError, match="frame bound"):
            socket.recv()
        snapshot = socket.reader_report()["overflow"]
        assert snapshot["reason"] == "frame_bytes_exceeded"
        assert snapshot["queue_depth"] == 0
        assert snapshot["oldest_queued_age_ms"] is None
        assert snapshot["receipt_ts_ms"] == 123
        assert snapshot["writer"]["elapsed_ms"] == 0
    finally:
        socket.close(timeout=1)
    assert capsys.readouterr().err.count("READER_OVERFLOW ") == 1


def test_subcapacity_burst_drains_in_order_without_loss(monkeypatch):
    clock = ArrivalClock()
    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock.wall)
    source = GatedFrames(clock, 16, 100_000)
    socket = QueuedSocket(
        source, (TimeoutError,), max_frames=16, monotonic_ns=clock.mono
    )
    try:
        socket.send("{}")
        source.release.set()
        assert socket.done.wait(5)
        frames = [socket.recv(), *socket.recv_ready(15)]
        assert [frame.receipt_ns for frame in frames] == [
            1_000_000_000 + i * 100_000 for i in range(1, 17)
        ]
        assert socket.recv() == ""
        assert socket.reader_report() == dict(
            received=16,
            delivered=16,
            received_not_delivered=0,
            sampled_high_water=16,
            overflow=None,
        )
    finally:
        socket.close(timeout=1)

===== BEGIN SOURCE 103 | tests/test_instant_real_indexed_pilot.py | sha256=d70c7c445b4f44c985f299488076aa37e5e4a83be3bb929837e0c305e9534d16 =====
"""Exercise reader timing, overflow and separate outbox observation."""

from dataclasses import asdict
import json
import sys
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from test_instant_source_pilot import FakeSocket, acknowledgement
from test_instant_stream_journal import Clock
from instant_delayed_decision import prepare_decision
from instant_real_indexed_pilot import (
    QUEUE_CAPACITY,
    DiagnosticCohorts,
    QueuedSocket,
    _observe,
    _observation_report,
    main,
)
from instant_source_pilot import (
    PRODUCTS,
    WARMUP_MS,
    PilotCallbacks,
    QueueOverflowError,
    ReceivedFrame,
    capture,
)
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal, iter_packets


def test_original_reader_stamp_survives_the_writer_queue(tmp_path):
    clock = Clock()
    journal = StreamJournal(
        tmp_path / "capture.sqlite",
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        35000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        indexed=True,
    )
    with journal:
        callbacks = PilotCallbacks(journal, duration_ns=clock.mono)
        callbacks.on_open(FakeSocket(clock, []))
        clock.now = 180
        callbacks.on_message(
            ReceivedFrame(json.dumps(acknowledgement()), 100, 100000000)
        )
        assert callbacks.ack == 100
        assert callbacks.last_reader_receipt_ts_ms == 100
        assert (
            callbacks.report()["latencies"]["subscriptions"]["reader_to_writer"][
                "p95_ms"
            ]
            == 80
        )
        callbacks.on_close()
    row = list(iter_packets(tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"))[1]
    assert row["processing"]["receipt_ts_ms"] == 100
    assert row["processing"]["available_ts_ms"] == 180


def test_reader_overflow_stops_before_dequeueing_across_loss():
    raw = FakeSocket(Clock(), [dict(type="ticker")] * (QUEUE_CAPACITY + 1))
    socket = QueuedSocket(raw, (TimeoutError,))
    socket.send("{}")
    assert socket.done.wait(2)
    with pytest.raises(QueueOverflowError, match="frame cap"):
        socket.recv()
    assert socket.delivered == 0
    socket.close(timeout=1)
    assert raw.closed


def test_capture_persists_queue_loss_as_explicit_barrier(tmp_path):
    clock = Clock()
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    raw = FakeSocket(clock, [acknowledgement(), QueueOverflowError("limit")])
    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        35000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
    ) as journal:
        result = capture(
            journal,
            lambda: raw,
            monotonic=lambda: clock.now / 1000,
            duration_ns=clock.mono,
            max_connections=1,
            progress=lambda *args, **kwargs: None,
        )
        assert result["stop"] == "queue_overflow"
        assert result["attempts"][0]["error"] == "limit"
    assert (
        list(iter_packets(path))[-1]["envelope"]["message"]["type"] == "queue_overflow"
    )


def test_observer_excludes_pending_and_detects_bad_clock(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    clock = Clock()
    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        35000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        indexed=True,
    ) as journal:
        clock.now = 30000
        snapshot = prepare_decision(
            journal.evidence, PRODUCTS[0], 30000, "up", asof=30000
        )
        receipt = journal.append_publication("ready", snapshot)
        with journal._db:
            journal._db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO publications SELECT 'pending', decision_sha256, body, digest, NULL FROM publications"
            )
        stop = tmp_path / "observer.stop"
        stop.touch()
        _observe(path, tmp_path / "observed.jsonl", stop)
    original = dict(asdict(receipt))
    original["commit_observed_ts_ms"] = original.pop("local_commit_observed_ts_ms")
    rows = [dict(original, cutoff_ts_ms=30000)]
    report = _observation_report(tmp_path, rows)
    assert report["missing_or_invalid"] == 0
    assert report["commit_observed_to_reader_seen"]["n"] == 1
    assert rows[0]["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] > 30000
    with (tmp_path / "observed.jsonl").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        out.write('{"unfinished":\n')
    assert _observation_report(tmp_path, rows)["discrepancies"] == {
        "unreadable_observation": 1
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "profile_args, profile, queue_frames, batch_frames",
    [
        ([], "single-128", 128, 1),
        (["--profile", "batched-256"], "batched-256", 256, 16),
    ],
)
def test_real_cli_selects_and_records_the_writer_profile(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, profile_args, profile, queue_frames, batch_frames
):
    path = tmp_path / "pilot"
    socket = FakeSocket(Clock(), [acknowledgement(), dict(type="ticker")])
    socket.settimeout = lambda _timeout: None

    def open_socket(*args, **kwargs):
        plan = json.loads((path / "PLAN.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert plan["profile"] == profile
        assert plan["max_reader_queue_frames"] == queue_frames
        assert plan["max_batch_frames"] == batch_frames
        return socket

    websocket = SimpleNamespace(
        create_connection=open_socket,
        WebSocketTimeoutException=TimeoutError,
        WebSocketException=OSError,
    )
    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "websocket", websocket)
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, "argv", ["pilot", "--out-dir", str(path), *profile_args])

    main()

    report = json.loads((path / "REPORT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert report["stop"] == "remote_close"
    assert report["segment"]["manifest"]["batch_frames"] == batch_frames
    assert report["recovered_pending_ingress_frames"] == 0
    assert report["reader"][0]["received_not_delivered"] == 0
    assert report["reader"][0]["overflow"] is None
    data = list(iter_packets(path / "capture.sqlite"))[1:-1]
    assert [row["envelope"]["message"]["type"] for row in data] == [
        "subscriptions",
        "ticker",
    ]
    assert all(
        ("ingress_commit_observed_ts_ms" in row) == (batch_frames > 1) for row in data
    )


def test_first_cohort_uses_the_ack_stamp_even_when_batch_ends_on_next_grid(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    clock = Clock()

    class BatchSocket(FakeSocket):
        def recv(self):
            if clock.now:
                return ""
            clock.now = 6000
            return ReceivedFrame(json.dumps(acknowledgement()), 4999, 4999000000)

        def recv_ready(self, limit):
            assert limit >= 1
            return [ReceivedFrame('{"type":"ticker"}', 5001, 5001000000)]

    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock)
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        4320000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        batch_frames=16,
    ) as journal:
        cohorts = DiagnosticCohorts(journal, tmp_path)

        def after_commit(first_ack):
            assert len(list(iter_packets(path))) == 3
            cohorts.after_commit(first_ack)

        result = capture(
            journal,
            lambda: BatchSocket(clock, []),
            duration_ns=clock.mono,
            monotonic=lambda: clock.now / 1000,
            max_connections=1,
            after_commit=after_commit,
            progress=lambda *args, **kwargs: None,
        )
        assert result["first_ack_ts_ms"] == 4999
        assert result["last_reader_receipt_ts_ms"] == 5001
        assert cohorts.next_cutoff == 5000 + WARMUP_MS


def test_wider_roster_is_declared_before_subscribing_and_missing_slots_are_counted(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    from instant_real_indexed_pilot import TIMING_PRODUCTS

    class EuropeClock(datetime):
        @classmethod
        def now(cls, tz):
            return datetime(2026, 11, 9, 8, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    path = tmp_path / "pilot"
    ack = acknowledgement()
    for channel in ack["channels"]:
        channel["product_ids"] = TIMING_PRODUCTS
    socket = FakeSocket(Clock(), [ack])
    socket.settimeout = lambda _timeout: None

    def open_socket(*args, **kwargs):
        plan = json.loads((path / "PLAN.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert plan["products"] == list(TIMING_PRODUCTS)
        assert plan["cohort_evidence_offsets_ms"] == [5000, 1500] * 10
        assert plan["diagnostic_deadlines_ms"] == {"1500": 2500, "5000": 6000}
        return socket

    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.datetime", EuropeClock)
    monkeypatch.setitem(
        sys.modules,
        "websocket",
        SimpleNamespace(
            create_connection=open_socket,
            WebSocketTimeoutException=TimeoutError,
            WebSocketException=OSError,
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        sys,
        "argv",
        [
            "pilot",
            "--out-dir",
            str(path),
            "--profile",
            "eligibility-timing-256",
            "--window",
            "europe",
        ],
    )
    main()

    assert socket.sent[0]["product_ids"] == list(TIMING_PRODUCTS)
    result = json.loads((path / "REPORT.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert result["first_ack_ts_ms"] is not None
    assert result["diagnostic_cohorts"] == {"planned": 20, "complete": 0}
    for arm in result["timing_readiness"].values():
        assert set(arm["products"]) == set(TIMING_PRODUCTS)
        assert all(
            row["planned_rows"] == 20 and row["observed_rows"] == 0
            for row in arm["products"].values()
        )


def test_staggered_cohorts_use_their_own_asof_and_actual_observer_clock(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    from instant_real_indexed_pilot import TIMING_PRODUCTS, _timing_readiness

    clock = Clock()
    first = 5000 + WARMUP_MS
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr("instant_real_indexed_pilot.utc_ms", clock)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "instant_real_indexed_pilot.subprocess.Popen",
        lambda args, **kwargs: calls.append(args) or object(),
    )
    with StreamJournal(
        tmp_path / "capture.sqlite",
        TIMING_PRODUCTS,
        first,
        first + 200000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        indexed=True,
    ) as journal:
        cohorts = DiagnosticCohorts(
            journal,
            tmp_path,
            products=TIMING_PRODUCTS,
            offsets=(1500, 5000) * 10,
            step_ms=10000,
        )
        clock.now = first + 1499
        cohorts.after_commit(4999)
        assert len(cohorts.rows) == 0
        assert calls[0][-2:] == ["--observer-limit-s", "240"]
        clock.now = first + 1500
        cohorts.after_commit(4999)
        clock.now = first + 15000
        cohorts.after_commit(4999)
        assert [r["knowledge_asof_ts_ms"] for r in cohorts.rows] == (
            [first + 1500] * 12 + [first + 15000] * 12
        )
        cohorts.rows[0]["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] = first + 2501
        cohorts.rows[12]["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] = first + 16000
        report = _timing_readiness(cohorts)
    for offset, bound in (("1500", 0), ("5000", 1)):
        btc = report[offset]["products"]["BTC-USD"]
        assert (btc["planned_rows"], btc["committed_rows"], btc["observed_rows"]) == (
            20,
            2,
            1,
        )
        assert btc["observed_by_bound_rows"] == bound
        assert btc["complete_inputs_rows"] == 0

===== BEGIN SOURCE 104 | tests/test_instant_source_pilot.py | sha256=5f1ae2f02a86bef1a226ab4f398e765640487c320624d9f81d4e75b33af182d7 =====
"""Dry-run the public socket callback path without opening a network socket."""

import json

import pytest

from test_instant_stream_journal import Clock
from instant_source_pilot import (
    CHANNELS,
    PRODUCTS,
    PilotCallbacks,
    ReceivedFrame,
    capture,
    _coverage,
)
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal, iter_packets


def acknowledgement():
    return dict(
        type="subscriptions",
        channels=[dict(name=channel, product_ids=PRODUCTS) for channel in CHANNELS],
    )


class FakeSocket:
    def __init__(self, clock, frames):
        self.clock = clock
        self.frames = iter(frames)
        self.closed = False
        self.sent = []

    def send(self, raw):
        self.sent.append(json.loads(raw))

    def recv(self):
        self.clock.now += 1000
        item = next(self.frames, "")
        if isinstance(item, Exception):
            raise item
        return json.dumps(item) if isinstance(item, dict) else item

    def close(self, timeout):
        self.closed = True


def run(tmp_path, frames, **options):
    path = tmp_path / "fake.sqlite"
    clock = Clock()
    sockets = [FakeSocket(clock, script) for script in frames]
    connections = iter(sockets)
    with StreamJournal(
        path, PRODUCTS, 30000, 180000, clock_ms=clock, monotonic_ns=clock.mono
    ) as journal:
        result = capture(
            journal,
            lambda: next(connections),
            monotonic=lambda: clock.now / 1000,
            duration_ns=clock.mono,
            progress=lambda *args, **kwargs: None,
            **options,
        )
        if result["first_ack_ts_ms"] is not None:
            result["coverage"] = _coverage(
                journal.evidence, result["first_ack_ts_ms"], clock.now
            )
        journal.finish()
    return result, list(iter_packets(path)), sockets


def test_callbacks_subscribe_record_a_reconnect_and_keep_trade_side_pairs(tmp_path):
    trade = dict(
        product_id="BTC-USD",
        trade_id=12,
        price="100",
        size="1",
        side="sell",
        time="1970-01-01T00:00:00.500Z",
    )
    result, packets, sockets = run(
        tmp_path,
        [
            [acknowledgement(), dict(type="match", **trade), OSError("lost transport")],
            [
                acknowledgement(),
                dict(type="ticker", **(trade | {"side": "buy"})),
                "",
            ],
        ],
    )
    assert all(socket.closed for socket in sockets)
    assert sockets[0].sent == [
        dict(type="subscribe", channels=list(CHANNELS), product_ids=list(PRODUCTS))
    ]
    assert result["first_ack_ts_ms"] == 1000
    assert result["stop"] == "remote_close"
    assert result["attempts"][0]["stop"] == "transport_error"
    assert (
        result["attempts"][0]["connection_id"] != result["attempts"][1]["connection_id"]
    )
    assert result["ticker_match_sides"]["BTC-USD"] == dict(paired=1, opposite=1)
    assert (
        result["latencies"]["match:BTC-USD"]["exchange_stamp_to_callback"]["p95_ms"]
        == 1500
    )
    assert any(
        r["envelope"]["message"]["type"] == "audit_transport_error" for r in packets
    )
    assert len({r["envelope"]["connection_id"] for r in packets}) == 2


def test_bounds_stop_cleanly_with_space_for_the_final_marker(tmp_path):
    result, packets, sockets = run(
        tmp_path,
        [[acknowledgement(), dict(type="ticker", product_id="BTC-USD")]],
        packet_stop=3,
    )
    assert result["stop"] == "packet_budget"
    assert len(packets) == 4
    assert packets[-1]["envelope"]["message"]["type"] == "connection_closed"
    assert sockets[0].closed


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("frames", "stop"),
    [
        ([TimeoutError()] * 30, "transport_idle"),
        ([acknowledgement(), "invalid-json"], "callback_failure"),
        (
            [dict(type="error", message="invalid subscription")],
            "exchange_or_source_error",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_failed_feed_preserves_termination_evidence(tmp_path, frames, stop):
    result, packets, sockets = run(tmp_path, [frames])
    assert result["stop"] == stop
    assert sockets[0].closed
    assert packets[-1]["envelope"]["message"]["type"] in (
        "audit_parse_error",
        "audit_transport_error",
        "error",
    )


def test_duration_does_not_restart_after_first_acknowledgment(tmp_path):
    result, packets, _ = run(
        tmp_path,
        [
            [
                acknowledgement(),
                dict(type="ticker"),
                dict(type="ticker"),
                dict(type="ticker"),
            ]
        ],
        duration_s=2,
    )
    assert result["stop"] == "duration"
    assert [p["envelope"]["message"]["type"] for p in packets] == [
        "connection_opened",
        "subscriptions",
        "ticker",
        "ticker",
        "connection_closed",
    ]


def test_exchange_legacy_ack_name_activates_book_and_heartbeat(tmp_path):
    ack = acknowledgement()
    ack["channels"][0]["name"] = "level2_50"
    result, packets, _ = run(
        tmp_path,
        [
            [
                ack,
                dict(type="heartbeat", product_id="BTC-USD", last_trade_id=50),
                dict(
                    type="snapshot",
                    product_id="BTC-USD",
                    bids=[["99", "1"]],
                    asks=[["101", "2"]],
                ),
                dict(type="heartbeat", product_id="BTC-USD", last_trade_id=50),
            ]
        ],
        duration_s=3,
    )
    assert result["first_ack_ts_ms"] == 1000
    assert result["stop"] == "duration"
    assert packets[1]["envelope"]["message"]["channels"][0]["name"] == "level2_50"
    assert result["frames"]["snapshot:BTC-USD"] == 1


def test_callback_duration_is_independent_of_receipt_wall_clock(tmp_path):
    clock = Clock()
    socket = FakeSocket(clock, [])
    samples = iter([1_000_000, 1_125_000])
    with StreamJournal(
        tmp_path / "clocks.sqlite",
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        35000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
    ) as journal:
        callbacks = PilotCallbacks(journal, duration_ns=lambda: next(samples))
        callbacks.on_open(socket)
        callbacks.on_message(json.dumps(acknowledgement()))
        callbacks.on_close()
        metrics = callbacks.report()["latencies"]["subscriptions"]
        assert metrics["callback_through_commit"]["p95_ms"] == 0.125
        assert metrics["receipt_to_processing"]["p95_ms"] == 0


def test_batch_ack_uses_membership_at_the_original_receipt(tmp_path):
    class Socket:
        def send(self, raw):
            pass

    early = acknowledgement()
    next(channel for channel in early["channels"] if channel["name"] == "matches")[
        "product_ids"
    ] = [PRODUCTS[0]]
    now = 0
    with StreamJournal(
        tmp_path / "batch-ack.sqlite",
        PRODUCTS,
        30000,
        35000,
        batch_frames=4,
        clock_ms=lambda: now,
        monotonic_ns=lambda: 0,
    ) as journal:
        callbacks = PilotCallbacks(journal, duration_ns=lambda: 4000000000)
        callbacks.on_open(Socket())
        now = 4000
        callbacks.on_batch(
            [
                ReceivedFrame(json.dumps(ack), stamp, stamp * 1000000)
                for stamp, ack in [
                    (1000, early),
                    (2000, acknowledgement()),
                    (3000, acknowledgement()),
                ]
            ]
        )
        assert callbacks.ack == 2000
        assert callbacks.last_reader_receipt_ts_ms == 3000
        assert callbacks.frames["subscriptions"] == 3
