"""Synthetic streaming, transactional write and restart probes."""

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from dataclasses import asdict
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

from test_instant_input_evidence_review import BTC, COIN
from instant_delayed_decision import prepare_decision
from instant_stream_journal import (
    MAX_FRAME_BYTES,
    StreamJournal,
    iter_packets,
    iter_pending_ingress,
    read_local_publication,
    read_segment,
)


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.now = 0
        self.steps = []

    def __call__(self):
        if self.steps:
            self.now = self.steps.pop(0)
        return self.now

    def mono(self):
        return self.now * 1000000


def journal(path, clock, **bounds):
    return StreamJournal(
        path,
        [COIN, BTC],
        30000,
        35000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        **bounds,
    )


def send(stream, connection, **fields):
    return stream.receive(connection, json.dumps(fields))


def subscribe(stream, connection):
    send(
        stream,
        connection,
        type="subscriptions",
        channels=[
            dict(name=c, product_ids=[COIN, BTC])
            for c in ("matches", "heartbeat", "level2_batch")
        ],
    )


def test_reconnect_allocates_real_lifecycle_ids_and_persists_first_trade(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    clock = Clock()
    with journal(path, clock) as stream:
        first = stream.open_connection()
        subscribe(stream, first)
        clock.now = 1000
        send(stream, first, type="heartbeat", product_id=COIN, last_trade_id=9)
        clock.now = 2000
        message = dict(
            type="match",
            product_id=COIN,
            trade_id=10,
            price="100",
            size="2",
            side="sell",
        )
        result = send(stream, first, **message)
        stream.end_connection(first, reason="queue_overflow")
        second = stream.open_connection()
        assert first != second
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="active connection"):
            send(stream, first, **message)
        subscribe(stream, second)
        duplicate = send(stream, second, **message)
        stream.end_connection(second)
        stream.finish()
        assert (
            result.receipt_ts_ms
            <= result.processing_completed_ts_ms
            <= result.commit_observed_ts_ms
        )
    rows = list(iter_packets(path))
    original, repeated = rows[result.receipt_seq - 1], rows[duplicate.receipt_seq - 1]
    assert original["trade"]["connection_id"] == first
    assert original["trade"]["taker_side"] == "buy"
    assert original["processing"]["available_ts_ms"] == 2001
    assert repeated["trade"] is None
    assert repeated["envelope"]["connection_id"] == second
    assert any(r["envelope"]["message"]["type"] == "queue_overflow" for r in rows)
    report = read_segment(path)
    assert report["status"] == "finished"
    assert report["packets"] == len(rows)
    assert report["manifest"]["synchronous"] == 3
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        journal(path, clock)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "frame", [b"\xff", "[]", '{"type":"heartbeat","last_trade_id":NaN}']
)
def test_invalid_frame_leaves_a_durable_barrier_and_requires_a_new_connection(
    tmp_path, frame
):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock()) as stream:
        first = stream.open_connection()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="invalid WebSocket frame"):
            stream.receive(first, frame)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="active connection"):
            send(stream, first, type="heartbeat")
        second = stream.open_connection()
        assert second != first
    packets = list(iter_packets(path))
    marker = packets[1]["envelope"]["message"]
    assert marker["type"] == "audit_parse_error"
    assert marker["frame_bytes"] > 0
    assert len(marker["frame_sha256"]) == 64
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "recording"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("bounds", "status", "expected"),
    [
        ({"max_packets": 1}, "packet_limit", 1),
        ({"max_record_bytes": 1}, "record_byte_limit", 0),
    ],
)
def test_journal_limits_keep_only_committed_prefix(tmp_path, bounds, status, expected):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock(), **bounds) as stream:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="limit exceeded"):
            connection = stream.open_connection()
            send(stream, connection, type="ticker")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not writable"):
            stream.open_connection()
        assert stream.evidence.failed
    recovered = read_segment(path)
    assert recovered["status"] == status
    assert recovered["packets"] == expected
    assert len(list(iter_packets(path))) == expected


def test_frame_bound_and_failed_packet_are_visible(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "oversize.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock()) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="frame byte limit"):
            stream.receive(connection, b" " * (MAX_FRAME_BYTES + 1))
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "frame_limit"

    path = tmp_path / "invalid-match.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock()) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="trade_id"):
            send(stream, connection, type="match", product_id=COIN)
        assert stream.evidence.failed
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "packet_processing_failed"
    assert len(list(iter_packets(path))) == 1


def test_failed_transaction_cannot_acknowledge_an_uncommitted_packet(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock()) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        stream._db.execute(
            """
            CREATE TRIGGER refuse_packets BEFORE INSERT ON packets
            BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'simulated disk write failure'); END
            """
        )
        with pytest.raises(
            sqlite3.IntegrityError, match="simulated disk write failure"
        ):
            send(stream, connection, type="ticker")
        assert stream.evidence.failed
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not writable"):
            stream.end_connection(connection)
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "recording"
    assert len(list(iter_packets(path))) == 1


def test_local_publication_is_reopenable_and_idempotent(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    clock = Clock()
    with journal(path, clock) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        clock.now = 30000
        decision = prepare_decision(stream.evidence, COIN, 30000, "up", asof=30000)
        other = prepare_decision(stream.evidence, COIN, 30000, "down", asof=30000)
        original_ack = stream._acknowledge

        def observe_pending(publication_id, observed):
            receipt, record = read_local_publication(path, publication_id)
            assert receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms is None
            assert record["decision"] == asdict(decision)
            original_ack(publication_id, observed)

        monkeypatch.setattr(stream, "_acknowledge", observe_pending)
        clock.steps = [30000, 30008]
        published = stream.append_publication("local-1", decision)
        clock.now += 7
        assert stream.append_publication("local-1", decision) == published
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="another decision"):
            stream.append_publication("local-1", other)
        stream.end_connection(connection)
        stream.finish()
    assert published.local_commit_observed_ts_ms == 30008
    assert read_local_publication(path, "local-1")[0] == published
    assert read_segment(path)["publications"] == 1
    assert read_local_publication(path, "missing") is None


def test_hard_exit_rolls_back_uncommitted_publication_observation(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "interrupted.sqlite"
    source = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts" / "scoring_experiment")
    child = """
import os
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[1])
from instant_delayed_decision import prepare_decision
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal
with StreamJournal(sys.argv[2], ['ALT-USD', 'BTC-USD'], 30000, 35000, clock_ms=lambda: 30000) as journal:
    journal.open_connection()
    decision = prepare_decision(journal.evidence, 'ALT-USD', 30000, 'up', asof=30000)
    def interrupt(publication_id, observed):
        journal._db.execute(
            'UPDATE publications SET local_commit_observed_ts_ms = ? WHERE publication_id = ?',
            (observed, publication_id),
        )
        os._exit(23)
    journal._acknowledge = interrupt
    journal.append_publication('interrupted', decision)
"""
    result = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-c", child, source, str(path)],
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=30,
    )
    assert result.returncode == 23, result.stderr.decode()
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "recording"
    assert len(list(iter_packets(path))) == 1
    receipt, _ = read_local_publication(path, "interrupted")
    assert receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms is None
    with journal(tmp_path / "new.sqlite", Clock()) as stream:
        first = next(iter_packets(path))["envelope"]["connection_id"]
        assert stream.open_connection() != first


def test_archive_reader_detects_packet_damage(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock()) as stream:
        stream.open_connection()
    db = sqlite3.connect(path)
    try:
        with db:
            db.execute("UPDATE packets SET body = '{}'")
    finally:
        db.close()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="digest mismatch"):
        list(iter_packets(path))


def test_foreign_writer_cannot_mutate_the_in_memory_or_committed_prefix(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock()) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as workers:
            write = workers.submit(send, stream, connection, type="ticker")
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="owner thread"):
                write.result()
        assert len(stream.evidence.packets) == 1
        assert send(stream, connection, type="ticker").receipt_seq == 2
    assert read_segment(path)["packets"] == 2


def test_explicit_warmup_capacity_reaches_the_monitor_without_changing_legacy_cap(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    import instant_coverage

    clock = Clock()
    with (
        journal(
            tmp_path / "extended.sqlite",
            clock,
            max_packets=instant_coverage.WARMUP_PACKET_LIMIT,
        ) as extended,
        journal(tmp_path / "ordinary.sqlite", clock) as ordinary,
    ):
        direct = instant_coverage.CoverageMonitor([COIN])
        monkeypatch.setattr(instant_coverage, "MAX_PACKETS", 1)
        connection = extended.open_connection()
        send(extended, connection, type="ticker")
        assert extended.evidence.book.monitor.packet_count == 2
        assert ordinary.evidence.book.monitor._packet_limit == 100000
        assert direct._packet_limit is None
    report = read_segment(tmp_path / "extended.sqlite")
    assert report["manifest"]["max_packets"] == 250000
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="bounds"):
        journal(tmp_path / "oversize.sqlite", clock, max_packets=250001)


def test_batch_receipt_order_and_availability_begin_after_durable_ingress(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    path = tmp_path / "batch.sqlite"
    clock = Clock()
    with journal(path, clock, batch_frames=4, indexed=True) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        subscribe(stream, connection)
        prior = stream.evidence._prefix_at(9999)[:2]
        process = stream._process
        checked = []

        def check_ingress(envelope, observed):
            assert observed == 10000
            assert read_segment(path)["packets"] == 2
            assert [row["receipt_seq"] for row in iter_pending_ingress(path)] == [3, 4]
            assert stream.evidence._prefix_at(9999)[:2] == prior
            checked.append(envelope["receipt_seq"])
            return process(envelope, observed)

        monkeypatch.setattr(stream, "_process", check_ingress)
        clock.now = 10000
        receipts = stream.receive_batch(
            connection,
            [
                (
                    json.dumps(
                        dict(type="heartbeat", product_id=COIN, last_trade_id=9)
                    ),
                    (1000, 1000000000),
                ),
                (
                    json.dumps(
                        dict(
                            type="match",
                            product_id=COIN,
                            trade_id=10,
                            price="100",
                            size="2",
                            side="sell",
                        )
                    ),
                    (1001, 1001000000),
                ),
            ],
        )
        assert checked == [3, 4]
        assert [receipt.receipt_seq for receipt in receipts] == checked
        assert [receipt.receipt_ts_ms for receipt in receipts] == [1000, 1001]
        assert stream.evidence._prefix_at(9999)[:2] == prior
        assert stream.evidence._prefix_at(10000)[0] == 4
        assert stream.evidence.trades[-1].available_ts_ms == 10000
        assert list(iter_pending_ingress(path)) == []
    records = list(iter_packets(path))
    assert [row["ingress_commit_observed_ts_ms"] for row in records[2:]] == [
        10000,
        10000,
    ]
    assert records[-1]["trade"]["taker_side"] == "buy"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "phase, pending, processed",
    [("ingress", [], 1), ("audit", [2, 3], 1), ("complete", [], 3)],
)
def test_hard_exit_preserves_only_committed_batch_boundaries(
    tmp_path, phase, pending, processed
):
    path = tmp_path / "interrupted-batch.sqlite"
    source = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts" / "scoring_experiment")
    child = """
import os
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, sys.argv[1])
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal

phase = sys.argv[3]
with StreamJournal(sys.argv[2], ['ALT-USD', 'BTC-USD'], 30000, 35000, clock_ms=lambda: 30000, monotonic_ns=lambda: 0, batch_frames=4) as journal:
    connection = journal.open_connection()
    inserts = []
    def interrupt(sql):
        if sql.startswith('INSERT INTO pending_ingress'):
            inserts.append(sql)
            if phase == 'ingress' and len(inserts) == 2:
                os._exit(29)
        if phase == 'audit' and sql.startswith('DELETE FROM pending_ingress'):
            os._exit(29)
    journal._db.set_trace_callback(interrupt)
    frame = '{"type":"ticker","product_id":"ALT-USD"}'
    journal.receive_batch(connection, [(frame, (30000, 1)), (frame, (30000, 2))])
    if phase == 'complete':
        os._exit(29)
"""
    result = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-c", child, source, str(path), phase],
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=30,
    )
    assert result.returncode == 29, result.stderr.decode()
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "recording"
    assert [row["envelope"]["receipt_seq"] for row in iter_packets(path)] == list(
        range(1, processed + 1)
    )
    assert [row["receipt_seq"] for row in iter_pending_ingress(path)] == pending
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute("PRAGMA quick_check").fetchall() == [("ok",)]


def test_batched_parse_barrier_discards_frames_after_the_error(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "parse-barrier.sqlite"
    with journal(path, Clock(), batch_frames=4) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        ticker = '{"type":"ticker"}'
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="invalid WebSocket frame"):
            stream.receive_batch(
                connection, [(ticker, (0, 1)), (b"bad", (0, 2)), (ticker, (0, 3))]
            )
        assert not stream.connection_active
        stream.finish()
    assert [row["envelope"]["message"]["type"] for row in iter_packets(path)] == [
        "connection_opened",
        "ticker",
        "audit_parse_error",
    ]
    assert list(iter_pending_ingress(path)) == []


def test_batched_processing_failure_retains_raw_suffix_and_blocks_new_writes(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "failed-batch.sqlite"
    clock = Clock()
    with journal(path, clock, batch_frames=4) as stream:
        connection = stream.open_connection()
        clock.now = 30000
        with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="trade_id"):
            stream.receive_batch(
                connection,
                [
                    ('{"type":"ticker"}', (1000, 1)),
                    (json.dumps(dict(type="match", product_id=COIN)), (1001, 2)),
                ],
            )
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="failed evidence recorder"):
            prepare_decision(stream.evidence, COIN, 30000, "up", asof=30000)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not writable"):
            stream.end_connection(connection)
    assert read_segment(path)["status"] == "packet_processing_failed"
    assert len(list(iter_packets(path))) == 1
    assert [row["receipt_seq"] for row in iter_pending_ingress(path)] == [2, 3]


def test_timing_capacity_is_explicit_and_bounded_at_both_layers(tmp_path):
    from instant_coverage import CoverageMonitor, TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT
    from instant_stream_journal import TIMING_RECORD_LIMIT_BYTES

    clock = Clock()
    bounds = dict(
        max_packets=TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT,
        max_record_bytes=TIMING_RECORD_LIMIT_BYTES,
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="journal bounds"):
        journal(tmp_path / "legacy.sqlite", clock, **bounds)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="coverage packet limit"):
        CoverageMonitor([COIN], packet_limit=TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="journal bounds"):
        journal(
            tmp_path / "above-limit.sqlite",
            clock,
            extended_capacity=True,
            max_packets=TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT,
            max_record_bytes=TIMING_RECORD_LIMIT_BYTES + 1,
        )

    path = tmp_path / "timing.sqlite"
    with journal(path, clock, extended_capacity=True, **bounds) as stream:
        assert stream.evidence.book.monitor._packet_limit == TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT
        manifest = read_segment(path)["manifest"]
        assert manifest["extended_capacity"] is True
        assert manifest["max_packets"] == TIMING_PACKET_LIMIT
        assert manifest["max_record_bytes"] == TIMING_RECORD_LIMIT_BYTES

"""Hand-computable synthetic paths; no historical or prospective fitting."""

from dataclasses import replace
import importlib.util
import math
from pathlib import Path
import sys

import pytest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
    "instant_targets", ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_targets.py"
)
target = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
sys.modules[spec.name] = target
spec.loader.exec_module(target)
ANCHOR = 3_780_000


def baseline(**kwargs):
    return replace(
        target.Baseline(
            sigma_1m=0.0015,
            beta=1.0,
            return_count=60,
            end_ts_ms=ANCHOR,
            available_ts_ms=ANCHOR,
            beta_available_ts_ms=ANCHOR,
            beta_fallback=False,
            pace_trade_median_30s=2,
            pace_quote_usd_median_30s=20,
            pace_window_count=120,
            pace_end_ts_ms=ANCHOR - 30_000,
            pace_available_ts_ms=ANCHOR,
        ),
        **kwargs,
    )


def samples(direction="up", residual=None, btc=None):
    sign = 1 if direction == "up" else -1
    rows = []
    for ts in range(ANCHOR - 180_000, ANCHOR + 905_000, 5000):
        elapsed = (ts - ANCHOR) / 1000
        move = (
            residual(elapsed)
            if residual
            else 0.002 + 0.02 * min(max(elapsed / 300, 0), 1) if elapsed >= 0 else 0
        )
        market = btc(elapsed) if btc else 0
        mid, btc_mid = 100 * math.exp(sign * move + market), 50000 * math.exp(market)
        rows.append(
            target.Sample(
                product_id="TEST-USD",
                ts_ms=ts,
                bid=mid * (1 - 0.0001),
                ask=mid * (1 + 0.0001),
                btc_bid=btc_mid * (1 - 0.0001),
                btc_ask=btc_mid * (1 + 0.0001),
                quote_ts_ms=ts,
                btc_quote_ts_ms=ts,
                available_ts_ms=ts,
                trade_count=1,
                quote_volume_usd=10,
                coverage_verified=True,
                trade_count_at_bucket_start=0,
            )
        )
    return rows


def grade(rows=None, direction="up", horizon=300, base=None, asof=None):
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(rows if rows is not None else samples(direction))
    result = engine.evaluate(
        ANCHOR,
        base or baseline(),
        direction,
        horizon,
        asof if asof is not None else ANCHOR + horizon * 1000,
    )
    return engine, result


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("horizon", [300, 900])
def test_sustained_move_is_symmetric_and_peak_ties_choose_first(direction, horizon):
    _, result = grade(direction=direction, horizon=horizon)
    assert result["status"] == "ready"
    assert result["verified_forward"] and result["verified_onset"]
    assert result["terminal_residual_log"] == pytest.approx(0.02)
    assert result["retention"] == pytest.approx(1)
    assert result["peak_ts_ms"] == ANCHOR + 300_000
    assert result["breakout_ts_ms"] == ANCHOR + 35_000
    assert result["forward_trade_count"] == horizon / 5


def test_market_move_is_not_an_idiosyncratic_impulse():
    _, result = grade(
        samples(residual=lambda t: 0, btc=lambda t: 0.04 * max(t, 0) / 300)
    )
    assert result["terminal_residual_log"] == pytest.approx(0, abs=1e-12)
    assert result["verified_forward"] is False


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
def test_opposite_direction_is_never_verified_by_absolute_magnitude(direction):
    opposite = "down" if direction == "up" else "up"
    _, result = grade(samples(opposite), direction=direction)
    assert result["terminal_residual_log"] < 0
    assert result["verified_forward"] is False


def test_large_wick_with_good_terminal_magnitude_fails_retention():
    _, result = grade(
        samples(
            residual=lambda t: (
                0
                if t < 0
                else 0.002 + (0.05 if 0 < t < 300 else 0.02 if t >= 300 else 0)
            )
        )
    )
    assert result["terminal_residual_log"] > result["required_terminal_log"]
    assert result["retention"] == pytest.approx(0.4)
    assert result["verified_forward"] is False


def test_later_retrace_rejects_onset_but_preserves_forward_impulse():
    rows = samples()
    rows = [
        replace(row, bid=99, ask=99.02) if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR + 50_000 else row
        for row in rows
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows)
    assert result["verified_forward"] is True
    assert result["onset_candidate"] is True
    assert result["no_retrace"] is False
    assert result["verified_onset"] is False
    assert result["adverse_residual_log"] < 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "change,reason",
    [
        ({"coverage_verified": False}, "unverified_coverage"),
        ({"price_source": "last_trade_fallback"}, "price_fallback"),
        ({"btc_price_source": "last_trade_fallback"}, "price_fallback"),
        ({"quote_ts_ms": ANCHOR - 20_000}, "stale_or_future_quote"),
        ({"quote_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 10_001}, "stale_or_future_quote"),
        ({"bid": 110, "ask": 100}, "invalid_quote"),
        ({"ask": None}, "invalid_quote"),
        ({"btc_bid": float("nan")}, "invalid_quote"),
        ({"trade_count": -1}, "invalid_trade_count"),
        ({"quote_volume_usd": float("nan")}, "invalid_volume"),
        ({"available_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 10_001}, "future_receipt"),
    ],
)
def test_unknown_data_never_becomes_a_negative_label(change, reason):
    rows = [
        replace(row, **change) if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR + 10_000 else row
        for row in samples()
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows)
    assert result["status"] == "unavailable"
    assert result["reason"] == reason
    assert result["verified_forward"] is None
    assert result["verified_onset"] is None


def test_missing_grid_point_and_nonexact_endpoint_are_unknown():
    for missing in (ANCHOR + 5000, ANCHOR + 300_000):
        _, result = grade([row for row in samples() if row.ts_ms != missing])
        assert result["reason"] == "missing_grid_point"
        assert result["verified_forward"] is None


def test_label_waits_for_full_horizon_instead_of_using_partial_success():
    _, result = grade(asof=ANCHOR + 299_999)
    assert result["status"] == "pending"
    assert result["verified_forward"] is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "change,reason",
    [
        ({"available_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 1}, "future_baseline"),
        ({"beta_available_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 1}, "future_baseline"),
        ({"end_ts_ms": ANCHOR - 60_000}, "baseline_window_misaligned"),
        ({"return_count": 59}, "baseline_warming"),
        ({"sigma_1m": float("nan")}, "invalid_baseline"),
    ],
)
def test_baselines_are_causal_complete_and_current(change, reason):
    _, result = grade(base=baseline(**change))
    assert result["status"] == "unavailable"
    assert result["reason"] == reason


def test_sigma_floor_and_beta_fallback_are_visible():
    _, result = grade(base=baseline(sigma_1m=0, beta_fallback=True))
    assert result["sigma_1m"] == 0.0015
    assert result["sigma_floor_applied"] is True
    assert result["beta_fallback"] is True


def test_forward_trade_quantum_excludes_anchor_bucket():
    rows = [
        replace(row, trade_count=100 if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR else 0) for row in samples()
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows)
    assert result["forward_trade_count"] == 0
    assert result["verified_forward"] is False


def test_exact_pace_threshold_and_single_print_do_not_create_onset():
    _, result = grade(
        base=baseline(pace_trade_median_30s=3, pace_quote_usd_median_30s=30)
    )
    assert result["pace_multiple"] == 2
    assert result["onset_candidate"] is False
    rows = [
        replace(row, trade_count=0) if ANCHOR - 30_000 < row.ts_ms < ANCHOR else row
        for row in samples()
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows)
    assert result["recent_trade_count"] == 1
    assert result["onset_candidate"] is False


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "change",
    [
        {"pace_window_count": 119},
        {"pace_end_ts_ms": ANCHOR},
        {"pace_available_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 1},
        {"pace_trade_median_30s": 0, "pace_quote_usd_median_30s": 0},
    ],

def test_onset_unavailable_does_not_erase_complete_forward_label(change):
    _, result = grade(base=baseline(**change))
    assert result["verified_forward"] is True
    assert result["verified_onset"] is None
    assert result["onset_status"] == "unavailable"


def test_appending_or_changing_after_horizon_cannot_change_label():
    rows = samples()
    _, expected = grade([row for row in rows if row.ts_ms <= ANCHOR + 300_000])
    changed = [
        (
            replace(row, bid=1, ask=2, coverage_verified=False)
            if row.ts_ms > ANCHOR + 300_000
            else row
        )
        for row in rows
    ]
    _, actual = grade(changed)
    assert actual == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
def test_prediction_detection_and_half_consumed_boundary(direction):
    engine, label = grade(direction=direction)
    before = engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR - 5000)
    assert before["timing"] == "pre_onset_prediction"
    assert before["detection_delay_seconds"] == -5
    assert before["consumed_fraction"] < 0  # deliberately not clipped
    assert engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)["timing"] == "early_detection"
    half = engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR + 150_000)
    assert half["consumed_fraction"] == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert half["timing"] == "late_detection"
    assert (
        engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR - 185_000)["timing"] == "outside_event_window"
    )


def test_unaligned_publication_uses_only_backward_asof_snapshot():
    engine, label = grade()
    result = engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR + 4999)
    assert result["price_snapshot_ts_ms"] == ANCHOR
    assert result["consumed_fraction"] == 0


def test_costs_apply_once_to_coin_return_not_market_residual():
    _, label = grade(samples(btc=lambda t: 0.01 * max(t, 0) / 300))
    expected = math.expm1(0.03) * 10000
    assert label["terminal_residual_log"] == pytest.approx(0.02)
    assert label["gross_coin_return_bps"] == pytest.approx(expected)
    assert label["cost_scenarios"][0]["net_coin_return_bps"] == pytest.approx(
        expected - 2
    )
    assert label["cost_scenarios"][1]["net_coin_return_bps"] == pytest.approx(
        expected - 2 - 20
    )


def test_catalog_deduplicates_events_without_merging_directions_or_horizons():
    _, label = grade()
    later = {
        **label,
        "anchor_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 5000,
        "label_maturity_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 305_000,
    }
    boundary = {
        **label,
        "anchor_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 300_000,
        "label_maturity_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 600_000,
    }
    down = {**label, "direction": "down"}
    longer = {**label, "horizon_seconds": 900, "label_maturity_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 900_000}
    assert target.event_catalog([later, boundary, down, label, longer]) == [
        down,
        label,
        longer,
        boundary,
    ]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="mix"):
        target.event_catalog([label, {**label, "contract_sha256": "different"}])


def test_invalid_series_cannot_silently_sort_or_mix_products():
    rows = samples()
    for bad in (
        [rows[1], rows[0]],
        [rows[0], rows[0]],
        [rows[0], replace(rows[1], product_id="OTHER-USD")],
        [replace(rows[0], ts_ms=1)],
    ):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            target.TargetEvaluator(bad)


def test_trades_exactly_at_anchor_are_excluded_and_unknown_boundary_is_unavailable():
    rows = [
        (
            replace(row, trade_count=3, trade_count_at_bucket_start=3)
            if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR + 5000
            else replace(row, trade_count=0)
        )
        for row in samples()
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows)
    assert result["forward_trade_count"] == 0
    assert result["verified_forward"] is False
    rows = [
        (
            replace(row, trade_count_at_bucket_start=None)
            if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR + 5000
            else row
        )
        for row in samples()
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows)
    assert result["reason"] == "unknown_boundary_trade_count"
    assert result["verified_forward"] is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("sigma,spread", [(0.005, 2), (0.0015, 100)])
def test_volatility_and_spread_each_can_raise_required_magnitude(sigma, spread):
    rows = samples()
    start_mid = next(row.mid for row in rows if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR)
    rows = [
        (
            replace(
                row,
                bid=start_mid * (1 - spread / 20000),
                ask=start_mid * (1 + spread / 20000),
            )
            if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR
            else row
        )
        for row in rows
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows, base=baseline(sigma_1m=sigma))
    assert result["required_terminal_log"] > result["terminal_residual_log"]
    assert result["verified_forward"] is False


def test_breakout_deadline_is_not_extended_to_get_a_success():
    _, result = grade(
        samples(residual=lambda t: 0 if t < 0 else 0.002 + (0.02 if t >= 200 else 0))
    )
    assert result["verified_forward"] is True
    assert result["onset_candidate"] is True
    assert result["breakout_ts_ms"] is None
    assert result["verified_onset"] is False


def test_healthy_zero_trade_buckets_are_known_observations():
    rows = [
        (
            replace(row, trade_count=0, quote_volume_usd=0)
            if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR + 50_000
            else row
        )
        for row in samples()
    ]
    _, result = grade(rows)
    assert result["status"] == "ready"
    assert result["verified_forward"] is True


def test_zero_move_is_a_complete_negative_and_is_retained_outside_boards():
    _, result = grade(samples(residual=lambda t: 0))
    assert result["status"] == "ready"
    assert result["verified_forward"] is False
    assert result["verified_onset"] is False
    assert result["retention"] is None


def test_exact_minimum_log_magnitude_passes_with_float_tolerance():
    _, result = grade(
        samples(residual=lambda t: 0 if t < 0 else 0.002 + 0.015 * min(t / 300, 1))
    )
    assert result["terminal_residual_log"] == pytest.approx(0.015)
    assert result["verified_forward"] is True


def test_unavailable_alert_quote_does_not_select_a_future_quote():
    engine, label = grade()
    with_gap = target.TargetEvaluator(
        [row for row in samples() if row.ts_ms != ANCHOR - 5000]
    )
    assert with_gap.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR - 1)["timing"] == "unavailable"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="verified onset"):
        engine.alert_timing({**label, "verified_onset": False}, ANCHOR)


def test_contract_hash_and_scope_travel_with_every_label():
    import hashlib

    _, result = grade()
    assert (
        result["contract_sha256"]
        == hashlib.sha256(
            target.CONTRACT_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
    )
    assert result["evaluation_only"] is True
    cfg, _ = target.load_contract()
    assert cfg["fitting_allowed"] is False


def test_contract_hash_is_portable_across_windows_and_unix_newlines(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
):
    cfg, expected = target.load_contract()
    portable = tmp_path / "contract.json"
    portable.write_bytes(
        target.CONTRACT_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        .replace("\n", "\r\n")
        .encode("utf-8")
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(target, "CONTRACT_PATH", portable)
    actual_cfg, actual = target.load_contract()
    assert actual == expected and actual_cfg == cfg


def test_missing_or_invalid_baseline_metadata_is_unavailable():
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(samples())
    for base, reason in (
        (None, "missing_baseline"),
        (baseline(available_ts_ms=None), "invalid_baseline_metadata"),
    ):
        result = engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, base, "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300_000)
        assert result["status"] == "unavailable" and result["reason"] == reason

    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(samples())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="as-of"):
        engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, baseline(), "up", 300, float("nan"))

===== BEGIN SOURCE 107 | tests/test_instant_targets_v2.py | sha256=2f0ab6f9ae35b5397bd644f1eb4185f0d809f5f3e7db303e5528c98ed8b8866e =====
"""Review counterexamples and the opt-in v2 draft; no empirical fitting."""

from dataclasses import replace
import hashlib
import math

import pytest

from test_instant_targets import ANCHOR, baseline, samples, target


def grade_v2(rows=None, direction="up", horizon=300, base=None, anchor=ANCHOR):
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(
        rows if rows is not None else samples(direction),
        contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH,
    )
    base = base or baseline(
        end_ts_ms=(anchor - 35000) // 60000 * 60000,
        pace_end_ts_ms=anchor - 30000,
    )
    return engine, engine.evaluate(
        anchor, base, direction, horizon, anchor + horizon * 1000
    )


def one_bps_dip(direction="up"):
    rows = samples(direction)
    at_anchor = next(row for row in rows if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR)
    # A favorable-direction negative residual of exactly 1 bp at +5s.
    sign = 1 if direction == "up" else -1
    price = at_anchor.mid * math.exp(-sign * 0.0001)
    return [
        (
            replace(row, bid=price * (1 - 0.0001), ask=price * (1 + 0.0001))
            if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR + 5000
            else row
        )
        for row in rows
    ]


def test_v1_contract_and_default_remain_pinned():
    raw = target.CONTRACT_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8").encode("utf-8")
    assert (
        hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        == "35d9b8a286d051aff08148a2af9987b379d65e92d57c3bea76112be52cfca7a4"
    )
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(samples())
    label = engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, baseline(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    assert label["contract_version"] == "instant-target-v1"
    assert engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)["consumed_fraction"] == 0
    assert "evaluator_sha256" not in label


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("horizon", [300, 900])
def test_named_reference_metrics_count_the_pre_trigger_leg(direction, horizon):
    engine, label = grade_v2(direction=direction, horizon=horizon)
    timing = engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)
    assert timing["post_anchor_consumed_fraction"] == 0
    assert timing["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] == pytest.approx(0.002 / 0.022)
    assert timing["pre_window_reference_ts_ms"] == ANCHOR - 30000
    assert timing["physical_onset_defined"] is False
    assert timing["pre_window_reference_is_physical_origin"] is False
    assert "consumed_fraction" not in timing
    assert timing["timing"] == "early_after_activity_trigger"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
def test_forward_catalog_retains_real_moves_with_small_retracement(direction):
    engine, label = grade_v2(one_bps_dip(direction), direction=direction)
    assert label["verified_forward"] is True
    assert label["verified_onset"] is False
    assert target.event_catalog([label]) == []
    forward = target.forward_event_catalog([label])
    assert len(forward) == 1
    assert forward[0]["catalog_kind"] == "forward_window_not_physical_impulse"
    timing = engine.forward_alert_timing(forward[0], ANCHOR)
    assert timing["association_kind"] == "forward_window_not_physical_impulse"
    assert timing["timing"] == "within_forward_window_before_peak"
    assert "pre_onset_prediction" not in timing.values()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="verified label"):
        engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)
    legacy = target.TargetEvaluator(one_bps_dip(direction))
    v1 = legacy.evaluate(ANCHOR, baseline(), direction, 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    assert v1["verified_forward"] is True and v1["verified_onset"] is False
    assert target.event_catalog([v1]) == []


def test_forward_target_and_catalog_do_not_require_activity_gate():
    engine, label = grade_v2(
        base=baseline(
            end_ts_ms=ANCHOR - 60000,
            pace_trade_median_30s=6,
            pace_quote_usd_median_30s=60,
        )
    )
    assert label["verified_forward"] is True
    assert label["onset_candidate"] is False
    assert len(target.forward_event_catalog([label])) == 1
    assert (
        engine.forward_alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] > 0
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("offset", [0, 5000, 30000, 35000, 55000])
def test_v2_baseline_cutoff_ends_before_both_velocity_windows(offset):
    anchor = ANCHOR + offset
    expected = (anchor - 35000) // 60000 * 60000
    engine, label = grade_v2(anchor=anchor)
    assert expected <= anchor - 35000
    assert label["status"] == "ready"
    if anchor // 60000 * 60000 > expected:
        bad = baseline(end_ts_ms=anchor // 60000 * 60000, pace_end_ts_ms=anchor - 30000)
        result = engine.evaluate(anchor, bad, "up", 300, anchor + 300000)
        assert result["reason"] == "baseline_window_misaligned"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("peak", [0, -1, None, float("nan"), float("inf")])
def test_invalid_external_peak_is_rejected_before_division(peak):
    engine, label = grade_v2()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="positive finite"):
        engine.forward_alert_timing({**label, "peak_residual_log": peak}, ANCHOR)


def test_pre_window_peak_includes_the_pre_trigger_path():
    # A previous wick exceeds the subsequent peak. Do not erase it by
    # merely shifting the post-anchor peak upward by the pre-anchor return.
    rows = samples()
    price = 100 * math.exp(0.04)
    rows = [
        (
            replace(row, bid=price * (1 - 0.0001), ask=price * (1 + 0.0001))
            if row.ts_ms == ANCHOR - 10000
            else row
        )
        for row in rows
    ]
    engine, label = grade_v2(rows)
    assert label["verified_forward"] is True
    assert label["pre_window_peak_ts_ms"] == ANCHOR - 10000
    timing = engine.forward_alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)
    assert timing["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] == pytest.approx(0.002 / 0.04)
    assert timing["lead_to_pre_window_peak_seconds"] == -10


def test_reference_missingness_does_not_erase_complete_forward_label():
    rows = [row for row in samples() if row.ts_ms != ANCHOR - 30000]
    engine, label = grade_v2(rows)
    assert label["verified_forward"] is True
    assert label["pre_window_reference_status"] == "unavailable"
    timing = engine.forward_alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)
    assert timing["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] is None
    assert timing["post_anchor_consumed_fraction"] == 0


def test_reference_may_have_no_positive_peak_for_an_independent_forward_move():
    # A deep pre-anchor decline followed by a qualifying rebound. The
    # forward label remains valid while this reference has no positive peak.
    rows = samples(residual=lambda t: 0.05 if t < 0 else 0.02 * min(t / 300, 1))
    engine, label = grade_v2(rows)
    assert label["verified_forward"] is True
    assert label["pre_window_peak_residual_log"] == 0
    assert (
        engine.forward_alert_timing(label, ANCHOR)["pre_window_consumed_fraction"]
        is None
    )


def test_crossing_does_not_algebraically_force_positive_F1_acceleration():
    def path(t):
        if t < -30:
            return 0.012 * (t + 180) / 150
        if t < 0:
            return 0.012
        return 0.014 + 0.02 * min(t / 300, 1)

    engine, label = grade_v2(samples(residual=path))
    assert label["verified_onset"] is True
    fast = engine._residual(ANCHOR - 30000, ANCHOR, 1, 1)
    prior = engine._residual(ANCHOR - 180000, ANCHOR - 30000, 1, 1)
    assert fast - prior / 5 < 0


def test_trade_count_pace_can_trigger_while_volume_pace_is_below_two():
    _, label = grade_v2(
        base=baseline(end_ts_ms=ANCHOR - 60000, pace_quote_usd_median_30s=100)
    )
    assert label["verified_onset"] is True
    assert label["pace_multiple"] == 3  # 6 trades / median 2
    assert 60 / 100 < 2  # volume ratio does not have to exceed 2


def test_forged_implementation_and_mixed_catalog_versions_are_rejected():
    engine, label = grade_v2()
    other = {**label, "evaluator_sha256": "different"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="this evaluator"):
        engine.forward_alert_timing(other, ANCHOR)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="implementations"):
        target.forward_event_catalog([label, other])


def test_v2_stays_opt_in_and_operational_metrics_remain_gated():
    engine, _ = grade_v2()
    assert engine.cfg["fitting_allowed"] is False
    assert engine.cfg["targets"]["primary_pointwise"] == "verified_forward"
    assert (
        engine.cfg["reference_metrics"][
            "operational_false_alert_and_missed_move_rates_enabled"
        ]
        is False
    )


def test_trigger_timing_uses_the_declared_pre_window_halfway_boundary():
    engine, label = grade_v2()
    before = engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR - 5000)
    assert before["timing"] == "before_activity_trigger"
    assert "pre_onset_prediction" not in before.values()
    half = engine.alert_timing(label, ANCHOR + 135000)
    assert half["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] == pytest.approx(0.5)
    assert half["post_anchor_consumed_fraction"] == pytest.approx(0.45)
    assert half["timing"] == "late_after_activity_trigger"

===== BEGIN SOURCE 108 | tests/test_instant_targets_v2_review.py | sha256=5710f8c93c53ab80143be4c9e67fa94eaa2084f15565ea14825b57ac18789709 =====
"""Independent Claude review probes for the v2 draft (8b5b266). Synthetic only; no fitting."""

from dataclasses import replace
import importlib.util
import math
import random
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

from test_instant_targets import ANCHOR, ROOT, baseline, samples, target

V1_REFERENCE_COMMIT = (
    "cb7034a48d5a533153dfe1d113921318db82f3ec"  # v1 implementation before any v2 change
)
V2_KEYS = {
    "evaluator_sha256",
    "primary_pointwise_target",
    "physical_onset_defined",
    "pre_window_reference_ts_ms",
    "pre_window_reference_is_physical_origin",
    "pre_window_reference_status",
    "pre_window_reference_reason",
    "pre_window_peak_ts_ms",
    "pre_window_peak_residual_log",
}


def reference_v1(tmp_path):
    try:
        src = subprocess.run(
            [
                "git",
                "show",
                f"{V1_REFERENCE_COMMIT}:scripts/scoring_experiment/instant_targets.py",
            ],
            cwd=ROOT,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            check=True,
        ).stdout
    except (OSError, subprocess.CalledProcessError):
        pytest.skip("v1 reference commit not available")
    path = tmp_path / "instant_targets_v1_ref.py"
    path.write_text(src, encoding="utf-8")
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("instant_targets_v1_ref", path)
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[spec.name] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    mod.CONTRACT_PATH = target.CONTRACT_PATH  # same pinned v1 contract file
    return mod


def random_path(seed):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    knots = [rng.gauss(0, 0.006) for _ in range(12)]
    drift = rng.choice([0.0, 0.01, 0.025, -0.02])

    def residual(elapsed):
        i = int((elapsed + 180) // 90) % len(knots)
        return (
            knots[i] * 0.3
            + drift * max(min(elapsed / 300, 1), 0)
            + 0.002 * math.sin(elapsed / 7)
        )

    return residual


def rows_for(seed, direction):
    rows = samples(direction, residual=random_path(seed))
    rng = random.Random(seed + 1)
    return [
        replace(
            r,
            trade_count=rng.randint(0, 4),
            trade_count_at_bucket_start=0,
            quote_volume_usd=rng.uniform(0, 60),
        )
        for r in rows
    ]


def v2_base(anchor=ANCHOR, **kw):
    return baseline(
        end_ts_ms=(anchor - 35000) // 60000 * 60000, pace_end_ts_ms=anchor - 30000, **kw
    )


# 1. v1 valid-label behaviour is unchanged ------------------------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(40))
def test_v1_outputs_identical_to_pre_v2_reference(seed, tmp_path):
    ref = reference_v1(tmp_path)
    for direction in ("up", "down"):
        rows = rows_for(seed, direction)
        ref_rows = [ref.Sample(**r.__dict__) for r in rows]
        for horizon in (300, 900):
            new = target.TargetEvaluator(rows).evaluate(
                ANCHOR, baseline(), direction, horizon, ANCHOR + horizon * 1000
            )
            old = ref.TargetEvaluator(ref_rows).evaluate(
                ANCHOR,
                ref.Baseline(**baseline().__dict__),
                direction,
                horizon,
                ANCHOR + horizon * 1000,
            )
            assert new == old
            if new.get("verified_onset"):
                for pub in (ANCHOR - 60000, ANCHOR, ANCHOR + 7000, new["peak_ts_ms"]):
                    assert target.TargetEvaluator(rows).alert_timing(
                        new, pub
                    ) == ref.TargetEvaluator(ref_rows).alert_timing(old, pub)


# 2. the v2 delta is only the baseline cutoff and added named keys -------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(40))
def test_v2_differs_from_v1_only_by_cutoff_and_added_keys(seed):
    for direction in ("up", "down"):
        rows = rows_for(seed, direction)
        for horizon in (300, 900):
            v1 = target.TargetEvaluator(rows).evaluate(
                ANCHOR, baseline(), direction, horizon, ANCHOR + horizon * 1000
            )
            v2 = target.TargetEvaluator(
                rows, contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH
            ).evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), direction, horizon, ANCHOR + horizon * 1000)
            assert set(v2) - set(v1) <= V2_KEYS
            strip = {
                k: v
                for k, v in v2.items()
                if k not in V2_KEYS | {"contract_version", "contract_sha256"}
            }
            assert strip == {
                k: v
                for k, v in v1.items()
                if k not in ("contract_version", "contract_sha256")
            }


@pytest.mark.parametrize("offset_s", [0, 5, 30, 34, 35, 36, 55])
def test_v2_rejects_the_v1_cutoff_when_they_differ(offset_s):
    anchor = 60000 * 63 + offset_s * 1000
    anchor -= anchor % 5000
    v1_end, v2_end = anchor // 60000 * 60000, (anchor - 35000) // 60000 * 60000
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(samples(), contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH)
    assert engine._baseline_quality(v2_base(anchor), anchor) is None
    if v1_end != v2_end:
        assert (
            engine._baseline_quality(replace(v2_base(anchor), end_ts_ms=v1_end), anchor)
            == "baseline_window_misaligned"
        )


# 3. pre-window and post-anchor peaks, including earlier wicks ------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(60))
def test_pre_window_peak_equals_forward_peak_unless_an_earlier_wick_is_higher(seed):
    """log additivity: residual(ref, ts) = residual(ref, anchor) + residual(anchor, ts) for ts >= anchor."""
    for direction in ("up", "down"):
        engine = target.TargetEvaluator(
            rows_for(seed, direction), contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH
        )
        lab = engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), direction, 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
        if lab["status"] != "ready" or lab["pre_window_reference_status"] != "ready":
            continue
        lead = engine._residual(
            ANCHOR - 30000, ANCHOR, lab["beta"], 1 if direction == "up" else -1
        )
        if lab["pre_window_peak_ts_ms"] >= ANCHOR:
            assert lab["pre_window_peak_ts_ms"] == lab["peak_ts_ms"]
            assert (
                abs(
                    lab["pre_window_peak_residual_log"]
                    - (lead + lab["peak_residual_log"])
                )
                < 1e-12
            )
        else:
            assert lab["pre_window_peak_residual_log"] > lead + lab["peak_residual_log"]


def wick_rows(direction):
    """Verified strict onset whose highest point is a wick inside the 30s activity window."""

    def residual(elapsed):
        if elapsed < 0:
            return (
                0.045 if elapsed == -10 else 0.0
            )  # 4.5% one-point wick at anchor-10s; flat otherwise, so the
        return 0.004 + 0.02 * min(
            elapsed / 300, 1
        )  # crossing happens in the last 5s; forward leg +2%, no retrace

    rows = samples(direction, residual=residual)
    return [replace(r, trade_count=3) for r in rows]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("direction", ["up", "down"])
def test_earlier_wick_makes_every_strict_publication_late(direction):
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(
        wick_rows(direction), contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH
    )
    lab = engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), direction, 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    assert lab["verified_onset"] is True
    assert lab["pre_window_peak_ts_ms"] == ANCHOR - 10000 < ANCHOR <= lab["peak_ts_ms"]
    timing = engine.alert_timing(lab, ANCHOR)
    assert timing["timing"] == "late_after_activity_trigger"
    assert timing["lead_to_pre_window_peak_seconds"] < 0
    assert (
        0 < timing["pre_window_consumed_fraction"] < 0.5
    )  # under half consumed, yet classified late
    assert (
        engine.forward_alert_timing(lab, ANCHOR)["timing"]
        == "within_forward_window_before_peak"
    )


# 4. mirror symmetry of up/down on arbitrary paths -----------------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", range(30))
def test_up_and_down_labels_mirror(seed):
    up = target.TargetEvaluator(
        rows_for(seed, "up"), contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH
    ).evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    dn = target.TargetEvaluator(
        rows_for(seed, "down"), contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH
    ).evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "down", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    for k in (
        "status",
        "verified_forward",
        "verified_onset",
        "peak_ts_ms",
        "pre_window_peak_ts_ms",
        "breakout_ts_ms",
    ):
        assert up.get(k) == dn.get(k), k
    for k in (
        "terminal_residual_log",
        "peak_residual_log",
        "adverse_residual_log",
        "pre_window_peak_residual_log",
    ):
        assert abs(up[k] - dn[k]) < 1e-9, k


# 5. maturity and missingness ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_maturity_boundary_and_reference_does_not_extend_it():
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(samples(), contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH)
    assert (
        engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 299999)["status"]
        == "pending"
    )
    assert (
        engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)["status"]
        == "ready"
    )


def test_gap_only_before_the_pre_window_keeps_reference_but_removes_onset():
    rows = [r for r in samples() if r.ts_ms != ANCHOR - 35000]
    lab = target.TargetEvaluator(rows, contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH).evaluate(
        ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000
    )
    assert lab["status"] == "ready" and lab["pre_window_reference_status"] == "ready"
    assert (
        lab["onset_status"] == "unavailable" and lab["verified_onset"] is None
    )  # missing, never False


def test_mixed_quote_provenance_inside_pre_window_is_unavailable_not_false():
    rows = [
        replace(r, price_source="last_trade") if r.ts_ms == ANCHOR - 15000 else r
        for r in samples()
    ]
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(rows, contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH)
    lab = engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    assert lab["status"] == "ready"
    assert (lab["pre_window_reference_status"], lab["pre_window_reference_reason"]) == (
        "unavailable",
        "price_fallback",
    )
    assert lab["onset_status"] == "unavailable" and lab["verified_onset"] is None


# 6. backward publication lookup ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def verified_v2():
    rows = [replace(r, trade_count=3) for r in samples()]
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(rows, contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH)
    lab = engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    assert lab["verified_forward"] is True
    return rows, engine, lab


def test_publication_uses_the_grid_point_at_or_before_it_never_the_next():
    _, engine, lab = verified_v2()
    for pub in (ANCHOR + 60000, ANCHOR + 64999):
        assert (
            engine.forward_alert_timing(lab, pub)["price_snapshot_ts_ms"]
            == ANCHOR + 60000
        )


def test_quote_age_is_measured_at_publication_not_at_the_grid_point():
    rows, _, lab = verified_v2()
    ts = ANCHOR + 60000
    rows = [replace(r, quote_ts_ms=ts - 6000) if r.ts_ms == ts else r for r in rows]
    engine = target.TargetEvaluator(rows, contract_path=target.V2_CONTRACT_PATH)
    lab = engine.evaluate(ANCHOR, v2_base(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    assert (
        engine.forward_alert_timing(lab, ts)["timing"] != "unavailable"
    )  # 6.0s old at publication
    assert engine.forward_alert_timing(lab, ts + 4999) == dict(
        evaluation_only=True, timing="unavailable", reason="stale_alert_quote"
    )  # 10.999s old


# 7. provenance on both catalogs ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_forward_catalog_refuses_v1_labels_like_forward_timing_does():
    """Reproduced at 8b5b266: forward_event_catalog accepted v1 labels and stamped them as v2 forward windows,
    although forward_alert_timing refuses v1. Fixed on claude/instant-radar-v2-review.
    """
    rows = [replace(r, trade_count=3) for r in samples()]
    v1 = target.TargetEvaluator(rows)
    lab = v1.evaluate(ANCHOR, baseline(), "up", 300, ANCHOR + 300000)
    assert (
        lab["contract_version"] == "instant-target-v1"
        and lab["verified_forward"] is True
    )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        v1.forward_alert_timing(lab, ANCHOR)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        target.forward_event_catalog([lab])
    assert target.forward_event_catalog([]) == []
    assert (
        len(target.event_catalog([lab], target="verified_forward")) == 1
    )  # v1 generic catalog unchanged


def test_catalog_rejects_v2_labels_with_and_without_evaluator_hash():
    _, _, lab = verified_v2()
    forged = {k: v for k, v in lab.items() if k != "evaluator_sha256"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        target.forward_event_catalog([lab, {**forged, "anchor_ts_ms": ANCHOR + 400000}])

===== BEGIN SOURCE 109 | tests/test_instant_timed_baseline.py | sha256=bec7ae1bd1083c0480ec3fa7e03c04b692a40bce01c5dee8e0781b2c134b4607 =====
"""Synthetic ledger cases for baseline evidence timing and window boundaries."""

import math

import pytest

from test_instant_input_evidence_review import BTC, COIN, Tape
from instant_timed_baseline import build_timed_baseline


CUTOFF = 3_780_000
SIGMA_START, SIGMA_END = 120_000, 3_720_000
PACE_START, PACE_END = 150_000, 3_750_000


def history(*, flat_btc=False, missing_btc_close=None, active=True):
    tape = Tape(first=CUTOFF, stop=CUTOFF + 5000)
    tape.ack()
    watermark = 10
    for ts in range(100_000, PACE_END + 5001, 5000):
        steps = [ts, ts, ts, ts, ts + 180] if ts == PACE_END else None
        tape.hb(ts, watermark, steps=steps)
        tape.hb(ts, 10, BTC)
        if active and ts % 30000 == 0 and PACE_START <= ts < PACE_START + 60 * 30000:
            watermark += 1
            tape.trade(ts, watermark)
        if ts == PACE_END:
            # This receipt is just outside the baseline window.
            watermark += 1
            tape.trade(ts, watermark, size="10000")
        close = ts + 5000
        if SIGMA_START <= close <= SIGMA_END and close % 60000 == 0:
            n = (close - SIGMA_START) // 60000
            path = 0.015 * n - 0.005 * (n % 2)
            for pid, mid in (
                (COIN, 100 * math.exp(2 * path)),
                (BTC, 50000 if flat_btc else 50000 * math.exp(path)),
            ):
                if pid == BTC and close == missing_btc_close:
                    continue
                tape.send(
                    close - 1000,
                    "snapshot",
                    pid,
                    bids=[[str(mid * 0.9999), "2"]],
                    asks=[[str(mid * 1.0001), "1"]],
                )
    return tape


def test_timed_baseline_separates_closing_proof_from_computation_time():
    tape = history()
    early = build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=PACE_END + 100)
    assert early["volatility"]["return_count"] == 60
    assert early["pace"]["reason"] == "trade_row_processing_incomplete"

    ready = build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=PACE_END + 180)
    assert (ready["sigma_start_ts_ms"], ready["sigma_end_ts_ms"]) == (
        SIGMA_START,
        SIGMA_END,
    )
    assert (ready["pace_start_ts_ms"], ready["pace_end_ts_ms"]) == (
        PACE_START,
        PACE_END,
    )
    assert ready["volatility"]["beta"] == pytest.approx(2)
    assert ready["volatility"]["sigma_1m"] == pytest.approx(0.01 * math.sqrt(60 / 59))
    assert ready["pace"] == dict(
        status="ready",
        inputs_available_ts_ms=PACE_END + 180,
        window_count=120,
        pace_trade_median_30s=0.5,
        pace_quote_usd_median_30s="100",
    )
    assert ready["computation_completed_ts_ms"] == PACE_END + 5000
    assert early["known_packet_prefix_sha256"] != ready["known_packet_prefix_sha256"]

    # Later computation with the same evidence limit retains the same inputs.
    tape.hb(CUTOFF + 5000, 10, BTC)
    late = build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=PACE_END + 180)
    assert late["computation_completed_ts_ms"] == CUTOFF + 5000
    assert late["known_packet_prefix_sha256"] == ready["known_packet_prefix_sha256"]
    assert late["volatility"] == ready["volatility"] and late["pace"] == ready["pace"]


def test_missing_benchmark_close_does_not_discard_verified_activity():
    tape = history(missing_btc_close=SIGMA_START + 60000)
    result = build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=PACE_END + 180)
    assert result["volatility"] == dict(
        status="unavailable",
        reason="stale_book_observation",
        product_id=BTC,
        missing_close_ts_ms=SIGMA_START + 60000,
    )
    assert result["pace"]["status"] == "ready"


def test_flat_benchmark_fallback_and_proven_empty_pace():
    tape = history(flat_btc=True, active=False)
    result = build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=PACE_END + 180)
    assert result["volatility"]["beta"] == 1
    assert result["volatility"]["beta_fallback"] is True
    assert result["pace"]["pace_trade_median_30s"] == 0
    assert result["pace"]["pace_quote_usd_median_30s"] == "0"


def test_elapsed_time_alone_cannot_supply_baseline_evidence():
    tape = Tape(first=CUTOFF, stop=CUTOFF + 5000)
    tape.now = CUTOFF
    result = build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=CUTOFF)
    for component in (result["volatility"], result["pace"]):
        assert component["status"] == "unavailable"
        assert component["reason"] == "trade_coverage_not_yet_proven"
        assert "inputs_available_ts_ms" not in component


def test_baseline_rejects_evidence_time_before_inventory_or_after_actual_clock():
    tape = Tape(first=CUTOFF, stop=CUTOFF + 5000, start_clock=1000)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="inventory was not yet recorded"):
        build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=999)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="evidence time has not been reached"):
        build_timed_baseline(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, asof=1001)

===== BEGIN SOURCE 110 | tests/test_instant_timed_features.py | sha256=a714f39b0f2239984c310b4099b0d5648a5fc7e96dc7dbc11b49415e86b3301c =====
"""Synthetic receipt boundaries and formula oracles for the timed feature row."""

import math

import pytest

from test_instant_input_evidence_review import BTC, COIN, Tape
from instant_timed_features import build_timed_features


CUTOFF = 3810000
FAST = CUTOFF - 30000
SLOW = CUTOFF - 180000
CANDLE_START = FAST - 900000


def feature_tape(*, traded_candles=15, unknown_side=False, tape_type=Tape):
    tape = tape_type(first=CUTOFF, stop=CUTOFF + 5000)
    tape.ack()
    watermark = 10
    for ts in range(100000, CUTOFF + 1, 5000):
        steps = [ts, ts, ts, ts, ts + 180] if ts == CUTOFF else None
        tape.hb(ts, watermark, steps=steps)
        tape.hb(ts, 10, BTC)
        if ts >= FAST:
            # A trade exactly at FAST is used by F2 and excluded by F4.
            if ts <= FAST + 20000 or ts == CUTOFF:
                watermark += 1
                tape.trade(
                    ts,
                    watermark,
                    price="10000" if ts in (FAST, CUTOFF) else "100",
                    size="0.01" if ts == FAST else "1",
                    side=(
                        "unknown"
                        if unknown_side and ts == FAST
                        else "buy" if ts == FAST + 20000 else "sell"
                    ),
                )
        elif ts % 30000 == 0 and (
            ts < CANDLE_START or ts < CANDLE_START + traded_candles * 60000
        ):
            watermark += 1
            tape.trade(
                ts, watermark, price="99" if ts % 60000 == 0 else "101", size="1"
            )

        if ts == CUTOFF:
            # A BBO at b is excluded by endpoints and F3.
            tape.send(ts, "snapshot", bids=[["1000", "1"]], asks=[["1002", "9"]])
            break
        next_cut = ts + 5000
        i = ts // 60000
        coin_mid, btc_mid = 100 + i % 2, 50000 + 100 * (i % 2)
        if next_cut in (SLOW, FAST, CUTOFF):
            coin_mid, btc_mid = {
                SLOW: (101, 50000),
                FAST: (103, 50300),
                CUTOFF: (110, 51000),
            }[next_cut]
        for pid, mid in ((COIN, coin_mid), (BTC, btc_mid)):
            tape.send(
                ts + 1000,
                "snapshot",
                pid,
                bids=[[str(mid - 1), "3"]],
                asks=[[str(mid + 1), "1"]],
            )
    return tape


def features(tape, direction="up", *, asof=CUTOFF + 180):
    return build_timed_features(tape.rec, COIN, CUTOFF, direction, asof=asof)


def test_all_five_formulas_with_direction_and_receipt_boundaries():
    tape = feature_tape()
    row = features(tape)
    down = features(tape, "down")
    vol = row["baseline"]["volatility"]
    sigma = row["sigma_used"]
    median = float(row["baseline"]["pace"]["pace_quote_usd_median_30s"])
    recent, prior = math.log(110 / 103), math.log(103 / 101)
    expected = {
        "F1": (recent - prior / 5) / sigma,
        "F2": 0.6 * math.log1p(500 / median),
        "F3": 0.5,
        "F4": (recent / sigma) / (1 + math.log(101 / 99) / (sigma * math.sqrt(15))),
        "F5": (math.log(110 / 101) - vol["beta"] * math.log(51000 / 50000)) / sigma,
    }
    for name, value in expected.items():
        assert row["features"][name]["value"] == pytest.approx(value)
        assert down["features"][name]["value"] == pytest.approx(
            0 if name == "F4" else -value
        )
    assert row["controls"]["cutoff_spread_bps"] == pytest.approx(2 / 110 * 10000)
    assert row["controls"]["unsigned_activity"]["verified_match_count"] == 5
    assert row["candle_end_ts_ms"] == FAST
    assert row["candle_end_ts_ms"] != row["baseline"]["sigma_end_ts_ms"]
    assert row["features"]["F2"]["evidence_available_ts_ms"] == CUTOFF + 180
    assert row["baseline"]["computation_completed_ts_ms"] == CUTOFF + 180
    assert row["computation_completed_ts_ms"] == CUTOFF + 180
    assert row["later_diagnostic"] is True


def test_late_proof_cannot_rewrite_cutoff_evidence():
    tape = feature_tape()
    before = features(tape, asof=CUTOFF + 1)
    after = features(tape)
    repeated = features(tape, asof=CUTOFF + 1)
    assert before["features"]["F2"]["reason"] == "trade_row_processing_incomplete"
    assert after["features"]["F2"]["status"] == "ready"
    assert before["features"] == repeated["features"]
    assert (
        before["baseline"]["known_packet_prefix_sha256"]
        != after["baseline"]["known_packet_prefix_sha256"]
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("traded", [9, 10])
def test_f4_uses_verified_empty_minutes_without_making_up_candles(traded):
    row = features(feature_tape(traded_candles=traded))
    candles = row["controls"]["candle_range"]
    assert candles["traded_candles"] == traded
    assert candles["verified_empty_candles"] == 15 - traded
    if traded == 9:
        assert row["features"]["F4"]["reason"] == "too_few_traded_candles"
    else:
        assert row["features"]["F4"]["status"] == "ready"


def test_unknown_taker_side_keeps_unsigned_evidence():
    row = features(feature_tape(unknown_side=True))
    assert row["features"]["F2"]["reason"] == "unknown_match_side"
    assert row["controls"]["unsigned_activity"]["unknown_side_usd"] == "100.00"
    assert row["controls"]["unsigned_activity"]["verified_match_count"] == 5


@pytest.mark.parametrize("stale", [False, True])
def test_f3_carry_same_millisecond_and_full_window_age(stale):
    cutoff = 60000
    tape = Tape(first=cutoff, stop=cutoff + 5000)
    tape.ack()
    for ts in range(25000, cutoff, 5000):
        tape.hb(ts, 10)
        if ts in (25000, 40000, 50000) or (ts == 35000 and not stale):
            tape.snap(ts)
        if ts == 40000:
            # The final state at 40000 holds for ten seconds, with no weight
            # on the previous snapshot at the same millisecond.
            tape.send(ts, "snapshot", bids=[["99", "1"]], asks=[["101", "3"]])
    tape.now = cutoff
    row = build_timed_features(tape.rec, COIN, cutoff, "up", asof=cutoff)
    f3 = row["features"]["F3"]
    if stale:
        assert f3 == dict(
            status="unavailable", reason="stale_book_span", first_invalid_ts_ms=35000
        )
    else:
        assert f3["value"] == pytest.approx((1 / 3 - 1 / 2 + 1 / 3) / 3)
        assert row["features"]["F4"]["status"] == "unavailable"


def test_f3_detects_a_heartbeat_outage_inside_an_otherwise_fresh_window():
    cutoff = 60000
    tape = Tape(first=cutoff, stop=cutoff + 5000)
    tape.ack()
    for ts in range(25000, cutoff, 5000):
        if ts != 40000:
            tape.hb(ts, 10)
        tape.snap(ts)
    tape.now = cutoff
    row = build_timed_features(tape.rec, COIN, cutoff, "up", asof=cutoff)
    assert row["controls"]["cutoff_spread_bps"] is not None
    assert row["features"]["F3"] == dict(
        status="unavailable",
        reason="stale_heartbeat_span",
        first_invalid_ts_ms=40000,
    )


def test_f3_delayed_payload_only_changes_a_later_evidence_view():
    cutoff = 60000
    tape = Tape(first=cutoff, stop=cutoff + 5000)
    tape.ack()
    for ts in range(25000, cutoff, 5000):
        tape.hb(ts, 10)
        if ts in (25000, 35000, 45000, 50000):
            tape.snap(ts)
    tape.send(
        59000,
        "snapshot",
        steps=[59000, 59000, 59000, 59000, 60100],
        bids=[["99", "1"]],
        asks=[["101", "3"]],
    )
    early = build_timed_features(tape.rec, COIN, cutoff, "up", asof=cutoff)
    late = build_timed_features(tape.rec, COIN, cutoff, "up", asof=60100)
    assert early["features"]["F3"]["value"] == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
    assert late["features"]["F3"]["value"] == pytest.approx((29000 / 3 - 500) / 30000)
    assert late["features"]["F3"]["evidence_available_ts_ms"] == 60100
    assert early["computation_completed_ts_ms"] == 60100
    assert early["later_diagnostic"]

===== BEGIN SOURCE 111 | tests/test_instant_timing_attempt_verifier.py | sha256=e97ee3633c55f2c766633399cff68e28a928a8fdf723efe7948a33cb6cdee86d =====
import json

import pytest

from test_instant_stream_journal import BTC, COIN, Clock, send
from instant_delayed_decision import prepare_decision
from instant_input_evidence import _file_sha
from instant_stream_journal import StreamJournal, read_segment
from verify_instant_timing_attempt import verify


def test_timing_verifier_keeps_missed_slots_and_checks_consumer_receipts(tmp_path):
    channels = ["matches", "heartbeat", "level2_batch", "ticker"]
    plan = dict(
        products=[BTC, COIN],
        channels=channels,
        warmup_ms=0,
        cohort_count=2,
        cohort_step_ms=5000,
        cohort_evidence_offsets_ms=[1500, 5000],
        diagnostic_deadlines_ms={"1500": 2500, "5000": 6000},
    )
    path = tmp_path / "capture.sqlite"
    clock = Clock()
    with StreamJournal(
        path,
        [BTC, COIN],
        30000,
        40000,
        clock_ms=clock,
        monotonic_ns=clock.mono,
        indexed=True,
    ) as journal:
        connection = journal.open_connection()
        clock.now = 30000
        send(
            journal,
            connection,
            type="subscriptions",
            channels=[dict(name=c, product_ids=[BTC, COIN]) for c in channels],
        )
        clock.now = 31500
        decision = prepare_decision(journal.evidence, COIN, 30000, "up", asof=31500)
        receipt = journal.append_publication("fast", decision)
        clock.now = 41000
        journal.end_connection(connection)
        journal.finish()

    (tmp_path / "PLAN.json").write_text(json.dumps(plan), encoding="utf-8")
    observation = dict(
        publication_id="fast",
        decision_sha256=receipt.decision_sha256,
        commit_observed_ts_ms=receipt.local_commit_observed_ts_ms,
        consumer_seen_ts_ms=32500,
    )
    reported = dict(
        observation,
        cutoff_ts_ms=30000,
        knowledge_asof_ts_ms=31500,
        decision_completed_ts_ms=31500,
        product_id=COIN,
        direction="up",
    )
    report = dict(
        plan,
        stop="transport_error",
        segment=read_segment(path),
        journal_sha256=_file_sha(path),
        first_ack_ts_ms=30000,
        recovered_pending_ingress_frames=0,
        diagnostic_rows=[reported],
    )
    (tmp_path / "REPORT.json").write_text(json.dumps(report), encoding="utf-8")
    receipt_path = tmp_path / "observed.jsonl"
    receipt_path.write_text(json.dumps(observation) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    result = verify(tmp_path, plan_sha256=_file_sha(tmp_path / "PLAN.json"))
    assert result["publication_prefixes_verified"] == 1
    assert result["schedule_results"]["1500"]["products"][COIN] == dict(
        planned=2,
        journaled=1,
        complete_inputs=0,
        observed=1,
        within_bound=1,
        complete_and_within_bound=0,
    )
    missing = result["schedule_results"]["5000"]["products"][COIN]
    assert (missing["planned"], missing["journaled"], missing["observed"]) == (2, 0, 0)

    observation["consumer_seen_ts_ms"] = 31499
    receipt_path.write_text(json.dumps(observation) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="consumer observation differs"):
        verify(tmp_path)

===== END SOURCE 111 =====
